last_fcy = 0

# Settings — loaded from settings_store
_settings = settings_store.load()

STATE.reduced_motion = _settings["reduced_motion"]
display_mode = _settings["display_mode"]
res_idx = _settings["resolution_idx"]
master_volume = _settings["master_volume"]
music_volume = _settings["music_volume"]
sfx_volume = _settings["sfx_volume"]

settings_active_tab = 0

def _persist_settings():
    settings_store.save({
        "display_mode": display_mode,
        "resolution_idx": res_idx,
        "master_volume": master_volume,
        "music_volume": music_volume,
        "sfx_volume": sfx_volume,
        "reduced_motion": STATE.reduced_motion,
    })

audio.set_master_volume(master_volume)
if hasattr(audio, "set_music_volume"):
    audio.set_music_volume(music_volume / 100.0)

def handle_settings_event(event):
    global settings_active_tab
    global display_mode, res_idx
    global master_volume, music_volume, sfx_volume

    if event.type != pygame.MOUSEBUTTONDOWN or event.button != 1:
        return None

    mp = get_scaled_mouse()

    # Back button
    if menu.settings_back_btn.rect.collidepoint(mp):
        _persist_settings()
        return "back"

    # Tab switching
    for i, rect in enumerate(menu.settings_tab_rects):
        if rect.collidepoint(mp):
            settings_active_tab = i
            audio.play("menu_click", volume=0.4)
            return None

    if settings_active_tab == 0:
        # DISPLAY
        if menu.settings_res_left_rect.collidepoint(mp):
            res_idx = (res_idx - 1) % len(menu.SETTINGS_RESOLUTIONS)
            _persist_settings()
        elif menu.settings_res_right_rect.collidepoint(mp):
            res_idx = (res_idx + 1) % len(menu.SETTINGS_RESOLUTIONS)
            _persist_settings()
        elif menu.settings_display_left_rect.collidepoint(mp):
            display_mode = (display_mode - 1) % len(menu.DISPLAY_MODES)
            _persist_settings()
        elif menu.settings_display_right_rect.collidepoint(mp):
            display_mode = (display_mode + 1) % len(menu.DISPLAY_MODES)
            _persist_settings()

    elif settings_active_tab == 1:
        # AUDIO — arrows step by 5, clicking the bar jumps to that position
        step = 5

        # Master
        if menu.settings_master_left_rect.collidepoint(mp):
            master_volume = max(0, master_volume - step)
        elif menu.settings_master_right_rect.collidepoint(mp):
            master_volume = min(100, master_volume + step)
        elif menu.settings_master_bar_rect.collidepoint(mp):
            t = (mp[0] - menu.settings_master_bar_rect.x) / menu.settings_master_bar_rect.width
            master_volume = max(0, min(100, int(t * 100)))
        # Music
        elif menu.settings_music_left_rect.collidepoint(mp):
            music_volume = max(0, music_volume - step)
        elif menu.settings_music_right_rect.collidepoint(mp):
            music_volume = min(100, music_volume + step)
        elif menu.settings_music_bar_rect.collidepoint(mp):
            t = (mp[0] - menu.settings_music_bar_rect.x) / menu.settings_music_bar_rect.width
            music_volume = max(0, min(100, int(t * 100)))
        # SFX
        elif menu.settings_sfx_left_rect.collidepoint(mp):
            sfx_volume = max(0, sfx_volume - step)
        elif menu.settings_sfx_right_rect.collidepoint(mp):
            sfx_volume = min(100, sfx_volume + step)
        elif menu.settings_sfx_bar_rect.collidepoint(mp):
            t = (mp[0] - menu.settings_sfx_bar_rect.x) / menu.settings_sfx_bar_rect.width
            sfx_volume = max(0, min(100, int(t * 100)))
        else:
            return None

        # Apply audio changes immediately
        audio.set_master_volume(master_volume)
        if hasattr(audio, "set_music_volume"):
            audio.set_music_volume(music_volume / 100.0)
        if hasattr(audio, "set_sfx_volume"):
            audio.set_sfx_volume(sfx_volume / 100.0)
        _persist_settings()

    else:
        # ACCESSIBILITY
        if menu.settings_reduced_motion_rect.collidepoint(mp):
            STATE.reduced_motion = not STATE.reduced_motion
            audio.play("menu_click", volume=0.4)
            _persist_settings()

    return None
def trigger_transition(target_state):
    global transitioning, next_state, fade_speed
    transitioning, next_state, fade_speed = True, target_state, 12
def start_cutscene(shots, after_state, use_fades=True):
    global active_cutscene_shots, cutscene_shot_idx, cutscene_char_idx
    global cutscene_timer, cutscene_start_time, cutscene_after
    global cutscene_transition_timer, cutscene_use_fades
    active_cutscene_shots = shots
    cutscene_shot_idx = 0
    cutscene_char_idx = 0
    cutscene_timer = 0
    cutscene_start_time = pygame.time.get_ticks()
    cutscene_after = after_state
    cutscene_transition_timer = 0
    cutscene_use_fades = use_fades
    gc.reset_dialogue_slide()
_current_ambience = set()
_prev_ambience_state = ""
MENU_MUSIC_FILE = "menu_theme.mp3"
_menu_music_active = False
_menu_music_failed = False
MENU_MUSIC_STATES = {"MENU", "SLOT_SELECT", "SETTINGS", "MULTIPLAYER"}

def _set_menu_music(should_play):
    """Start/stop streamed menu music without restarting it every frame."""
    global _menu_music_active, _menu_music_failed
    if should_play:
        if not _menu_music_active and not _menu_music_failed:
            _menu_music_active = bool(audio.play_music(MENU_MUSIC_FILE, volume=0.48))
            _menu_music_failed = not _menu_music_active
    else:
        if _menu_music_active:
            audio.stop_music()
            _menu_music_active = False

def _play_typewriter_tick(ch, idx, volume=0.11):
    """Subtle audio feedback for typewriter text reveal."""
    if not ch or ch.isspace():
        return
    # Avoid a machine-gun sound: skip some characters, but always tick punctuation lightly.
    if idx % 2 != 0 and ch not in ".!?":
        return
    try:
        audio.play("type_tick", volume=volume)
    except Exception:
        pass
def _set_ambience(sounds_to_play):
    global _current_ambience
    for name in list(_current_ambience):
        if name not in sounds_to_play:
            audio.stop(name)
    for name in sounds_to_play:
        if name not in _current_ambience:
            audio.play(name, volume=0.3, loops=-1)
    _current_ambience = set(sounds_to_play)
def apply_display_mode(mode):
    """Apply display mode: 0=Windowed, 1=Fullscreen"""
    global screen, display_mode
    flags = pygame.SCALED | pygame.RESIZABLE
    if mode == 1:  # Fullscreen
        flags = pygame.SCALED | pygame.FULLSCREEN
    try:
        new_surf = pygame.display.set_mode((WIDTH, HEIGHT), flags)
        display_mode = mode
        # Propagate to all modules
        import config, game_core, menu as m
        config.screen = new_surf
        game_core.screen = new_surf
        m.screen = new_surf
        screen = new_surf
        print(f"Display mode set to: {['Windowed', 'Fullscreen'][mode]}")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Display mode change failed ({e}), falling back to Windowed")
        # Fallback to safe windowed mode
        try:
            fallback = pygame.display.set_mode((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SCALED)
            import config, game_core, menu as m
            config.screen = fallback
            game_core.screen = fallback
            m.screen = fallback
            screen = fallback
            display_mode = 0
        except Exception as e2:
            print(f"FATAL: Cannot create display surface: {e2}")
def _mission_report_data(success=True):
    """Build after-action stats for mission complete / failure screens."""
    end_t = gc.mission_end_time or pygame.time.get_ticks()
    start_t = gc.mission_start_time or end_t
    elapsed_sec = max(0, (end_t - start_t) // 1000)
    accuracy = int((gc.shots_hit / gc.shots_fired) * 100) if gc.shots_fired > 0 else 0
    civilians_helped = 0
    try:
        civilians_helped = sum(1 for n in gc.walk_npcs if n.get("has_given"))
    except Exception:
        civilians_helped = 0
    try:
        rations_used = max(0, 2 - int(gc.player_rations))
    except Exception:
        rations_used = 0
    if getattr(gc, "active_mission", "MISSION1") == "MISSION2":
        intel_found = bool(getattr(gc, "mission2_evidence_uploaded", False))
    else:
        intel_found = bool(getattr(gc, "notebook_collected", False) or getattr(gc, "warehouse_intel_given", False))
    stealth_clean = bool(getattr(gc, "stealth_clean", False))
    score = 0
    if success:
        score += 40
    score += min(25, int(gc.hostiles_killed) * 3)
    score += min(15, accuracy // 5)
    score += civilians_helped * 8
    score += 10 if intel_found else 0
    score += 8 if stealth_clean else 0
    score -= max(0, (elapsed_sec - 900) // 45) * 2
    score = max(0, min(100, score))
    if not success:
        rank = "FAILED"
    elif score >= 92:
        rank = "S"
    elif score >= 82:
        rank = "A"
    elif score >= 68:
        rank = "B"
    elif score >= 52:
        rank = "C"
    else:
        rank = "D"
    return {
        "kills": int(gc.hostiles_killed),
        "shots_fired": int(gc.shots_fired),
        "shots_hit": int(gc.shots_hit),
        "accuracy": accuracy,
        "time_str": f"{elapsed_sec // 60:02d}:{elapsed_sec % 60:02d}",
        "civilians_helped": civilians_helped,
        "rations_used": rations_used,
        "intel_found": intel_found,
        "stealth_clean": stealth_clean,
        "score": score,
        "rank": rank,
    }
def draw_slot_select_screen():
    """Choose a save slot for New Campaign or Continue."""
    global _slot_rects
    menu.draw_menu_background()
    dim = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    dim.fill((0, 0, 0, 180))
    screen.blit(dim, (0, 0))
    title_text = "SELECT SAVE SLOT" if slot_select_purpose == "new" else "CONTINUE — SELECT SAVE"
    sub_text = ("Choose a slot to overwrite/use for the new campaign."
                if slot_select_purpose == "new"
                else "Choose an existing save file to continue from.")
    title = pygame.font.SysFont("Consolas", 44, bold=True).render(title_text, True, C_WHITE)
    sub = font_terminal_small.render(sub_text, True, (155, 155, 145))
    screen.blit(title, (WIDTH // 2 - title.get_width() // 2, 86))
    screen.blit(sub, (WIDTH // 2 - sub.get_width() // 2, 136))
    _slot_rects = []
    data = save_system.load_data()
    mouse = pygame.mouse.get_pos()
    card_w, card_h = 260, 230
    gap = 28
    start_x = WIDTH // 2 - (card_w * 3 + gap * 2) // 2
    y = 220
    for i in range(3):
        x = start_x + i * (card_w + gap)
        rect = pygame.Rect(x, y, card_w, card_h)
        _slot_rects.append(rect)
        slot = data["slots"][i]
        selectable = slot_select_purpose == "new" or slot.get("occupied")
        hovered = rect.collidepoint(mouse) and selectable
        card = pygame.Surface((card_w, card_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        card.fill((7, 12, 18, 238 if hovered else (150 if not selectable else 218)))
        pygame.draw.rect(card, C_ORANGE if hovered else ((50, 50, 55) if not selectable else (45, 78, 105)), card.get_rect(), 2 if hovered else 1)
        pygame.draw.rect(card, C_KRAP_BLUE, (0, 0, 4, card_h))
        screen.blit(card, (x, y))
        header = font_terminal.render(f"SAVE SLOT {i + 1}", True, C_ORANGE if hovered else (120, 180, 220))
        screen.blit(header, (x + 22, y + 20))
        if slot.get("occupied"):
            status = font_prompt.render("ACTIVE FILE", True, (90, 220, 135))
            mission = slot.get("last_completed_label", "Campaign started")
            completed = slot.get("last_completed_mission")
            if completed:
                l1 = "LAST COMPLETED"
                l2 = mission
            else:
                l1 = "IN PROGRESS"
                l2 = mission
            tline = f"TOTAL TIME: {slot.get('total_time', '00:00')}"
            updated = "UPDATED: " + save_system.format_timestamp(slot.get("updated_at"))
        else:
            status = font_prompt.render("EMPTY", True, (120, 120, 120))
            l1 = "NO CAMPAIGN DATA"
            l2 = "Start a new campaign"
            tline = "TOTAL TIME: --:--"
            updated = "UPDATED: never"
        screen.blit(status, (x + 22, y + 58))
        for j, line in enumerate((l1, l2[:30], tline, updated)):
            col = (170, 170, 160) if j != 0 else (80, 140, 180)
            surf = font_terminal_small.render(line, True, col)
            screen.blit(surf, (x + 22, y + 96 + j * 28))
        prompt_text = "CLICK TO USE SLOT" if selectable else "EMPTY"
        prompt = font_terminal_small.render(prompt_text, True, C_ORANGE if hovered else (90, 95, 100))
        screen.blit(prompt, (x + card_w // 2 - prompt.get_width() // 2, y + card_h - 34))
    footer = font_terminal_small.render("Save file: save_data.json", True, (85, 90, 95))
    screen.blit(footer, (WIDTH // 2 - footer.get_width() // 2, HEIGHT - 70))
def start_briefing(kind="warehouse"):
    """Start a purely cosmetic interstitial screen. No gameplay state is changed here."""
    global briefing_timer, briefing_kind, _last_briefing_title_chars
    briefing_timer = 0
    briefing_kind = kind
    _last_briefing_title_chars = 0
    audio.play("radio_static", volume=0.22)
    audio.play("menu_click", volume=0.35)
def finish_briefing():
    """End the cosmetic briefing and enter gameplay."""
    global game_state
    if briefing_kind == "warehouse":
        gc.start_mission(preserve_timer=True)
        game_state = "GAME"
        pygame.mouse.set_visible(False)
def draw_briefing_screen():
    """Draw KRAP signal / mission briefing presentation screen. Cosmetic only."""
    global _last_briefing_title_chars
    t = briefing_timer
    # Background: terminal-blue dark gradient, no fade-to-black dependency.
    for y in range(HEIGHT):
        p = y / HEIGHT
        col = (int(4 + p * 6), int(8 + p * 12), int(15 + p * 22))
        pygame.draw.line(screen, col, (0, y), (WIDTH, y))
    # Moving scan bands / signal noise.
    rng = random.Random(t // 2)
    for _ in range(55):
        nx = rng.randint(0, WIDTH - 1)
        ny = rng.randint(0, HEIGHT - 1)
        shade = rng.randint(12, 32)
        screen.set_at((nx, ny), (shade, shade + 4, shade + 10))
    for y in range((t * 2) % 6, HEIGHT, 6):
        pygame.draw.line(screen, (0, 0, 0, 32), (0, y), (WIDTH, y), 1)
    sweep_y = int((t * 2.4) % (HEIGHT + 120)) - 60
    sweep = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, 80), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    for i in range(80):
        a = int(36 * (1 - abs(i - 40) / 40))
        pygame.draw.line(sweep, (45, 110, 160, max(0, a)), (0, i), (WIDTH, i))
    screen.blit(sweep, (0, sweep_y))
    # Main terminal panel. Wider/taller so long status lines and objectives do not clip.
    panel_w, panel_h = min(860, WIDTH - 110), 470
    panel_x = WIDTH // 2 - panel_w // 2
    panel_y = HEIGHT // 2 - panel_h // 2 - 4
    panel = pygame.Surface((panel_w, panel_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    panel.fill((5, 10, 17, 232))
    pygame.draw.rect(panel, (45, 88, 126, 170), panel.get_rect(), 1)
    pygame.draw.rect(panel, C_KRAP_BLUE, (0, 0, 4, panel_h))
    pygame.draw.line(panel, (45, 88, 126, 120), (18, 64), (panel_w - 18, 64), 1)
    screen.blit(panel, (panel_x, panel_y))
    # Slight glitch strips.
    if 18 < t < 80 or (t // 45) % 5 == 0:
        for _ in range(3):
            gy = panel_y + rng.randint(20, panel_h - 20)
            gh = rng.randint(2, 7)
            gx = panel_x + rng.randint(-16, 16)
            strip = pygame.Surface((panel_w, gh), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            strip.fill((90, 170, 220, rng.randint(18, 45)))
            screen.blit(strip, (gx, gy))
    def blit_alpha(surf, x, y, alpha):
        surf.set_alpha(max(0, min(255, int(alpha))))
        screen.blit(surf, (x, y))
    boot_alpha = min(255, t * 7)
    eyebrow = font_terminal_small.render("KRAP SIGNAL INTERCEPTED", True, (85, 170, 220))
    blit_alpha(eyebrow, panel_x + 24, panel_y + 20, boot_alpha)
    stamp = font_terminal_small.render("FIELD COPY // UNVERIFIED", True, (190, 80, 60))
    blit_alpha(stamp, panel_x + panel_w - stamp.get_width() - 22, panel_y + 20, boot_alpha)
    # Type-on title.
    title_text = "MISSION BRIEFING"
    title_chars = min(len(title_text), max(0, (t - 28) // 2))
    if title_chars > _last_briefing_title_chars:
        ch = title_text[title_chars - 1]
        _play_typewriter_tick(ch, title_chars - 1, volume=0.095)
        _last_briefing_title_chars = title_chars
    title = pygame.font.SysFont("Consolas", 40, bold=True).render(title_text[:title_chars], True, (220, 225, 220))
    blit_alpha(title, panel_x + 36, panel_y + 86, 255)
    loc_alpha = min(255, max(0, (t - 62) * 6))
    lines = [
        ("OPERATION", "BLACK NOTEBOOK"),
        ("LOCATION", "KILEMENO HARBOR DISTRICT / WAREHOUSE ROW"),
        ("STATUS", "OFFSHORE RIGS DESTROYED — UNKNOWN ATTACK CRAFT CONFIRMED"),
    ]
    value_x = panel_x + 170
    max_value_w = panel_w - 210
    for i, (label, value) in enumerate(lines):
        y = panel_y + 150 + i * 42
        lbl = font_terminal_small.render(label, True, (65, 120, 160))
        # The STATUS string is long, so use the smaller prompt font if needed.
        val_font = font_terminal
        val = val_font.render(value, True, (190, 190, 176))
        if val.get_width() > max_value_w:
            val_font = font_prompt
            val = val_font.render(value, True, (190, 190, 176))
        blit_alpha(lbl, panel_x + 42, y, loc_alpha - i * 35)
        blit_alpha(val, value_x, y - 4, loc_alpha - i * 35)
    obj_alpha = min(255, max(0, (t - 128) * 5))
    obj_divider_y = panel_y + 292
    pygame.draw.line(screen, (35, 68, 96), (panel_x + 36, obj_divider_y), (panel_x + panel_w - 36, obj_divider_y), 1)
    obj_title = font_terminal.render("OBJECTIVES", True, (255, 150, 60))
    blit_alpha(obj_title, panel_x + 42, panel_y + 312, obj_alpha)
    objectives = [
        "Infiltrate the warehouse perimeter",
        "Locate and recover the black notebook",
        "Escape through the service exit",
    ]
    for i, txt in enumerate(objectives):
        alpha = obj_alpha - i * 30
        bullet = font_terminal.render("■", True, (65, 150, 210))
        line = font_prompt.render(txt, True, (205, 205, 195))
        row_y = panel_y + 350 + i * 26
        blit_alpha(bullet, panel_x + 48, row_y, alpha)
        blit_alpha(line, panel_x + 72, row_y, alpha)
    # Bottom status / prompt.
    bar_w = int((panel_w - 72) * min(1.0, max(0, (t - 42) / max(1, BRIEFING_DURATION - 92))))
    bar_y = panel_y + panel_h - 34
    pygame.draw.rect(screen, (10, 20, 30), (panel_x + 36, bar_y, panel_w - 72, 5))
    pygame.draw.rect(screen, C_KRAP_BLUE, (panel_x + 36, bar_y, bar_w, 5))
    prompt_txt = "PRESS SPACE TO DEPLOY" if t > 115 else "DECODING FIELD TRANSMISSION..."
    prompt_col = C_ORANGE if t > 115 else (95, 125, 145)
    prompt = font_terminal_small.render(prompt_txt, True, prompt_col)
    if t <= 115 or (pygame.time.get_ticks() // 450) % 2 == 0:
        blit_alpha(prompt, WIDTH // 2 - prompt.get_width() // 2, panel_y + panel_h + 22, min(255, t * 5))
    draw_scanlines(screen)
    draw_vignette(screen, strength=155)
def _apply_dev_args():
    """Small developer shortcuts for testing campaign entry points."""
    global game_state, mission_complete_saved
    args = set(sys.argv[1:])
    if "--dev-mission2" in args or "--m2" in args:
        # Start at the full Mission 2 intro cutscene, then enter gameplay normally.
        gc.mission_start_time = pygame.time.get_ticks()
        gc.mission_end_time = 0
        mission_complete_saved = False
        start_cutscene(gc.mission2_intro_shots, "MISSION2", use_fades=False)
        game_state = "CUTSCENE"
        pygame.mouse.set_visible(True)
    elif "--dev-mission2-game" in args or "--m2-game" in args:
        # Skip the intro and spawn directly into Route 12 gameplay.
        gc.mission_start_time = pygame.time.get_ticks()
        gc.mission_end_time = 0
        mission_complete_saved = False
        gc.start_mission2(preserve_timer=True)
        game_state = "GAME"
        pygame.mouse.set_visible(False)
_apply_dev_args()
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# MAIN LOOP
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
running = True
while running:
    mouse_x, mouse_y = pygame.mouse.get_pos()
    keys = pygame.key.get_pressed()
    if hasattr(audio, "update"):
        audio.update()
    pygame.mouse.set_visible(game_state not in ("GAME", "WALK"))
    for event in pygame.event.get():
        if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
            running = False
        if not transitioning:
            if game_state == "SLOT_SELECT":
                if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key == pygame.K_ESCAPE:
                    game_state = "MENU"
                if event.type == pygame.MOUSEBUTTONDOWN and event.button == 1:
                    data = save_system.load_data()
                    for i, rect in enumerate(_slot_rects):
                        if rect.collidepoint(mouse_x, mouse_y):
                            if slot_select_purpose == "continue" and not data["slots"][i].get("occupied"):
                                continue
                            save_system.select_slot(i)
                            audio.play("menu_click", volume=0.5)
                            if slot_select_purpose == "new":
                                save_system.start_new_campaign(i)
                                gc.mission_start_time = pygame.time.get_ticks()
                                gc.mission_end_time = 0
                                mission_complete_saved = False
                                start_cutscene(gc.cutscene1_shots, "WALK", use_fades=False)
                                trigger_transition("CUTSCENE")
                            else:
                                slot = save_system.get_slot(i)
                                if slot.get("last_completed_mission") == "MISSION_1":
                                    gc.mission_start_time = pygame.time.get_ticks()
                                    gc.mission_end_time = 0
                                    mission_complete_saved = False
                                    start_cutscene(gc.mission2_intro_shots, "MISSION2", use_fades=False)
                                    trigger_transition("CUTSCENE")
                                elif slot.get("last_completed_mission") == "MISSION_2":
                                    menu_notice = "MISSION 2 COMPLETE — MISSION 3 NOT AVAILABLE YET"
                                    menu_notice_timer = 220
                                    game_state = "MENU"
                                else:
                                    menu_notice = "SAVE SELECTED — NO CHECKPOINT RESUME YET"
                                    menu_notice_timer = 200
                                    game_state = "MENU"
                            break
            elif game_state == "MENU":
                if menu.buttons[0].is_clicked(event, (mouse_x, mouse_y)) and save_system.has_any_save():
                    slot_select_purpose = "continue"
                    game_state = "SLOT_SELECT"
                if menu.buttons[1].is_clicked(event, (mouse_x, mouse_y)):
                    slot_select_purpose = "new"
                    game_state = "SLOT_SELECT"
                if menu.buttons[2].is_clicked(event, (mouse_x, mouse_y)):
                    mp_state = "browser"
                    mp_selected = -1
                    mp_kick_msg = ""
                    game_state = "MULTIPLAYER"
                if menu.buttons[3].is_clicked(event, (mouse_x, mouse_y)):
                    game_state = "SETTINGS"
                if menu.buttons[4].is_clicked(event, (mouse_x, mouse_y)):
                    running = False
            elif game_state == "MULTIPLAYER":
                if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key == pygame.K_ESCAPE:
                    if mp_state == "kicked":
                        mp_state = "browser"
                        mp_kick_msg = ""
                        mp_selected = -1
                    elif menu._dm_open_user is not None:
                        menu.close_dm()
                    else:
                        game_state = "MENU"
                if event.type == pygame.MOUSEWHEEL and mp_state == "browser":
                    if menu._mp_tab == "SERVERS":
                        menu.scroll_server_list(-event.y)
                    elif menu._mp_tab == "CONNECTIONS":
                        menu.scroll_dm_list(-event.y)
                if mp_state == "browser":
                    if menu._mp_tab == "CONNECTIONS" and menu._dm_open_user is not None:
                        if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN:
                            if event.key == pygame.K_RETURN:
                                menu.send_dm_message()
                            elif event.key == pygame.K_BACKSPACE:
                                menu._dm_input = menu._dm_input[:-1]
                            elif event.unicode and event.unicode.isprintable() and len(menu._dm_input) < 200:
                                menu._dm_input += event.unicode
                    if event.type == pygame.MOUSEBUTTONDOWN and event.button == 1:
                        menu.server_back_btn.is_hovered = False
                        if menu.server_back_btn.rect.collidepoint(get_scaled_mouse()):
                            game_state = "MENU"
                        elif menu.servers_tab_rect.collidepoint(get_scaled_mouse()):
                            menu.switch_mp_tab("SERVERS")
                        elif menu.connections_tab_rect.collidepoint(get_scaled_mouse()):
                            menu.switch_mp_tab("CONNECTIONS")
                        elif menu._mp_tab == "SERVERS":
                            if menu.refresh_btn_rect.collidepoint(get_scaled_mouse()):
                                menu.trigger_server_refresh()
                            else:
                                cur_y = menu.SERVER_LIST_TOP
                                mp_scaled = get_scaled_mouse()
                                for vi in range(5):
                                    i = vi + menu._scroll_offset
                                    if i >= len(menu.server_list):
                                        break
                                    name = menu.server_list[i][0]
                                    name_max_w = 320 - 24
                                    lines = menu._wrap_server_name(name, menu.font_terminal, name_max_w)
                                    row_h = 64 if len(lines) > 1 else 50
                                    row_rect = pygame.Rect(
                                        menu.SERVER_ROW_X,
                                        cur_y,
                                        menu.SERVER_ROW_W,
                                        row_h - 4,
                                    )
                                    if row_rect.collidepoint(mp_scaled):
                                        mp_selected = i
                                        mp_state = "connecting"
                                        mp_timer = 180
                                        break
                                    cur_y += row_h + 5
                        elif menu._mp_tab == "CONNECTIONS":
                            list_x = menu.SERVER_PANEL_X + 28
                            list_y = menu.SERVER_LIST_TOP - 4
                            list_w = 340
                            row_h = 38
                            list_bottom_limit = menu.SERVER_PANEL_Y + menu.SERVER_PANEL_H - 90
                            visible_friends = (list_bottom_limit - list_y) // row_h
                            visible_friends = min(visible_friends, len(menu.CONNECTIONS))
                            mp_scaled = get_scaled_mouse()
                            for vi in range(visible_friends):
                                i = vi + menu._dm_scroll_offset
                                if i >= len(menu.CONNECTIONS):
                                    break
                                ry = list_y + vi * row_h
                                row_rect = pygame.Rect(list_x, ry, list_w, row_h - 4)
                                if row_rect.collidepoint(mp_scaled):
                                    menu.open_dm(i)
                                    break
                elif mp_state == "kicked":
                    if event.type == pygame.MOUSEBUTTONDOWN and event.button == 1:
                        if menu.kick_dismiss_btn_rect.collidepoint(get_scaled_mouse()):
                            mp_state = "browser"
                            mp_kick_msg = ""
                            mp_selected = -1
            elif game_state == "SETTINGS":
                if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key == pygame.K_ESCAPE:
                    _persist_settings()
                    game_state = "MENU"
                result = handle_settings_event(event)
                if result == "back":
                    game_state = "MENU"
            elif game_state == "CUTSCENE":
                if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key == pygame.K_SPACE:
                    shot = active_cutscene_shots[cutscene_shot_idx]
                    if cutscene_char_idx < len(shot["text"]):
                        cutscene_char_idx = len(shot["text"])
                    else:
                        elapsed_ms = pygame.time.get_ticks() - cutscene_start_time
                        if elapsed_ms < shot.get("min_ms", 0):
                            audio.play("menu_hover", volume=0.12)
                            continue
                        if cutscene_shot_idx < len(active_cutscene_shots) - 1:
                            audio.play("menu_click", volume=0.3)
                            if cutscene_use_fades:
                                cutscene_transition_timer = 15  # fade out
                            else:
                                cutscene_shot_idx += 1
                                cutscene_char_idx = 0
                                cutscene_timer = 0
                                cutscene_start_time = pygame.time.get_ticks()
                                gc.reset_dialogue_slide()
                        else:
                            after = cutscene_after
                            if after == "WALK":
                                gc.start_walking_sim()
                                trigger_transition("WALK")
                            elif after == "GAME":
                                gc.start_mission(preserve_timer=True)
                                trigger_transition("GAME")
                            elif after == "MISSION2":
                                gc.start_mission2(preserve_timer=True)
                                mission_complete_saved = False
                                trigger_transition("GAME")
                            elif after == "MISSION2_COMPLETE":
                                game_state = "MISSION_COMPLETE"
                                mission_complete_timer = 0
                                mission_complete_fade = 0
                                pygame.mouse.set_visible(True)
                            elif after == "BRIEFING":
                                start_briefing("warehouse")
                                game_state = "BRIEFING"
            elif game_state == "BRIEFING":
                if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key in (pygame.K_SPACE, pygame.K_RETURN):
                    finish_briefing()
            elif game_state == "WALK":
                if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key == pygame.K_ESCAPE:
                    prev_game_state = "WALK"
                    game_state = "PAUSED"
                if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key == pygame.K_SPACE:
                    if gc.walk_dialogue_active:
                        if gc.walk_dialogue_char_idx < len(gc.walk_dialogue_text):
                            gc.walk_dialogue_char_idx = len(gc.walk_dialogue_text)
                        else:
                            gc.walk_dialogue_active = False
                if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key == pygame.K_e:
                    gc.handle_walk_interact()
            elif game_state == "GAME":
                if getattr(gc, "active_mission", "MISSION1") == "MISSION2" and getattr(gc, "mission2_card", None):
                    # Mission 2 intel cards own input while open. Prevent shooting,
                    # pausing, or other actions from leaking through the card.
                    if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key in (pygame.K_e, pygame.K_SPACE, pygame.K_RETURN, pygame.K_ESCAPE):
                        gc.handle_interact()
                    continue
                if getattr(gc, "active_mission", "MISSION1") == "MISSION2" and getattr(gc, "blocks_player_actions", lambda: False)():
                    # Mission 2 authored panic/blackout beats lock player actions.
                    # Movement is already locked in mission2.py; this stops mouse
                    # clicks, reloads, molotovs, etc. from leaking through.
                    if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key == pygame.K_ESCAPE:
                        prev_game_state = "GAME"
                        game_state = "PAUSED"
                    continue
                if gc.active_read_note:
                    if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key in (pygame.K_SPACE, pygame.K_e, pygame.K_RETURN):
                        gc.dismiss_note()
                elif gc.safe_keypad_active:
                    if event.type == pygame.MOUSEBUTTONDOWN and event.button == 1:
                        gc.handle_keypad_click(event.pos)
                    elif event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN:
                        if event.key == pygame.K_ESCAPE:
                            gc.safe_keypad_active = False
                            pygame.mouse.set_visible(False)
                        else:
                            gc.handle_keypad_keypress(event.key)
                else:
                    if event.type == pygame.MOUSEBUTTONDOWN and event.button == 1:
                        if gc.truck_hotwiring:
                            gc.handle_hotwire_click(event.pos)
                        else:
                            gc.handle_shoot()
                    if event.type == pygame.MOUSEBUTTONDOWN and event.button == 3:
                        if not gc.truck_hotwiring:
                            gc.handle_throw_bottle()
                    if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key == pygame.K_g:
                        gc.handle_throw_bottle()
                    if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key == pygame.K_r:
                        gc.start_reload()
                    if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key == pygame.K_e:
                        if gc.truck_hotwiring:
                            gc.handle_hotwire_press()
                        else:
                            gc.handle_interact()
                    if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key == pygame.K_v:
                        gc.handle_melee()
                    if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key == pygame.K_m:
                        gc.show_minimap = not gc.show_minimap
                    if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key == pygame.K_F9:
                        gc.debug_jump_to_exfil()
                    if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key == pygame.K_ESCAPE:
                        prev_game_state = "GAME"
                        game_state = "PAUSED"
            elif game_state == "ESCAPE_CUTSCENE":
                if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key in (pygame.K_SPACE, pygame.K_RETURN):
                    gc.mission_end_time = pygame.time.get_ticks()
                    game_state = "MISSION_COMPLETE"
                    mission_complete_timer = 0
                    mission_complete_fade = 0
                    pygame.mouse.set_visible(True)
            elif game_state == "NOTEBOOK_INSPECT":
                if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key in (pygame.K_SPACE, pygame.K_RETURN):
                    game_state = "GAME"
                    pygame.mouse.set_visible(False)
            elif game_state == "PAUSED":
                if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key == pygame.K_ESCAPE:
                    game_state = prev_game_state
                    pygame.mouse.set_visible(False)
                if menu.pause_buttons[0].is_clicked(event, (mouse_x, mouse_y)):
                    game_state = prev_game_state
                    pygame.mouse.set_visible(False)
                if menu.pause_buttons[1].is_clicked(event, (mouse_x, mouse_y)):
                    gc.restart_current_mission()
                    game_state = "GAME"
                    pygame.mouse.set_visible(False)
                if menu.pause_buttons[2].is_clicked(event, (mouse_x, mouse_y)):
                    trigger_transition("MENU")
            elif game_state == "DEATH":
                if menu.death_buttons[0].is_clicked(event, (mouse_x, mouse_y)):
                    gc.restart_current_mission()
                    game_state = "GAME"
                    pygame.mouse.set_visible(False)
                    menu.reset_death_animation()
                if menu.death_buttons[1].is_clicked(event, (mouse_x, mouse_y)):
                    trigger_transition("MENU")
                    menu.reset_death_animation()
            elif game_state == "MISSION_COMPLETE":
                if event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN and event.key in (pygame.K_SPACE, pygame.K_RETURN):
                    if getattr(gc, "active_mission", "MISSION1") == "MISSION1":
                        gc.mission_start_time = pygame.time.get_ticks()
                        gc.mission_end_time = 0
                        mission_complete_saved = False
                        start_cutscene(gc.mission2_intro_shots, "MISSION2", use_fades=False)
                        trigger_transition("CUTSCENE")
                    else:
                        trigger_transition("MENU")
    # --- UPDATE ---
    if game_state == "CUTSCENE":
        cutscene_timer += 1
        shot = active_cutscene_shots[cutscene_shot_idx]
        # ── Handle shot-to-shot transition ──
        if cutscene_transition_timer > 0:
            cutscene_transition_timer -= 1
            if cutscene_transition_timer == 8:
                # Mid-transition: advance shot
                cutscene_shot_idx += 1
                cutscene_char_idx = 0
                cutscene_timer = 0
                cutscene_start_time = pygame.time.get_ticks()
                cutscene_transition_timer = -12  # fade in
        elif cutscene_transition_timer < 0:
            cutscene_transition_timer += 1
        if cutscene_char_idx < len(shot["text"]):
            if cutscene_timer >= TEXT_SPEED:
                cutscene_timer = 0
                last = shot["text"][max(0, cutscene_char_idx - 1)]
                if last in ".!?":
                    cutscene_timer -= 25
                elif last in ",;:":
                    cutscene_timer -= 12
                elif last == "-":
                    cutscene_timer -= 15
                next_ch = shot["text"][cutscene_char_idx] if cutscene_char_idx < len(shot["text"]) else ""
                _play_typewriter_tick(next_ch, cutscene_char_idx, volume=0.11)
                cutscene_char_idx += 1
    elif game_state == "WALK":
        cx, cy, complete = gc.update_walking_sim(keys)
        if complete:
            # Van cutscene remains fade-free; the briefing appears only after it ends.
            start_cutscene(gc.cutscene2_shots, "BRIEFING", use_fades=False)
            trigger_transition("CUTSCENE")
    elif game_state == "BRIEFING":
        briefing_timer += 1
        if briefing_timer >= BRIEFING_DURATION:
            finish_briefing()
    elif game_state == "MULTIPLAYER":
        menu.update_server_browser_animation()
        menu.update_dm_state()
        if mp_state == "connecting":
            mp_timer -= 1
            if mp_timer <= 0:
                mp_state = "kicked"
                # Use server-specific kick if available
                if 0 <= mp_selected < len(menu.server_list):
                    server_name = menu.server_list[mp_selected][0]
                    mp_kick_msg = menu.get_kick_message_for_server(server_name)
                else:
                    mp_kick_msg = random.choice(menu.KICK_MESSAGES)
                mp_timer = 0
    elif game_state == "GAME":
        if gc.request_death_screen:
            gc.request_death_screen = False
            if not gc.mission_end_time:
                gc.mission_end_time = pygame.time.get_ticks()
            game_state = "DEATH"
            pygame.mouse.set_visible(True)
            menu.reset_death_animation()
        elif gc.request_notebook_inspect:
            game_state = "NOTEBOOK_INSPECT"
            gc.request_notebook_inspect = False
            pygame.mouse.set_visible(True)
            import ui_hud; ui_hud.reset_notebook_inspect()
        else:
            last_fcx, last_fcy = gc.game_update(keys)
            if getattr(gc, "request_mission2_ending", False):
                gc.request_mission2_ending = False
                start_cutscene(gc.mission2_ending_shots, "MISSION2_COMPLETE", use_fades=True)
                trigger_transition("CUTSCENE")
                pygame.mouse.set_visible(True)
            elif getattr(gc, "request_escape_cutscene", False):
                gc.request_escape_cutscene = False
                game_state = "ESCAPE_CUTSCENE"
                escape_cutscene_timer = 0
                pygame.mouse.set_visible(False)
            elif gc.request_mission_complete:
                gc.request_mission_complete = False
                if not gc.mission_end_time:
                    gc.mission_end_time = pygame.time.get_ticks()
                game_state = "MISSION_COMPLETE"
                mission_complete_timer = 0
                mission_complete_fade = 0
    elif game_state == "ESCAPE_CUTSCENE":
        escape_cutscene_timer += 1
        if escape_cutscene_timer == 18:
            audio.play("door_open", volume=0.45)
        if escape_cutscene_timer == 38:
            audio.play("rig_low_boom", volume=0.25)
        if escape_cutscene_timer == 310:
            audio.play("missile_launch", volume=0.32)
        if escape_cutscene_timer == 650:
            audio.play("radio_static", volume=0.22)
        if escape_cutscene_timer == 735:
            audio.play("terminal_glitch", volume=0.24)
        if escape_cutscene_timer == 820:
            audio.play("menu_click", volume=0.34)
        if escape_cutscene_timer >= ESCAPE_CUTSCENE_DURATION:
            gc.mission_end_time = pygame.time.get_ticks()
            game_state = "MISSION_COMPLETE"
            mission_complete_timer = 0
            mission_complete_fade = 0
            pygame.mouse.set_visible(True)
    # --- AMBIENCE ---
    if game_state == "CUTSCENE":
        shot = active_cutscene_shots[cutscene_shot_idx]
        rain_scenes = {"ocean_wide", "city_skyline", "biribel_arrival", "krap_convoy", "route12_checkpoint", "route12_tanker", "route12_aftermath"}
        amb = {"ambient_drone"}
        if shot["scene"] in rain_scenes:
            amb.add("rain")
            amb.add("wind")
        _set_ambience(amb)
    elif game_state == "WALK":
        _set_ambience({"ambient_drone", "rain", "wind"})
    elif game_state == "DEATH":
        _set_ambience({"heartbeat"})
    elif game_state == "ESCAPE_CUTSCENE":
        _set_ambience({"rain", "wind", "ambient_drone"})
    elif game_state == "MISSION_COMPLETE":
        _set_ambience(set())
        if mission_complete_timer == 1:
            audio.play("mission_complete", volume=0.6)
    elif game_state == "BRIEFING":
        _set_ambience({"ambient_drone"})
    elif game_state == "GAME":
        if getattr(gc, "active_mission", "MISSION1") == "MISSION2":
            _set_ambience({"ambient_drone", "rain", "wind"})
        else:
            _set_ambience(set())
    elif game_state in ("MENU", "MULTIPLAYER", "SETTINGS", "PAUSED", "NOTEBOOK_INSPECT"):
        _set_ambience(set())
    # Stream the main menu theme only on menu-family screens.
    music_state = next_state if transitioning and fade_speed > 0 else game_state
    _set_menu_music(music_state in MENU_MUSIC_STATES)
    # --- RENDER ---
    if game_state == "SLOT_SELECT":
        draw_slot_select_screen()
    elif game_state == "MENU":
        menu.draw_menu()
        if menu_notice_timer > 0:
            notice = font_prompt.render(menu_notice, True, C_ORANGE)
            bg = pygame.Surface((notice.get_width() + 28, notice.get_height() + 12), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            bg.fill((0, 0, 0, 190))
            pygame.draw.rect(bg, (120, 70, 20), bg.get_rect(), 1)
            screen.blit(bg, (WIDTH // 2 - bg.get_width() // 2, HEIGHT - 120))
            screen.blit(notice, (WIDTH // 2 - notice.get_width() // 2, HEIGHT - 114))
            menu_notice_timer -= 1
    elif game_state == "MULTIPLAYER":
        menu.draw_menu_background()
        mp_connect_progress = 0.0
        if mp_state == "connecting":
            mp_connect_progress = 1.0 - max(0, min(180, mp_timer)) / 180.0
        menu.draw_server_browser(
            mp_selected,
            mp_state == "connecting",
            mp_kick_msg if mp_state == "kicked" else "",
            mp_connect_progress,
        )
    elif game_state == "SETTINGS":
        menu.draw_menu_background()
        menu.draw_settings(
            settings_active_tab,
            display_mode, res_idx,
            master_volume, music_volume, sfx_volume,
            STATE.reduced_motion,
        )
    elif game_state == "CUTSCENE":
        shot = active_cutscene_shots[cutscene_shot_idx]
        t_ms = pygame.time.get_ticks() - cutscene_start_time
        gc.draw_cutscene_scene(screen, shot, t_ms)
        if shot["scene"] == "title_card":
            lines = shot["text"].split("\n")
            gc.draw_typewriter_title(screen, lines, cutscene_char_idx)
        else:
            if shot["scene"] in ("ocean_wide", "city_skyline", "biribel_arrival", "krap_convoy", "route12_checkpoint", "route12_tanker", "route12_aftermath"):
                draw_rain(screen, gc.rain_drops, speed_mul=1.0)
                screen.blit(_cutscene_fog, (0, 0))
                screen.blit(_cutscene_top_fog, (0, LETTER_H))
            draw_vignette(screen, strength=180)
            draw_scanlines(screen)
            draw_letterbox(screen)
            can_continue = (pygame.time.get_ticks() - cutscene_start_time) >= shot.get("min_ms", 0)
            gc.draw_dialogue_box(
                screen,
                shot["speaker"],
                shot["text"],
                cutscene_char_idx,
                len(shot["text"]),
                can_continue,
            )
            grain_frame += 1
            if grain_frame >= 3:
                grain_frame = 0
                grain_idx = (grain_idx + 1) % len(GRAIN_VARIANTS)
            screen.blit(GRAIN_VARIANTS[grain_idx], (0, 0))
        # ── Shot transition fade ──
        if abs(cutscene_transition_timer) > 0:
            fade_alpha = 0
            if cutscene_transition_timer > 0:
                fade_alpha = int(255 * (cutscene_transition_timer / 15))
            else:
                fade_alpha = int(255 * (1 - abs(cutscene_transition_timer) / 12))
            fade_surf = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT))
            fade_surf.fill((0, 0, 0))
            fade_surf.set_alpha(min(255, fade_alpha))
            screen.blit(fade_surf, (0, 0))
    elif game_state == "BRIEFING":
        draw_briefing_screen()
    elif game_state == "WALK":
        gc.draw_walking_sim()
        grain_frame += 1
        if grain_frame >= 3:
            grain_frame = 0
            grain_idx = (grain_idx + 1) % len(GRAIN_VARIANTS)
        screen.blit(GRAIN_VARIANTS[grain_idx], (0, 0))
    elif game_state == "GAME":
        gc.game_draw(last_fcx, last_fcy)
        grain_frame += 1
        if grain_frame >= 3:
            grain_frame = 0
            grain_idx = (grain_idx + 1) % len(GRAIN_VARIANTS)
        screen.blit(GRAIN_VARIANTS[grain_idx], (0, 0))
        gc.draw_desk_note()
        gc.draw_safe_keypad()
    elif game_state == "ESCAPE_CUTSCENE":
        gc.draw_biribel_escape_cutscene(screen, escape_cutscene_timer)
    elif game_state == "NOTEBOOK_INSPECT":
        gc.game_draw(last_fcx, last_fcy)
        gc.draw_notebook_inspection()
    elif game_state == "PAUSED":
        gc.game_draw(last_fcx, last_fcy)
        grain_frame += 1
        if grain_frame >= 3:
            grain_frame = 0
            grain_idx = (grain_idx + 1) % len(GRAIN_VARIANTS)
        screen.blit(GRAIN_VARIANTS[grain_idx], (0, 0))
        menu.draw_pause_menu()
    elif game_state == "DEATH":
        gc.game_draw(last_fcx, last_fcy)
        grain_frame += 1
        if grain_frame >= 3:
            grain_frame = 0
            grain_idx = (grain_idx + 1) % len(GRAIN_VARIANTS)
        screen.blit(GRAIN_VARIANTS[grain_idx], (0, 0))
        menu.draw_death_screen()
    elif game_state == "MISSION_COMPLETE":
        mission_complete_timer += 1
        mission_complete_fade = min(255, mission_complete_timer * 5)
        if not mission_complete_saved:
            report = _mission_report_data(success=True)
            if getattr(gc, "active_mission", "MISSION1") == "MISSION2":
                save_system.save_mission_complete(
                    "MISSION_2",
                    "Mission 2 — Quiet on Route 12",
                    report["time_str"],
                    stats=report,
                    flags={"mission1_complete": True, "mission2_complete": True},
                )
            else:
                save_system.save_mission_complete(
                    "MISSION_1",
                    "Mission 1 — Black Notebook",
                    report["time_str"],
                    stats=report,
                    flags={"mission1_complete": True},
                )
            mission_complete_saved = True
        # ── Background ──
        for i in range(HEIGHT):
            t = i / HEIGHT
            r = int(5 + t * 8)
            g = int(8 + t * 12)
            b = int(14 + t * 20)
            pygame.draw.line(screen, (r, g, b), (0, i), (WIDTH, i))
        # Subtle noise
        if mission_complete_timer % 3 == 0:
            rng_n = random.Random(mission_complete_timer // 3)
            for _ in range(60):
                nx = rng_n.randint(0, WIDTH - 1)
                ny = rng_n.randint(0, HEIGHT - 1)
                screen.set_at((nx, ny), (12, 16, 22))
        # ── Card ──
        card_w, card_h = 520, 440
        card_x = WIDTH // 2 - card_w // 2
        card_y = HEIGHT // 2 - card_h // 2 - 10
        # Card shadow
        shadow = pygame.Surface((card_w + 16, card_h + 16), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        shadow.fill((0, 0, 0, 80))
        screen.blit(shadow, (card_x + 8, card_y + 8))
        # Card body
        card = pygame.Surface((card_w, card_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        card.fill((8, 12, 18, 235))
        pygame.draw.rect(card, (35, 60, 85, 160), (0, 0, card_w, card_h), 1)
        # Left accent
        pygame.draw.rect(card, (50, 120, 180), (0, 0, 4, card_h))
        screen.blit(card, (card_x, card_y))
        def _mc_text(surf, x, y, alpha):
            surf.set_alpha(min(255, alpha))
            screen.blit(surf, (x, y))
        # ── Header area ──
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, (6, 10, 16, 240),
                          (card_x + 4, card_y, card_w - 4, 42))
        pygame.draw.line(screen, (35, 60, 85),
                          (card_x + 16, card_y + 42),
                          (card_x + card_w - 16, card_y + 42), 1)
        eyebrow = font_terminal_small.render(
            "KRAP INTEL — AFTER ACTION REPORT", True, (60, 120, 170))
        _mc_text(eyebrow,
                 card_x + card_w // 2 - eyebrow.get_width() // 2,
                 card_y + 8, mission_complete_fade)
        # Classification stamp
        classif = font_terminal_small.render("CLASSIFIED", True, (180, 60, 50))
        _mc_text(classif, card_x + card_w - classif.get_width() - 20,
                 card_y + 8, mission_complete_fade)
        # ── Title — smaller, cleaner ──
        title_font = pygame.font.SysFont("Consolas", 32, bold=True)
        title_surf = title_font.render("MISSION COMPLETE", True, (200, 198, 190))
        _mc_text(title_surf,
                 card_x + card_w // 2 - title_surf.get_width() // 2,
                 card_y + 56, mission_complete_fade)
        # ── Operation name ──
        op_name = getattr(gc, "active_mission_operation", "WAREHOUSE RAID — BLACK NOTEBOOK")
        op = font_terminal.render(op_name, True, (160, 155, 145))
        _mc_text(op,
                 card_x + card_w // 2 - op.get_width() // 2,
                 card_y + 96, mission_complete_fade)
        # ── Outcome line ──
        outcome_line = getattr(gc, "active_mission_outcome", "Target escaped  ·  Notebook recovered  ·  Questions remain")
        out = font_terminal_small.render(
            outcome_line,
            True, (150, 120, 90))
        _mc_text(out,
                 card_x + card_w // 2 - out.get_width() // 2,
                 card_y + 120, mission_complete_fade)
        pygame.draw.line(screen, (30, 50, 70),
                          (card_x + 30, card_y + 145),
                          (card_x + card_w - 30, card_y + 145), 1)
        # ── Stats grid ──
        if mission_complete_timer > 25:
            stats_fade = min(255, (mission_complete_timer - 25) * 7)
            report = _mission_report_data(success=True)
            stats = [
                ("KILLS", str(report["kills"])),
                ("SHOTS FIRED", str(report["shots_fired"])),
                ("ACCURACY", f"{report['accuracy']}%"),
                ("TOTAL TIME", report["time_str"]),
                ("CIVILIANS HELPED", str(report["civilians_helped"])),
                ("INTEL FOUND", "YES" if report["intel_found"] else "NO"),
                ("RATIONS USED", str(report["rations_used"])),
                ("RANK", report["rank"]),
            ]
            stat_x = card_x + 36
            stat_y = card_y + 158
            for i, (label, value) in enumerate(stats):
                col = i % 2
                row = i // 2
                sx = stat_x + col * 240
                sy = stat_y + row * 42
                lbl = font_terminal_small.render(label, True, (55, 95, 130))
                lbl.set_alpha(stats_fade)
                screen.blit(lbl, (sx, sy))
                val_font = pygame.font.SysFont("Consolas", 22, bold=True)
                val_col = (255, 220, 120) if label == "RANK" else (180, 220, 245)
                val = val_font.render(value, True, val_col)
                val.set_alpha(stats_fade)
                screen.blit(val, (sx, sy + 14))
                line_alpha = min(stats_fade, 60)
                ul = pygame.Surface((200, 1), pygame.SRCALPHA)
                ul.fill((35, 60, 85, line_alpha))
                screen.blit(ul, (sx, sy + 34))
        # ── Divider before quote ──
        pygame.draw.line(screen, (30, 50, 70),
                          (card_x + 30, card_y + 325),
                          (card_x + card_w - 30, card_y + 325), 1)
        # ── Vasile quote ──
        if mission_complete_timer > 70:
            q_fade = min(255, (mission_complete_timer - 70) * 5)
            attr = font_terminal_small.render(
                getattr(gc, "active_mission_quote_attr", "Vasile, after a long pause:"), True, (90, 80, 70))
            attr.set_alpha(q_fade)
            screen.blit(attr, (card_x + 36, card_y + 338))
            quote = font_dialogue.render(
                getattr(gc, "active_mission_quote", '"Bring it straight to me. Don\'t read it."'),
                True, C_VASILE)
            quote.set_alpha(q_fade)
            screen.blit(quote,
                         (card_x + card_w // 2 - quote.get_width() // 2,
                          card_y + 362))
        # ── Bottom bar ──
        pygame.draw.line(screen, (35, 60, 85),
                          (card_x + 16, card_y + card_h - 34),
                          (card_x + card_w - 16, card_y + card_h - 34), 1)
        footer = font_terminal_small.render(
            "KRAP INTELLIGENCE DIVISION — FIELD COPY", True, (45, 65, 80))
        _mc_text(footer,
                 card_x + card_w // 2 - footer.get_width() // 2,
                 card_y + card_h - 26, mission_complete_fade)
        # ── Continue prompt ──
        if mission_complete_timer > 120 and (pygame.time.get_ticks() // 600) % 2 == 0:
            next_prompt = "PRESS SPACE TO START MISSION 2" if getattr(gc, "active_mission", "MISSION1") == "MISSION1" else "PRESS SPACE TO RETURN TO MENU"
            prompt = font_btn.render(next_prompt, True, C_ORANGE)
            prompt.set_alpha(mission_complete_fade)
            screen.blit(prompt,
                         (WIDTH // 2 - prompt.get_width() // 2,
                          card_y + card_h + 18))
    # --- FADE TRANSITION ---
    if transitioning:
        fade_alpha += fade_speed
        if fade_alpha >= 255:
            fade_alpha = 255
            fade_speed = -12
            game_state = next_state
        elif fade_alpha <= 0 and fade_speed < 0:
            fade_alpha = 0
            transitioning = False
        _fade_surf.fill((0, 0, 0))
        _fade_surf.set_alpha(fade_alpha)
        screen.blit(_fade_surf, (0, 0))
    pygame.display.flip()
    clock.tick(60)
pygame.quit()
sys.exit()

import pygame
import math
import sys
import random
import os
import time
def resource_path(relative_path):
    try:
        base_path = sys._MEIPASS
    except AttributeError:
        base_path = os.path.abspath(".")
    return os.path.join(base_path, relative_path)
pygame.init()
from audio import AudioManager
audio = AudioManager()
WIDTH, HEIGHT = 1000, 700
screen = pygame.display.set_mode((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SCALED | pygame.RESIZABLE)

# ── Grain ──
def make_grain(w, h):
    s = pygame.Surface((w, h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    for _ in range(800):
        a = random.randint(3, 10)
        s.set_at((random.randint(0, w - 1), random.randint(0, h - 1)), (0, 0, 0, a))
    for _ in range(300):
        a = random.randint(2, 7)
        s.set_at(
            (random.randint(0, w - 1), random.randint(0, h - 1)), (255, 255, 240, a)
        )
    return s
GRAIN_VARIANTS = [make_grain(WIDTH, HEIGHT) for _ in range(6)]
grain_idx = 0
grain_frame = 0
# ── Drawing helpers ──
def draw_sky_gradient(
    surface, top_color, bot_color, y_start=SCENE_TOP, y_end=SCENE_BOT
):
    for i in range(y_end - y_start):
        t = i / (y_end - y_start)
        r = int(top_color[0] + (bot_color[0] - top_color[0]) * t)
        g = int(top_color[1] + (bot_color[1] - top_color[1]) * t)
        b = int(top_color[2] + (bot_color[2] - top_color[2]) * t)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (r, g, b), (0, y_start + i), (WIDTH, y_start + i))
def draw_building(surface, x, ground_y, w, h, base_col, win_col, win_lit_col, seed=0):
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    shadow = pygame.Surface((w + 8, h + 8), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    shadow.fill((0, 0, 0, 60))
    surface.blit(shadow, (x + 4, ground_y - h + 4))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, base_col, (x, ground_y - h, w, h))
    for row in range(0, h, 18):
        pygame.draw.line(
            surface,
            (
                max(0, base_col[0] - 10),
                max(0, base_col[1] - 10),
                max(0, base_col[2] - 10),
            ),
            (x, ground_y - h + row),
            (x + w, ground_y - h + row),
            1,
        )
    for wy in range(ground_y - h + 12, ground_y - 10, 22):
        for wx in range(x + 8, x + w - 8, 16):
            lit = rng.random() > 0.45
            col = win_lit_col if lit else win_col
            pygame.draw.rect(surface, col, (wx, wy, 8, 12))
            if lit:
                glow = pygame.Surface((12, 16), pygame.SRCALPHA)
                glow.fill((*win_lit_col, 30))
                surface.blit(glow, (wx - 2, wy - 2))
    pygame.draw.rect(
        surface,
        (max(0, base_col[0] + 15), max(0, base_col[1] + 15), max(0, base_col[2] + 15)),
        (x, ground_y - h, w, 6),
    )
def draw_cityscape(surface, ground_y, palette, time_ticks, parallax=0.0):
    buildings_far = [
        (0, 180, 60, (22, 26, 32), (18, 18, 22), (55, 50, 35), 1),
        (55, 200, 70, (20, 24, 30), (18, 18, 22), (55, 50, 35), 2),
        (120, 160, 55, (24, 28, 34), (18, 18, 22), (55, 50, 35), 3),
        (170, 220, 65, (20, 24, 30), (18, 18, 22), (55, 50, 35), 4),
        (230, 190, 80, (22, 26, 32), (18, 18, 22), (55, 50, 35), 5),
        (305, 170, 60, (24, 28, 34), (18, 18, 22), (55, 50, 35), 6),
        (360, 240, 70, (20, 24, 30), (18, 18, 22), (55, 50, 35), 7),
        (425, 200, 90, (22, 26, 32), (18, 18, 22), (55, 50, 35), 8),
        (510, 180, 65, (24, 28, 34), (18, 18, 22), (55, 50, 35), 9),
        (570, 220, 75, (20, 24, 30), (18, 18, 22), (55, 50, 35), 10),
        (640, 195, 60, (22, 26, 32), (18, 18, 22), (55, 50, 35), 11),
        (695, 210, 80, (24, 28, 34), (18, 18, 22), (55, 50, 35), 12),
        (770, 175, 70, (20, 24, 30), (18, 18, 22), (55, 50, 35), 13),
        (835, 230, 65, (22, 26, 32), (18, 18, 22), (55, 50, 35), 14),
        (895, 185, 75, (24, 28, 34), (18, 18, 22), (55, 50, 35), 15),
        (960, 205, 60, (20, 24, 30), (18, 18, 22), (55, 50, 35), 16),
    ]
    px_far = parallax * 0.3
    for bx, bh, bw, bc, wc, wlc, seed in buildings_far:
        draw_building(surface, int(bx - px_far), ground_y, bw, bh, bc, wc, wlc, seed)
    buildings_mid = [
        (0, 270, 90, palette[0], (15, 15, 20), (70, 60, 38), 20),
        (85, 310, 80, palette[1], (15, 15, 20), (70, 60, 38), 21),
        (160, 250, 100, palette[0], (15, 15, 20), (70, 60, 38), 22),
        (255, 330, 75, palette[1], (15, 15, 20), (70, 60, 38), 23),
        (325, 280, 85, palette[0], (15, 15, 20), (70, 60, 38), 24),
        (405, 350, 90, palette[1], (15, 15, 20), (70, 60, 38), 25),
        (490, 270, 80, palette[0], (15, 15, 20), (70, 60, 38), 26),
        (565, 300, 95, palette[1], (15, 15, 20), (70, 60, 38), 27),
        (655, 320, 85, palette[0], (15, 15, 20), (70, 60, 38), 28),
        (735, 260, 90, palette[1], (15, 15, 20), (70, 60, 38), 29),
        (820, 340, 80, palette[0], (15, 15, 20), (70, 60, 38), 30),
        (895, 290, 95, palette[1], (15, 15, 20), (70, 60, 38), 31),
    ]
    px_mid = parallax * 0.6
    for bx, bh, bw, bc, wc, wlc, seed in buildings_mid:
        draw_building(surface, int(bx - px_mid), ground_y, bw, bh, bc, wc, wlc, seed)
    lamp_positions = [80, 220, 400, 580, 760, 920]
    for lx in lamp_positions:
        lx_offset = int(lx - parallax * 0.9)
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (30, 32, 38), (lx_offset - 2, ground_y - 120, 4, 120))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (30, 32, 38), (lx_offset - 12, ground_y - 122, 14, 4))
        halo = pygame.Surface((60, 40), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        for r in range(20, 0, -1):
            alpha = int(6 * r)
            pygame.draw.circle(halo, (200, 180, 100, alpha), (30, 20), r)
        surface.blit(halo, (lx_offset - 42, ground_y - 136))
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (220, 200, 130), (lx_offset - 6, ground_y - 122), 4)
def draw_rain(surface, drops, speed_mul=1.0):
    for i, (x, y, spd) in enumerate(drops):
        ex = x - int(spd * 0.4)
        ey = y + int(spd * speed_mul)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, C_RAIN, (x, y), (ex, ey), 1)
        drops[i] = (x - 1, ey, spd)
        if ey > SCENE_BOT:
            drops[i] = (
                random.randint(0, WIDTH),
                SCENE_TOP - random.randint(0, 40),
                spd,
            )
def _make_vignette(strength=130):
    vig = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    cx, cy = WIDTH // 2, (SCENE_TOP + SCENE_BOT) // 2
    max_r = int(math.hypot(WIDTH // 2, SCENE_H // 2)) + 10
    step = 6
    for r in range(max_r, 0, -step):
        t = 1.0 - (r / max_r)
        alpha = int(strength * t * t)
        alpha = min(alpha, strength)
        rw = int(r * 2)
        rh = int(r * 1.15)
        rect = (cx - r, cy - rh // 2, rw, rh)
        pygame.draw.ellipse(vig, (0, 0, 0, alpha), rect)
    return vig
VIGNETTE = _make_vignette(130)
def draw_vignette(surface, strength=130):
    surface.blit(VIGNETTE, (0, 0))
def draw_letterbox(surface):
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (0, 0, 0), (0, 0, WIDTH, LETTER_H))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (0, 0, 0), (0, HEIGHT - LETTER_H, WIDTH, LETTER_H))
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (40, 40, 40), (0, LETTER_H), (WIDTH, LETTER_H), 1)
    pygame.draw.line(
        surface, (40, 40, 40), (0, HEIGHT - LETTER_H), (WIDTH, HEIGHT - LETTER_H), 1
    )
def _make_scanlines():
    sl = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    for y in range(SCENE_TOP, SCENE_BOT, 4):
        pygame.draw.line(sl, (0, 0, 0, 18), (0, y), (WIDTH, y))
    return sl
SCANLINES = _make_scanlines()
def draw_scanlines(surface):
    surface.blit(SCANLINES, (0, 0))
def _make_floor_texture():
    rng = random.Random(42)
    ts = 128
    surf = pygame.Surface((ts, ts))
    surf.fill(C_BG_GAME)
    for _ in range(400):
        x, y = rng.randint(0, ts - 1), rng.randint(0, ts - 1)
        v = rng.randint(-10, 10)
        base = C_BG_GAME
        c = (
            max(0, min(255, base[0] + v)),
            max(0, min(255, base[1] + v)),
            max(0, min(255, base[2] + v)),
        )
        surf.set_at((x, y), c)
    seam_col = (
        max(0, C_BG_GAME[0] - 10),
        max(0, C_BG_GAME[1] - 10),
        max(0, C_BG_GAME[2] - 10),
    )
    pygame.draw.line(surf, seam_col, (0, 0), (ts - 1, 0), 1)
    pygame.draw.line(surf, seam_col, (0, 0), (0, ts - 1), 1)
    for _ in range(6):
        sx = rng.randint(10, ts - 10)
        sy = rng.randint(10, ts - 10)
        sr = rng.randint(5, 18)
        patch = pygame.Surface((sr * 2, sr * 2), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.ellipse(
            patch, (0, 0, 0, rng.randint(8, 16)), (0, 0, sr * 2, sr * 2)
        )
        surf.blit(patch, (sx - sr, sy - sr))
    for _ in range(2):
        cx = rng.randint(10, ts - 10)
        cy = rng.randint(10, ts - 10)
        pts = [(cx, cy)]
        for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 3)):
            cx += rng.randint(-15, 15)
            cy += rng.randint(-10, 20)
            pts.append((cx, cy))
        crack_col = (
            max(0, C_BG_GAME[0] - 16),
            max(0, C_BG_GAME[1] - 16),
            max(0, C_BG_GAME[2] - 16),
        )
        for i in range(len(pts) - 1):
            pygame.draw.line(surf, crack_col, pts[i], pts[i + 1], 1)
    return surf
FLOOR_TILE = _make_floor_texture()
FLOOR_TILE_SIZE = 128
def _make_slum_floor():
    rng = random.Random(99)
    ts = 128
    base = (32, 30, 28)
    surf = pygame.Surface((ts, ts))
    surf.fill(base)
    for _ in range(600):
        x, y = rng.randint(0, ts - 1), rng.randint(0, ts - 1)
        v = rng.randint(-8, 8)
        c = (
            max(0, min(255, base[0] + v)),
            max(0, min(255, base[1] + v)),
            max(0, min(255, base[2] + v)),
        )
        surf.set_at((x, y), c)
    for _ in range(8):
        px = rng.randint(5, ts - 5)
        py = rng.randint(5, ts - 5)
        pr = rng.randint(8, 20)
        puddle = pygame.Surface((pr * 2, pr), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.ellipse(puddle, (25, 30, 40, 60), (0, 0, pr * 2, pr))
        surf.blit(puddle, (px - pr, py - pr // 2))
    for _ in range(4):
        sx = rng.randint(0, ts)
        sy = rng.randint(0, ts)
        ex = sx + rng.randint(-30, 30)
        ey = sy + rng.randint(-20, 20)
        pygame.draw.line(
            surf,
            (max(0, base[0] - 12), max(0, base[1] - 12), max(0, base[2] - 12)),
            (sx, sy),
            (ex, ey),
            1,
        )
    return surf
SLUM_FLOOR = _make_slum_floor()
def _make_container_tile(r_or_color=65, g=55, b=50):
    if isinstance(r_or_color, tuple):
        r, g, b = r_or_color
    else:
        r = r_or_color
    t = pygame.Surface((TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE))
    t.fill((r, g, b))
    for row in range(0, TILE_SIZE, 8):
        shade = -5 if (row // 8) % 2 == 0 else 5
        c = (
            max(0, min(255, r + shade)),
            max(0, min(255, g + shade)),
            max(0, min(255, b + shade)),
        )
        pygame.draw.line(t, c, (0, row), (TILE_SIZE, row), 1)
    rng = random.Random(hash((r, g, b)))
    for _ in range(4):
        rx = rng.randint(5, TILE_SIZE - 15)
        ry = rng.randint(5, TILE_SIZE - 15)
        rw = rng.randint(6, 18)
        rh = rng.randint(4, 12)
        rust = pygame.Surface((rw, rh), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        rust.fill((90, 50, 30, 40))
        t.blit(rust, (rx, ry))
    for corner_x, corner_y in [
        (4, 4),
        (TILE_SIZE - 6, 4),
        (4, TILE_SIZE - 6),
        (TILE_SIZE - 6, TILE_SIZE - 6),
    ]:
        pygame.draw.circle(
            t,
            (min(255, r + 20), min(255, g + 20), min(255, b + 20)),
            (corner_x, corner_y),
            3,
        )
        pygame.draw.circle(
            t,
            (max(0, r - 10), max(0, g - 10), max(0, b - 10)),
            (corner_x + 1, corner_y + 1),
            2,
        )
    return t
def _make_warehouse_wall():
    t = pygame.Surface((TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE))
    t.fill((50, 48, 45))
    rng = random.Random(42)
    for _ in range(200):
        x = rng.randint(0, TILE_SIZE - 1)
        y = rng.randint(0, TILE_SIZE - 1)
        v = rng.randint(-6, 6)
        c = (50 + v, 48 + v, 45 + v)
        t.set_at((x, y), c)
    for row in range(0, TILE_SIZE, 16):
        shade = -8 if (row // 16) % 2 == 0 else 8
        pygame.draw.line(
            t, (42 + shade, 40 + shade, 38 + shade), (0, row), (TILE_SIZE, row), 1
        )
    pygame.draw.line(
        t, (40, 38, 35), (0, TILE_SIZE - 1), (TILE_SIZE - 1, TILE_SIZE - 1), 1
    )
    for _ in range(5):
        dx = rng.randint(0, TILE_SIZE - 1)
        dy = rng.randint(0, TILE_SIZE - 1)
        pygame.draw.circle(t, (55, 52, 48), (dx, dy), 2)
    return t

CONTAINER_TILE_RUST = _make_container_tile(70, 55, 40)
CONTAINER_TILE_GREY = _make_container_tile(55, 55, 58)
WAREHOUSE_WALL = _make_warehouse_wall()
def _make_muzzle_flash():
    size = 80
    surf = pygame.Surface((size, size), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    cx, cy = size // 2, size // 2
    for r in range(36, 0, -2):
        a = int(180 * (1 - r / 36))
        ratio = r / 36
        cr = int(255 * (1 - ratio * 0.3))
        cg = int(240 * (1 - ratio * 0.5))
        cb = int(180 * (1 - ratio * 0.8))
        pygame.draw.circle(surf, (cr, cg, cb, a), (cx, cy), r)
    pygame.draw.circle(surf, (255, 255, 240, 200), (cx, cy), 6)
    return surf
MUZZLE_FLASH = _make_muzzle_flash()
class Particle:
    def __init__(self, x, y, color):
        self.x, self.y = x, y
        angle = random.uniform(0, math.pi * 2)
        speed = random.uniform(1, 6)
        self.dx = math.cos(angle) * speed
        self.dy = math.sin(angle) * speed
        self.life = random.randint(15, 35)
        self.color = color
        self.size = random.randint(2, 4)
    def update(self):
        self.x += self.dx
        self.y += self.dy
        self.dx *= 0.9
        self.dy *= 0.9
        self.life -= 1
        self.size = max(1, int(self.life / 8))
    def draw(self, surface, offset_x, offset_y):
        if self.life > 0:
            pygame.draw.rect(
                surface,
                self.color,
                (self.x - offset_x, self.y - offset_y, self.size, self.size),
            )
class Casing:
    CASING_W, CASING_H = 8, 4
    LIFETIME = 180
    def __init__(self, x, y, eject_angle):
        self.x, self.y = x, y
        spread = random.uniform(-0.3, 0.3)
        speed = random.uniform(5, 9)
        self.dx = math.cos(eject_angle + spread) * speed
        self.dy = math.sin(eject_angle + spread) * speed
        self.rot = random.uniform(0, math.pi * 2)
        self.rot_speed = random.uniform(-0.4, 0.4)
        self.life = self.LIFETIME
        self.grounded = False
    def rect(self):
        return pygame.Rect(
            self.x - self.CASING_W // 2,
            self.y - self.CASING_H // 2,
            self.CASING_W,
            self.CASING_H,
        )
    def update(self, walls=()):
        if not self.grounded:
            self.rot += self.rot_speed
            new_x = self.x + self.dx
            new_rect = pygame.Rect(
                new_x - self.CASING_W // 2,
                self.y - self.CASING_H // 2,
                self.CASING_W,
                self.CASING_H,
            )
            for w in walls:
                if new_rect.colliderect(w):
                    if self.dx > 0:
                        new_rect.right = w.left
                    elif self.dx < 0:
                        new_rect.left = w.right
                    self.x = new_rect.centerx
                    self.dx *= -0.3
                    self.rot_speed *= 0.5
                    break
            else:
                self.x = new_x
            new_y = self.y + self.dy
            new_rect = pygame.Rect(
                self.x - self.CASING_W // 2,
                new_y - self.CASING_H // 2,
                self.CASING_W,
                self.CASING_H,
            )
            for w in walls:
                if new_rect.colliderect(w):
                    if self.dy > 0:
                        new_rect.bottom = w.top
                    elif self.dy < 0:
                        new_rect.top = w.bottom
                    self.y = new_rect.centery
                    self.dy *= -0.3
                    self.rot_speed *= 0.5
                    break
            else:
                self.y = new_y
            self.dx *= 0.85
            self.dy *= 0.85
            self.rot_speed *= 0.88
            if abs(self.dx) < 0.05 and abs(self.dy) < 0.05:
                self.dx = 0
                self.dy = 0
                self.grounded = True
        self.life -= 1
    def draw(self, surface, offset_x, offset_y):
        if self.life <= 0:
            return
        sx = self.x - offset_x
        sy = self.y - offset_y
        hw = self.CASING_W / 2
        hh = self.CASING_H / 2
        cos_a = math.cos(self.rot)
        sin_a = math.sin(self.rot)
        corners = []
        for lx, ly in ((-hw, -hh), (hw, -hh), (hw, hh), (-hw, hh)):
            rx = lx * cos_a - ly * sin_a
            ry = lx * sin_a + ly * cos_a
            corners.append((sx + rx, sy + ry))
        pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (190, 155, 70), corners)
        pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (140, 110, 40), corners, 1)

def get_scaled_mouse():
    win_w, win_h = screen.get_size()
    win_x, win_y = pygame.mouse.get_pos()
    
    # Maintain aspect ratio scaling
    s = min(win_w / WIDTH, win_h / HEIGHT)
    off_x = (win_w - WIDTH * s) / 2
    off_y = (win_h - HEIGHT * s) / 2
    
    scaled_x = (win_x - off_x) / s
    scaled_y = (win_y - off_y) / s
    
    return (int(max(0, min(WIDTH, scaled_x))), int(max(0, min(HEIGHT, scaled_y))))

"""Central game state singleton.
Everything that used to be a module-level global in game_core.py now lives
on STATE. Every submodule does:
    from state import STATE
    STATE.player.hp -= 10
reset() is called by mission.start_mission().
"""
import pygame
class GameState:
    def __init__(self):
        # Persistent across missions (NOT reset)
        self.show_minimap = True
        self.minimap = None
        self.rain_drops = []
        self._footstep_timer = 0
        self._walk_footstep_timer = 0
        self._last_explosion_triggers = set()
        self.reset()
    def reset(self):
        """Reset everything that should be fresh at the start of a mission."""
        # ─── Entities ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
        self.player = None
        self.enemies = []
        self.bullets = []
        self.walls = []
        self.doors = []
        self.particles = []
        self.casings = []
        self.pickups = []
        self.desks = []
        self.blood_decals = []
        self.floating_texts = []
        self.breach_markers = []
        self.breach_spawn_queue = []
        self.breach_spawn_timer = 0
        self.breach_spawn_started = False
        self.bay_door_forced_open = False
        self.rear_door_forced_open = False
        # ─── Camera / FX ──────────────────────────────────────────────
        self.camera_x = 0
        self.camera_y = 0
        self.cam_kick_x = 0.0
        self.cam_kick_y = 0.0
        self.screen_shake = 0
        self.muzzle_flash_timer = 0
        self.gc_flash_timer = 0
        # ─── Combat / inventory ───────────────────────────────────────
        self.ammo_in_mag = 12
        self.mag_size = 12
        self.spare_mags = 3
        self.shot_cooldown = 0
        self.is_reloading = False
        self.reload_timer = 0
