        self.walk_attack_active = False
        self.walk_attack_timer = 0
        self.walk_attack_events_done = set()
        self.walk_after_firefight = False
        self.walk_boat_active = False
        self.walk_boat_x = 0.0
        self.walk_boat_y = 0.0
        self.walk_missiles = []
        self.walk_explosions = []
        self.walk_terminal_active = False
        self.walk_terminal_status = ""
        self.rig_status = ["ONLINE", "ONLINE", "ONLINE"]
# Module-level singleton
STATE = GameState()

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# game_core.py
# ------------------------------------------------------------

"""game_core.py — Proxy facade that delegates to split modules.

This keeps old code like:
    import game_core as gc
    gc.start_mission()
    gc.player
    gc.show_minimap = False
working while the real implementation lives in split files.

Mission 2 integration note:
    The original game used gc.game_update()/gc.game_draw() for Mission 1.
    Those calls now route to Mission 2 when STATE.active_mission == "MISSION2".
"""
import sys
import types

from state import STATE
import cutscenes
import walking_sim
import mission
import mission2
import ui_hud
import weapons
import interactions
import effects

_MODULES = [
    cutscenes,
    walking_sim,
    mission,
    mission2,
    ui_hud,
    weapons,
    interactions,
    effects,
]

# These are still owned by walking_sim module-level globals.
# Everything else should prefer STATE first.
_MODULE_FIRST_NAMES = {
    "walk_dialogue_active",
    "walk_dialogue_speaker",
    "walk_dialogue_text",
    "walk_dialogue_char_idx",
    "walk_dialogue_timer",
    "rain_drops",
    "warehouse_hp_bonus",
    "warehouse_mag_bonus",
    "warehouse_intel_given",
    "player_rations",
}


def _is_mission2_active():
    return getattr(STATE, "active_mission", "MISSION1") == "MISSION2"


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# Routed mission entry/update/draw helpers
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
def start_mission(preserve_timer=False):
    """Start Mission 1."""
    return mission.start_mission(preserve_timer=preserve_timer)


def start_mission2(preserve_timer=False):
    """Start Mission 2: Quiet on Route 12."""
    return mission2.start_mission2(preserve_timer=preserve_timer)


def restart_current_mission():
    """Restart whichever playable mission is currently active."""
    if _is_mission2_active():
        return mission2.start_mission2(preserve_timer=False)
    return mission.start_mission(preserve_timer=False)


def game_update(keys):
    if _is_mission2_active():
        return mission2.game_update(keys)
    return mission.game_update(keys)


def game_draw(fcx, fcy):
    if _is_mission2_active():
        return mission2.game_draw(fcx, fcy)
    return mission.game_draw(fcx, fcy)


def handle_interact():
    if _is_mission2_active():
        return mission2.handle_interact()
    return interactions.handle_interact()


def debug_jump_to_exfil():
    if _is_mission2_active():
        return mission2.debug_jump_to_evidence()
    return mission.debug_jump_to_exfil()


class _GameCoreModule(types.ModuleType):
    """Proxy module — routes old game_core globals to new modules/STATE."""

    def __getattr__(self, name):
        if name in _MODULE_FIRST_NAMES:
            for mod in _MODULES:
                if hasattr(mod, name):
                    return getattr(mod, name)
        if hasattr(STATE, name):
            return getattr(STATE, name)
        for mod in _MODULES:
            if hasattr(mod, name):
                return getattr(mod, name)
        raise AttributeError(f"module 'game_core' has no attribute {name!r}")

    def __setattr__(self, name, value):
        wrote = False
        if hasattr(STATE, name):
            setattr(STATE, name, value)
            wrote = True
        # Important: propagate to ALL modules that imported screen/config globals.
        for mod in _MODULES:
            if hasattr(mod, name):
                setattr(mod, name, value)
                wrote = True
        if not wrote:
            super().__setattr__(name, value)


_proxy = _GameCoreModule(__name__)
_proxy.__dict__.update({
    "__doc__": __doc__,
    "__file__": __file__,
    "__package__": __package__,
    "__loader__": __loader__,
    "__spec__": __spec__,
    # Explicit routed functions override same-named functions in split modules.
    "start_mission": start_mission,
    "start_mission2": start_mission2,
    "restart_current_mission": restart_current_mission,
    "game_update": game_update,
    "game_draw": game_draw,
    "handle_interact": handle_interact,
    "debug_jump_to_exfil": debug_jump_to_exfil,
})
sys.modules[__name__] = _proxy

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# player.py
# ------------------------------------------------------------

"""Player character: movement, drawing, weapon rig."""
import math
import time
import pygame
from config import (
    TILE_SIZE, C_PLAYER_UNIFORM, C_PLAYER_SKIN,
)

_player_shadow = pygame.Surface((36, 18), pygame.SRCALPHA)
pygame.draw.ellipse(_player_shadow, (0, 0, 0, 55), (0, 0, 36, 18))


class Player:
    def __init__(self, x, y, max_hp=100):
        self.rect = pygame.Rect(x, y, 32, 32)
        self.speed = 5
        self.angle = 0
        self.hp = max_hp
        self.max_hp = max_hp
        self.breathing = 0
        self.blink_timer = 0
        self.weapon_raised = False
        self.walk_cycle = 0.0
        self.was_moving = False
        self._recoil_timer = 0
        self._recoil_offset = 0.0
        self._slide_timer = 0

    def move(self, dx, dy, walls):
        self.rect.x += dx
        for wall in walls:
            if self.rect.colliderect(wall):
                if dx > 0:
                    self.rect.right = wall.left
                if dx < 0:
                    self.rect.left = wall.right
        self.rect.y += dy
        for wall in walls:
            if self.rect.colliderect(wall):
                if dy > 0:
                    self.rect.bottom = wall.top
                if dy < 0:
                    self.rect.top = wall.bottom

    def barrel_tip(self):
        """World-space pistol muzzle position.

        Must match the pistol drawing proportions in draw().
        """
        a = self.angle

        arm_len = 16
        grip_offset = 4
        gun_len = 17
        muzzle_extra = 2

        reach = arm_len + grip_offset + gun_len + muzzle_extra

        return (
            self.rect.centerx + math.cos(a) * reach,
            self.rect.centery + math.sin(a) * reach,
        )

    def draw(
        self,
        surface,
        offset_x,
        offset_y,
        walls=(),
        reload_active=False,
        reload_progress=0.0,
        melee_active=False,
        melee_progress=0.0,
        no_gun=False,
        is_moving=False,
    ):
        draw_x = self.rect.centerx - offset_x
        draw_y = self.rect.centery - offset_y

        if is_moving and not self.was_moving:
            self.walk_cycle = 0.0
        self.was_moving = is_moving

        self.breathing = math.sin(time.time() * 0.5) * 1.0
        draw_y += self.breathing

        # ── Walk bob ──
        if is_moving:
            self.walk_cycle += 0.12
        else:
            self.walk_cycle *= 0.85
        sway_offset = 0.0

        # ── Recoil ──
        if self._recoil_timer > 0:
            self._recoil_timer -= 1
            self._recoil_offset = -3.0 * (self._recoil_timer / 8.0)
        else:
            self._recoil_offset *= 0.8

        if self._slide_timer > 0:
            self._slide_timer -= 1
        self.blink_timer += 1
        blinking = 2 if (120 < self.blink_timer < 140) else 0
        if self.blink_timer > 140:
            self.blink_timer = 0
        a = self.angle
        d_x = math.cos(a)
        d_y = math.sin(a)
        perp_x = math.cos(a + math.pi / 2)
        perp_y = math.sin(a + math.pi / 2)

        arm_angle = a
        if is_moving:
            # Weapon hand lags behind direction changes
            arm_angle += sway_offset * 0.06
        if reload_active:
            p = reload_progress
            if p < 0.25:
                arm_factor = 1.0 - (p / 0.25) * 0.5
            elif p < 0.75:
                arm_factor = 0.5
                wobble = math.sin(p * math.pi * 4) * 0.08
                arm_angle = a + wobble
            else:
                t = (p - 0.75) / 0.25
                arm_factor = 0.5 + t * 0.5
                wobble = math.sin(p * math.pi * 4) * 0.08 * (1.0 - t)
                arm_angle = a + wobble
        elif melee_active:
            p = melee_progress
            if p < 0.25:
                t = p / 0.25
                arm_factor = 1.0 - t * 0.6
                arm_angle = a - t * math.pi / 4
            elif p < 0.75:
                t = (p - 0.25) / 0.5
                arm_factor = 0.4 + t * 0.8
                arm_angle = a - math.pi / 4 + t * (math.pi / 4 + math.pi / 3)
            else:
                t = (p - 0.75) / 0.25
                arm_factor = 1.2 - t * 0.2
                arm_angle = a + math.pi / 3 - t * math.pi / 3
        else:
            arm_factor = 1.0

        surface.blit(_player_shadow, (int(draw_x - 18), int(draw_y - 6)))
        body_w, body_h = 22, 14
        cos_b, sin_b = math.cos(a), math.sin(a)
        torso_pts = []
        for bx, by in [
            (-body_w // 2, -body_h // 2),
            (body_w // 2, -body_h // 2),
            (body_w // 2, body_h // 2),
            (-body_w // 2, body_h // 2),
        ]:
            rx = bx * cos_b - by * sin_b
            ry = bx * sin_b + by * cos_b
            torso_pts.append((draw_x + rx, draw_y + ry))
        pygame.draw.polygon(surface, C_PLAYER_UNIFORM, torso_pts)
        if self.max_hp > 100:
            kevlar_w, kevlar_h = 16, 12
            kevlar_pts = []
            for bx, by in [
                (-kevlar_w // 2, -kevlar_h // 2),
                (kevlar_w // 2, -kevlar_h // 2),
                (kevlar_w // 2, kevlar_h // 2),
                (-kevlar_w // 2, kevlar_h // 2),
            ]:
                rx = bx * cos_b - by * sin_b
                ry = bx * sin_b + by * cos_b
                kevlar_pts.append((draw_x + rx, draw_y + ry))
            pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (50, 52, 56), kevlar_pts)
            pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (28, 30, 32), kevlar_pts, 1)
            for off in (-5, 5):
                sx1 = draw_x + perp_x * off - d_x * 5
                sy1 = draw_y + perp_y * off - d_y * 5
                sx2 = draw_x + perp_x * off + d_x * 5
                sy2 = draw_y + perp_y * off + d_y * 5
                pygame.draw.line(surface, (25, 27, 30), (sx1, sy1), (sx2, sy2), 2)
            ridge_w, ridge_h = 8, 8
            ridge_pts = []
            for bx, by in [
                (-ridge_w // 2, -ridge_h // 2),
                (ridge_w // 2, -ridge_h // 2),
                (ridge_w // 2, ridge_h // 2),
                (-ridge_w // 2, ridge_h // 2),
            ]:
                rx = bx * cos_b - by * sin_b
                ry = bx * sin_b + by * cos_b
                ridge_pts.append((draw_x + rx, draw_y + ry))
            pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (38, 40, 44), ridge_pts)
        pygame.draw.line(
            surface,
            (
                C_PLAYER_UNIFORM[0] + 12,
                C_PLAYER_UNIFORM[1] + 12,
                C_PLAYER_UNIFORM[2] + 8,
            ),
            (draw_x - d_x * 5, draw_y - d_y * 5),
            (draw_x + d_x * 5, draw_y + d_y * 5),
            2,
        )
        for off in (-6, 6):
            vx1 = draw_x + perp_x * off - d_x * 3
            vy1 = draw_y + perp_y * off - d_y * 3
            vx2 = draw_x + perp_x * off + d_x * 3
            vy2 = draw_y + perp_y * off + d_y * 3
            pygame.draw.line(
                surface,
                (
                    C_PLAYER_UNIFORM[0] - 8,
                    C_PLAYER_UNIFORM[1] - 5,
                    C_PLAYER_UNIFORM[2] - 3,
                ),
                (vx1, vy1),
                (vx2, vy2),
                2,
            )

        test_arm_len = int(16 * arm_factor)
        test_arm_dx = math.cos(arm_angle)
        test_arm_dy = math.sin(arm_angle)
        test_grip_x = draw_x + test_arm_dx * (test_arm_len + 4)
        test_grip_y = draw_y + test_arm_dy * (test_arm_len + 4)

        test_gun_end_x = test_grip_x + test_arm_dx * 16
        test_gun_end_y = test_grip_y + test_arm_dy * 16

        min_t = float("inf")
        if not no_gun and walls:
            for wall in walls:
                wr = pygame.Rect(
                    wall.x - offset_x, wall.y - offset_y, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE
                )
                clipped = wr.clipline(
                    (test_grip_x, test_grip_y), (test_gun_end_x, test_gun_end_y)
                )
                if clipped:
                    ex, ey = clipped[0]
                    t = (ex - test_grip_x) * test_arm_dx + (
                        ey - test_grip_y
                    ) * test_arm_dy
                    if t < min_t:
                        min_t = t

        raise_factor = 0.0
        if not no_gun and min_t < 16:
            raise_factor = max(0.0, min(1.0, (16.0 - min_t) / 16.0))

        self.weapon_raised = raise_factor > 0.15

        arm_angle = arm_angle - raise_factor * (math.pi * 0.35)
        arm_factor = arm_factor * (1.0 - raise_factor * 0.4)

        arm_len = int(16 * arm_factor)
        arm_dx = math.cos(arm_angle)
        arm_dy = math.sin(arm_angle)
        arm_perp_x = math.cos(arm_angle + math.pi / 2)
        arm_perp_y = math.sin(arm_angle + math.pi / 2)

        ls_x = draw_x + perp_x * 12
        ls_y = draw_y + perp_y * 12
        rs_x = draw_x - perp_x * 12
        rs_y = draw_y - perp_y * 12
        hand_x = draw_x + arm_dx * arm_len
        hand_y = draw_y + arm_dy * arm_len
        pygame.draw.line(
            surface,
            C_PLAYER_UNIFORM,
            (ls_x, ls_y),
            (hand_x - arm_perp_x * 3, hand_y - arm_perp_y * 3),
            5,
        )
        pygame.draw.line(
            surface,
            C_PLAYER_UNIFORM,
            (rs_x, rs_y),
            (hand_x + arm_perp_x * 3, hand_y + arm_perp_y * 3),
            5,
        )
        grip_x = draw_x + arm_dx * (arm_len + 4)
        grip_y = draw_y + arm_dy * (arm_len + 4)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, C_PLAYER_SKIN, (int(grip_x), int(grip_y)), 4)

        if not no_gun:
            # Compact service pistol — intentionally small/chunky.
            draw_gun_len = int(18.0 * (1.0 - raise_factor * 0.35))

            gun_start_x = grip_x + arm_dx * 2
            gun_start_y = grip_y + arm_dy * 2
            gun_end_x = grip_x + arm_dx * draw_gun_len
            gun_end_y = grip_y + arm_dy * draw_gun_len

            perp_dx = -arm_dy
            perp_dy = arm_dx

            def quad(ax, ay, bx, by, rear_w, front_w):
                return [
                    (ax - perp_dx * rear_w, ay - perp_dy * rear_w),
                    (ax + perp_dx * rear_w, ay + perp_dy * rear_w),
                    (bx + perp_dx * front_w, by + perp_dy * front_w),
                    (bx - perp_dx * front_w, by - perp_dy * front_w),
                ]

            # Main pistol frame
            frame = quad(gun_start_x, gun_start_y, gun_end_x, gun_end_y, 3.2, 2.3)
            pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (24, 25, 27), frame)

            # Slide, slightly offset upward
            slide_start_x = gun_start_x + arm_dx * 1.5 - perp_dx * 1.5
            slide_start_y = gun_start_y + arm_dy * 1.5 - perp_dy * 1.5
            slide_end_x = gun_end_x - arm_dx * 2 - perp_dx * 1.2
            slide_end_y = gun_end_y - arm_dy * 2 - perp_dy * 1.2
            slide = quad(slide_start_x, slide_start_y, slide_end_x, slide_end_y, 2.2, 1.7)
            pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (52, 55, 58), slide)
            pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (12, 13, 15), slide, 1)

            # Short barrel / muzzle
            muzzle_x = gun_end_x + arm_dx * 2
            muzzle_y = gun_end_y + arm_dy * 2
            pygame.draw.line(
                surface,
                (70, 74, 78),
                (gun_end_x, gun_end_y),
                (muzzle_x, muzzle_y),
                3,
            )
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, (8, 8, 10), (int(muzzle_x), int(muzzle_y)), 2)

            # Grip angled downward/back
            grip_base_x = gun_start_x - arm_dx * 2 + perp_dx * 3
            grip_base_y = gun_start_y - arm_dy * 2 + perp_dy * 3
            grip_end_x = grip_base_x - arm_dx * 4 + perp_dx * 5
            grip_end_y = grip_base_y - arm_dy * 4 + perp_dy * 5
            pygame.draw.line(
                surface,
                (18, 19, 20),
                (grip_base_x, grip_base_y),
                (grip_end_x, grip_end_y),
                5,
            )

            # Tiny trigger guard
            tg_x = gun_start_x + arm_dx * 4 + perp_dx * 2.5
            tg_y = gun_start_y + arm_dy * 4 + perp_dy * 2.5
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, (18, 19, 20), (int(tg_x), int(tg_y)), 3, 1)

            # Small red optic dot, much less bulky than the old laser module
            dot_x = gun_start_x + arm_dx * 9 - perp_dx * 3
            dot_y = gun_start_y + arm_dy * 9 - perp_dy * 3
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, (180, 35, 35), (int(dot_x), int(dot_y)), 1)

        head_x = draw_x + d_x * 2
        head_y = draw_y + d_y * 2
        head_r = 9
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (30, 35, 28), (int(head_x), int(head_y)), head_r)
        pygame.draw.arc(
            surface,
            (50, 58, 42),
            (int(head_x - head_r), int(head_y - head_r), head_r * 2, head_r * 2),
            a - 0.8,
            a + 0.8,
            2,
        )
        face_x = head_x + d_x * 3
        face_y = head_y + d_y * 3
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, C_PLAYER_SKIN, (int(face_x), int(face_y)), 5)
        for ang_off in (-0.25, 0.25):
            ex = face_x + math.cos(a + ang_off) * 3
            ey = face_y + math.sin(a + ang_off) * 3
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, (30, 20, 10), (int(ex), int(ey)), 1 + blinking)

# weapons.py

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# weapons.py
# ------------------------------------------------------------

"""Player weapons: pistol, melee, molotov cocktails, fire zones."""
import math
import random
import pygame
from config import (
    WIDTH, HEIGHT, TILE_SIZE, audio, Particle, Casing, C_BLOOD,
)
from state import STATE
from effects import spawn_particles, shake
MOLOTOV_RADIUS = 92
MOLOTOV_DURATION = 300
MOLOTOV_ENEMY_DAMAGE = 0.72
MOLOTOV_PLAYER_DAMAGE = 0.28


def _mission2_actions_blocked():
    """Hard safety gate for authored Mission 2 beats.

    The main loop also blocks input, but weapon functions can be reached from
    multiple paths.  Keeping this guard here prevents shooting/reload/molotov
    leaks during sniper/blackout/cinematic locks.
    """
    if getattr(STATE, "active_mission", "") != "MISSION2":
        return False
    return (
        getattr(STATE, "mission2_card", None) is not None
        or getattr(STATE, "mission2_stage", "") in {"sniper", "blackout", "done"}
    )


class FireZone:
    """Persistent ground fire created by a Molotov impact."""
    def __init__(self, x, y):
        self.x = x
        self.y = y
        self.radius = 48
        self.life = 420
        self.max_life = 420
        self.damage_tick = 0
    def update(self, enemies, player_rect, audio_sys):
        self.life -= 1
        self.damage_tick += 1
        player_center = (player_rect.centerx, player_rect.centery) if player_rect else (self.x, self.y)
        if self.damage_tick >= 12:
            self.damage_tick = 0
            for e in enemies:
                if e.hp <= 0:
                    continue
                dist = math.hypot(e.rect.centerx - self.x, e.rect.centery - self.y)
                if dist < self.radius:
                    dmg = 15 if dist < self.radius * 0.4 else 8
                    fire_mult = getattr(e, "fire_weakness", 1.0)
                    e.hp -= dmg * fire_mult
                    e.alerted = True
                    spark_count = 4 if getattr(e, "is_breach_enemy", False) else 3
                    for _ in range(spark_count):
                        STATE.particles.append(Particle(
                            e.rect.centerx + random.randint(-6, 6),
                            e.rect.centery + random.randint(-6, 6),
                            (255, random.randint(80, 210), 0)
                        ))
        if random.random() < 0.04 and audio_sys:
            audio_sys.play_3d("explosion", (self.x, self.y), player_center, base_volume=0.08)
        return self.life > 0
    def draw(self, surface, cam_x, cam_y):
        sx = int(self.x - cam_x)
        sy = int(self.y - cam_y)
        if not (-self.radius - 60 < sx < WIDTH + self.radius + 60 and
                -self.radius - 60 < sy < HEIGHT + self.radius + 60):
            return
        life_ratio = max(0.0, self.life / self.max_life)
        flicker = 0.75 + 0.25 * math.sin(pygame.time.get_ticks() * 0.018)
        # Scorched irregular ground patch
        scorch = pygame.Surface((self.radius * 3, self.radius * 3), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        cx = cy = self.radius * 3 // 2
        rng = random.Random(int(self.x * 13 + self.y * 7))
        points = []
        for i in range(18):
            ang = i / 18.0 * math.pi * 2
            rr = self.radius * rng.uniform(0.75, 1.25)
            points.append((cx + math.cos(ang) * rr, cy + math.sin(ang) * rr * 0.72))
        pygame.draw.polygon(scorch, (10, 7, 5, int(115 * life_ratio)), points)
        pygame.draw.polygon(scorch, (45, 22, 8, int(80 * life_ratio)), points, 2)
        surface.blit(scorch, (sx - cx, sy - cy))
        # Low orange glow, not a giant perfect circle
        glow = pygame.Surface((self.radius * 3, self.radius * 2), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        for r, a in [
            (self.radius + 25, 24),
            (self.radius, 40),
            (self.radius - 20, 65),
        ]:
            if r > 0:
                pygame.draw.ellipse(
                    glow,
                    (255, 95, 20, int(a * life_ratio * flicker)),
                    (
                        self.radius * 3 // 2 - r,
                        self.radius - int(r * 0.45),
                        r * 2,
                        int(r * 0.9),
                    ),
                )
        surface.blit(glow, (sx - self.radius * 3 // 2, sy - self.radius))
        # Pixel flame tongues
        flame_count = int(9 * life_ratio) + 3
        for i in range(flame_count):
            seed = i * 31
            ang = seed * 0.77 + math.sin(pygame.time.get_ticks() * 0.004 + i)
            rr = self.radius * (0.15 + (i % 5) * 0.13)
            fx = sx + math.cos(ang) * rr
            fy = sy + math.sin(ang) * rr * 0.55
            h = int((18 + (i % 4) * 5) * life_ratio * flicker)
            w = max(4, h // 3)
            outer = [
                (fx, fy - h),
                (fx - w, fy + h * 0.25),
                (fx + w, fy + h * 0.25),
            ]
            inner = [
                (fx, fy - h * 0.6),
                (fx - w * 0.45, fy + h * 0.05),
                (fx + w * 0.45, fy + h * 0.05),
            ]
            pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (255, 85, 18), outer)
            pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (255, 205, 80), inner)
        # Smoke wisps
        if life_ratio > 0.2:
            for i in range(5):
                drift = math.sin(pygame.time.get_ticks() * 0.002 + i) * 12
                smoke_x = sx + int(drift) + (i - 2) * 12
                smoke_y = sy - 35 - i * 8
                sr = 7 + i
                smoke_a = int(32 * life_ratio)
                sm = pygame.Surface((sr * 2, sr * 2), pygame.SRCALPHA)
                pygame.draw.circle(sm, (45, 43, 40, smoke_a), (sr, sr), sr)
                surface.blit(sm, (smoke_x - sr, smoke_y - sr))
        if life_ratio > 0.15:
            for _ in range(2):
                smoke_x = sx + random.randint(-int(self.radius * 0.5), int(self.radius * 0.5))
                smoke_y = sy - random.randint(10, 35)
                smoke_r = random.randint(4, 10)
                smoke_a = int(40 * life_ratio)
                smoke_s = pygame.Surface((smoke_r * 2, smoke_r * 2), pygame.SRCALPHA)
                pygame.draw.circle(smoke_s, (50, 45, 40, smoke_a),
                                   (smoke_r, smoke_r), smoke_r)
                surface.blit(smoke_s, (smoke_x - smoke_r, smoke_y - smoke_r))
def _wall_blocks_line(x1, y1, x2, y2, solid_walls):
    for w in solid_walls:
        if w.clipline((x1, y1), (x2, y2)):
            return True
    return False
def _solid_walls():
    out = list(STATE.walls)
    for d in STATE.doors:
        if d.collision_rect:
            out.append(d.collision_rect)
    return out
def start_reload():
    if _mission2_actions_blocked():
        return
    if (STATE.spare_mags <= 0
            or STATE.ammo_in_mag == STATE.mag_size
            or STATE.is_reloading):
        return
    STATE.is_reloading = True
    STATE.reload_timer = 60
    audio.play("reload", volume=0.6)
    if STATE.player:
        for _ in range(6):
            STATE.particles.append(Particle(
                STATE.player.rect.centerx + random.randint(-6, 6),
                STATE.player.rect.centery + random.randint(-4, 4),
                (160, 155, 140),
            ))
def handle_shoot():
    from enemy_ai import enemy_hear_shot
    if _mission2_actions_blocked():
        return
    if (STATE.player_dead or not STATE.player
            or STATE.is_reloading or STATE.shot_cooldown > 0):
        return
    if getattr(STATE.player, "weapon_raised", False):
        return
    if STATE.ammo_in_mag <= 0:
        audio.play("empty_click", volume=0.5)
        return
    p = STATE.player
    if hasattr(p, "barrel_tip"):
        bx, by = p.barrel_tip()
    else:
        bx = p.rect.centerx + math.cos(p.angle) * 39
        by = p.rect.centery + math.sin(p.angle) * 39
    STATE.bullets.append({
        "x": bx,
        "y": by,
        "prev_x": bx,
        "prev_y": by,
        "dx": math.cos(p.angle) * 24,
        "dy": math.sin(p.angle) * 24,
        "life": 20,
        "owner": "player",
    })
    enemy_hear_shot(bx, by, STATE.enemies, _solid_walls())
    shake(13)
    STATE.muzzle_flash_timer = 4
    # ── Recoil — slide snaps back, hand pushes back ──
    p._recoil_timer = 8
    p._recoil_offset = -6.0
    p._slide_timer = 6
    # ── Camera kickback — shifts screen backwards along firing angle ──
    recoil_dir_x = -math.cos(p.angle)
    recoil_dir_y = -math.sin(p.angle)
    STATE.cam_kick_x = recoil_dir_x * 15.0
    STATE.cam_kick_y = recoil_dir_y * 15.0
    # ── Casing ejects 1 frame after shot (visual delay) ──
    eject_x = (p.rect.centerx + math.cos(p.angle) * 8
               + math.cos(p.angle - math.pi / 2) * 10)
    eject_y = (p.rect.centery + math.sin(p.angle) * 8
               + math.sin(p.angle - math.pi / 2) * 10)
    STATE.casings.append(Casing(eject_x, eject_y, p.angle - math.pi / 2))
    STATE.ammo_in_mag -= 1
    STATE.shot_cooldown = 12
    STATE.shots_fired += 1
    audio.play("gunshot", volume=0.7)
    if STATE.ammo_in_mag <= 0 and STATE.spare_mags > 0:
        start_reload()
def handle_melee():
    if _mission2_actions_blocked():
        return
    if STATE.player_dead or not STATE.player or STATE.melee_active:
        return
    STATE.is_reloading = False
    STATE.reload_timer = 0
    STATE.melee_active = True
    STATE.melee_timer = 18
    STATE.melee_hit_done = False
    audio.play("melee_hit", volume=0.6)
def handle_throw_bottle():
    if _mission2_actions_blocked():
        return
    if STATE.player_dead or not STATE.player:
        return
    if STATE.player_bottles <= 0:
        STATE.floating_texts.append([
            STATE.player.rect.centerx,
            STATE.player.rect.centery - 40,
            "NO MOLOTOVS", (220, 60, 60), 60,
        ])
        return
    mx, my = pygame.mouse.get_pos()
    world_mx = mx + STATE.camera_x
    world_my = my + STATE.camera_y
    dx = world_mx - STATE.player.rect.centerx
    dy = world_my - STATE.player.rect.centery
    dist = math.hypot(dx, dy)
    if dist > 400:
        world_mx = STATE.player.rect.centerx + (dx / dist) * 400
        world_my = STATE.player.rect.centery + (dy / dist) * 400
    STATE.player_bottles -= 1
    STATE.thrown_bottles.append({
        "x": STATE.player.rect.centerx,
        "y": STATE.player.rect.centery,
        "start_x": STATE.player.rect.centerx,
        "start_y": STATE.player.rect.centery,
        "tx": world_mx, "ty": world_my,
        "t": 0.0, "speed": 0.035,
        "spin": random.uniform(0, 360),
    })
    audio.play("molotov_throw", volume=0.6)
def spawn_molotov_fire(tx, ty, solid_walls):
    STATE.molotov_fires.append({
        "x": tx, "y": ty,
        "life": MOLOTOV_DURATION,
        "max_life": MOLOTOV_DURATION,
    })
    STATE.fire_zones.append(FireZone(tx, ty))
    shake(12)
    listener = ((STATE.player.rect.centerx, STATE.player.rect.centery)
                if STATE.player else (tx, ty))
    audio.play_3d("molotov_explode", (tx, ty), listener, base_volume=0.9)
    # ── Impact burst ──
    for _ in range(40): spawn_particles(tx, ty, (255, 200, 80), 1)
    for _ in range(30): spawn_particles(tx, ty, (255, 120, 35), 1)
    for _ in range(24): spawn_particles(tx, ty, (80, 75, 70), 1)
    for _ in range(15): spawn_particles(tx, ty, (190, 220, 190), 1)
    # Fire bloom ring expands outward
    for ring_r in range(10, 80, 8):
        for ang in range(0, 360, 30):
            ang_r = math.radians(ang)
            px = tx + math.cos(ang_r) * ring_r
            py = ty + math.sin(ang_r) * ring_r
            STATE.particles.append(Particle(px, py, (255, random.randint(80, 180), 0)))
            STATE.particles[-1].life = random.randint(12, 24)
            STATE.particles[-1].dy -= random.uniform(0.5, 1.5)
    for e in STATE.enemies:
        d = math.hypot(e.rect.centerx - tx, e.rect.centery - ty)
        if d < MOLOTOV_RADIUS * 2.4 and not _wall_blocks_line(
                tx, ty, e.rect.centerx, e.rect.centery, solid_walls):
            e.alerted = True
            e.last_seen_pos = (tx, ty)
            if d < MOLOTOV_RADIUS:
                e.state = "CHASE"
            elif e.state in ("IDLE", "PATROL"):
                e.state = "SEARCH"
                e.search_timer = 240
                e.distraction_target = (tx, ty)
                e.path = []

def _tc(col, row):
    """Tile center in world pixels."""
    return col * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE // 2, row * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE // 2
def _lerp(a, b, t):
    t = max(0.0, min(1.0, t))
    return a + (b - a) * t

                    (e.rect.centerx + 5 * TILE_SIZE, e.rect.centery),

                e.patrol_points = [
                    (e.rect.centerx, e.rect.centery),

            e.state = "PATROL"

                (e.rect.centerx + 4 * TILE_SIZE, e.rect.centery),
