            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (220, 215, 200), (dx - 20, dy - 14, 28, 20), border_radius=1)
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (200, 195, 178), (dx - 20, dy - 14, 28, 20), 1, border_radius=1)
            for li in range(3):
                pygame.draw.line(screen, (140, 135, 120), (dx - 16, dy - 8 + li * 5), (dx + 4, dy - 8 + li * 5), 1)
            # ── Folded note / envelope ──
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (230, 220, 190), (dx + 6, dy - 6, 18, 14), border_radius=1)
            pygame.draw.line(screen, (190, 180, 155), (dx + 6, dy - 6), (dx + 15, dy + 1), 1)
            pygame.draw.line(screen, (190, 180, 155), (dx + 24, dy - 6), (dx + 15, dy + 1), 1)
            # ── Coffee mug ──
            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (42, 32, 24), (dx + 22, dy + 12), 7)
            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (58, 44, 30), (dx + 22, dy + 12), 7, 1)
            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (80, 55, 20), (dx + 22, dy + 12), 4)  # coffee inside
            # ── Unread/read indicator dot ──
            dot_col = (100, 220, 100) if desk["read"] else (240, 190, 50)
            glow2 = pygame.Surface((14, 14), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            pygame.draw.circle(glow2, (*dot_col, 70), (7, 7), 7)
            screen.blit(glow2, (dx - 3, dy - 21))
            pygame.draw.circle(screen, dot_col, (dx, dy - 14), 4)
            label = font_terminal_small.render("DESK", True, (150, 140, 120))
            screen.blit(label, (dx - label.get_width() // 2, dy + 28))
    bay_x = 15 * TILE_SIZE + 32 - fcx
    bay_y = 19 * TILE_SIZE + 32 - fcy
    if -50 < bay_x < WIDTH + 50 and -50 < bay_y < HEIGHT + 50:
        label = font_terminal_small.render("BAY DOOR", True, (150, 140, 120))
        screen.blit(label, (bay_x - label.get_width() // 2, bay_y - 40))
    left_bay = None
    for d in STATE.doors:
        if d.is_bay and (left_bay is None or d.rect.x < left_bay.rect.x):
            left_bay = d
    for d in STATE.doors:
        dr = pygame.Rect(d.rect.x - fcx, d.rect.y - fcy, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE)
        if d.is_bay:
            if d is not left_bay:
                if STATE.player and not STATE.player_dead:
                    pdist = math.hypot(
                        STATE.player.rect.centerx - d.rect.centerx,
                        STATE.player.rect.centery - d.rect.centery,
                    )
                    if pdist <= DOOR_INTERACT_RANGE:
                        prompt = font_terminal_small.render("[E]", True, C_TERMINAL)
                        screen.blit(
                            prompt,
                            (
                                dr.x + TILE_SIZE // 2 - prompt.get_width() // 2,
                                dr.y - 18,
                            ),
                        )
                continue
            bw = TILE_SIZE * 3
            bdr = pygame.Rect(d.rect.x - fcx, d.rect.y - fcy, bw, TILE_SIZE)
            if d.anim_progress < 1.0:
                draw_h = int(TILE_SIZE * (1.0 - d.anim_progress))
                src = pygame.Rect(0, 0, bw, draw_h)
                screen.blit(bay_door_shadow, (bdr.x - 4, bdr.y - 4))
                screen.blit(BAY_DOOR_TILE_3W, (bdr.x, bdr.y), src)
            else:
                screen.blit(BAY_DOOR_FRAME_3W, (bdr.x, bdr.y))
            if STATE.player and not STATE.player_dead:
                pdist = math.hypot(
                    STATE.player.rect.centerx - d.rect.centerx,
                    STATE.player.rect.centery - d.rect.centery,
                )
                if pdist <= DOOR_INTERACT_RANGE:
                    prompt = font_terminal_small.render("[E]", True, C_TERMINAL)
                    screen.blit(
                        prompt, (bdr.x + bw // 2 - prompt.get_width() // 2, bdr.y - 18)
                    )
        else:
            door_tile = DOOR_TILE
            door_frame = DOOR_FRAME_TILE
            if d.anim_progress < 1.0:
                draw_h = int(TILE_SIZE * (1.0 - d.anim_progress))
                src = pygame.Rect(0, 0, TILE_SIZE, draw_h)
                screen.blit(wall_shadow, (dr.x - 4, dr.y - 4))
                screen.blit(door_tile, (dr.x, dr.y), src)
            else:
                screen.blit(door_frame, (dr.x, dr.y))
            if STATE.player and not STATE.player_dead:
                pdist = math.hypot(
                    STATE.player.rect.centerx - d.rect.centerx,
                    STATE.player.rect.centery - d.rect.centery,
                )
                if pdist <= DOOR_INTERACT_RANGE:
                    prompt = font_terminal_small.render("[E]", True, C_TERMINAL)
                    screen.blit(
                        prompt,
                        (dr.x + TILE_SIZE // 2 - prompt.get_width() // 2, dr.y - 18),
                    )
    for decal in STATE.blood_decals[:]:
        d_surf = pygame.Surface((decal[3] * 2, decal[3] * 2), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.circle(
            d_surf, (130, 10, 10, int(decal[2])), (decal[3], decal[3]), decal[3]
        )
        screen.blit(d_surf, (decal[0] - decal[3] - fcx, decal[1] - decal[3] - fcy))
        decal[2] -= 0.5
        if decal[2] <= 0:
            STATE.blood_decals.remove(decal)
    # Draw fire zones
    for fz in STATE.fire_zones:
        fz.draw(screen, fcx, fcy)
    for b in STATE.bullets:
        screen.blit(bullet_glow, (int(b["x"] - fcx - 8), int(b["y"] - fcy - 8)))
        pygame.draw.circle(screen, C_BULLET, (int(b["x"] - fcx), int(b["y"] - fcy)), 2)
        pygame.draw.line(
            screen,
            (255, 220, 140),
            (b["x"] - fcx, b["y"] - fcy),
            (b.get("prev_x", b["x"] - b["dx"]) - fcx,
             b.get("prev_y", b["y"] - b["dy"]) - fcy),
            2,
        )
    # ── Thrown bottles (in flight) ──
    for b in STATE.thrown_bottles:
        t = b["t"]
        arc_height = 40
        arc_y_off = -math.sin(t * math.pi) * arc_height
        bx = b["x"] - fcx
        by = b["y"] - fcy + arc_y_off
        shadow_alpha = int(60 * (1 - abs(arc_y_off / arc_height)))
        shadow_surf = pygame.Surface((14, 5), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.ellipse(shadow_surf, (0, 0, 0, shadow_alpha), (0, 0, 14, 5))
        screen.blit(shadow_surf, (int(bx - 7), int(b["y"] - fcy - 2)))
        rot = b.get("spin", 0) + t * 900
        cos_r = math.cos(math.radians(rot))
        sin_r = math.sin(math.radians(rot))
        def _rot(lx, ly):
            return (
                bx + lx * cos_r - ly * sin_r,
                by + lx * sin_r + ly * cos_r,
            )
        # Molotov bottle silhouette
        bottle_pts = [
            _rot(-2.5, -12), _rot(2.5, -12),
            _rot(2.5, -8),
            _rot(1.5, -5),
            _rot(4.5, 0),
            _rot(4.5, 12),
            _rot(-4.5, 12),
            _rot(-4.5, 0),
            _rot(-1.5, -5),
            _rot(-2.5, -8),
        ]
        pygame.draw.polygon(screen, (95, 70, 35), bottle_pts)
        pygame.draw.polygon(screen, (210, 150, 60), bottle_pts, 1)
        pygame.draw.line(screen, (255, 120, 35), _rot(0, 1), _rot(0, 10), 3)
        pygame.draw.line(screen, (255, 210, 110), _rot(-2.5, 0), _rot(-2.5, 8), 1)
        pygame.draw.line(screen, (70, 55, 35), _rot(0, -13), _rot(0, -19), 3)
        flame_tip = _rot(0, -27)
        flame_l = _rot(-5, -17)
        flame_r = _rot(5, -17)
        flame_mid_l = _rot(-2.5, -18)
        flame_mid_r = _rot(2.5, -18)
        flame_mid_t = _rot(0, -24)
        flame_glow = pygame.Surface((34, 34), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.circle(flame_glow, (255, 100, 20, 80), (17, 17), 15)
        screen.blit(flame_glow, (int(flame_tip[0] - 17), int(flame_tip[1] - 17)))
        pygame.draw.polygon(screen, (255, 80, 15), [flame_tip, flame_l, flame_r])
        pygame.draw.polygon(screen, (255, 220, 80), [flame_mid_t, flame_mid_l, flame_mid_r])
        for trail_i in range(5):
            trail_t = max(0, t - 0.025 * (trail_i + 1))
            trail_arc = -math.sin(trail_t * math.pi) * arc_height
            trail_x = b["start_x"] + (b["tx"] - b["start_x"]) * trail_t - fcx
            trail_y = b["start_y"] + (b["ty"] - b["start_y"]) * trail_t - fcy + trail_arc
            ta = 110 - trail_i * 18
            ts = pygame.Surface((10, 10), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            if trail_i < 2:
                pygame.draw.circle(ts, (255, 120, 30, ta), (5, 5), 4)
            else:
                pygame.draw.circle(ts, (70, 65, 60, ta), (5, 5), 4)
            screen.blit(ts, (int(trail_x - 5), int(trail_y - 5)))
    if STATE.firefight_triggered and STATE.volkov_escape_timer > 0:
        if STATE.volkov_escape_pos and not STATE.volkov_escaped:
            vx = STATE.volkov_escape_pos[0] - fcx
            vy = STATE.volkov_escape_pos[1] - fcy + 30
            if -100 < vx < WIDTH + 100 and -100 < vy < HEIGHT + 100:
                facing = STATE.volkov_escape_pos[0] < (15 * TILE_SIZE + 32)
                draw_volkov(screen, int(vx), int(vy), scale=0.35, facing_right=facing)
        if STATE.commander_escape_pos and not STATE.commander_escaped:
            kx = STATE.commander_escape_pos[0] - fcx
            ky = STATE.commander_escape_pos[1] - fcy + 30

                facing = STATE.commander_escape_pos[0] < (15 * TILE_SIZE + 32)
                draw_commander(
                    screen, int(kx), int(ky), scale=0.35, facing_right=facing
                )
    for e in STATE.enemies:
        e.draw(screen, fcx, fcy)
    reload_progress = 0.0
    if STATE.is_reloading and STATE.reload_timer > 0:
        reload_progress = 1.0 - STATE.reload_timer / 60.0
    melee_progress = 0.0
    if STATE.melee_active and STATE.melee_timer > 0:
        melee_progress = 1.0 - STATE.melee_timer / 18.0
    if STATE.player:
        STATE.player.draw(
            screen,
            fcx,
            fcy,
            STATE.walls,
            STATE.is_reloading,

            is_moving=not STATE.player_dead and (

                    _last_keys[pygame.K_a] or _last_keys[pygame.K_d]
                )
            ),
        )
    for p in STATE.particles:
        p.draw(screen, fcx, fcy)
    for c in STATE.casings:
        c.draw(screen, fcx, fcy)
    for pu in STATE.pickups:
        px = pu[0] - fcx
        py = pu[1] - fcy + math.sin(time.time() * 3 + pu[0]) * 3
        glow = pygame.Surface((32, 32), pygame.SRCALPHA)

            pygame.draw.circle(glow, (100, 255, 100, 40), (16, 16), 16)
        elif pu[2] == "bottle":
            pygame.draw.circle(glow, (100, 200, 130, 40), (16, 16), 16)
        else:
            pygame.draw.circle(glow, (255, 210, 40, 40), (16, 16), 16)
        screen.blit(glow, (int(px - 16), int(py - 16)))
        if pu[2] == "mag":
            top_w, bot_w, h = 14, 11, 24
            taper = (top_w - bot_w) // 2
            mg_pts = [
                (px - top_w // 2, py - h // 2),

            pygame.draw.polygon(screen, (255, 210, 40), mg_pts, 1)
            for bi in range(5):
                by = py - h // 2 + 5 + bi * 3
                pygame.draw.circle(screen, (220, 185, 45), (px, by), 2)
        elif pu[2] == "keycard":
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (200, 200, 200), (px - 8, py - 12, 16, 24))
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (255, 50, 50), (px - 8, py - 6, 16, 6))
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (100, 100, 100), (px - 8, py - 12, 16, 24), 1)
        elif pu[2] == "bandage":
            pygame.draw.rect(

            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (50, 30, 15), (px - 5, py - 6, 10, 16), 1, border_radius=2)
    if STATE.firefight_triggered and not STATE.notebook_collected and STATE.NOTEBOOK_POS:
        nx = STATE.NOTEBOOK_POS[0] - fcx
        ny = STATE.NOTEBOOK_POS[1] - fcy + math.sin(time.time() * 2) * 3

        pygame.draw.rect(screen, (15, 15, 15), (nx - 8, ny - 10, 16, 20))

        pygame.draw.line(screen, (40, 40, 40), (nx, ny - 10), (nx, ny + 10), 1)
        if (
            STATE.player
            and math.hypot(
                STATE.player.rect.centerx - STATE.NOTEBOOK_POS[0],
                STATE.player.rect.centery - STATE.NOTEBOOK_POS[1],
            )
            < 80
        ):
            nearby = [
                e
                for e in STATE.enemies

            else:
                txt = font_prompt.render("CLEAR HOSTILES FIRST", True, (220, 60, 60))
            screen.blit(txt, (int(nx) - txt.get_width() // 2, int(ny) - 35))
    if STATE.notebook_collected and STATE.EXFIL_POS:
        ex = STATE.EXFIL_POS[0] - fcx
        ey = STATE.EXFIL_POS[1] - fcy
        # ──── Draw top-down military truck ────
        # Body shadow

        # Cab (front box, right side — truck faces right)

        pygame.draw.rect(screen, (38, 48, 34), (cab_x, int(ey - 22), 34, 44), border_radius=3)
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, (52, 62, 46), (cab_x, int(ey - 22), 34, 44), 1, border_radius=3)
        # Cab windshield (darker tinted rectangle)
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, (22, 30, 28), (cab_x + 18, int(ey - 16), 12, 32), border_radius=2)
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, (34, 44, 40), (cab_x + 18, int(ey - 16), 12, 32), 1, border_radius=2)

        pygame.draw.rect(screen, (32, 40, 28), (cab_x + 30, int(ey - 14), 10, 28), 1, border_radius=2)
        # Headlights
        pygame.draw.ellipse(screen, (200, 200, 140), (cab_x + 38, int(ey - 14), 8, 6))
        pygame.draw.ellipse(screen, (200, 200, 140), (cab_x + 38, int(ey + 8),  8, 6))
        # Tail-lights (rear left)
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, (180, 40, 30), (int(ex - 52), int(ey - 22), 4, 8))
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, (180, 40, 30), (int(ex - 52), int(ey + 14),  4, 8))
        # Wheels (6 wheels – 3 axles visible from top)
        wheel_col = (16, 16, 18)
        rim_col   = (55, 58, 62)
        for wx, wy_pair in [
            (int(ex - 36), (int(ey - 26), int(ey + 20))),
            (int(ex +  4), (int(ey - 26), int(ey + 20))),
            (int(ex + 28), (int(ey - 26), int(ey + 20))),
        ]:
            for wy in wy_pair:
                pygame.draw.ellipse(screen, wheel_col, (wx - 7, wy - 4, 14, 8))
                pygame.draw.ellipse(screen, rim_col,   (wx - 4, wy - 2,  8, 5))
        # KRAP marking on cab door
        krap_lbl = font_terminal_small.render("KRAP", True, C_KRAP_BLUE)
        screen.blit(krap_lbl, (cab_x + 2, int(ey - 6)))
        # ──── Exfil pulsing ring and label (above truck) ────
        pulse = int(80 + math.sin(time.time() * 3) * 40)
        circle_surf = pygame.Surface((100, 100), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.circle(circle_surf, (50, 200, 100, pulse), (50, 50), 40, 3)
        pygame.draw.circle(circle_surf, (50, 200, 100, pulse // 2), (50, 50), 30, 2)
        screen.blit(circle_surf, (int(ex - 50), int(ey - 50)))
        ext = font_prompt.render("EXFIL", True, (50, 200, 100))
        screen.blit(ext, (int(ex) - ext.get_width() // 2, int(ey) - 72))

            STATE.player

            )
            < 80
        ):
            if not STATE.truck_hotwiring:
                if STATE.enemies:

                        f"CLEAR HOSTILES FIRST [{len(STATE.enemies)} LEFT]", True, (230, 80, 60)
                    )
                else:
                    prompt_txt = font_prompt.render(
                        "[E] OPEN IGNITION PANEL", True, (100, 255, 100)
                    )
                screen.blit(
                    prompt_txt, (int(ex) - prompt_txt.get_width() // 2, int(ey) - 85)
                )
    if STATE.truck_hotwiring:
        # First-person-ish ignition panel overlay with a short open animation.
        open_t = min(1.0, getattr(STATE, "hotwire_panel_timer", 0) / 34.0)
        ease = 1 - (1 - open_t) ** 3
        overlay = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        overlay.fill((0, 0, 0, int(112 * ease)))
        screen.blit(overlay, (0, 0))
        panel_w, panel_h = 640, 410
        panel_x = WIDTH // 2 - panel_w // 2
        panel_y = int(HEIGHT // 2 - panel_h // 2 + (1.0 - ease) * 90)
        panel = pygame.Surface((panel_w, panel_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        panel.fill((10, 12, 15, 236))
        pygame.draw.rect(panel, (80, 82, 78), panel.get_rect(), 2)
        pygame.draw.rect(panel, C_KRAP_BLUE, (0, 0, 5, panel_h))
        # scratched metal plate
        rng_panel = random.Random(74)
        for _ in range(34):
            x1 = rng_panel.randint(24, panel_w - 24)
            y1 = rng_panel.randint(58, panel_h - 20)
            pygame.draw.line(panel, (30, 34, 38, 100), (x1, y1), (x1 + rng_panel.randint(8, 40), y1 + rng_panel.randint(-2, 2)), 1)
        screen.blit(panel, (panel_x, panel_y))
        title = font_terminal.render("VAN IGNITION PANEL", True, (180, 205, 210))
        screen.blit(title, (panel_x + 26, panel_y + 18))
        sub = font_terminal_small.render("Bridge the correct exposed wires. Wrong pairs short the panel.", True, (150, 150, 135))
        screen.blit(sub, (panel_x + 28, panel_y + 48))
        nodes = _hotwire_wire_nodes()
        used = {w for pair in STATE.hotwire_connections for w in pair}
        # Draw loose wire tails from side openings.
        for name, data in nodes.items():
            x, y = data["pos"]
            col = data["color"]
            side = data["side"]
            start_x = panel_x + 36 if side == "L" else panel_x + panel_w - 36
            mid_x = x - 34 if side == "L" else x + 34
            pygame.draw.line(screen, (18, 18, 20), (start_x, y + 3), (mid_x, y + 3), 7)
            pygame.draw.line(screen, col, (start_x, y), (mid_x, y), 5)
            pygame.draw.line(screen, col, (mid_x, y), (x, y), 4)
            # copper exposed tip
            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (210, 135, 70), (x, y), 9)
            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (40, 24, 12), (x, y), 9, 1)
            if name == STATE.hotwire_selected_wire:
                glow = pygame.Surface((58, 58), pygame.SRCALPHA)
                pygame.draw.circle(glow, (*col, 85), (29, 29), 26)
                screen.blit(glow, (x - 29, y - 29))
            if name in used:
                pygame.draw.circle(screen, (100, 255, 150), (x, y), 14, 2)
            label = font_terminal_small.render(name, True, col if name != "BLACK" else (120, 120, 125))
            lx = x - label.get_width() - 18 if side == "L" else x + 18
            screen.blit(label, (lx, y - 8))
        # Draw completed bridges.
        for pair in STATE.hotwire_connections:
            a, b = pair
            ax, ay = nodes[a]["pos"]
            bx, by = nodes[b]["pos"]
            pair_key = frozenset(pair)
            purpose = HOTWIRE_TARGET_PAIRS.get(pair_key, "")
            col = (95, 240, 145)
            # cable curve with three segments
            mid_y = (ay + by) // 2 - 24
            pygame.draw.line(screen, (18, 35, 24), (ax, ay), (WIDTH // 2, mid_y), 8)
            pygame.draw.line(screen, (18, 35, 24), (WIDTH // 2, mid_y), (bx, by), 8)
            pygame.draw.line(screen, col, (ax, ay), (WIDTH // 2, mid_y), 4)
            pygame.draw.line(screen, col, (WIDTH // 2, mid_y), (bx, by), 4)
            # Purpose text is shown in the status lights below; avoid center overlap.
        # Status lights.
        statuses = ["POWER", "IGNITION", "STARTER"]
        connected = {HOTWIRE_TARGET_PAIRS.get(frozenset(pair)) for pair in STATE.hotwire_connections}
        for i, st in enumerate(statuses):
            sx = panel_x + 128 + i * 150
            sy = panel_y + panel_h - 76
            live = st in connected
            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (70, 240, 120) if live else (50, 55, 55), (sx, sy), 9)
            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (10, 15, 12), (sx, sy), 9, 1)
            stxt = font_terminal_small.render(st, True, (160, 220, 170) if live else (90, 95, 95))
            screen.blit(stxt, (sx + 16, sy - 8))
        # Sparks on wrong pair / fresh connection.
        if STATE.hotwire_spark_timer > 0:
            rng_s = random.Random(pygame.time.get_ticks() // 20)
            center = nodes.get(STATE.hotwire_selected_wire or "RED", {"pos": (WIDTH // 2, HEIGHT // 2)})["pos"]
            for _ in range(18 if STATE.hotwire_fail_flash > 0 else 8):
                ang = rng_s.random() * math.tau
                dist = rng_s.randint(8, 46)
                sx = center[0] + math.cos(ang) * dist
                sy = center[1] + math.sin(ang) * dist
                pygame.draw.line(screen, (255, 210, 90), center, (sx, sy), 1)
        if STATE.hotwire_fail_flash > 0:
            flash = pygame.Surface((panel_w, panel_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            flash.fill((255, 55, 35, min(95, STATE.hotwire_fail_flash * 8)))
            screen.blit(flash, (panel_x, panel_y))
        msg_col = (220, 90, 70) if STATE.hotwire_fail_flash > 0 else (180, 215, 190)
        msg = font_prompt.render(STATE.hotwire_message or "Click a wire endpoint", True, msg_col)
        screen.blit(msg, (WIDTH // 2 - msg.get_width() // 2, panel_y + panel_h - 34))
        hint = font_terminal_small.render("VASILE'S HINT: red feeds yellow / blue wakes green / black bites white", True, (115, 125, 120))
        screen.blit(hint, (WIDTH // 2 - hint.get_width() // 2, panel_y + panel_h + 10))
    pinned = [ft for ft in STATE.floating_texts if len(ft) >= 6 and ft[5] == "pin"]
    unpinned = [ft for ft in STATE.floating_texts if not (len(ft) >= 6 and ft[5] == "pin")]
    for ft in unpinned:
        txt = font_terminal_small.render(ft[2], True, ft[3])
        screen.blit(txt, (ft[0] - fcx - txt.get_width() // 2, ft[1] - fcy))
    radio_top_y = HEIGHT - 260
    for i, ft in enumerate(pinned):
        alpha = min(255, ft[4] * 3)
        txt_surf = font_terminal_small.render(ft[2], True, ft[3])
        bg = pygame.Surface(
            (txt_surf.get_width() + 16, txt_surf.get_height() + 6), pygame.SRCALPHA
        )
        pygame.draw.rect(bg, (0, 0, 0, min(180, alpha)), bg.get_rect())
        pygame.draw.line(
            bg,
            (ft[3][0], ft[3][1], ft[3][2], min(200, alpha)),
            (0, 0),
            (0, bg.get_height()),
            3,
        )
        faded = pygame.Surface(txt_surf.get_size(), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        faded.blit(txt_surf, (0, 0))
        faded.set_alpha(alpha)
        row_y = radio_top_y + i * (txt_surf.get_height() + 8)
        screen.blit(bg, (20, row_y - 3))
        screen.blit(faded, (28, row_y))
    ch_x, ch_y = get_scaled_mouse()
    if STATE.truck_hotwiring:
        # Compact pointing-hand cursor. The ACTUAL click/contact point is the
        # fingertip at (ch_x, ch_y), so it no longer feels offset.
        skin = (218, 178, 136)
        skin_hi = (238, 205, 165)
        outline = (54, 36, 24)
        glove_shadow = (120, 82, 55)
        cuff_col = (56, 68, 50)
        # soft shadow
        pygame.draw.polygon(
            screen, (0, 0, 0, 95),
            [(ch_x + 2, ch_y + 3), (ch_x - 7, ch_y + 13), (ch_x - 4, ch_y + 39),
             (ch_x - 19, ch_y + 47), (ch_x - 9, ch_y + 66), (ch_x + 22, ch_y + 62),
             (ch_x + 26, ch_y + 40), (ch_x + 10, ch_y + 34), (ch_x + 8, ch_y + 12)],
        )
        # index finger: pointed tip exactly at mouse position
        finger = [
            (ch_x, ch_y),
            (ch_x - 6, ch_y + 9),
            (ch_x - 5, ch_y + 35),
            (ch_x + 6, ch_y + 35),
            (ch_x + 7, ch_y + 9),
        ]
        pygame.draw.polygon(screen, outline, finger)
        inner_finger = [
            (ch_x, ch_y + 2),
            (ch_x - 4, ch_y + 10),
            (ch_x - 3, ch_y + 33),
            (ch_x + 4, ch_y + 33),
            (ch_x + 5, ch_y + 10),
        ]
        pygame.draw.polygon(screen, skin, inner_finger)
        pygame.draw.line(screen, skin_hi, (ch_x - 2, ch_y + 10), (ch_x - 2, ch_y + 29), 1)
        # palm
        palm = pygame.Rect(ch_x - 17, ch_y + 30, 35, 27)
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, outline, palm.inflate(4, 4), border_radius=8)
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, skin, palm, border_radius=7)
        # folded fingers
        for i, fx in enumerate((-12, -3, 6)):
            r = pygame.Rect(ch_x + fx, ch_y + 43 + (i % 2), 9, 17)
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, outline, r.inflate(2, 2), border_radius=4)
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, skin, r, border_radius=3)
            pygame.draw.line(screen, glove_shadow, (r.x + 2, r.y + 6), (r.x + r.w - 2, r.y + 6), 1)
        # thumb wrapped left
        thumb = [(ch_x - 15, ch_y + 37), (ch_x - 28, ch_y + 42), (ch_x - 26, ch_y + 54), (ch_x - 12, ch_y + 50)]
        pygame.draw.polygon(screen, outline, thumb)
        pygame.draw.polygon(screen, skin, [(ch_x - 15, ch_y + 39), (ch_x - 25, ch_y + 43), (ch_x - 23, ch_y + 51), (ch_x - 12, ch_y + 48)])
        # sleeve/cuff
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, outline, (ch_x - 13, ch_y + 59, 31, 16), border_radius=4)
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, cuff_col, (ch_x - 10, ch_y + 60, 26, 14), border_radius=3)
        pygame.draw.line(screen, (34, 42, 30), (ch_x - 8, ch_y + 64), (ch_x + 14, ch_y + 64), 1)
        # exact contact point marker on top of the fingertip
        pygame.draw.circle(screen, C_ORANGE, (ch_x, ch_y), 3, 1)
    else:
        pygame.draw.circle(screen, C_ORANGE, (ch_x, ch_y), 14, 1)
        pygame.draw.circle(screen, C_ORANGE, (ch_x, ch_y), 2)
        gap = 3
        for x1, y1, x2, y2 in [
            (ch_x - 12, ch_y, ch_x - gap, ch_y),
            (ch_x + gap, ch_y, ch_x + 12, ch_y),
            (ch_x, ch_y - 12, ch_x, ch_y - gap),
            (ch_x, ch_y + gap, ch_x, ch_y + 12),
        ]:
            pygame.draw.line(screen, C_ORANGE, (x1, y1), (x2, y2), 1)
    if STATE.muzzle_flash_timer > 0:
        if STATE.player:
            if hasattr(STATE.player, "barrel_tip"):
                tip_x, tip_y = STATE.player.barrel_tip()
            else:
                tip_x = STATE.player.rect.centerx + math.cos(STATE.player.angle) * 39
                tip_y = STATE.player.rect.centery + math.sin(STATE.player.angle) * 39
            flash_x = tip_x - fcx
            flash_y = tip_y - fcy
        else:
            flash_x, flash_y = ch_x, ch_y
        screen.blit(MUZZLE_FLASH, (int(flash_x - 40), int(flash_y - 40)))
        STATE.muzzle_flash_timer -= 1
    if STATE.player_dead:
        death_overlay = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pulse = abs(STATE.death_timer - 60) / 60.0
        death_overlay.fill((120, 10, 10, int(80 * pulse)))
        screen.blit(death_overlay, (0, 0))
    if STATE.gc_flash_timer > 0 and not getattr(STATE, "reduced_motion", False):
        flash_surf = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        flash_surf.fill((255, 255, 255, min(255, STATE.gc_flash_timer * 22)))
        screen.blit(flash_surf, (0, 0))
    # ── Breach stage flicker ──
    if STATE.breach_stage in (3, 4):
        if (pygame.time.get_ticks() // 80) % 3 == 0:
            flicker = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            flicker.fill((0, 0, 0, random.randint(40, 120)))
            screen.blit(flicker, (0, 0))
    draw_scanlines(screen)
    draw_vignette(screen)
    if STATE.player and not STATE.truck_hotwiring:
        hb_x, hb_y, hb_w, hb_h = 30, HEIGHT - 50, 160, 14
        hp_ratio = max(0.0, STATE.player.hp / STATE.player.max_hp)
        hud_bg = pygame.Surface((hb_w + 20, hb_h + 28), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.rect(hud_bg, (0, 0, 0, 140), (0, 0, hb_w + 20, hb_h + 28))
        screen.blit(hud_bg, (hb_x - 10, hb_y - 16))
        screen.blit(
            font_terminal_small.render("HP", True, C_TERMINAL), (hb_x, hb_y - 14)
        )
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, (30, 30, 30), (hb_x, hb_y, hb_w, hb_h))
        if hp_ratio > 0.5:
            bar_col = (int(200 * hp_ratio), int(220 * hp_ratio), 40)
        elif hp_ratio > 0.25:
            bar_col = (220, int(200 * hp_ratio * 2), 40)
        else:
            bar_col = (220, 40, 40)
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, bar_col, (hb_x, hb_y, int(hb_w * hp_ratio), hb_h))
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, C_TERMINAL, (hb_x, hb_y, hb_w, hb_h), 1)
        obj_text = "OBJECTIVE: Locate Volkov in the warehouse"
        if STATE.mission_phase == "firefight":
            if STATE.notebook_collected:
                obj_text = "OBJECTIVE: Reach exfil"
            elif len(STATE.enemies) > 1:
                obj_text = f"OBJECTIVE: Clear hostiles ({len(STATE.enemies)} remaining)"
            else:
                obj_text = "OBJECTIVE: Recover the notebook"
        elif STATE.mission_phase == "escape":
            obj_text = "OBJECTIVE: Reach exfil zone"
        obj_bg = pygame.Surface((450, 30), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.rect(obj_bg, (0, 0, 0, 150), (0, 0, 450, 30))
        pygame.draw.line(obj_bg, C_KRAP_BLUE, (0, 0), (0, 30), 3)
        screen.blit(obj_bg, (20, LETTER_H + 10))
        obj_surf = font_prompt.render(obj_text, True, C_WHITE)
        screen.blit(obj_surf, (30, LETTER_H + 18))
        ammo_x, ammo_y = 30, hb_y - 96
        bg_w, bg_h = 246, 92
        bg = pygame.Surface((bg_w, bg_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.rect(bg, (0, 0, 0, 140), (0, 0, bg_w, bg_h), border_radius=4)
        pygame.draw.rect(bg, (40, 42, 38), (0, 0, bg_w, bg_h), 1, border_radius=4)
        screen.blit(bg, (ammo_x - 6, ammo_y))
        for i in range(4):
            mx = ammo_x + 8 + i * 36
            my = ammo_y + 8
            is_active = i == 0
            if is_active:
                bullets_fill = min(STATE.ammo_in_mag, 12)
                is_empty = STATE.ammo_in_mag <= 0
            else:
                bullets_fill = 12 if STATE.spare_mags >= i else 0
                is_empty = STATE.spare_mags < i
            top_w, bot_w, h = 24, 20, 46
            taper = (top_w - bot_w) // 2
            body_pts = [
                (mx, my),
                (mx + top_w, my),
                (mx + taper + bot_w, my + h),
                (mx + taper, my + h),
            ]
            if is_empty:
                base = (45, 42, 38)
                hl = (55, 52, 48)
                rim = (55, 50, 45)
            elif is_active:
                base = (90, 86, 78)
                hl = (115, 110, 100)
                rim = (70, 66, 58)
            else:
                base = (72, 68, 62)
                hl = (92, 88, 80)
                rim = (60, 56, 50)
            pygame.draw.polygon(screen, base, body_pts)
            pygame.draw.polygon(
                screen,
                hl,
                [
                    (mx + 1, my + 2),
                    (mx + 5, my + 2),
                    (mx + taper + 3, my + h - 3),
                    (mx + taper + 1, my + h - 3),
                ],
            )
            outline = (
                C_TERMINAL if is_active else (rim if not is_empty else (55, 50, 45))
            )
            pygame.draw.polygon(screen, outline, body_pts, 1)
            bullet_y = my + 8
            gold = (220, 185, 45)
            dark = (55, 52, 48) if not is_empty else (40, 38, 35)
            for bi in range(12):
                col = gold if bi < bullets_fill else dark
                pygame.draw.circle(screen, col, (mx + top_w // 2, bullet_y + bi * 3), 2)
            if is_empty:
                pygame.draw.line(
                    screen,
                    (90, 55, 55),
                    (mx + 2, my + 2),
                    (mx + top_w - 2, my + h - 2),
                    1,
                )
                pygame.draw.line(
                    screen,
                    (90, 55, 55),
                    (mx + top_w - 2, my + 2),
                    (mx + 2, my + h - 2),
                    1,
                )
        ammo_text = f"{STATE.ammo_in_mag}/{STATE.mag_size}"
        text_col = C_TERMINAL if STATE.ammo_in_mag > 0 else (220, 60, 60)
        screen.blit(
            font_terminal_small.render(ammo_text, True, text_col),
            (ammo_x + 154, ammo_y + 8),
        )
        screen.blit(
            font_terminal_small.render("9MM", True, (100, 110, 100)),
            (ammo_x + 154, ammo_y + 26),
        )
        if STATE.is_reloading:
            bar_x, bar_y = ammo_x + 148, ammo_y + 46
            bar_w, bar_h = 80, 8
            progress = 1.0 - STATE.reload_timer / 60.0
            pygame.draw.rect(
                screen, (50, 50, 50), (bar_x, bar_y, bar_w, bar_h), border_radius=2
            )
            pygame.draw.rect(
                screen,
                (220, 160, 40),
                (bar_x, bar_y, int(bar_w * progress), bar_h),
                border_radius=2,
            )
            reload_lbl = font_terminal_small.render("RELOADING", True, (220, 160, 40))
            screen.blit(reload_lbl, (ammo_x + 150, ammo_y + 58))
        else:
            mag_label = f"MAGS: {STATE.spare_mags}"
            screen.blit(
                font_terminal_small.render(mag_label, True, (140, 150, 130)),
                (ammo_x + 154, ammo_y + 44),
            )
        # ── Bottle HUD ──
        bot_x = ammo_x - 6
        bot_y = ammo_y - 42
        bot_bg_w, bot_bg_h = 246, 36
        bot_bg = pygame.Surface((bot_bg_w, bot_bg_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.rect(bot_bg, (0, 0, 0, 140), (0, 0, bot_bg_w, bot_bg_h), border_radius=4)
        pygame.draw.rect(bot_bg, (40, 42, 38), (0, 0, bot_bg_w, bot_bg_h), 1, border_radius=4)
        screen.blit(bot_bg, (bot_x, bot_y))
        for bi in range(4):
            icon_x = bot_x + 18 + bi * 26
            icon_y = bot_y + 23
            _draw_molotov_icon(screen, icon_x, icon_y, bi < STATE.player_bottles)
        bot_lbl = font_terminal_small.render(
            f"MOLOTOVS  [G]", True,
            (255, 150, 60) if STATE.player_bottles > 0 else (130, 80, 80),
        )
        screen.blit(bot_lbl, (bot_x + 120, bot_y + 4))
        bot_count = font_terminal_small.render(
            f"{STATE.player_bottles} / 4", True,
            C_TERMINAL if STATE.player_bottles > 0 else (180, 60, 60),
        )
        screen.blit(bot_count, (bot_x + 120, bot_y + 18))
        hud_bg2 = pygame.Surface((180, 26), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.rect(hud_bg2, (0, 0, 0, 120), (0, 0, 180, 26))
        screen.blit(hud_bg2, (20, 20))
        screen.blit(
            font_terminal_small.render(f"HOSTILES: {len(STATE.enemies)}", True, C_TERMINAL),
            (30, 25),
        )
    if STATE.show_minimap and STATE.minimap:
        STATE.minimap.draw(
            screen,
            STATE.player,
            STATE.enemies,
            fcx,
            fcy,
            STATE.NOTEBOOK_POS,
            STATE.EXFIL_POS,
            STATE.volkov_pos,
            STATE.commander_pos,
        )

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# cutscenes.py
# ------------------------------------------------------------

"""Cutscene visuals and dialogue: character drawings, scene backgrounds, shot lists."""
import math
import random
import pygame
from config import *
def draw_volkov(surface, cx, foot_y, scale=1.0, facing_right=True):
    d = int(facing_right) * 2 - 1
    s = scale
    head_r = int(28 * s)
    torso_h = int(110 * s)
    torso_w = int(60 * s)
    leg_h = int(90 * s)
    leg_w = int(22 * s)
    arm_w = int(16 * s)
    arm_h = int(90 * s)
    foot_l = (cx - int(18 * s), foot_y)
    foot_r = (cx + int(18 * s), foot_y)
    hip_y = foot_y - leg_h
    sho_y = hip_y - torso_h
    head_y = sho_y - head_r - int(8 * s)
    shadow = pygame.Surface((int(80 * s), int(16 * s)), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    shadow.fill((0, 0, 0, 60))
    surface.blit(shadow, (cx - int(40 * s), foot_y - int(8 * s)))
    leg_col = (18, 18, 24)
    for lx in (cx - int(18 * s), cx + int(18 * s)):
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, leg_col, (lx - leg_w // 2, hip_y, leg_w, leg_h))
    shoe_col = (12, 10, 10)
    pygame.draw.ellipse(
        surface,
        shoe_col,
        (foot_l[0] - int(14 * s), foot_y - int(8 * s), int(28 * s), int(16 * s)),
    )
    pygame.draw.ellipse(
        surface,
        shoe_col,
        (foot_r[0] - int(14 * s), foot_y - int(8 * s), int(28 * s), int(16 * s)),
    )
    coat_col = (14, 14, 20)
    rim_col = (255, 110, 40)
    rim_pts = [
        (cx - int(torso_w // 2 + 4) * d, sho_y),
        (cx + int(torso_w // 2 + 4) * d, sho_y),
        (cx + int(torso_w // 2 + 8) * d, hip_y),
        (cx - int(torso_w // 2 + 8) * d, hip_y),
    ]
    pygame.draw.polygon(
        surface,
        (int(rim_col[0] * 0.4), int(rim_col[1] * 0.4), int(rim_col[2] * 0.4)),
        rim_pts,
    )
    coat_pts = [
        (cx - torso_w // 2, sho_y),
        (cx + torso_w // 2, sho_y),
        (cx + torso_w // 2 + int(6 * s), hip_y),
        (cx - torso_w // 2 - int(6 * s), hip_y),
    ]
    pygame.draw.polygon(surface, coat_col, coat_pts)
    pygame.draw.line(
        surface,
        (30, 28, 32),
        (cx, sho_y),
        (cx - int(8 * s) * d, sho_y + int(40 * s)),
        int(3 * s),
    )
    arm_side_x = cx + int(torso_w // 2) * d
    back_arm_x = cx - int(torso_w // 2) * d
    pygame.draw.rect(
        surface, (18, 18, 24), (back_arm_x - arm_w // 2, sho_y, arm_w, arm_h)
    )
    pygame.draw.rect(
        surface, (18, 18, 24), (arm_side_x - arm_w // 2, sho_y, arm_w, arm_h)
    )
    pygame.draw.circle(
        surface,
        (int(rim_col[0] * 0.6), int(rim_col[1] * 0.3), int(rim_col[2] * 0.1)),
        (cx + int(rim_col[0] * 0.01 * d), head_y),
        head_r + int(5 * s),
    )
    skin_col = (42, 36, 32)
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, skin_col, (cx, head_y), head_r)
    pygame.draw.arc(
        surface,
        (8, 6, 6),
        (cx - head_r, head_y - head_r, head_r * 2, head_r * 2),
        0,
        math.pi,
        int(head_r * 0.45),
    )
    eye_x = cx + int(10 * s) * d
    eye_y = head_y - int(4 * s)
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (100, 35, 35), (eye_x, eye_y), int(4 * s))
    pygame.draw.circle(
        surface, (180, 60, 40), (eye_x + int(1 * s) * d, eye_y - int(1 * s)), int(2 * s)
    )
    cig_x = cx + int(16 * s) * d
    cig_y = head_y + int(10 * s)
    pygame.draw.line(
        surface,
        (200, 200, 200),
        (cig_x, cig_y),
        (cig_x + int(10 * s) * d, cig_y - int(2 * s)),
        int(2 * s),
    )
    if int(pygame.time.get_ticks() / 350) % 2 == 0:
        pygame.draw.circle(
            surface,
            (255, 80, 20),
            (cig_x + int(10 * s) * d, cig_y - int(2 * s)),
            int(3 * s),
        )
    ember = pygame.Surface((int(12 * s), int(12 * s)), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.circle(ember, (255, 100, 30, 60), (int(6 * s), int(6 * s)), int(6 * s))
    surface.blit(ember, (cig_x + int(4 * s) * d, cig_y - int(8 * s)))
def draw_vasile(surface, cx, foot_y, scale=1.0, facing_right=True):
    d = int(facing_right) * 2 - 1
    s = scale
    head_r = int(25 * s)
    torso_h = int(112 * s)
    torso_w = int(62 * s)
    leg_h = int(92 * s)
    leg_w = int(22 * s)
    hip_y = foot_y - leg_h
    sho_y = hip_y - torso_h
    head_y = sho_y - head_r - int(8 * s)
    shadow = pygame.Surface((int(80 * s), int(16 * s)), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    shadow.fill((0, 0, 0, 60))
    surface.blit(shadow, (cx - int(40 * s), foot_y - int(8 * s)))
    leg_col = (35, 42, 55)
    for lx in (cx - int(18 * s), cx + int(18 * s)):
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, leg_col, (lx - leg_w // 2, hip_y, leg_w, leg_h))
    pygame.draw.ellipse(
        surface,
        (15, 15, 18),
        (cx - int(30 * s), foot_y - int(8 * s), int(28 * s), int(14 * s)),
    )
    pygame.draw.ellipse(
        surface,
        (15, 15, 18),
        (cx + int(2 * s), foot_y - int(8 * s), int(28 * s), int(14 * s)),
    )
    uni_col = C_KRAP_UNIFORM
    coat_pts = [
        (cx - torso_w // 2, sho_y),
        (cx + torso_w // 2, sho_y),
        (cx + torso_w // 2 + int(5 * s), hip_y),
        (cx - torso_w // 2 - int(5 * s), hip_y),
    ]
    pygame.draw.polygon(surface, uni_col, coat_pts)
    pygame.draw.line(
        surface,
        C_KRAP_BLUE,
        (cx - int(20 * s), sho_y + int(20 * s)),
        (cx + int(20 * s), sho_y + int(20 * s)),
        int(3 * s),
    )
    for i in range(3):
        px = cx + int((torso_w // 2 - 6 - i * 5) * s)
        pygame.draw.circle(
            surface, (200, 180, 80), (px, sho_y + int(8 * s)), int(2 * s)
        )
    arm_col = uni_col
    pygame.draw.rect(
        surface,
        arm_col,
        (cx - int(torso_w // 2) * d - int(8 * s), sho_y, int(16 * s), int(95 * s)),
    )
    pygame.draw.rect(
        surface,
        arm_col,
        (cx + int(torso_w // 2) * d - int(8 * s), sho_y, int(16 * s), int(95 * s)),
    )
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (180, 155, 130), (cx, head_y), head_r)
    pygame.draw.arc(
        surface,
        (60, 50, 45),
        (cx - head_r, head_y - head_r, head_r * 2, head_r * 2),
        math.pi * 0.2,
        math.pi * 0.8,
        3,
    )
    cig_x = cx + int(14 * s) * d
    cig_y = head_y + int(8 * s)
    pygame.draw.line(
        surface,
        (220, 220, 220),
        (cig_x, cig_y),
        (cig_x + int(8 * s) * d, cig_y - int(1 * s)),
        int(2 * s),
    )
    if int(pygame.time.get_ticks() / 350) % 2 == 0:
        pygame.draw.circle(
            surface,
            (255, 80, 20),
            (cig_x + int(8 * s) * d, cig_y - int(1 * s)),
            int(2 * s),
        )
    eye_x = cx + int(8 * s) * d
    eye_y = head_y - int(3 * s)
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (40, 50, 70), (eye_x, eye_y), int(2 * s))
def draw_biribel(
    surface, cx, foot_y, scale=1.0, facing_right=True, in_silhouette=False
):
    d = int(facing_right) * 2 - 1
    s = scale
    head_r = int(24 * s)
    torso_h = int(108 * s)
    torso_w = int(58 * s)
    leg_h = int(90 * s)
    leg_w = int(22 * s)
    hip_y = foot_y - leg_h
    sho_y = hip_y - torso_h
    head_y = sho_y - head_r - int(8 * s)
    shadow = pygame.Surface((int(80 * s), int(16 * s)), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    shadow.fill((0, 0, 0, 60))
    surface.blit(shadow, (cx - int(40 * s), foot_y - int(8 * s)))
    if in_silhouette:
        for lx in (cx - int(15 * s), cx + int(15 * s)):
            pygame.draw.rect(
                surface, (8, 8, 12), (lx - leg_w // 2, hip_y, leg_w, leg_h)
            )
        coat_pts = [
            (cx - torso_w // 2, sho_y),
            (cx + torso_w // 2, sho_y),
            (cx + torso_w // 2 + int(4 * s), hip_y),
            (cx - torso_w // 2 - int(4 * s), hip_y),
        ]
        pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (8, 8, 12), coat_pts)
        pygame.draw.rect(
            surface,
            (5, 5, 8),
            (cx - int(22 * s), sho_y + int(10 * s), int(44 * s), int(60 * s)),
        )
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (8, 8, 12), (cx, head_y), head_r)
        pygame.draw.arc(
            surface,
            (3, 3, 6),
            (
                cx - head_r - int(3 * s),
                head_y - head_r - int(2 * s),
                (head_r + int(3 * s)) * 2,
                head_r + int(6 * s),
            ),
            0,
            math.pi,
            int(head_r * 0.5),
        )
        return
    leg_col = (30, 35, 40)
    for lx in (cx - int(15 * s), cx + int(15 * s)):
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, leg_col, (lx - leg_w // 2, hip_y, leg_w, leg_h))
    for lx in (cx - int(15 * s), cx + int(15 * s)):
        pygame.draw.rect(
            surface,
            (15, 18, 22),
            (lx - leg_w // 2, hip_y + int(40 * s), leg_w, int(14 * s)),
        )
    pygame.draw.ellipse(
        surface,
        (12, 12, 14),
        (cx - int(28 * s), foot_y - int(8 * s), int(26 * s), int(14 * s)),
    )
    pygame.draw.ellipse(
        surface,
        (12, 12, 14),
        (cx + int(2 * s), foot_y - int(8 * s), int(26 * s), int(14 * s)),
    )
    uni_col = (40, 50, 45)
    coat_pts = [
        (cx - torso_w // 2, sho_y),
        (cx + torso_w // 2, sho_y),
        (cx + torso_w // 2 + int(4 * s), hip_y),
        (cx - torso_w // 2 - int(4 * s), hip_y),
    ]
    pygame.draw.polygon(surface, uni_col, coat_pts)
    vest_col = (28, 35, 30)
    pygame.draw.rect(
        surface,
        vest_col,
        (cx - int(24 * s), sho_y + int(15 * s), int(48 * s), int(55 * s)),
    )
    for i in range(3):
        px = cx - int(20 * s) + i * int(14 * s)
        pygame.draw.rect(
            surface, (20, 25, 22), (px, sho_y + int(30 * s), int(10 * s), int(18 * s))
        )
        pygame.draw.line(
            surface,
            (15, 18, 16),
            (px, sho_y + int(30 * s)),
            (px + int(10 * s), sho_y + int(30 * s)),
            1,
        )
    pygame.draw.rect(
        surface,
        C_KRAP_BLUE,
        (cx + int(8 * s) * d, sho_y + int(18 * s), int(10 * s), int(8 * s)),
    )
    pygame.draw.rect(
        surface,
        (200, 200, 200),
        (cx + int(8 * s) * d, sho_y + int(18 * s), int(10 * s), int(8 * s)),
        1,
    )
    arm_col = uni_col
    pygame.draw.rect(
        surface,
        arm_col,
        (cx - int(torso_w // 2) * d - int(7 * s), sho_y, int(14 * s), int(90 * s)),
    )
    pygame.draw.rect(
        surface,
        arm_col,
        (cx + int(torso_w // 2) * d - int(7 * s), sho_y, int(14 * s), int(90 * s)),
    )
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (190, 165, 140), (cx, head_y), head_r)
    pygame.draw.arc(
        surface,
        (25, 30, 28),
        (
            cx - head_r - int(2 * s),
            head_y - head_r - int(2 * s),
            (head_r + int(2 * s)) * 2,
            head_r + int(4 * s),
        ),
        0,
        math.pi,
        int(head_r * 0.5),
    )
    pygame.draw.ellipse(
        surface,
        (25, 30, 28),
        (
            cx - head_r - int(2 * s),
            head_y - int(6 * s),
            (head_r + int(2 * s)) * 2,
            int(8 * s),
        ),
    )
    eye_x = cx + int(8 * s) * d
    eye_y = head_y - int(2 * s)
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (35, 45, 55), (eye_x, eye_y), int(2 * s))
    pygame.draw.arc(
        surface,
        (130, 105, 85),
        (
            cx - head_r + int(2 * s),
            head_y + int(2 * s),
            head_r * 2 - int(4 * s),
            head_r,
        ),
        math.pi * 1.1,
        math.pi * 1.9,
        2,
    )
def draw_old_fisherman(surface, cx, foot_y, scale=1.0, facing_right=True):
    d = int(facing_right) * 2 - 1
    s = scale
    head_r = int(22 * s)
    torso_h = int(95 * s)
    torso_w = int(54 * s)
    leg_h = int(80 * s)
    leg_w = int(20 * s)
    hip_y = foot_y - leg_h
    sho_y = hip_y - torso_h
    head_y = sho_y - head_r - int(8 * s)
    shadow = pygame.Surface((int(70 * s), int(14 * s)), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    shadow.fill((0, 0, 0, 55))
    surface.blit(shadow, (cx - int(35 * s), foot_y - int(7 * s)))
    leg_col = (50, 45, 38)
    for lx in (cx - int(14 * s), cx + int(14 * s)):
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, leg_col, (lx - leg_w // 2, hip_y, leg_w, leg_h))
    pygame.draw.ellipse(
        surface,
        (25, 20, 16),
        (cx - int(26 * s), foot_y - int(7 * s), int(24 * s), int(12 * s)),
    )
    pygame.draw.ellipse(
        surface,
        (25, 20, 16),
        (cx + int(2 * s), foot_y - int(7 * s), int(24 * s), int(12 * s)),
    )
    coat_col = (90, 80, 50)
    coat_pts = [
        (cx - torso_w // 2 - int(4 * s), sho_y),
        (cx + torso_w // 2 + int(4 * s), sho_y),
        (cx + torso_w // 2 + int(10 * s), hip_y),
        (cx - torso_w // 2 - int(10 * s), hip_y),
    ]
    pygame.draw.polygon(surface, coat_col, coat_pts)
    pygame.draw.line(
        surface,
        (110, 100, 65),
        (cx - int(15 * s), sho_y + int(10 * s)),
        (cx - int(20 * s), hip_y - int(10 * s)),
        2,
    )
    pygame.draw.line(
        surface,
        (110, 100, 65),
        (cx + int(15 * s), sho_y + int(10 * s)),
        (cx + int(20 * s), hip_y - int(10 * s)),
        2,
    )
    pygame.draw.rect(
        surface,
        coat_col,
        (
            cx - int(torso_w // 2) - int(6 * s),
            sho_y + int(5 * s),
            int(14 * s),
            int(85 * s),
        ),
    )
    pygame.draw.rect(
        surface,
        coat_col,
        (
            cx + int(torso_w // 2) - int(6 * s),
            sho_y + int(5 * s),
            int(14 * s),
            int(85 * s),
        ),
    )
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (170, 140, 115), (cx, head_y), head_r)
    pygame.draw.arc(
        surface,
        (200, 200, 195),
        (cx - head_r, head_y - int(2 * s), head_r * 2, head_r + int(6 * s)),
        math.pi,
        math.pi * 2,
        int(head_r * 0.7),
    )
    pygame.draw.ellipse(
        surface,
        (60, 55, 45),
        (
            cx - head_r - int(6 * s),
            head_y - int(18 * s),
            (head_r + int(6 * s)) * 2,
            int(14 * s),
        ),
    )
    pygame.draw.rect(
        surface,
        (60, 55, 45),
        (
            cx - head_r - int(2 * s),
            head_y - int(16 * s),
            (head_r + int(2 * s)) * 2,
            int(10 * s),
        ),
    )
    eye_x = cx + int(7 * s) * d
    eye_y = head_y - int(2 * s)
    pygame.draw.line(
        surface,
        (30, 25, 20),
        (eye_x - int(2 * s), eye_y),
        (eye_x + int(2 * s), eye_y),
        int(2 * s),
    )
def draw_commander(surface, cx, foot_y, scale=1.0, facing_right=True):
    d = int(facing_right) * 2 - 1
    s = scale
    head_r = int(26 * s)
    torso_h = int(115 * s)
    torso_w = int(65 * s)
    leg_h = int(95 * s)
    leg_w = int(24 * s)
    hip_y = foot_y - leg_h
    sho_y = hip_y - torso_h
    head_y = sho_y - head_r - int(8 * s)
    shadow = pygame.Surface((int(80 * s), int(16 * s)), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    shadow.fill((0, 0, 0, 60))
    surface.blit(shadow, (cx - int(40 * s), foot_y - int(8 * s)))
    leg_col = (20, 22, 28)
    for lx in (cx - int(18 * s), cx + int(18 * s)):
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, leg_col, (lx - leg_w // 2, hip_y, leg_w, leg_h))
    pygame.draw.ellipse(
        surface,
        (10, 10, 12),
        (cx - int(30 * s), foot_y - int(8 * s), int(28 * s), int(14 * s)),
    )
    pygame.draw.ellipse(
        surface,
        (10, 10, 12),
        (cx + int(2 * s), foot_y - int(8 * s), int(28 * s), int(14 * s)),
    )
    uni_col = (30, 32, 38)
    coat_pts = [
        (cx - torso_w // 2, sho_y),
        (cx + torso_w // 2, sho_y),
        (cx + torso_w // 2 + int(5 * s), hip_y),
        (cx - torso_w // 2 - int(5 * s), hip_y),
    ]
    pygame.draw.polygon(surface, uni_col, coat_pts)
    pygame.draw.rect(
        surface,
        (160, 30, 30),
        (
            cx + int(torso_w // 2 - 8) * d - int(8 * s),
            sho_y + int(20 * s),
            int(16 * s),
            int(20 * s),
        ),
    )
    pygame.draw.polygon(
        surface,
        (140, 20, 20),
        [
            (cx - int(4 * s), sho_y + int(2 * s)),
            (cx + int(4 * s), sho_y + int(2 * s)),
            (cx + int(3 * s), sho_y + int(60 * s)),
            (cx - int(3 * s), sho_y + int(60 * s)),
        ],
    )
    arm_col = uni_col
    pygame.draw.rect(
        surface,
        arm_col,
        (cx - int(torso_w // 2) * d - int(8 * s), sho_y, int(16 * s), int(95 * s)),
    )
    pygame.draw.rect(
        surface,
        arm_col,
        (cx + int(torso_w // 2) * d - int(8 * s), sho_y, int(16 * s), int(95 * s)),
    )
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (175, 145, 120), (cx, head_y), head_r)
    pygame.draw.arc(
        surface,
        (15, 12, 10),
        (cx - head_r, head_y - head_r, head_r * 2, head_r * 2),
        0,
        math.pi,
        int(head_r * 0.5),
    )
    pygame.draw.ellipse(
        surface,
        (15, 12, 10),
        (cx - head_r, head_y - int(8 * s), head_r * 2, int(10 * s)),
    )
    eye_x = cx + int(10 * s) * d
    eye_y = head_y - int(3 * s)
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (25, 30, 35), (eye_x, eye_y), int(3 * s))
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# SCENE DRAWERS
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
def draw_factory(surface, t):
    ground_y = SCENE_BOT - 60
    draw_sky_gradient(surface, (18, 20, 26), (28, 24, 22))
    fac_left = 150
    fac_right = 850
    fac_top = SCENE_TOP + 80
    fac_h = ground_y - fac_top
    pygame.draw.rect(
        surface, (22, 26, 32), (fac_left, fac_top, fac_right - fac_left, fac_h)
    )
    for i in range(fac_left, fac_right, 12):
        shade = 28 + (i % 24 > 12) * 4
        pygame.draw.line(
            surface, (shade, shade + 2, shade + 6), (i, fac_top), (i, ground_y), 1
        )
    for wx in range(fac_left + 40, fac_right - 40, 90):
        for wy_off in range(0, 3):
            wy = fac_top + 30 + wy_off * 70
            is_lit = (wx // 90 + wy_off) % 3 != 0
            win_c = (55, 50, 32) if is_lit else (18, 20, 24)
            pygame.draw.rect(surface, win_c, (wx, wy, 50, 45))
            pygame.draw.rect(surface, (18, 20, 26), (wx, wy, 50, 45), 2)
            if is_lit:
                glow = pygame.Surface((54, 49), pygame.SRCALPHA)
                glow.fill((60, 55, 30, 25))
                surface.blit(glow, (wx - 2, wy - 2))
    for chx, chh in [(620, 130), (680, 160), (750, 110)]:
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (26, 28, 34), (chx, fac_top - chh, 28, chh))
        for pi in range(4):
            poff = (t // 20 + pi * 15) % 80
            palpha = max(0, 70 - poff)
            pr = 15 + pi * 4
            psurf = pygame.Surface((pr * 2, pr * 2), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            pygame.draw.circle(psurf, (35, 38, 44, palpha), (pr, pr), pr)
            surface.blit(psurf, (chx + 14 - pr, fac_top - chh - 20 - poff))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (15, 16, 18), (0, ground_y, WIDTH, SCENE_BOT - ground_y))
def draw_oil_rig(surface, x, ground_y, t, scale=1.0, burning=False, lights_only=False):
    s = scale
    base_w = int(120 * s)
    base_h = int(20 * s)
    deck_y = ground_y - int(60 * s)
    tower_h = int(100 * s)
    tx = x
    ty = deck_y - tower_h
    if not lights_only:
        leg_col = (45, 48, 55)
        deck_col = (60, 65, 75)
        tower_col = (55, 60, 70)
        for lx in (-base_w // 2 + 10, base_w // 2 - 10):
            pygame.draw.line(
                surface,
                leg_col,
                (x + lx, deck_y + base_h),
                (x + lx, ground_y + 20),
                int(4 * s),
            )
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, deck_col, (x - base_w // 2, deck_y, base_w, base_h))
        pygame.draw.line(
            surface, tower_col, (tx - int(20 * s), deck_y), (tx, ty), int(3 * s)
        )
        pygame.draw.line(
            surface, tower_col, (tx + int(20 * s), deck_y), (tx, ty), int(3 * s)
        )
        for i in range(4):
            ratio = i / 4.0
            ly = deck_y - int(tower_h * ratio)
            lw = int(20 * s * (1 - ratio))
            pygame.draw.line(
                surface, tower_col, (tx - lw, ly), (tx + lw, ly), int(2 * s)
            )
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (120, 100, 80), (tx, ty), int(4 * s))
    else:
        if (t // 18) % 2 == 0:
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, (255, 50, 50), (tx, ty), int(6 * s))
            beacon_glow = pygame.Surface((int(36 * s), int(36 * s)), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            pygame.draw.circle(
                beacon_glow, (255, 50, 50, 75), (int(18 * s), int(18 * s)), int(18 * s)
            )
            surface.blit(beacon_glow, (tx - int(18 * s), ty - int(18 * s)))
        for lx_off in range(-base_w // 2 + 15, base_w // 2 - 10, int(25 * s)):
            pygame.draw.circle(
                surface,
                (255, 200, 100),
                (x + lx_off, deck_y + base_h // 2),
                int(2.5 * s),
            )
            deck_glow = pygame.Surface((int(12 * s), int(12 * s)), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            pygame.draw.circle(
                deck_glow, (255, 200, 100, 50), (int(6 * s), int(6 * s)), int(6 * s)
            )
            surface.blit(
                deck_glow, (x + lx_off - int(6 * s), deck_y + base_h // 2 - int(6 * s))
            )
        spot_surf = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        beam_len = 220 * s
        phase_offset = x * 0.13
        beam_angle = math.sin(t * 0.02 + phase_offset) * 0.5 - math.pi / 2
        end_cx = x + math.cos(beam_angle) * beam_len
        end_cy = deck_y + math.sin(beam_angle) * beam_len
        perp_angle = beam_angle + math.pi / 2
        cone_half_w = 30 * s
        bx1 = end_cx - math.cos(perp_angle) * cone_half_w
        by1 = end_cy - math.sin(perp_angle) * cone_half_w
        bx2 = end_cx + math.cos(perp_angle) * cone_half_w
        by2 = end_cy + math.sin(perp_angle) * cone_half_w
        pygame.draw.polygon(
            spot_surf, (255, 255, 220, 20), [(x, deck_y), (bx1, by1), (bx2, by2)]
        )
        surface.blit(spot_surf, (0, 0))
        if burning:
            fire_x = x + int(math.sin(t * 0.01) * 5)
            fire_y = deck_y - int(5 * s)
            for ri, (rc, ra) in enumerate([(255, 180), (220, 130), (160, 80)]):
                rr = max(2, int((15 - ri * 4) * s))
                pygame.draw.circle(
                    surface,
                    (rc, max(0, 80 - ri * 20), 10, ra),
                    (fire_x, fire_y - ri * int(5 * s)),
                    rr,
                )
            for pi in range(5):
                poff = (t // 15 + pi * 12) % 80
                palpha = max(0, 100 - poff)
                pr = int((10 + pi * 3) * s)
                psurf = pygame.Surface((pr * 2, pr * 2), pygame.SRCALPHA)
                pygame.draw.circle(psurf, (40, 40, 45, palpha), (pr, pr), pr)
                surface.blit(
                    psurf,
                    (fire_x - pr + random.randint(-3, 3), deck_y - int(40 * s) - poff),
                )
def draw_propaganda_poster(surface, x, y, w, h, defaced=False):
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (200, 180, 140), (x, y, w, h))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (60, 50, 40), (x, y, w, h), 2)
    face_x = x + w // 2
    face_y = y + h // 3
    face_r = min(w, h) // 6
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (40, 30, 25), (face_x, face_y), face_r)
    pygame.draw.rect(
        surface, (40, 30, 25), (face_x - face_r, face_y, face_r * 2, face_r * 2)
    )
    for i in range(3):
        ty = y + h * 2 // 3 + i * 8
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (60, 50, 40), (x + 10, ty, w - 20, 4))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (160, 30, 30), (x, y, w, 12))
    if defaced:
        pygame.draw.circle(
            surface, (15, 15, 15), (face_x - 4, face_y - 2), face_r + 2, 3
        )
        pygame.draw.line(
            surface, (15, 15, 15), (x + 4, y + 4), (x + w - 4, y + h - 4), 3
        )
        tag = font_terminal_small.render("MARU", True, (15, 15, 15))
        surface.blit(tag, (x + w // 2 - tag.get_width() // 2, y + h - 18))
def draw_mag_graffiti(surface, x, y, scale=1.0):
    s = scale
    pts = [
        (x, y - int(8 * s)),
        (x - int(6 * s), y - int(12 * s)),
        (x - int(10 * s), y - int(2 * s)),
        (x - int(8 * s), y + int(8 * s)),
        (x, y + int(12 * s)),
        (x + int(8 * s), y + int(8 * s)),
        (x + int(10 * s), y - int(2 * s)),
        (x + int(6 * s), y - int(12 * s)),
    ]
    pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (15, 15, 15), pts)
    pygame.draw.circle(
        surface, (180, 30, 30), (x - int(3 * s), y - int(2 * s)), int(1 * s)
    )
    pygame.draw.circle(
        surface, (180, 30, 30), (x + int(3 * s), y - int(2 * s)), int(1 * s)
    )
def draw_van_exterior(surface, t):
    """Simple cinematic exterior shot before the van briefing."""
    draw_sky_gradient(surface, (10, 12, 18), (24, 18, 22))
    road_y = SCENE_BOT - 150
    # Distant slum silhouettes
    rng = random.Random(91)
    for bx in range(-20, WIDTH + 40, 56):
        bh = rng.randint(30, 105)
        col = rng.choice([(13, 15, 20), (16, 17, 22), (18, 16, 18)])
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, col, (bx, road_y - bh, 48, bh))
        if rng.random() > 0.45:
            pygame.draw.rect(surface, (150, 115, 60), (bx + 16, road_y - bh + 16, 5, 6))
    # Wet road
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (10, 12, 15), (0, road_y, WIDTH, SCENE_BOT - road_y))
    for y in range(road_y + 16, SCENE_BOT, 24):
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (22, 28, 36), (0, y), (WIDTH, y), 1)
    # Reflections
    for i in range(12):
        rx = (i * 97 + int(t * 0.08)) % WIDTH
        rw = 30 + (i % 3) * 20
        col = [(40, 75, 110), (90, 65, 45), (120, 45, 35)][i % 3]
        refl = pygame.Surface((rw, 8), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.ellipse(refl, (*col, 35), (0, 0, rw, 8))
        surface.blit(refl, (rx, road_y + 25 + (i % 5) * 16))
    # Van side silhouette
    van_x = WIDTH // 2 - 170 + int(math.sin(t * 0.004) * 2)
    van_y = road_y - 58
    # Shadow
    shadow = pygame.Surface((350, 55), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.ellipse(shadow, (0, 0, 0, 105), (0, 10, 350, 36))
    surface.blit(shadow, (van_x - 18, van_y + 45))
    # Body
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (15, 17, 20), (van_x, van_y, 310, 72), border_radius=5)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (40, 46, 54), (van_x, van_y, 310, 72), 2, border_radius=5)
    # Cab slope / hood
    pygame.draw.polygon(
        surface,
        (18, 22, 27),
        [
            (van_x + 230, van_y),
            (van_x + 290, van_y + 12),
            (van_x + 310, van_y + 45),
            (van_x + 230, van_y + 45),
        ],
    )
    pygame.draw.polygon(
        surface,
        (42, 48, 56),
        [
            (van_x + 230, van_y),
            (van_x + 290, van_y + 12),
            (van_x + 310, van_y + 45),
            (van_x + 230, van_y + 45),
        ],
        1,
    )
    # Windows
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (22, 34, 46), (van_x + 236, van_y + 10, 42, 26), border_radius=2)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (18, 28, 38), (van_x + 90, van_y + 12, 48, 24), border_radius=2)
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (80, 100, 120), (van_x + 240, van_y + 13), (van_x + 272, van_y + 13), 1)
    # KRAP stripe
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, C_KRAP_BLUE, (van_x + 24, van_y + 22, 185, 7))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (220, 220, 225), (van_x + 24, van_y + 30, 185, 2))
    krap = font_terminal_small.render("KRAP", True, (190, 210, 230))
    surface.blit(krap, (van_x + 38, van_y + 40))
    # Lights
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (190, 40, 35), (van_x - 4, van_y + 28, 6, 14))
    pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, (220, 210, 140), (van_x + 304, van_y + 24, 12, 10))
    # Wheels
    for wx in (van_x + 62, van_x + 235):
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (4, 5, 7), (wx, van_y + 72), 22)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (36, 40, 45), (wx, van_y + 72), 10)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (15, 16, 18), (wx, van_y + 72), 4)
    # Light beam
    beam = pygame.Surface((250, 70), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.polygon(beam, (220, 210, 150, 25), [(0, 25), (235, 0), (235, 55), (0, 38)])
    surface.blit(beam, (van_x + 306, van_y + 12))
    # Foreground rain
    rain_layer = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    rng_r = random.Random(44)
    for i in range(90):
        rx = (rng_r.randint(0, WIDTH) + int(t * 1.9)) % WIDTH
        ry = SCENE_TOP + ((rng_r.randint(0, SCENE_H) + int(t * 5.0)) % SCENE_H)
        pygame.draw.line(rain_layer, (135, 155, 190, rng_r.randint(25, 65)), (rx, ry), (rx - 5, ry + 20), 1)
    surface.blit(rain_layer, (0, 0))
    # Caption-style location tag
    tag = font_location_sub.render("ACARISTAN CITY — SLUM DISTRICT", True, (130, 150, 170))
    bg = pygame.Surface((tag.get_width() + 24, tag.get_height() + 10), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    bg.fill((0, 0, 0, 150))
    surface.blit(bg, (42, SCENE_TOP + 24))
    surface.blit(tag, (54, SCENE_TOP + 29))
def draw_van_interior(surface, t):
    """Cinematic but still simple/pixel-style KRAP van interior."""
    surface.fill((8, 8, 10))
    # Rear wall gradient
    for y in range(SCENE_TOP, SCENE_BOT):
        p = (y - SCENE_TOP) / max(1, SCENE_H)
        r = int(17 + p * 10)
        g = int(13 + p * 7)
        b = int(14 + p * 8)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (r, g, b), (0, y), (WIDTH, y))
    # Metal wall panel lines
    for x in range(80, WIDTH - 80, 96):
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (26, 22, 24), (x, SCENE_TOP + 10), (x, SCENE_BOT - 90), 1)
    for y in range(SCENE_TOP + 50, SCENE_BOT - 90, 48):
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (22, 18, 20), (40, y), (WIDTH - 40, y), 1)
    # Rear door seams
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (36, 30, 32), (WIDTH // 2, SCENE_TOP + 20), (WIDTH // 2, SCENE_BOT - 92), 2)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (28, 24, 26), (60, SCENE_TOP + 40, WIDTH - 120, SCENE_H - 155), 2)
    # Overhead red cabin light, softer and less empty
    lamp_x = WIDTH // 2 - 95
    lamp_y = SCENE_TOP + 28
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (145, 28, 30), (lamp_x, lamp_y, 190, 7))
    glow = pygame.Surface((360, 120), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    for r in range(160, 0, -8):
        a = int(20 * (1 - r / 160))
        pygame.draw.ellipse(glow, (180, 30, 35, a), (180 - r, 45 - r // 3, r * 2, r))
    surface.blit(glow, (WIDTH // 2 - 180, SCENE_TOP + 0))
    # Right window with moving city/rain outside
    win_x = WIDTH - 310
    win_y = SCENE_TOP + 70
    win_w = 220
    win_h = 180
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (7, 9, 13), (win_x, win_y, win_w, win_h))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (42, 34, 32), (win_x - 6, win_y - 6, win_w + 12, win_h + 12), 5)
    # Passing colored lights
    for i in range(16):
        lx = win_x + ((i * 41 + int(t * 0.7)) % win_w)
        ly = win_y + 20 + (i * 23) % (win_h - 40)
        col = [(150, 80, 55), (80, 95, 130), (170, 130, 55)][i % 3]
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, col, (lx, ly, 22, 4))
    # Window rain
    for i in range(18):
        rx = win_x + ((i * 29 + int(t * 1.1)) % win_w)
        ry = win_y + ((i * 47 + int(t * 2.8)) % win_h)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (140, 150, 175), (rx, ry), (rx - 2, ry + 18), 1)
    # Tactical map board / route chart
    board_x = WIDTH - 560
    board_y = SCENE_TOP + 82
    board_w = 170
    board_h = 140
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (12, 14, 18), (board_x, board_y, board_w, board_h))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (45, 38, 32), (board_x, board_y, board_w, board_h), 3)
    # Route lines
    route_cols = [(120, 70, 60), (95, 105, 135), (130, 95, 45)]
    pts = [
        (board_x + 22, board_y + 100),
        (board_x + 48, board_y + 82),
        (board_x + 68, board_y + 92),
        (board_x + 96, board_y + 58),
        (board_x + 120, board_y + 66),
        (board_x + 140, board_y + 32),
    ]
    for i in range(len(pts) - 1):
        pygame.draw.line(surface, route_cols[i % len(route_cols)], pts[i], pts[i + 1], 3)
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, C_ORANGE, pts[-1], 4)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (70, 50, 30), (board_x + board_w - 5, board_y + 80, 14, 5))
    # Floor / foreground
    floor_y = SCENE_BOT - 95
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (10, 10, 12), (0, floor_y, WIDTH, SCENE_BOT - floor_y))
    for sx in range(0, WIDTH, 32):
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (18, 18, 20), (sx, floor_y), (sx + 12, SCENE_BOT), 1)
    # Benches
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (18, 16, 16), (40, floor_y - 35, 260, 48), border_radius=4)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (28, 24, 22), (40, floor_y - 35, 260, 8), border_radius=3)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (18, 16, 16), (WIDTH - 360, floor_y - 35, 300, 48), border_radius=4)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (28, 24, 22), (WIDTH - 360, floor_y - 35, 300, 8), border_radius=3)
    # Gear crates
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (55, 58, 48), (325, floor_y - 52, 86, 46), border_radius=3)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (85, 88, 72), (325, floor_y - 52, 86, 46), 2, border_radius=3)
    krap = font_terminal_small.render("KRAP", True, C_KRAP_BLUE)
    surface.blit(krap, (350, floor_y - 38))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (70, 54, 38), (430, floor_y - 44, 72, 38), border_radius=2)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (45, 32, 22), (430, floor_y - 44, 72, 38), 2, border_radius=2)
    # Character rim-light zones
    vasile_glow = pygame.Surface((160, 220), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.ellipse(vasile_glow, (100, 160, 220, 28), (0, 20, 160, 180))
    surface.blit(vasile_glow, (75, floor_y - 215))
    biribel_glow = pygame.Surface((180, 240), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.ellipse(biribel_glow, (180, 190, 200, 18), (0, 20, 180, 200))
    surface.blit(biribel_glow, (WIDTH // 2 + 50, floor_y - 230))
    # Characters larger and better staged
    draw_vasile(surface, 160, floor_y + 2, scale=0.72, facing_right=True)
    draw_biribel(surface, WIDTH // 2 + 150, floor_y + 2, scale=0.76, facing_right=False, in_silhouette=True)
    # Foreground silhouettes: radio / rifle case, gives cramped depth
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (5, 6, 8), (WIDTH // 2 - 70, SCENE_BOT - 112, 140, 28), border_radius=3)
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (12, 14, 16), (WIDTH // 2 - 52, SCENE_BOT - 98), (WIDTH // 2 + 50, SCENE_BOT - 98), 3)
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (18, 20, 24), (WIDTH // 2 + 58, SCENE_BOT - 98), 6)
    # Subtle red wash
    red_glow = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    red_glow.fill((60, 10, 12, 22))
    surface.blit(red_glow, (0, 0))
# ── Handoff insert-shot helpers (van cutscene) ──
def _ease_cutscene(t):
    t = max(0.0, min(1.0, t))
    return 1.0 - (1.0 - t) ** 3
def _lerp_cutscene(a, b, t):
    return a + (b - a) * t
def _draw_handoff_pistol(surface, cx, cy, scale=1.0, angle=-8):
    s = scale
    w, h = int(150 * s), int(80 * s)
    gun = pygame.Surface((w, h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    def R(x, y, ww, hh):
        return pygame.Rect(int(x * s), int(y * s), int(ww * s), int(hh * s))
    pygame.draw.rect(gun, (52, 56, 62), R(32, 24, 72, 15), border_radius=int(3 * s))
    pygame.draw.rect(gun, (18, 20, 24), R(32, 24, 72, 15), int(2 * s), border_radius=int(3 * s))
    pygame.draw.rect(gun, (70, 74, 80), R(98, 28, 24, 7), border_radius=int(2 * s))
    pygame.draw.circle(gun, (8, 8, 10), (int(123 * s), int(31 * s)), int(4 * s))
    pygame.draw.rect(gun, (28, 30, 34), R(36, 38, 58, 13), border_radius=int(3 * s))
    grip_pts = [(int(48 * s), int(48 * s)), (int(74 * s), int(48 * s)),
                (int(82 * s), int(73 * s)), (int(56 * s), int(75 * s))]
    pygame.draw.polygon(gun, (22, 23, 25), grip_pts)
    pygame.draw.polygon(gun, (10, 11, 13), grip_pts, int(1 * s))
    pygame.draw.circle(gun, (16, 17, 19), (int(83 * s), int(49 * s)), int(8 * s), int(2 * s))
    pygame.draw.line(gun, (105, 112, 120), (int(38 * s), int(27 * s)), (int(93 * s), int(27 * s)), int(1 * s))
    pygame.draw.circle(gun, (180, 35, 35), (int(82 * s), int(22 * s)), int(2 * s))
    rot = pygame.transform.rotate(gun, angle)
    rect = rot.get_rect(center=(int(cx), int(cy)))
    surface.blit(rot, rect)
def _draw_handoff_molotov(surface, cx, cy, scale=1.0, angle=0):
    """Unlit molotov: glass bottle, amber fuel, rag stuffed in the neck."""
    s = scale
    w, h = int(64 * s), int(150 * s)
    bottle = pygame.Surface((w, h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    bx = w // 2
    rag_col = (196, 178, 130)
    rag_dark = (150, 132, 92)
    pygame.draw.polygon(bottle, rag_col, [
        (bx - int(4 * s), int(18 * s)),
        (bx - int(16 * s), int(34 * s)),
        (bx - int(11 * s), int(40 * s)),
        (bx - int(1 * s), int(26 * s)),
    ])
    pygame.draw.line(bottle, rag_dark,
                     (bx - int(6 * s), int(22 * s)),
                     (bx - int(14 * s), int(36 * s)), max(1, int(1 * s)))
    pygame.draw.ellipse(bottle, rag_col,
                        (bx - int(8 * s), int(10 * s), int(16 * s), int(12 * s)))
    pygame.draw.ellipse(bottle, rag_dark,
                        (bx - int(8 * s), int(10 * s), int(16 * s), int(12 * s)), max(1, int(1 * s)))
    glass = (96, 66, 30)
    glass_dark = (52, 36, 18)
    pygame.draw.rect(bottle, glass_dark,
                     (bx - int(7 * s), int(18 * s), int(14 * s), int(26 * s)),
                     border_radius=int(3 * s))
    pygame.draw.rect(bottle, glass,
                     (bx - int(5 * s), int(20 * s), int(10 * s), int(23 * s)),
                     border_radius=int(3 * s))
    pygame.draw.polygon(bottle, glass_dark, [
        (bx - int(7 * s), int(42 * s)),
        (bx + int(7 * s), int(42 * s)),
        (bx + int(20 * s), int(62 * s)),
        (bx - int(20 * s), int(62 * s)),
    ])
    pygame.draw.polygon(bottle, glass, [
        (bx - int(5 * s), int(44 * s)),
        (bx + int(5 * s), int(44 * s)),
        (bx + int(17 * s), int(62 * s)),
        (bx - int(17 * s), int(62 * s)),
    ])
    body_rect = pygame.Rect(bx - int(20 * s), int(60 * s), int(40 * s), int(78 * s))
    pygame.draw.rect(bottle, glass_dark, body_rect, border_radius=int(8 * s))
    inner = body_rect.inflate(int(-6 * s), int(-6 * s))
    pygame.draw.rect(bottle, glass, inner, border_radius=int(7 * s))
    fuel_h = int(inner.height * 0.65)
    fuel_rect = pygame.Rect(inner.x, inner.bottom - fuel_h, inner.width, fuel_h)
    pygame.draw.rect(bottle, (212, 116, 30), fuel_rect, border_radius=int(6 * s))
    pygame.draw.rect(bottle, (245, 160, 55),
                     (fuel_rect.x, fuel_rect.y, fuel_rect.width, int(4 * s)))
    pygame.draw.line(bottle, (255, 220, 160),
                     (inner.x + int(4 * s), inner.y + int(6 * s)),
                     (inner.x + int(4 * s), inner.bottom - int(8 * s)), max(1, int(2 * s)))
    pygame.draw.rect(bottle, (60, 60, 64),
                     (bx - int(7 * s), int(30 * s), int(14 * s), int(5 * s)))
    rot = pygame.transform.rotate(bottle, angle)
    rect = rot.get_rect(center=(int(cx), int(cy)))
    surface.blit(rot, rect)
def draw_van_handoff(surface, t, item="weapon"):
    draw_van_interior(surface, t)
    dim = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    dim.fill((0, 0, 0, 105))
    surface.blit(dim, (0, 0))
    panel_w = 650
    panel_h = 355
    panel_x = WIDTH // 2 - panel_w // 2
    panel_y = SCENE_TOP + 42
    panel = pygame.Rect(panel_x, panel_y, panel_w, panel_h)
    shadow = pygame.Surface((panel_w + 28, panel_h + 28), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.rect(shadow, (0, 0, 0, 145), shadow.get_rect(), border_radius=8)
    surface.blit(shadow, (panel_x + 14, panel_y + 14))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (9, 10, 13), panel, border_radius=6)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (52, 62, 74), panel, 2, border_radius=6)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, C_KRAP_BLUE, (panel_x, panel_y, 5, panel_h), border_radius=4)
    for x in range(panel_x + 35, panel.right - 35, 80):
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (22, 24, 29), (x, panel_y + 10), (x, panel.bottom - 10), 1)
    for y in range(panel_y + 45, panel.bottom - 20, 42):
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (18, 20, 25), (panel_x + 15, y), (panel.right - 15, y), 1)
    label = "LOADOUT TRANSFER // SERVICE SIDEARM" if item == "weapon" else "LOADOUT TRANSFER // INCENDIARY PACKAGE"
    tag = font_terminal_small.render(label, True, (110, 150, 180))
    surface.blit(tag, (panel_x + 18, panel_y + 12))
    rec = font_terminal_small.render("REC", True, (220, 55, 45))
    surface.blit(rec, (panel.right - rec.get_width() - 22, panel_y + 12))
    if (pygame.time.get_ticks() // 400) % 2 == 0:
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (220, 55, 45), (panel.right - rec.get_width() - 34, panel_y + 19), 4)
    table_y = panel_y + 250
    table_pts = [(panel_x + 110, table_y), (panel.right - 110, table_y),
                 (panel.right - 55, panel.bottom - 28), (panel_x + 55, panel.bottom - 28)]
    pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (48, 36, 24), table_pts)
    pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (80, 60, 38), table_pts, 2)
    for yy in range(table_y + 18, panel.bottom - 35, 22):
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (35, 25, 17), (panel_x + 75, yy), (panel.right - 75, yy), 1)
    crate_lbl = font_terminal_small.render("KRAP FIELD KIT", True, (130, 110, 80))
    if item == "weapon":
        surface.blit(crate_lbl, (WIDTH // 2 - crate_lbl.get_width() // 2, panel.bottom - 58))
    p = _ease_cutscene((t - 250) / 1100.0)
    idle = math.sin(t * 0.006) * 2
    if item == "weapon":
        hand_y = panel_y + 202 + idle
        left_start = (panel_x + 90, panel_y + 190)
        right_start = (panel.right - 90, panel_y + 206)
        left_hand_x = _lerp_cutscene(panel_x + 230, WIDTH // 2 - 35, p)
        left_hand_y = _lerp_cutscene(panel_y + 190, hand_y, p)
        right_hand_x = _lerp_cutscene(panel.right - 205, WIDTH // 2 + 38, p)
        right_hand_y = _lerp_cutscene(panel_y + 215, hand_y + 5, p)
        glow = pygame.Surface((300, 210), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        for r in range(130, 0, -8):
            a = int(22 * (1 - r / 130))
            pygame.draw.ellipse(glow, (120, 160, 220, a), (150 - r, 105 - r // 2, r * 2, r))
        surface.blit(glow, (WIDTH // 2 - 150, panel_y + 95))
        pygame.draw.line(surface, C_KRAP_UNIFORM, left_start, (left_hand_x, left_hand_y), 22)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (25, 30, 38), left_start, (left_hand_x, left_hand_y), 3)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (178, 150, 125), (int(left_hand_x), int(left_hand_y)), 15)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (70, 82, 100), (int(left_hand_x - 6), int(left_hand_y + 3)), 7)
        biribel_sleeve = (45, 55, 48)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, biribel_sleeve, right_start, (right_hand_x, right_hand_y), 22)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (24, 30, 26), right_start, (right_hand_x, right_hand_y), 3)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (54, 44, 36), (int(right_hand_x), int(right_hand_y)), 16)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (170, 140, 112), (int(right_hand_x - 4), int(right_hand_y + 1)), 7)
        item_x = _lerp_cutscene(left_hand_x, WIDTH // 2, 0.55)
        item_y = hand_y - 20
        _draw_handoff_pistol(surface, item_x, item_y, scale=1.3, angle=-8)
        small = font_terminal_small.render("9MM SIDEARM // 12-RD MAG", True, (150, 165, 175))
        surface.blit(small, (WIDTH // 2 - small.get_width() // 2, panel_y + 292))
    else:
        # ── MOLOTOVS: crate slides across the table toward Biribel ──
        crate_w, crate_h = 190, 86
        crate_start_x = panel_x + 175
        crate_end_x = WIDTH // 2 + 30
        crate_x = _lerp_cutscene(crate_start_x, crate_end_x, p)
        crate_y = table_y - 14 + idle * 0.5
        shad = pygame.Surface((crate_w + 30, 26), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.ellipse(shad, (0, 0, 0, 110), shad.get_rect())
        surface.blit(shad, (int(crate_x - crate_w // 2 - 15), int(crate_y + crate_h - 28)))
        crate_rect = pygame.Rect(int(crate_x - crate_w // 2), int(crate_y), crate_w, crate_h)
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (84, 58, 32), crate_rect, border_radius=4)
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (46, 30, 16), crate_rect, 3, border_radius=4)
        interior = crate_rect.inflate(-18, -18)
        interior.y -= 6
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (26, 18, 12), interior, border_radius=3)
        for px2 in range(crate_rect.x + 28, crate_rect.right - 16, 38):
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (58, 38, 20),
                             (px2, crate_rect.y + 4), (px2, crate_rect.bottom - 4), 2)
        stencil = font_terminal_small.render("FLAMMABLE", True, (160, 60, 40))
        surface.blit(stencil, (crate_rect.centerx - stencil.get_width() // 2,
                               crate_rect.bottom - 22))
        _draw_handoff_molotov(surface, crate_rect.centerx - 34, crate_rect.y - 26,
                              scale=0.62, angle=-12)
        _draw_handoff_molotov(surface, crate_rect.centerx + 34, crate_rect.y - 24,
                              scale=0.62, angle=10)
        glow = pygame.Surface((280, 180), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        for r in range(115, 0, -8):
            a = int(16 * (1 - r / 115))
            pygame.draw.ellipse(glow, (235, 170, 90, a), (140 - r, 90 - r // 2, r * 2, r))
        surface.blit(glow, (int(crate_rect.centerx - 140), int(crate_rect.y - 95)))
        push_x = crate_rect.x - 6
        push_y = crate_rect.centery - 6
        left_start = (panel_x + 80, panel_y + 195)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, C_KRAP_UNIFORM, left_start, (push_x, push_y), 22)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (25, 30, 38), left_start, (push_x, push_y), 3)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (178, 150, 125), (int(push_x), int(push_y)), 14)
        reach_p = _ease_cutscene((p - 0.55) / 0.45)
        if reach_p > 0:
            right_start = (panel.right - 80, panel_y + 210)
            reach_x = _lerp_cutscene(panel.right - 190, crate_rect.right + 4, reach_p)
            reach_y = _lerp_cutscene(panel_y + 215, crate_rect.centery - 2, reach_p)
            biribel_sleeve = (45, 55, 48)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, biribel_sleeve, right_start, (reach_x, reach_y), 22)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (24, 30, 26), right_start, (reach_x, reach_y), 3)
