def update_walking_sim(keys):
    global walk_camera_x, walk_camera_y, walk_firefight_triggered, walk_firefight_timer
    global walk_dialogue_active, walk_dialogue_speaker, walk_dialogue_text
    global walk_dialogue_char_idx, walk_dialogue_timer, walk_complete
    global walk_active_hover, walk_after_firefight, walk_fp_cutscene, _walk_player_moving, walk_return_timer
    if walk_player is None:
        return walk_camera_x, walk_camera_y, False
    if walk_return_timer > 0:
        walk_return_timer -= 1
    if walk_fp_cutscene:
        _update_walk_fp_cutscene()
        return walk_camera_x, walk_camera_y, False
    movement_locked = (walk_firefight_triggered and not walk_after_firefight) or walk_return_timer > 0
    if not movement_locked:
        dx, dy = 0, 0
        spd = 3
        if keys[pygame.K_w]:
            dy -= spd
        if keys[pygame.K_s]:
            dy += spd
        if keys[pygame.K_a]:
            dx -= spd
        if keys[pygame.K_d]:
            dx += spd
        if dx != 0 and dy != 0:
            dx *= 0.707
            dy *= 0.707
        is_moving = dx != 0 or dy != 0
        _walk_player_moving = is_moving
        walk_player.move(dx, dy, walk_walls)
        global _walk_footstep_timer
        if is_moving:
            _walk_footstep_timer -= 1
            if _walk_footstep_timer <= 0:
                audio.play("footstep_walk", volume=0.2)
                _walk_footstep_timer = 35 + random.randint(0, 10)
        else:
            _walk_footstep_timer = 0
        walk_camera_x += (walk_player.rect.centerx - WIDTH // 2 - walk_camera_x) * 0.1
        walk_camera_y += (walk_player.rect.centery - HEIGHT // 2 - walk_camera_y) * 0.1
    if walk_firefight_triggered and not walk_after_firefight:
        target_angle = 0.0
        cur = walk_player.angle
        diff = (target_angle - cur + math.pi) % (2 * math.pi) - math.pi
        walk_player.angle += diff * 0.08
    else:
        mx, my = pygame.mouse.get_pos()
        world_mx = mx + walk_camera_x
        world_my = my + walk_camera_y
        walk_player.angle = math.atan2(
            world_my - walk_player.rect.centery, world_mx - walk_player.rect.centerx
        )
    walk_active_hover = None
    for sx, sy, key, label, text in walk_story_points:
        if (
            math.hypot(walk_player.rect.centerx - sx, walk_player.rect.centery - sy)
            < 80
        ):
            walk_active_hover = (label, text)
            break
    if not walk_firefight_triggered:
        overlook_x = 38 * TILE_SIZE
        if walk_player.rect.centerx > overlook_x:
            walk_firefight_triggered = True
            STATE.walk_attack_active = True
            STATE.walk_attack_timer = -90        # pre-roll before anything happens
            STATE.walk_attack_events_done.clear()
            STATE.walk_boat_active = False
            STATE.walk_boat_x = -60.0
            STATE.walk_boat_y = SCENE_TOP + 165
            STATE.rig_status = ["ONLINE", "ONLINE", "ONLINE"]
            STATE.walk_missiles = []
            STATE.walk_explosions = []
            STATE.walk_terminal_active = False
            STATE.walk_terminal_status = ""
            walk_dialogue_active = False
            audio.play("radio_static", volume=0.25)
            audio.play("wind", volume=0.4, loops=-1)
    if STATE.walk_attack_active:
        STATE.walk_attack_timer += 1
        if STATE.walk_attack_timer >= 0:
            _update_walk_attack_timeline()
    if walk_dialogue_active and walk_dialogue_char_idx < len(walk_dialogue_text):
        walk_dialogue_timer += 1
        # First few characters type fast so it doesn't sit on "Thi"
        if walk_dialogue_char_idx < 8:
            threshold = 1
        else:
            threshold = 2
        if walk_dialogue_timer >= threshold:
            walk_dialogue_timer = 0
            next_ch = walk_dialogue_text[walk_dialogue_char_idx] if walk_dialogue_char_idx < len(walk_dialogue_text) else ""
            _play_walk_type_tick(next_ch, walk_dialogue_char_idx, volume=0.09)
            walk_dialogue_char_idx += 1
    if STATE.walk_dialogue_active and STATE.walk_dialogue_char_idx < len(STATE.walk_dialogue_text):
        STATE.walk_dialogue_timer += 1
        # Attack-cutscene dialogue should not crawl one letter at a time.
        state_dialogue_speed = 2 if STATE.walk_attack_active else 5
        if STATE.walk_dialogue_timer >= state_dialogue_speed:
            STATE.walk_dialogue_timer = 0
            next_ch = STATE.walk_dialogue_text[STATE.walk_dialogue_char_idx] if STATE.walk_dialogue_char_idx < len(STATE.walk_dialogue_text) else ""
            _play_walk_type_tick(next_ch, STATE.walk_dialogue_char_idx, volume=0.10 if STATE.walk_attack_active else 0.09)
            STATE.walk_dialogue_char_idx += 1
    if walk_after_firefight or STATE.walk_after_firefight:
        if walk_player.rect.centerx > walk_van_trigger_x:
            clear_walk_dialogue()
            walk_complete = True
    return walk_camera_x, walk_camera_y, walk_complete
def _ease_out_cubic(t):
    t = max(0.0, min(1.0, t))
    return 1 - (1 - t) ** 3
def _lerp(a, b, t):
    return a + (b - a) * t
def _start_walk_fp_cutscene(scene, reward, npc=None, stash=None):
    global walk_fp_cutscene, walk_dialogue_active
    durations = {
        "kid_kevlar": 520,
        "crate_ammo": 190,
        "crate_kevlar": 200,
    }
    _slow = {"kid_kevlar": 1.7}
    walk_dialogue_active = False
    speed = _slow.get(scene, 1.0)
    walk_fp_cutscene = {
        "scene": scene,
        "reward": reward,
        "npc": npc,
        "stash": stash,
        "timer": 0,
        "duration": durations.get(scene, 180),
        "speed": speed,
    }
    if scene.startswith("crate"):
        audio.play("door_creak", volume=0.45)
    else:
        audio.play("pickup", volume=0.25)
def _finish_walk_fp_cutscene():
    global walk_fp_cutscene
    global warehouse_hp_bonus, warehouse_mag_bonus, warehouse_intel_given
    global walk_dialogue_active, walk_dialogue_speaker, walk_dialogue_text
    global walk_dialogue_char_idx, walk_player
    if not walk_fp_cutscene:
        return
    scene = walk_fp_cutscene["scene"]
    reward = walk_fp_cutscene["reward"]
    npc = walk_fp_cutscene.get("npc")
    stash = walk_fp_cutscene.get("stash")
    walk_dialogue_active = True
    walk_dialogue_char_idx = 0
    if reward in ("kid_kevlar", "stash_kevlar"):
        warehouse_hp_bonus += 10
        if walk_player:
            walk_player.max_hp = 100 + warehouse_hp_bonus
            walk_player.hp = walk_player.max_hp
        walk_dialogue_speaker = npc["name"] if npc else "SYSTEM"
        if npc:
            walk_dialogue_text = (
                "The kid watches you strap the plate on.\n"
                "He gives a small nod, then disappears into the alley.\n"
                "Kevlar equipped. (+10 Max HP)"
            )
        else:
            walk_dialogue_text = (
                "You pry open the damaged container.\n"
                "Inside: a cracked but usable Kevlar plate.\n"
                "Kevlar salvaged. (+10 Max HP)"
            )
    elif reward == "stash_ammo":
        warehouse_mag_bonus += 1
        walk_dialogue_speaker = "SYSTEM"
        walk_dialogue_text = (
            "You open the KRAP ammo crate.\n"
            "Loose 9mm magazines sit under a torn supply manifest.\n"
            "Extra magazine acquired. (+1 starting mag)"
        )
    audio.play("pickup", volume=0.55)
    walk_fp_cutscene = None
def _update_walk_fp_cutscene():
    global walk_fp_cutscene
    if not walk_fp_cutscene:
        return
    walk_fp_cutscene["timer"] += 1.0 / walk_fp_cutscene.get("speed", 1.0)
    if walk_fp_cutscene["timer"] >= walk_fp_cutscene["duration"]:
        _finish_walk_fp_cutscene()
def _draw_fp_hands(surface, reach=0.0):
    cx = WIDTH // 2
    base_y = HEIGHT - LETTER_H - 55
    sleeve_col = (58, 68, 52)
    glove_col = (52, 42, 34)
    skin_col = (185, 150, 120)
    # Left hand
    lx = cx - 105 + int(reach * 25)
    ly = base_y + int(reach * -35)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, sleeve_col, (lx - 18, ly + 22, 42, 72), border_radius=8)
    pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, glove_col, (lx - 20, ly, 46, 42))
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, skin_col, (lx + 10, ly + 15), 7)
    # Right hand
    rx = cx + 80 - int(reach * 35)
    ry = base_y + int(reach * -45)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, sleeve_col, (rx - 18, ry + 22, 42, 72), border_radius=8)
    pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, glove_col, (rx - 20, ry, 46, 42))
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, skin_col, (rx - 10, ry + 15), 7)
def _draw_fp_kevlar(surface, x, y, scale=1.0):
    s = scale
    w = int(118 * s)
    h = int(92 * s)
    vest = pygame.Surface((w, h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pts = [
        (int(w * 0.18), 0),
        (int(w * 0.82), 0),
        (w - 4, int(h * 0.28)),
        (w - 8, h - 6),
        (int(w * 0.68), h),
        (int(w * 0.32), h),
        (8, h - 6),
        (4, int(h * 0.28)),
    ]
    pygame.draw.polygon(vest, (64, 76, 58), pts)
    pygame.draw.polygon(vest, (35, 45, 34), pts, 2)
    # Collar
    pygame.draw.rect(vest, (42, 50, 38), (int(w * 0.32), 2, int(w * 0.36), int(h * 0.18)), border_radius=3)
    # Center seam
    pygame.draw.line(vest, (34, 42, 30), (w // 2, 8), (w // 2, h - 8), 2)
    # Pouches
    for px in (int(w * 0.18), int(w * 0.52)):
        pygame.draw.rect(vest, (48, 58, 42), (px, int(h * 0.38), int(w * 0.28), int(h * 0.34)), border_radius=3)
        pygame.draw.rect(vest, (30, 38, 28), (px, int(h * 0.38), int(w * 0.28), int(h * 0.34)), 1, border_radius=3)
        pygame.draw.line(vest, (72, 84, 60), (px + 4, int(h * 0.48)), (px + int(w * 0.28) - 4, int(h * 0.48)), 1)
    # Scratches / wear
    pygame.draw.line(vest, (105, 110, 85), (int(w * 0.18), int(h * 0.2)), (int(w * 0.38), int(h * 0.28)), 1)
    pygame.draw.line(vest, (25, 30, 22), (int(w * 0.62), int(h * 0.74)), (int(w * 0.78), int(h * 0.7)), 1)
    surface.blit(vest, (int(x - w // 2), int(y - h // 2)))
def _draw_fp_mag(surface, x, y, rot=0):
    mag = pygame.Surface((26, 58), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pts = [
        (4, 2),
        (22, 2),
        (24, 54),
        (7, 56),
    ]
    pygame.draw.polygon(mag, (68, 66, 60), pts)
    pygame.draw.polygon(mag, (35, 35, 34), pts, 1)
    for i in range(6):
        pygame.draw.line(mag, (100, 95, 82), (7, 10 + i * 7), (20, 10 + i * 7), 1)
    pygame.draw.rect(mag, (170, 125, 45), (7, 46, 15, 7), border_radius=2)
    rotated = pygame.transform.rotate(mag, rot)
    rect = rotated.get_rect(center=(int(x), int(y)))
    surface.blit(rotated, rect)
def _draw_fp_crate(surface, cx, cy, open_t, contents="ammo"):
    cw, ch = 260, 130
    # Crate shadow
    shadow = pygame.Surface((cw + 40, ch + 40), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.ellipse(shadow, (0, 0, 0, 80), (0, 16, cw + 40, ch + 18))
    surface.blit(shadow, (cx - cw // 2 - 20, cy - ch // 2 + 45))
    # Crate body
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (92, 62, 35), (cx - cw // 2, cy - ch // 2, cw, ch), border_radius=5)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (52, 34, 20), (cx - cw // 2, cy - ch // 2, cw, ch), 3, border_radius=5)
    # Wood planks
    for i in range(4):
        py = cy - ch // 2 + 20 + i * 26
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (67, 43, 24), (cx - cw // 2 + 8, py), (cx + cw // 2 - 8, py), 2)
    # Metal bands
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (56, 58, 58), (cx - cw // 2 + 28, cy - ch // 2, 12, ch))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (56, 58, 58), (cx + cw // 2 - 40, cy - ch // 2, 12, ch))
    # Interior glow once opening
    if open_t > 0.25:
        glow_alpha = int(120 * open_t)
        glow = pygame.Surface((cw - 30, ch - 20), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.ellipse(glow, (255, 175, 50, glow_alpha), glow.get_rect())
        surface.blit(glow, (cx - cw // 2 + 15, cy - ch // 2 + 12))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (25, 20, 14), (cx - cw // 2 + 18, cy - ch // 2 + 18, cw - 36, ch - 30), border_radius=3)
        if contents == "ammo":
            _draw_fp_mag(surface, cx - 35, cy + 5, rot=-8)
            _draw_fp_mag(surface, cx + 5, cy + 3, rot=5)
            _draw_fp_mag(surface, cx + 42, cy + 8, rot=-3)
        else:
            _draw_fp_kevlar(surface, cx, cy + 10, scale=0.75)
    # Lid opening upward
    lid_h = 42
    lid_y = cy - ch // 2 - int(open_t * 95)
    lid_squash = max(10, int(lid_h * (1.0 - open_t * 0.65)))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (110, 75, 42), (cx - cw // 2, lid_y, cw, lid_squash), border_radius=4)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (55, 36, 22), (cx - cw // 2, lid_y, cw, lid_squash), 2, border_radius=4)
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (70, 45, 25), (cx - cw // 2 + 10, lid_y + lid_squash // 2), (cx + cw // 2 - 10, lid_y + lid_squash // 2), 2)
def _draw_walk_fp_cutscene(surface):
    if not walk_fp_cutscene:
        return
    scene = walk_fp_cutscene["scene"]
    timer = walk_fp_cutscene["timer"]
    duration = walk_fp_cutscene["duration"]
    p = timer / duration
    # Dark cinematic focus overlay
    overlay = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    overlay.fill((5, 7, 10, 145))
    surface.blit(overlay, (0, 0))
    # Subtle first-person vignette
    draw_vignette(surface, strength=180)
    # Fade in/out
    fade = 0
    if timer < 22:
        fade = int(255 * (1 - timer / 22))
    elif timer > duration - 28:
        fade = int(255 * ((timer - (duration - 28)) / 28))
    if scene == "kid_kevlar":
        ground_y = HEIGHT - LETTER_H - 100
        center_x = WIDTH // 2
        # ── ENVIRONMENT ──
        container_tiles = [
            CONTAINER_TILE_RUST, CONTAINER_TILE_RED,
            CONTAINER_TILE_GREY, CONTAINER_TILE_BLUE,
        ]
        for col_i, tx in enumerate(range(0, WIDTH + TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE)):
            for row_i, ty in enumerate(range(LETTER_H, ground_y, TILE_SIZE)):
                tile = container_tiles[(col_i + row_i) % 4]
                surface.blit(tile, (tx, ty))
        wall_shade = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, ground_y - LETTER_H), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        wall_shade.fill((5, 8, 12, 120))
        surface.blit(wall_shade, (0, LETTER_H))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (12, 12, 14), (0, ground_y - 8, WIDTH, 8))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (28, 26, 22), (0, ground_y, WIDTH, HEIGHT - ground_y - LETTER_H))
        # Debris on ground
        rng_debris = random.Random(42)
        for _ in range(35):
            dx = rng_debris.randint(20, WIDTH - 20)
            dy = ground_y + rng_debris.randint(5, HEIGHT - ground_y - LETTER_H - 15)
            dw = rng_debris.randint(8, 28)
            dh = rng_debris.randint(4, 10)
            shade = rng_debris.randint(-8, 8)
            pygame.draw.rect(surface, (42 + shade, 38 + shade, 32 + shade), (dx, dy, dw, dh))
        puddle = pygame.Surface((160, 35), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.ellipse(puddle, (18, 22, 30, 100), puddle.get_rect())
        surface.blit(puddle, (WIDTH // 2 + 60, ground_y + 30))
        # ── Flickering overhead light ──
        light_on = ((pygame.time.get_ticks() + timer * 37) // 180) % 5 != 0
        if light_on:
            light = pygame.Surface((280, 200), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            for r in range(100, 0, -4):
                a = int(18 * (1 - r / 100))
                pygame.draw.ellipse(light, (200, 170, 100, a),
                    (140 - r, 100 - r // 2, r * 2, r))
            surface.blit(light, (center_x - 140, LETTER_H + 20))
        # ── Dripping water detail ──
        drip_phase = (timer * 0.04) % (math.pi * 2)
        if math.sin(drip_phase) > 0.95:
            drip_x = center_x + 180
            drip_y = LETTER_H + 40 + int((timer * 3) % 60)
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, (30, 40, 55, 180), (drip_x, drip_y), 2)
        # ── RUBBLE PILE (always visible, dim until camera "notices" it) ──
        rubble_x = center_x + 130
        rubble_y = ground_y - 5
        rubble_alpha_t = _ease_out_cubic(min(1.0, max(0, (timer - 140) / 60)))
        rubble_brightness = 0.35 + 0.65 * rubble_alpha_t  # starts dim, brightens
        rng_r = random.Random(99)
        for _ in range(12):
            rx = rubble_x + rng_r.randint(-55, 55)
            ry = rubble_y + rng_r.randint(-30, 20)
            rw = rng_r.randint(12, 40)
            rh = rng_r.randint(8, 18)
            shade = rng_r.randint(-10, 10)
            base = int((55 + shade) * rubble_brightness)
            pygame.draw.rect(surface, (base, base - 7, base - 15), (rx, ry, rw, rh))
        # Bent rebar
        rebar_col = tuple(int(c * rubble_brightness) for c in (85, 70, 55))
        pygame.draw.line(surface, rebar_col, (rubble_x - 30, rubble_y - 25),
            (rubble_x - 22, rubble_y + 5), 3)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, rebar_col, (rubble_x + 20, rubble_y - 20),
            (rubble_x + 15, rubble_y + 10), 2)
        # Kevlar peeking out (becomes visible after 180)
        vest_peek_t = _ease_out_cubic(min(1.0, max(0, (timer - 180) / 40)))
        if vest_peek_t > 0.05:
            vest_vis = int(vest_peek_t * 60)
            vest_clip = pygame.Surface((100, vest_vis), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            vest_mini = pygame.Surface((100, 70), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            v_pts = [(8, 0), (92, 0), (98, 18), (96, 65), (72, 70), (28, 70), (4, 65), (2, 18)]
            pygame.draw.polygon(vest_mini, (64, 76, 58), v_pts)
            pygame.draw.polygon(vest_mini, (35, 45, 34), v_pts, 2)
            vest_clip.blit(vest_mini, (0, 0))
            surface.blit(vest_clip, (rubble_x - 50, rubble_y - vest_vis + 15))
            glow = pygame.Surface((120, 80), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            glow_a = int(30 * vest_peek_t * abs(math.sin(timer * 0.05)))
            pygame.draw.ellipse(glow, (255, 200, 80, glow_a), glow.get_rect())
            surface.blit(glow, (rubble_x - 60, rubble_y - vest_vis - 5))
        # ══════════════════════════════════════════════
        # KID — with breathing, blinking, weight shift
        # ══════════════════════════════════════════════
        kid_visible = timer > 35
        kid_entered = min(1.0, max(0, (timer - 35) / 55))
        kid_enter_t = _ease_out_cubic(kid_entered)
        if kid_visible:
            kid_x = _lerp(-120, center_x - 140, kid_enter_t)
            kid_base_y = ground_y - 8
            # ── Breathing ──
            breath = math.sin(timer * 0.06) * 1.5
            # ── Weight shift (subtle sway) ──
            sway = math.sin(timer * 0.025) * 2.0
            # ── Blinking ──
            blink_cycle = (timer * 7 + 43) % 300  # offset so it doesn't sync with anything
            is_blinking = 0 < blink_cycle < 12
            kid_draw_x = kid_x + sway
            kid_draw_y = kid_base_y + breath
            kid_scale = 0.7
            # Shadow
            shadow = pygame.Surface((50, 14), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            pygame.draw.ellipse(shadow, (0, 0, 0, 55), shadow.get_rect())
            surface.blit(shadow, (int(kid_draw_x - 25), int(kid_base_y - 6)))
            # Legs
            leg_w = int(9 * kid_scale)
            leg_h = int(42 * kid_scale)
            for lx_off in (-8, 8):
                pygame.draw.rect(surface, (32, 30, 28),
                    (int(kid_draw_x + lx_off - leg_w // 2), int(kid_draw_y - leg_h), leg_w, leg_h))
            # Shoes
            for lx_off in (-8, 8):
                pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, (22, 18, 16),
                    (int(kid_draw_x + lx_off - 7), int(kid_draw_y - 5), 14, 8))
            # Torso
            torso_y = int(kid_draw_y - leg_h - 52)
            torso_pts = [
                (int(kid_draw_x - 22), torso_y + 8),
                (int(kid_draw_x + 22), torso_y + 8),
                (int(kid_draw_x + 26), torso_y + 55),
                (int(kid_draw_x - 26), torso_y + 55),
            ]
            pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (105, 95, 78), torso_pts)
            pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (80, 72, 58), torso_pts, 1)
            # Wrinkles
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (82, 74, 60),
                (int(kid_draw_x - 10), torso_y + 15),
                (int(kid_draw_x - 15), torso_y + 45), 1)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (82, 74, 60),
                (int(kid_draw_x + 8), torso_y + 20),
                (int(kid_draw_x + 12), torso_y + 48), 1)
            # Collar
            pygame.draw.arc(surface, (85, 78, 62),
                (int(kid_draw_x - 16), torso_y + 2, 32, 16), 0, math.pi, 2)
            # Arms
            arm_col = (105, 95, 78)
            skin_col = (175, 145, 115)
            # Left arm (hangs, slight swing with breathing)
            arm_swing = math.sin(timer * 0.04) * 3
            pygame.draw.line(surface, arm_col,
                (int(kid_draw_x - 22), torso_y + 14),
                (int(kid_draw_x - 30 + arm_swing), torso_y + 52), 6)
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, skin_col,
                (int(kid_draw_x - 31 + arm_swing), torso_y + 54), 5)
            # Right arm — points after frame 175
            if timer > 175:
                point_prog = _ease_out_cubic(min(1.0, (timer - 175) / 40))
                arm_end_x = _lerp(kid_draw_x + 22, kid_draw_x + 95, point_prog)
                arm_end_y = _lerp(torso_y + 50, torso_y + 30, point_prog)
                pygame.draw.line(surface, arm_col,
                    (int(kid_draw_x + 22), torso_y + 14),
                    (int(_lerp(kid_draw_x + 30, kid_draw_x + 55, point_prog)),
                     int(_lerp(torso_y + 32, torso_y + 22, point_prog))), 6)
                pygame.draw.line(surface, arm_col,
                    (int(_lerp(kid_draw_x + 30, kid_draw_x + 55, point_prog)),
                     int(_lerp(torso_y + 32, torso_y + 22, point_prog))),
                    (int(arm_end_x), int(arm_end_y)), 5)
                pygame.draw.circle(surface, skin_col, (int(arm_end_x), int(arm_end_y)), 5)
                pygame.draw.line(surface, skin_col,
                    (int(arm_end_x), int(arm_end_y)),
                    (int(arm_end_x + 18), int(arm_end_y + 8)), 3)
            else:
                pygame.draw.line(surface, arm_col,
                    (int(kid_draw_x + 22), torso_y + 14),
                    (int(kid_draw_x + 28), torso_y + 52), 6)
                pygame.draw.circle(surface, skin_col,
                    (int(kid_draw_x + 29), torso_y + 54), 5)
            # Head
            head_y = torso_y - 8
            head_r = 14
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, skin_col, (int(kid_draw_x), head_y), head_r)
            # Hair
            hair_pts = [
                (int(kid_draw_x - 14), head_y - 2),
                (int(kid_draw_x - 12), head_y - 16),
                (int(kid_draw_x - 5), head_y - 18),
                (int(kid_draw_x + 3), head_y - 19),
                (int(kid_draw_x + 10), head_y - 16),
                (int(kid_draw_x + 14), head_y - 8),
                (int(kid_draw_x + 14), head_y - 2),
            ]
            pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (28, 22, 18), hair_pts)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (22, 18, 14),
                (int(kid_draw_x - 8), head_y - 18),
                (int(kid_draw_x - 14), head_y - 22), 1)
            # Eyes — blink, and track toward rubble after pointing
            eye_y = head_y - 2
            if is_blinking:
                # Closed eyes — just lines
                for ex_off in (-5, 5):
                    pygame.draw.line(surface, (45, 30, 20),
                        (int(kid_draw_x + ex_off - 3), eye_y),
                        (int(kid_draw_x + ex_off + 3), eye_y), 1)
            else:
                # Eye gaze direction shifts after pointing
                if timer > 200:
                    gaze_x = 1.5  # looking toward rubble (right)
                else:
                    gaze_x = 0    # looking at player (center)
                for ex_off in (-5, 5):
                    pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, (220, 215, 205),
                        (int(kid_draw_x + ex_off - 4), eye_y - 3, 8, 6))
                    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (45, 30, 20),
                        (int(kid_draw_x + ex_off + gaze_x), eye_y - 1), 3)
                    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (10, 8, 6),
                        (int(kid_draw_x + ex_off + gaze_x), eye_y - 1), 1)
                    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (255, 255, 250),
                        (int(kid_draw_x + ex_off + gaze_x + 1), eye_y - 2), 1)
                # Dark circles
                for ex_off in (-5, 5):
                    pygame.draw.arc(surface, (120, 95, 80),
                        (int(kid_draw_x + ex_off - 5), eye_y, 10, 6),
                        0, math.pi, 1)
            # Nose
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (155, 125, 100),
                (int(kid_draw_x), head_y + 2),
                (int(kid_draw_x + 2), head_y + 6), 1)
            # Mouth — changes with dialogue phase
            mouth_y = head_y + 8
            speaking_now = (50 < timer < 120) or (145 < timer < 210) or (380 < timer < 460)
            if speaking_now:
                # Open mouth, oscillating
                mouth_open = 2 + int(abs(math.sin(timer * 0.3)) * 3)
                pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, (80, 40, 35),
                    (int(kid_draw_x - 4), mouth_y - 1, 8, mouth_open))
            else:
                pygame.draw.line(surface, (130, 100, 80),
                    (int(kid_draw_x - 4), mouth_y),
                    (int(kid_draw_x + 4), mouth_y + 1), 1)
            # Dirt smudges
            smudge = pygame.Surface((10, 6), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            pygame.draw.ellipse(smudge, (90, 70, 50, 80), smudge.get_rect())
            surface.blit(smudge, (int(kid_draw_x + 7), head_y + 3))
        # ══════════════════════════════════════
        # DIALOGUE — clean phases, NO OVERLAP
        # ══════════════════════════════════════
        def _speech_bubble(text, x, y, alpha):
            if alpha <= 0:
                return
            txt = font_dialogue.render(text, True, (230, 220, 195))
            txt.set_alpha(alpha)
            tw = txt.get_width() + 24
            bg = pygame.Surface((tw, 34), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            pygame.draw.rect(bg, (0, 0, 0, min(180, alpha)), bg.get_rect(), border_radius=4)
            surface.blit(bg, (int(x - tw // 2), int(y)))
            surface.blit(txt, (int(x - txt.get_width() // 2), int(y + 6)))
        # Phase A: Cough + greeting (50–130)
        if 50 < timer < 130:
            a = min(255, int((timer - 50) * 6))
            if timer > 110:
                a = max(0, int(255 - (timer - 110) * 12))
            _speech_bubble("*cough* ...hey. Hey, mister.", kid_x + 40, ground_y - 180, a)
        # Phase B: "I haven't eaten" (140–220)
        elif 140 < timer < 220:
            a = min(255, int((timer - 140) * 5))
            if timer > 195:
                a = max(0, int(255 - (timer - 195) * 10))
            _speech_bubble("I haven't eaten in two days.", kid_x + 40, ground_y - 180, a)
        # Phase C: "Over there" (225–310) — after he starts pointing
        elif 225 < timer < 310:
            a = min(255, int((timer - 225) * 5))
            if timer > 285:
                a = max(0, int(255 - (timer - 285) * 10))
            _speech_bubble("Over there. Behind those crates.", kid_x + 60, ground_y - 185, a)
        # Phase D: "Be careful" — AFTER vest is picked up (390–480)
        elif 390 < timer < 480:
            a = min(255, int((timer - 390) * 4))
            if timer > 450:
                a = max(0, int(255 - (timer - 450) * 8))
            _speech_bubble("Be careful, mister. The guards are angry tonight.",
                max(30, int(kid_x - 20)), ground_y - 185, a)
        # ── Hands reach in (310–360) ──
        if timer > 305:
            reach_t = _ease_out_cubic(min(1.0, (timer - 305) / 45))
            _draw_fp_hands(surface, reach=reach_t)
        # ── Lift and inspect vest (350–400) ──
        if timer > 345:
            lift_t = _ease_out_cubic(min(1.0, (timer - 345) / 45))
            vest_end_x = center_x
            vest_end_y = HEIGHT // 2 - 20
            vx = _lerp(rubble_x, vest_end_x, lift_t)
            vy = _lerp(ground_y - 10, vest_end_y, lift_t)
            vs = _lerp(0.6, 1.3, lift_t)
            inspect_wobble = 0
            if timer > 370:
                inspect_wobble = math.sin((timer - 370) * 0.08) * 3
            _draw_fp_kevlar(surface, vx + inspect_wobble, vy, scale=vs)
            # Quality assessment
            if 375 < timer < 400:
                assess_alpha = min(255, int((timer - 375) * 10))
                assess = font_terminal_small.render(
                    "Cracked but functional. Better than nothing.", True, (180, 175, 160))
                assess.set_alpha(assess_alpha)
                surface.blit(assess, (center_x - assess.get_width() // 2, vy + 75))
        # ── Acquisition banner (380–500) ──
        if timer > 375:
            acq_alpha = min(255, int((timer - 375) * 5))
            if timer > 480:
                acq_alpha = max(0, int(255 - (timer - 480) * 8))
            label = font_speaker.render("KEVLAR PLATE ACQUIRED", True, (255, 220, 120))
            label.set_alpha(acq_alpha)
            surface.blit(label, (WIDTH // 2 - label.get_width() // 2, LETTER_H + 22))
            sub = font_terminal_small.render("+10 MAX HP", True, (100, 220, 100))
            sub.set_alpha(acq_alpha)
            surface.blit(sub, (WIDTH // 2 - sub.get_width() // 2, LETTER_H + 55))
    elif scene in ("crate_ammo", "crate_kevlar"):
        open_t = _ease_out_cubic((timer - 35) / 70)
        grab_t = _ease_out_cubic((timer - 115) / 45)
        contents = "ammo" if scene == "crate_ammo" else "kevlar"
        # Hands first, then crate
        _draw_fp_hands(surface, reach=min(1.0, open_t * 0.8))
        _draw_fp_crate(surface, WIDTH // 2, HEIGHT // 2 + 80, open_t, contents=contents)
        if scene == "crate_ammo" and timer > 115:
            # Mags float toward player
            for i, ox in enumerate((-38, 0, 38)):
                x = _lerp(WIDTH // 2 + ox, WIDTH // 2 - 35 + i * 35, grab_t)
                y = _lerp(HEIGHT // 2 + 90, HEIGHT - LETTER_H - 145, grab_t)
                _draw_fp_mag(surface, x, y, rot=int(math.sin(timer * 0.08 + i) * 8))
            if timer > 145:
                label = font_speaker.render("MAGAZINE ACQUIRED", True, (255, 220, 120))
                surface.blit(label, (WIDTH // 2 - label.get_width() // 2, LETTER_H + 25))
        elif scene == "crate_kevlar" and timer > 115:
            x = WIDTH // 2
            y = _lerp(HEIGHT // 2 + 90, HEIGHT // 2 - 10, grab_t)
            s = _lerp(0.75, 1.15, grab_t)
            _draw_fp_kevlar(surface, x, y, scale=s)
            if timer > 145:
                label = font_speaker.render("KEVLAR SALVAGED", True, (255, 220, 120))
                surface.blit(label, (WIDTH // 2 - label.get_width() // 2, LETTER_H + 25))
    # Instruction / cinematic footer
    footer = font_terminal_small.render("FIRST PERSON INTERACTION", True, (110, 120, 130))
    surface.blit(footer, (WIDTH // 2 - footer.get_width() // 2, HEIGHT - LETTER_H + 20))
    if fade > 0:
        fade_surf = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        fade_surf.fill((0, 0, 0, fade))
        surface.blit(fade_surf, (0, 0))
    draw_letterbox(surface)
def handle_walk_interact():
    global \
        walk_dialogue_active, \
        walk_dialogue_speaker, \
        walk_dialogue_text, \
        walk_dialogue_char_idx
    global \
        player_rations, \
        warehouse_hp_bonus, \
        warehouse_mag_bonus, \
        walk_npcs, \
        walk_stashes, \
        walk_fp_cutscene
    if walk_fp_cutscene:
        return
    if walk_dialogue_active:
        return
    for npc in walk_npcs:
        dist = math.hypot(
            walk_player.rect.centerx - npc["x"], walk_player.rect.centery - npc["y"]
        )
        if dist < 80:
            walk_dialogue_active = True
            walk_dialogue_speaker = npc["name"]
            walk_dialogue_char_idx = 0
            if npc["has_given"]:
                walk_dialogue_text = npc["done_dialogue"]
            else:
                if player_rations > 0:
                    npc["has_given"] = True
                    player_rations -= 1
                    if npc["reward"] == "kevlar":
                        _start_walk_fp_cutscene("kid_kevlar", "kid_kevlar", npc=npc)
                        return
                    else:
                        warehouse_intel_given = True
                        audio.play("pickup", volume=0.5)
                        walk_dialogue_text = (
                            npc["thanks"][0]
                            + "\n"
                            + npc["thanks"][1]
                            + "\n"
                            + npc["thanks"][2]
                            + "\n"
                            + npc["thanks"][3]
                        )
                else:
                    walk_dialogue_text = npc["dialogue"][0] + "\n" + npc["dialogue"][1]
            return
    for stash in walk_stashes:
        dist = math.hypot(
            walk_player.rect.centerx - stash["x"], walk_player.rect.centery - stash["y"]
        )
        if dist < 80:
            if stash["searched"]:
                walk_dialogue_active = True
                walk_dialogue_speaker = "SYSTEM"
                walk_dialogue_text = "Already searched."
                walk_dialogue_char_idx = 0
            else:
                stash["searched"] = True
                if stash["type"] == "ammo":
                    _start_walk_fp_cutscene("crate_ammo", "stash_ammo", stash=stash)
                    return
                elif stash["type"] == "kevlar":
                    _start_walk_fp_cutscene("crate_kevlar", "stash_kevlar", stash=stash)
                    return
            return
def _draw_walk_poster(surface, x, y):
    w, h = 18, 26
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (200, 180, 140), (x - w // 2, y - h // 2, w, h))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (60, 50, 40), (x - w // 2, y - h // 2, w, h), 1)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (160, 30, 30), (x - w // 2, y - h // 2, w, 4))
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (40, 30, 25), (x, y - 4), 3)
    pygame.draw.line(
        surface,
        (15, 15, 15),
        (x - w // 2 + 2, y - h // 2 + 2),
        (x + w // 2 - 2, y + h // 2 - 2),
        2,
    )
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (15, 15, 15), (x - 1, y - 4), 5, 1)
def _draw_walk_laundry(surface, x, y):
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (110, 90, 70), (x - 36, y - 8), (x + 36, y - 8), 1)
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (110, 90, 70), (x - 36, y - 6), (x + 36, y - 6), 1)
    clothes = [
        (-28, (140, 60, 50)),
        (-12, (200, 200, 190)),
        (4, (60, 80, 110)),
        (22, (180, 160, 90)),
    ]
    for ox, col in clothes:
        cw = 10
        ch = 14
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, col, (x + ox - cw // 2, y - 6, cw, ch))
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (50, 40, 30), (x + ox, y - 6), 1)
def _draw_walk_checkpoint(surface, x, y):
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (45, 50, 55), (x, y), 12)
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (25, 30, 35), (x, y), 12, 1)
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (60, 65, 70), (x, y), 8, 1)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (220, 220, 220), (x - 6, y - 2, 5, 7))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (220, 220, 220), (x + 1, y - 1, 5, 7))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (40, 40, 40), (x - 6, y - 2, 5, 7), 1)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (40, 40, 40), (x + 1, y - 1, 5, 7), 1)
    s1x, s1y = x - 24, y - 4
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, C_KRAP_UNIFORM, (s1x, s1y), 8)
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (180, 150, 125), (s1x, s1y), 4)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, C_KRAP_BLUE, (s1x - 7, s1y + 2, 4, 3))
    s2x, s2y = x + 24, y + 4
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, C_KRAP_UNIFORM, (s2x, s2y), 8)
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (180, 150, 125), (s2x, s2y), 4)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, C_KRAP_BLUE, (s2x + 3, s2y + 2, 4, 3))
def _draw_walk_fish(surface, x, y):
    crates = [(-18, -8), (-2, -10), (12, -4), (-10, 6), (4, 8)]
    for ox, oy in crates:
        cw, ch = 14, 12
        pygame.draw.rect(
            surface, (90, 75, 55), (x + ox - cw // 2, y + oy - ch // 2, cw, ch)
        )
        pygame.draw.rect(
            surface, (50, 40, 30), (x + ox - cw // 2, y + oy - ch // 2, cw, ch), 1
        )
        pygame.draw.line(
            surface,
            (50, 40, 30),
            (x + ox - cw // 2 + 4, y + oy - ch // 2),
            (x + ox - cw // 2 + 4, y + oy + ch // 2),
            1,
        )
        pygame.draw.line(
            surface,
            (50, 40, 30),
            (x + ox - cw // 2 + 9, y + oy - ch // 2),
            (x + ox - cw // 2 + 9, y + oy + ch // 2),
            1,
        )
        pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, (160, 170, 180), (x + ox - 4, y + oy - 2, 8, 3))
    for fx_o, fy_o in [(-22, -14), (8, -16), (16, 10), (-18, 14)]:
        if (pygame.time.get_ticks() // 100 + fx_o) % 4 < 2:
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, (10, 10, 10), (x + fx_o, y + fy_o), 1)

    )

    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (90, 80, 50), (x - 4, y + 4), 10)
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (60, 55, 45), (x - 4, y + 4), 5)

def _gen_walk_props():
    rng = random.Random(31)
    props = []
    map_w = len(walk_map[0]) * TILE_SIZE
    map_h = len(walk_map) * TILE_SIZE
    def is_walkable(wx, wy):
        col = wx // TILE_SIZE
        row = wy // TILE_SIZE
        if 0 <= row < len(walk_map) and 0 <= col < len(walk_map[row]):
            return walk_map[row][col] not in ("C", "W")
        return False
    lamps = [
        (3 * TILE_SIZE + 32, 4 * TILE_SIZE + 32),
        (9 * TILE_SIZE + 32, 7 * TILE_SIZE + 16),
        (16 * TILE_SIZE, 4 * TILE_SIZE + 48),
        (22 * TILE_SIZE + 16, 8 * TILE_SIZE + 16),
        (30 * TILE_SIZE + 16, 4 * TILE_SIZE + 32),
        (36 * TILE_SIZE, 7 * TILE_SIZE + 16),
    ]
    for lx, ly in lamps:
        props.append(("lamp", lx, ly))
    for _ in range(50):
        for _try in range(10):
            wx = rng.randint(2 * TILE_SIZE, map_w - 3 * TILE_SIZE)
            wy = rng.randint(2 * TILE_SIZE, map_h - 2 * TILE_SIZE)
            if is_walkable(wx, wy):
                kind = rng.choice(
                    [
                        "drum",
                        "drum",
                        "trash",
                        "trash",
                        "pallet",
                        "puddle",
                        "puddle",
                        "puddle",
                        "pipe",
                        "cable",
                        "debris",
                        "debris",
                    ]
                )
                props.append((kind, wx, wy))
                break
    for _ in range(25):
        for _try in range(10):
            col = rng.randint(2, len(walk_map[0]) - 3)
            row = rng.randint(2, len(walk_map) - 3)
            tile = walk_map[row][col]
            if tile == ".":
                if row > 0 and walk_map[row - 1][col] == "C":
                    wx = col * TILE_SIZE + rng.randint(8, TILE_SIZE - 8)
                    wy = row * TILE_SIZE + 4
                    kind = rng.choice(
                        ["wall_graffiti", "wall_poster", "wall_pipe", "wall_stain"]
                    )
                    props.append((kind, wx, wy))
                    break
    return props
def _draw_prop_lamp_pole(surface, x, y, fcx, fcy):
    sx = x - fcx
    sy = y - fcy
    pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, (0, 0, 0, 80), (sx - 6, sy - 2, 12, 5))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (28, 26, 28), (sx - 2, sy - 28, 4, 28))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (40, 38, 40), (sx - 8, sy - 32, 16, 6))
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (255, 200, 120), (sx, sy - 28), 3)
    glow = pygame.Surface((20, 20), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.circle(glow, (255, 200, 120, 90), (10, 10), 8)
    surface.blit(glow, (sx - 10, sy - 38))

    sx = x - fcx
    sy = y - fcy
    pts = [(sx - 18, sy + 4), (sx - 6, sy), (sx + 6, sy + 3), (sx + 18, sy - 2)]
    for i in range(len(pts) - 1):
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (15, 15, 18), pts[i], pts[i + 1], 3)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (35, 30, 28), pts[i], pts[i + 1], 1)
def _draw_prop_debris(surface, x, y, fcx, fcy):
    sx = x - fcx
    sy = y - fcy
    rng_d = random.Random(x * 7 + y)
    for _ in range(5):
        ox = rng_d.randint(-12, 12)
        oy = rng_d.randint(-6, 6)
        sz = rng_d.randint(2, 4)
        col = rng_d.choice([(50, 48, 45), (60, 56, 52), (40, 38, 36)])
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, col, (sx + ox, sy + oy, sz, sz))
def _draw_prop_wall_graffiti(surface, x, y, fcx, fcy):
    sx = x - fcx
    sy = y - fcy
    pts = [
        (sx, sy - 3),
        (sx - 3, sy - 5),
        (sx - 5, sy + 1),
        (sx - 4, sy + 4),
        (sx, sy + 5),
        (sx + 4, sy + 4),
        (sx + 5, sy + 1),
        (sx + 3, sy - 5),
    ]
    pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (12, 12, 12), pts)
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (12, 12, 12), (sx, sy + 5), (sx + 1, sy + 12), 1)

    pygame.draw.line(surface, (60, 50, 40), (sx - 4, sy + 5), (sx + 4, sy + 5), 1)

PROP_DRAWERS = {
    "drum": _draw_prop_drum,

def _draw_walk_props_pools(fcx, fcy):
    global WALK_PROPS
    if WALK_PROPS is None:
        WALK_PROPS = _gen_walk_props()
    for kind, x, y in WALK_PROPS:
        if kind == "lamp":
            sx = x - fcx
            sy = y - fcy
            if -200 < sx < WIDTH + 200 and -200 < sy < HEIGHT + 200:
                pool = pygame.Surface((180, 180), pygame.SRCALPHA)
                for r in range(80, 0, -4):
                    a = int(28 * (1 - r / 80))
                    pygame.draw.ellipse(
                        pool, (220, 160, 80, a), (90 - r, 90 - r // 2, r * 2, r)
                    )
                screen.blit(pool, (sx - 90, sy - 90))
def _draw_walk_props(fcx, fcy):
    global WALK_PROPS
    if WALK_PROPS is None:
        WALK_PROPS = _gen_walk_props()
    for kind, x, y in WALK_PROPS:
        sx = x - fcx
        sy = y - fcy
        if not (-100 < sx < WIDTH + 100 and -100 < sy < HEIGHT + 100):
            continue
        if kind == "lamp":
            _draw_prop_lamp_pole(screen, x, y, fcx, fcy)
        else:
            drawer = PROP_DRAWERS.get(kind)
            if drawer:
                drawer(screen, x, y, fcx, fcy)
# ─── Attack sequence timing (frames @ 60fps) ─────────────────────────
# Three-act cinematic:
#   ACT 1 — Setup     ( 0 – 360 / ~6 s)
#   ACT 2 — The Strike (400 – 980 / ~10 s)
#   ACT 3 — Aftermath (1100 – 1700 / ~10 s)
ATK_BOAT_APPEAR      = 90    # Boat enters frame from the left
ATK_FISHERMAN_1     = 135   # early warning before the terminal feed boots
ATK_TERMINAL_ON      = 260   # KRAP observation feed boots up
ATK_FISHERMAN_2     = 360   # warning just before the first missile run
ATK_TARGET_CENTER    = 400
ATK_MISSILE_1_LAUNCH = 460
ATK_IMPACT_CENTER    = 540
ATK_TARGET_LEFT      = 620
ATK_MISSILE_2_LAUNCH = 680
ATK_IMPACT_LEFT      = 760
ATK_TARGET_RIGHT     = 840
ATK_MISSILE_3_LAUNCH = 900
ATK_IMPACT_RIGHT     = 980
ATK_FISHERMAN_3     = 1220  # aftermath line after the last rig has burned for a beat
ATK_FISHERMAN_4     = 1600  # final fisherman line
ATK_END             = 2180  # extra breath before handoff to avoid an abrupt return
# ── Canonical world positions (used by BOTH update and draw) ──
HORIZON_Y_OVERLOOK  = SCENE_TOP + 180
BOAT_BASE_Y         = SCENE_TOP + 165
RIG_X_POSITIONS     = (225, 505, 760)   # LEFT, CENTER, RIGHT
RIG_NAMES           = ("LEFT RIG", "CENTER RIG", "RIGHT RIG")
RIG_IMPACT_FRAMES   = (ATK_IMPACT_LEFT, ATK_IMPACT_CENTER, ATK_IMPACT_RIGHT)
def _update_walk_attack_timeline():
    """Drives the attack cutscene: boat, missiles, rig destruction, dialogue.
    All side-effects (sounds, screen shake, dialogue, STATE mutations) happen
    here. The draw function only reads STATE — so the HUD and the visuals
    can never disagree with what actually happened.
    """
    if not STATE.walk_attack_active:
        return
    t = STATE.walk_attack_timer
    done = STATE.walk_attack_events_done
    # ── ACT 1: Setup ─────────────────────────────────────────────
    if t >= ATK_BOAT_APPEAR and "boat" not in done:
        done.add("boat")
        STATE.walk_boat_active = True
        STATE.walk_boat_x = 0.0
        STATE.walk_boat_y = BOAT_BASE_Y
        STATE.walk_terminal_status = ""
    if t >= ATK_FISHERMAN_1 and "fish1" not in done:
        done.add("fish1")
        _trigger_walk_dialogue(
            "OLD FISHERMAN",
            "Hear that engine?\nToo fast for a fishing boat.",
        )
    if t >= ATK_TERMINAL_ON and "terminal" not in done:
        done.add("terminal")
        STATE.walk_terminal_active = True
        STATE.walk_terminal_status = "CONTACT: UNKNOWN VESSEL — TRACKING"
        audio.play("menu_click", volume=0.5)
        audio.play("radio_static", volume=0.3)
    if t >= ATK_FISHERMAN_2 and "fish2" not in done:
        done.add("fish2")
        _trigger_walk_dialogue(
            "OLD FISHERMAN",
            "They come in low during the rain.\n"
            "The rigs never see them until the first rocket.",
        )
    # ── ACT 2: The strike (CENTER → LEFT → RIGHT) ───────────────
    _process_rig_strike(t, done, "center", 1,
        ATK_TARGET_CENTER, ATK_MISSILE_1_LAUNCH, ATK_IMPACT_CENTER)
    _process_rig_strike(t, done, "left", 0,
        ATK_TARGET_LEFT,   ATK_MISSILE_2_LAUNCH, ATK_IMPACT_LEFT)
    _process_rig_strike(t, done, "right", 2,
        ATK_TARGET_RIGHT,  ATK_MISSILE_3_LAUNCH, ATK_IMPACT_RIGHT)
    # ── ACT 3: Aftermath ────────────────────────────────────────
    if t >= ATK_FISHERMAN_3 and "fish3" not in done:
        done.add("fish3")
        STATE.walk_terminal_status = "ALL RIGS OFFLINE — OIL FIELD LOST"
        _trigger_walk_dialogue(
            "OLD FISHERMAN",
            "Third time this month.\n"
            "KRAP watches until the oil stops moving.",
        )
    if t >= ATK_FISHERMAN_4 and "fish4" not in done:
        done.add("fish4")
        _trigger_walk_dialogue(
            "OLD FISHERMAN",
            "You're new.\nYou'll learn.",
        )
    # Extra human reactions; timed right after visible events, not before them.
    if t >= ATK_IMPACT_CENTER + 34 and "react_center" not in done:
        done.add("react_center")
        _trigger_walk_dialogue(
            "OLD FISHERMAN",
            "There. Center rig's folding.",
        )
    if t >= ATK_IMPACT_LEFT + 35 and "react_left" not in done:
        done.add("react_left")
        _trigger_walk_dialogue(
            "OLD FISHERMAN",
            "Left rig's falling into the sea.",
        )
    if t >= ATK_IMPACT_RIGHT + 50 and "react_right" not in done:
        done.add("react_right")
        _trigger_walk_dialogue(
            "OLD FISHERMAN",
            "Now the last one burns.\nThree fires on the horizon.",
        )
    # Delayed sound layers: launch -> impact -> collapse -> steam/fire spread.
    for _idx, _impact in enumerate(RIG_IMPACT_FRAMES):
        if t >= _impact + 36 and f"collapse_sound_{_idx}" not in done:
            done.add(f"collapse_sound_{_idx}")
            STATE.walk_terminal_status = f"STRUCTURAL COLLAPSE — {RIG_NAMES[_idx]}"
            audio.play("rig_metal_collapse", volume=0.24)
            audio.schedule(10, "terminal_glitch", volume=0.14)
            STATE.screen_shake = max(STATE.screen_shake, 22 - _idx * 2)
        if t >= _impact + 68 and f"water_sound_{_idx}" not in done:
            done.add(f"water_sound_{_idx}")
            STATE.walk_terminal_status = f"FIRE SPREAD / STEAM PLUME — {RIG_NAMES[_idx]}"
            audio.play("rig_water_splash", volume=0.22)
            audio.schedule(8, "rig_fire_burst", volume=0.16)
            audio.schedule(4, "terminal_glitch", volume=0.12)
    # ── Boat runs left-to-right, then accelerates out of frame ──
    if STATE.walk_boat_active:
        if t < ATK_TARGET_CENTER:
            boat_speed = 0.32
        elif t < ATK_IMPACT_RIGHT + 120:
            boat_speed = 0.60
        elif t < ATK_FISHERMAN_4:
            boat_speed = 0.78
        else:
            boat_speed = 1.55
        STATE.walk_boat_x += boat_speed
    # ── Advance missile and explosion lifetimes ─────────────────
    for m in STATE.walk_missiles[:]:
        m["t"] = min(1.0, m["t"] + m["speed"])
        if m["t"] >= 1.0:
            STATE.walk_missiles.remove(m)
    for ex in STATE.walk_explosions[:]:
        ex["life"] -= 1
        if ex["life"] <= 0:
            STATE.walk_explosions.remove(ex)
    # ── End cutscene ────────────────────────────────────────────
    if t >= ATK_END and "end" not in done:
        done.add("end")
        STATE.walk_terminal_status = "SIGNAL LOST — RETURNING TO HARBOR"
        clear_walk_dialogue()
        STATE.walk_attack_active = False
        STATE.walk_terminal_active = False
        global walk_after_firefight, walk_return_timer
        walk_after_firefight = True
        walk_return_timer = 240
        STATE.walk_after_firefight = True
def _process_rig_strike(t, done, prefix, rig_index,
                        target_frame, launch_frame, impact_frame):
    """One full TARGETED → LAUNCH → DESTROYED sequence for a single rig.
    Uses RIG_X_POSITIONS as the single source of truth so the missile
    physically aims at the rig you can actually see.
    """
    rig_x    = RIG_X_POSITIONS[rig_index]
    rig_name = RIG_NAMES[rig_index]
    # TARGETED
    if t >= target_frame and f"{prefix}_target" not in done:
        done.add(f"{prefix}_target")
        STATE.rig_status[rig_index] = "TARGETED"
        STATE.walk_terminal_status = f"TARGET LOCK: {rig_name}"
        audio.play("menu_hover", volume=0.45)
    # LAUNCH — missile fires from current boat position
    if t >= launch_frame and f"{prefix}_launch" not in done:
        done.add(f"{prefix}_launch")
        STATE.walk_missiles.append({
            "start_x": float(STATE.walk_boat_x + 50),
            "start_y": float(STATE.walk_boat_y - 2),
            "tx":      float(rig_x),
            "ty":      float(HORIZON_Y_OVERLOOK - 70),
            "t":       0.0,
            "speed":   1.0 / max(1, impact_frame - launch_frame),
            "rig_index": rig_index,
        })
        STATE.walk_terminal_status = f"MISSILE INBOUND — {rig_name}"
        audio.play("missile_launch", volume=0.42)
        audio.schedule(8, "missile_flyby", volume=0.30)
        audio.schedule(2, "terminal_glitch", volume=0.16)
    # IMPACT
    if t >= impact_frame and f"{prefix}_impact" not in done:
        done.add(f"{prefix}_impact")
        STATE.rig_status[rig_index] = "DESTROYED"
        _life = 190 if rig_index == 2 else 155
        STATE.walk_explosions.append({
            "x":        float(rig_x),
            "y":        float(HORIZON_Y_OVERLOOK - 60),
            "life":     _life,
            "max_life": _life,
            "rig_index": rig_index,
        })
        STATE.walk_terminal_status = f"*** IMPACT — {rig_name} DESTROYED ***"
        audio.duck_ambience(frames=150, factor=0.25)
        audio.play("rig_impact_crack", volume=0.62)
        audio.schedule(4, "rig_low_boom", volume=0.82)
        audio.schedule(13, "rig_secondary_explosion", volume=0.42)
        audio.schedule(34, "rig_metal_collapse", volume=0.35)
        audio.schedule(56, "rig_water_splash", volume=0.28)
        audio.schedule(72, "rig_fire_burst", volume=0.20)
        audio.schedule(2, "terminal_glitch", volume=0.24)
        audio.schedule(26, "terminal_glitch", volume=0.14)
        STATE.screen_shake = 34 + rig_index * 3
def _trigger_walk_dialogue(speaker, text):
    """Show a dialogue line during the cutscene."""
    STATE.walk_dialogue_active = True
    STATE.walk_dialogue_speaker = speaker
    STATE.walk_dialogue_text = text
    STATE.walk_dialogue_char_idx = 0
    STATE.walk_dialogue_timer = 0
# ─── Attack overlook panorama helpers ────────────────────────────────
ATTACK_VANISH_X = WIDTH // 2 + 22
ATTACK_VANISH_Y = HORIZON_Y_OVERLOOK - 6
ATTACK_RIG_LAYOUT = (
    {"world_x": -250.0, "depth": 0.76, "tilt": -1.0},
    {"world_x":    0.0, "depth": 0.46, "tilt":  0.0},
    {"world_x":  250.0, "depth": 0.72, "tilt":  1.0},
)
def _attack_depth_scale(depth):
    near = 1.0 - depth
    return 0.82 + near * 1.28
def _attack_rig_pose(index, elapsed):
    layout = ATTACK_RIG_LAYOUT[index]
    depth = layout["depth"]
    near = 1.0 - depth
    scale = _attack_depth_scale(depth)
    x = ATTACK_VANISH_X + layout["world_x"] * (0.56 + depth * 0.34)
    water_y = (
        HORIZON_Y_OVERLOOK
        + 12
        + near * 76
        + math.sin(elapsed * 0.014 + index * 0.65) * (1.5 + near * 1.5)
    )
    deck_y = water_y - int(105 * scale)
    return {
        "x": x,
        "water_y": water_y,
        "deck_y": deck_y,
        "scale": scale,
        "depth": depth,
        "tilt": layout["tilt"],
    }
def _attack_boat_pose_from_world_x(world_x, elapsed=0):
    x = -170 + world_x * 1.65
    travel = max(0.0, min(1.25, (x + 170.0) / (WIDTH + 380.0)))
    depth = max(0.50, 0.90 - min(1.0, travel) * 0.26)
    near = 1.0 - depth
    scale = 0.76 + near * 1.08
    y = (
        HORIZON_Y_OVERLOOK
        + 18
        + near * 84
        + math.sin(elapsed * 0.035 + travel * 4.0) * (2 + near * 2)
    )
    heading = -0.12 + min(1.0, travel) * 0.14
    return {"x": x, "y": y, "scale": scale, "depth": depth, "heading": heading}
def _draw_attack_explosion(surface, cx, cy, t, scale=1.0):
    """Big cinematic oil-rig impact: flash, shockwave, fireball, smoke, debris and spray."""
    if t < 0:
        return
    s = max(0.35, float(scale))
    t = float(t)
    early = max(0.0, 1.0 - t / 22.0)
    burn = max(0.0, 1.0 - t / 135.0)
    grow = min(1.0, t / 50.0)
    # Sudden hot impact flash and expanding blast ring.
    if t < 30:
        flash_w = int(300 * s)
        flash_h = int(190 * s)
        flash = pygame.Surface((flash_w, flash_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        fw, fh = flash.get_size()
        center = (fw // 2, int(fh * 0.58))
        for rad, alpha, col in [
            (int((28 + t * 2.2) * s), int(235 * early), (255, 248, 212)),
            (int((58 + t * 3.0) * s), int(150 * early), (255, 178, 72)),
            (int((96 + t * 4.5) * s), int(70 * early), (255, 70, 24)),
        ]:
            if rad > 2 and alpha > 0:
                pygame.draw.circle(flash, (*col, alpha), center, rad)
        ring_rect = pygame.Rect(0, 0, int((78 + t * 7.0) * s), int((18 + t * 1.8) * s))
        ring_rect.center = (center[0], center[1] + int(22 * s))
        ring_alpha = int(170 * max(0.0, 1.0 - t / 30.0))
        pygame.draw.ellipse(flash, (255, 214, 135, ring_alpha), ring_rect, max(1, int(2 * s)))
        surface.blit(flash, (int(cx - fw // 2), int(cy - fh * 0.70)))
    # Main fireball / black smoke column.
    plume_w = max(80, int((190 + grow * 90) * s))
    plume_h = max(120, int((210 + grow * 110) * s))
    plume = pygame.Surface((plume_w, plume_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pw, ph = plume.get_size()
    base_x = pw // 2
    base_y = ph - int(48 * s)
    # Core roiling fireballs. Deterministic per frame so it flickers without teleporting wildly.
    for i in range(24):
        frac = i / 23.0
        phase = t * (0.052 + frac * 0.018) + i * 1.73
        column_spread = (13 + frac * 44 + grow * 18) * s
        ox = math.sin(phase) * column_spread + math.sin(phase * 0.41) * 8 * s
        oy = (frac * (118 + grow * 85) + math.sin(phase * 0.8) * 8) * s
        rr = max(4, int((21 - frac * 8 + early * 9 + math.sin(phase) * 2.0) * s))
        heat = max(0.0, 1.0 - frac * 0.88)
        alpha = int((215 * burn + 55 * early) * (1.0 - frac * 0.35))
        if alpha <= 0:
            continue
        col = (
            255,
            int(92 + 140 * heat),
            int(24 + 94 * heat),
            max(0, min(255, alpha)),
        )
        pos = (int(base_x + ox), int(base_y - oy))
        pygame.draw.circle(plume, col, pos, rr)
        if i < 9 and t < 58:
            pygame.draw.circle(plume, (255, 245, 198, int(125 * max(0.0, 1.0 - t / 70.0))), pos, max(2, rr // 2))
    # Secondary fuel pops, offset from the main blast.
    for pop_i, pop_t in enumerate((13, 27, 44, 67)):
        dt = t - pop_t
        if 0 <= dt < 20:
            pop_alpha = int(180 * (1.0 - dt / 20.0))
            off = (-42, 36, -18, 50)[pop_i] * s
            py = base_y - (26 + pop_i * 18 + dt * 1.8) * s
            for r, col in [(22 + dt * 1.5, (255, 230, 175)), (38 + dt * 2.2, (255, 105, 36))]:
                pygame.draw.circle(plume, (*col, max(0, pop_alpha // (1 if r < 30 else 2))), (int(base_x + off), int(py)), max(3, int(r * s)))
    # Thick rolling smoke, rising above the fire.
    if t > 8:
        smoke_age = min(1.0, (t - 8) / 100.0)
        for i in range(22):
            frac = i / 21.0
            phase = i * 1.21 + t * 0.027
            sx = base_x + int(math.sin(phase) * (18 + frac * 62) * s + math.sin(phase * 0.33) * 18 * s)
            sy = base_y - int((58 + frac * (110 + smoke_age * 125) + smoke_age * 38) * s)
            sr = max(8, int((18 + frac * 24 + smoke_age * 15) * s))
            shade = int(36 + frac * 22)
            sa = max(0, int((120 - frac * 42) * burn + 28 * smoke_age))
            pygame.draw.circle(plume, (shade, shade - 4, shade - 2, min(150, sa)), (sx, sy), sr)
    # Sparks and debris arcing out from the impact.
    rng = random.Random(int(cx * 13 + cy * 7))
    for i in range(34):
        seed_ang = -math.pi * 0.94 + (math.pi * 1.88) * (i / 33.0)
        ang = seed_ang + math.sin(i * 2.31) * 0.24
        speed = (2.8 + rng.random() * 5.2) * s
        age = t - rng.randint(0, 10)
        if age < 0 or age > 78:
            continue
        dx = math.cos(ang) * speed * age
        dy = math.sin(ang) * speed * age + 0.055 * age * age * s
        px = base_x + dx
        py = base_y + dy - 20 * s
        if 0 <= px < pw and 0 <= py < ph:
            hot = max(0, 1.0 - age / 55.0)
            col = (255, int(118 + 82 * hot), 48, int(190 * hot)) if i % 3 else (78, 80, 84, int(170 * max(0, 1 - age / 78)))
            pygame.draw.circle(plume, col, (int(px), int(py)), max(1, int((2.6 if i % 3 else 3.6) * s * max(0.55, hot))))
            if hot > 0.25:
                tx = int(px - math.cos(ang) * 7 * s)
                ty = int(py - math.sin(ang) * 7 * s)
                pygame.draw.line(plume, (255, 150, 56, int(110 * hot)), (int(px), int(py)), (tx, ty), max(1, int(1.5 * s)))
    surface.blit(plume, (int(cx - pw // 2), int(cy - ph + 44 * s)))
    # Water impact: white-hot steam, vertical spray, oily orange reflection.
    if t < 70:
        splash_w = int(260 * s)
        splash_h = int(88 * s)
        splash = pygame.Surface((splash_w, splash_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        sw, sh = splash.get_size()
        fade = max(0.0, 1.0 - t / 70.0)
        for i in range(5):
            rect = pygame.Rect(0, 0, int((70 + t * (4.2 + i * 0.8)) * s), int((12 + t * 0.45 + i * 2) * s))
            rect.center = (sw // 2, sh - int((22 - i * 2) * s))
            pygame.draw.ellipse(splash, (190, 220, 240, int((120 - i * 16) * fade)), rect, 1)
        for i in range(20):
            frac = i / 19.0
            side = -1 if i < 10 else 1
            local = frac if side > 0 else 1.0 - frac
            px = sw // 2 + side * int((14 + local * 88 + t * 0.55) * s)
            py = sh - int((22 + math.sin(local * math.pi) * (28 + t * 0.28)) * s)
            pygame.draw.circle(splash, (205, 226, 238, int(145 * fade)), (px, py), max(1, int((3 - local * 1.4) * s)))
        for i in range(8):
            alpha = int((80 - i * 7) * fade)
            pygame.draw.line(splash, (255, 122, 42, alpha), (sw // 2 - int((34 - i * 2) * s), sh - int((18 - i) * s)), (sw // 2 + int((34 - i * 2) * s), sh - int((18 - i) * s)), 1)
        surface.blit(splash, (int(cx - sw // 2), int(cy + 4 * s)))
def _draw_attack_tracer(surface, x1, y1, x2, y2, alpha):
    """Draw a soft tracer line with a dim halo."""
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (200, 180, 120, alpha), (x1, y1), (x2, y2), 2)
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (255, 240, 180, alpha // 2), (x1, y1), (x2, y2), 4)
def _draw_attack_rocket(surface, x, y, angle, scale, glow_phase=0.0):
    """A tiny anti-ship rocket with exhaust and a hot nose cone."""
    body_len = max(8, int(11 * scale))
    tail_len = max(5, int(7 * scale))
    for i in range(8):
        dist = i * (3.2 + scale)
        ex = x - math.cos(angle) * dist
        ey = y - math.sin(angle) * dist
        ex += math.sin(glow_phase + i * 0.6) * 0.8
        alpha = max(0, 185 - i * 22)
        r = max(1, int(4 * scale) - i // 3)
        trail = pygame.Surface((r * 4, r * 4), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.circle(trail, (255, 165, 65, alpha), (r * 2, r * 2), r)
        surface.blit(trail, (int(ex - r * 2), int(ey - r * 2)))
    tip = (int(x + math.cos(angle) * body_len), int(y + math.sin(angle) * body_len))
    tail = (int(x - math.cos(angle) * tail_len), int(y - math.sin(angle) * tail_len))
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (205, 208, 216), tip, tail, max(2, int(3 * scale)))
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (255, 235, 190), tip, max(1, int(2 * scale)))
def _draw_attack_boat(surface, bx, by, scale, heading, elapsed):
    """Small fast-attack craft in a 3/4 view so it feels less flat."""
    s = scale
    hull_dark = (28, 33, 42)
    hull_mid = (45, 52, 64)
    hull_hi = (74, 84, 96)
    wake_alpha = int(55 + (1.0 - min(1.0, s / 1.6)) * 25)
    hull = [
        (bx - 38 * s, by + 10 * s),
        (bx + 8 * s, by + 4 * s),
        (bx + 42 * s, by + 10 * s),
        (bx + 28 * s, by + 20 * s),
        (bx - 28 * s, by + 20 * s),
    ]
    pygame.draw.polygon(surface, hull_dark, hull)
    pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (18, 22, 28), hull, 1)
    deck = [
        (bx - 18 * s, by + 4 * s),
        (bx + 14 * s, by - 1 * s),
        (bx + 27 * s, by + 7 * s),
        (bx - 8 * s, by + 11 * s),
    ]
    pygame.draw.polygon(surface, hull_mid, deck)
    cabin = pygame.Rect(int(bx - 6 * s), int(by - 10 * s), int(24 * s), int(13 * s))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, hull_mid, cabin, border_radius=max(1, int(2 * s)))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, hull_hi, cabin, 1, border_radius=max(1, int(2 * s)))
    win = pygame.Rect(int(bx + 3 * s), int(by - 7 * s), int(10 * s), int(5 * s))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (28, 42, 58), win, border_radius=1)
    mast_x = int(bx + 8 * s)
    mast_y = int(by - 10 * s)
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (45, 50, 60), (mast_x, mast_y), (mast_x + int(2 * s), mast_y - int(13 * s)), 1)
    if elapsed // 14 % 2 == 0:
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (255, 95, 70), (mast_x + int(2 * s), mast_y - int(13 * s)), max(1, int(2 * s)))
    nose_flash = False
    for launch in (ATK_MISSILE_1_LAUNCH, ATK_MISSILE_2_LAUNCH, ATK_MISSILE_3_LAUNCH):
        if launch <= elapsed < launch + 9:
            nose_flash = True
            break
    if nose_flash:
        flash_pos = (int(bx + 36 * s), int(by + 8 * s))
        for r in (int(10 * s), int(6 * s), int(3 * s)):
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, (255, 220, 150, 180), flash_pos, max(2, r))
    for i in range(6):
        ww = int((30 - i * 4) * s)
        wake = pygame.Surface((max(8, ww), max(2, int(4 * s))), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.ellipse(wake, (115, 150, 190, max(10, wake_alpha - i * 8)), wake.get_rect())
        wx = int(bx - (55 + i * 12) * s)
        wy = int(by + (10 + math.sin(elapsed * 0.05 + i * 0.7) * 2) * s)
        surface.blit(wake, (wx, wy))
def _draw_attack_helicopter(surface, elapsed):
    """A very distant helicopter silhouette crossing the horizon."""
    if elapsed < ATK_FISHERMAN_2:
        return
    heli_t = (elapsed - ATK_FISHERMAN_2) / 140.0
    if heli_t > 1.0:
        return
    hx = int(WIDTH * 0.68 + heli_t * 210)
    hy = int(SCENE_TOP + 24 - heli_t * 34)
    alpha = int(96 * (1.0 - heli_t * 0.55))
    body = pygame.Surface((46, 12), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.ellipse(body, (80, 86, 98, alpha), body.get_rect())
    surface.blit(body, (hx, hy))
    rotor_angle = elapsed * 0.32
    rotor_len = 32
    rx = hx + 23
    ry = hy + 2
    pygame.draw.line(
        surface,
        (60, 66, 78, alpha),
        (rx + math.cos(rotor_angle) * rotor_len, ry + math.sin(rotor_angle) * rotor_len),
        (rx - math.cos(rotor_angle) * rotor_len, ry - math.sin(rotor_angle) * rotor_len),
        2,
    )
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (66, 72, 84, alpha), (hx + 34, hy + 6), (hx + 42, hy + 6), 2)
def _draw_attack_soft_rain(surface, elapsed):
    """Multi-layer rain with depth variation instead of one flat scratch layer."""
    rain = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    scene_h = SCENE_BOT - SCENE_TOP
    layer_specs = [
        (36, 1.2, 14, (120, 135, 165)),
        (56, 1.8, 18, (130, 150, 182)),
        (42, 2.5, 26, (145, 165, 198)),
    ]
    for layer_idx, (count, drift, length, base_col) in enumerate(layer_specs):
        rng_r = random.Random(47 + layer_idx)
        for _ in range(count):
            base_x = rng_r.randint(0, WIDTH)
            base_y = rng_r.randint(0, scene_h)
            rx = (base_x + int(elapsed * drift)) % WIDTH
            ry = SCENE_TOP + ((base_y + int(elapsed * (4.0 + layer_idx * 1.3))) % scene_h)
            alpha = 22 + layer_idx * 14 + rng_r.randint(0, 18)
            slant = 4 + layer_idx * 2
            pygame.draw.line(
                rain,
                (*base_col, alpha),
                (rx, ry),
                (rx - slant, ry + length + rng_r.randint(-3, 3)),
                1,
            )
    surface.blit(rain, (0, 0))
def _draw_attack_water(surface, elapsed, horizon_y):
    """Perspective-biased ocean with converging guide-lines and layered chop."""
    w, h = surface.get_size()
    for y in range(horizon_y, h):
        p = (y - horizon_y) / max(1, h - horizon_y)
        r = int(10 + p * 12)
        g = int(20 + p * 16)
        b = int(34 + p * 24)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (r, g, b), (0, y), (w, y))
    grid = pygame.Surface((w, h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    for lane in range(-8, 9):
        x0 = ATTACK_VANISH_X + lane * 30
        x1 = ATTACK_VANISH_X + lane * 112
        pygame.draw.line(grid, (28, 46, 72, 22), (x0, horizon_y + 6), (x1, h - 34), 1)
    for band in range(15):
        frac = band / 14.0
        y = horizon_y + int((frac ** 1.86) * (h - horizon_y - 34))
        alpha = int(58 * (1.0 - frac * 0.55))
        pygame.draw.line(grid, (56, 84, 122, alpha), (0, y), (w, y), 1)
    surface.blit(grid, (0, 0))
    wave_layer = pygame.Surface((w, h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    for band in range(12):
        frac = band / 11.0
        y = horizon_y + 12 + int((frac ** 1.75) * (h - horizon_y - 90))
        seg_w = 14 + int(frac * 28)
        alpha = int(60 - frac * 22)
        step = 34 + int(frac * 28)
        for wx in range(-50, w + 50, step):
            wave_y = y + math.sin(elapsed * 0.028 + wx * 0.03 + band) * (1.2 + frac * 3.2)
            pygame.draw.line(
                wave_layer,
                (64, 98, 136, alpha),
                (wx, int(wave_y)),
                (wx + seg_w, int(wave_y + 1)),
                1,
            )
    surface.blit(wave_layer, (0, 0))
def _draw_attack_perspective_rig(surface, cx, water_y, scale, elapsed, state="ONLINE", tilt=0.0):
    """Pseudo-3D rig with visible top/front faces and converging support legs."""
    s = scale
    cx = float(cx)
    water_y = float(water_y)
    deck_w = 150 * s
    deck_h = max(8, int(18 * s))
    deck_d = 28 * s
    deck_y = water_y - 104 * s
    x_skew = tilt * 6 * s
    top_back_left = (cx - deck_w * 0.48 - x_skew, deck_y - deck_d * 0.08)
    top_back_right = (cx + deck_w * 0.18 - x_skew, deck_y - deck_d * 0.46)
    top_front_right = (cx + deck_w * 0.54 + x_skew, deck_y - deck_d * 0.10)
    top_front_left = (cx - deck_w * 0.14 + x_skew, deck_y + deck_d * 0.24)
    front_drop = deck_h
    leg_col = (44, 48, 58)
    side_col = (38, 42, 50)
    top_col = (68, 75, 88)
    underside_col = (24, 28, 36)
    refl = pygame.Surface((int(deck_w * 1.55), int(120 * s)), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    refl_w = refl.get_width()
    refl_h = refl.get_height()
    for i in range(9):
        frac = i / 8.0
        ry = int(8 + frac * (refl_h - 20))
        rw = int(deck_w * (0.44 - frac * 0.18))
        alpha = int(30 * (1.0 - frac))
        wobble = int(math.sin(elapsed * 0.035 + i + cx * 0.01) * (4 + frac * 7))
        pygame.draw.line(
            refl,
            (46, 70, 102, alpha),
            (refl_w // 2 - rw // 2 + wobble, ry),
            (refl_w // 2 + rw // 2 + wobble, ry),
            1,
        )
    surface.blit(refl, (int(cx - refl_w // 2), int(water_y + 4)))
    pygame.draw.polygon(surface, underside_col, [
        top_front_left,
        top_front_right,
        (top_front_right[0], top_front_right[1] + front_drop),
        (top_front_left[0], top_front_left[1] + front_drop),
    ])
    pygame.draw.polygon(surface, side_col, [
        top_back_right,
        top_front_right,
        (top_front_right[0], top_front_right[1] + front_drop),
        (top_back_right[0], top_back_right[1] + front_drop),
    ])
    pygame.draw.polygon(surface, top_col, [
        top_back_left, top_back_right, top_front_right, top_front_left
    ])
    pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (28, 34, 40), [
        top_back_left, top_back_right, top_front_right, top_front_left
    ], 1)
    leg_top_xs = [
        cx - deck_w * 0.30,
        cx - deck_w * 0.08,
        cx + deck_w * 0.17,
        cx + deck_w * 0.39,
    ]
    leg_bottom_xs = [
        cx - deck_w * 0.41,
        cx - deck_w * 0.15,
        cx + deck_w * 0.18,
        cx + deck_w * 0.46,
    ]
    leg_tops = [
        top_front_left[1] + front_drop - 2,
        top_front_left[1] + front_drop - 8,
        top_front_right[1] + front_drop - 10,
        top_front_right[1] + front_drop - 2,
    ]
    for tx, bx, ty in zip(leg_top_xs, leg_bottom_xs, leg_tops):
        leg_w = 5 * s
        leg_poly = [
            (tx - leg_w * 0.55, ty),
            (tx + leg_w * 0.55, ty),
            (bx + leg_w * 0.85, water_y + 28 * s),
            (bx - leg_w * 0.85, water_y + 28 * s),
        ]
        pygame.draw.polygon(surface, leg_col, leg_poly)
        pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (28, 30, 36), leg_poly, 1)
    mast_base_x = cx + x_skew * 0.35
    mast_base_y = deck_y - 5 * s
    mast_top_y = mast_base_y - 72 * s
    mast_left = mast_base_x - 18 * s
    mast_right = mast_base_x + 18 * s
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (66, 74, 88), (mast_left, mast_base_y), (mast_base_x - 3 * s, mast_top_y), max(2, int(3 * s)))
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (66, 74, 88), (mast_right, mast_base_y), (mast_base_x + 3 * s, mast_top_y), max(2, int(3 * s)))
    for i in range(4):
        frac = i / 3.0
        ly = mast_base_y - frac * 62 * s
        half_w = (14 - i * 3) * s
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (72, 82, 98), (mast_base_x - half_w, ly), (mast_base_x + half_w, ly), max(1, int(2 * s)))
    pipe_bottom = water_y + 12 * s
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (44, 58, 74), (mast_base_x, mast_base_y + 4 * s), (mast_base_x, pipe_bottom), max(1, int(2 * s)))
    ring_y = mast_top_y + 8 * s
    pygame.draw.ellipse(
        surface,
        (82, 150, 210),
        pygame.Rect(int(mast_base_x - 17 * s), int(ring_y - 12 * s), int(34 * s), int(26 * s)),
        1,
    )
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (82, 150, 210), (mast_base_x - 22 * s, ring_y), (mast_base_x + 22 * s, ring_y), 1)
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (82, 150, 210), (mast_base_x, ring_y - 18 * s), (mast_base_x, ring_y + 18 * s), 1)
    beacon_phase = elapsed // 18 % 2 == 0
    if beacon_phase:
        glow = pygame.Surface((int(34 * s), int(34 * s)), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.circle(glow, (255, 78, 62, 85), (glow.get_width() // 2, glow.get_height() // 2), max(5, int(15 * s)))
        surface.blit(glow, (int(mast_base_x - glow.get_width() // 2), int(mast_top_y - glow.get_height() // 2)))
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (255, 92, 74), (int(mast_base_x), int(mast_top_y)), max(2, int(3 * s)))
    deck_light_y = top_front_left[1] + front_drop * 0.45
    for li in range(4):
        lx = cx - deck_w * 0.22 + li * deck_w * 0.16
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (255, 208, 118), (int(lx), int(deck_light_y)), max(1, int(2 * s)))
    if state == "TARGETED":
        lock = pygame.Surface((int(88 * s), int(88 * s)), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        lc = (255, 205, 70, 70)
        pygame.draw.ellipse(lock, lc, lock.get_rect(), 1)
        pygame.draw.line(lock, lc, (lock.get_width() // 2, 0), (lock.get_width() // 2, lock.get_height()), 1)
        pygame.draw.line(lock, lc, (0, lock.get_height() // 2), (lock.get_width(), lock.get_height() // 2), 1)
        surface.blit(lock, (int(mast_base_x - lock.get_width() // 2), int(deck_y - 26 * s)))
def _draw_destroyed_rig(surface, cx, water_y, scale, elapsed_since_impact, tilt=0.0, rig_index=None):
    """Three different rig deaths.
    LEFT   rig: side-slam collapse, it folds hard into the sea.
    CENTER rig: vertical implosion, legs fold inward and the deck pancakes down.
    RIGHT  rig: twisting break-up, the upper deck tears in two and corkscrews down.
    """
    if elapsed_since_impact < 0:
        return False
    s = max(0.35, float(scale))
    ix = float(cx)
    waterline = float(water_y)
    t = float(max(0, elapsed_since_impact))
    if rig_index is None:
        if tilt < -0.15:
            style = 0      # left / side-slam
        elif tilt > 0.15:
            style = 2      # right / twist-break
        else:
            style = 1      # center / implosion
    else:
        style = int(rig_index) % 3
    # Style tuning. These few values make the three silhouettes behave differently.
    if style == 0:          # LEFT: slow heavy fall to the left
        fall_sign = -1.0
        collapse_delay = 3.0
        collapse_len = 78.0
        sink_start = 92.0
        twist_mult = 1.05
        smoke_drift = -1.0
        deck_mode = "side"
    elif style == 1:        # CENTER: fast straight-down implosion
        fall_sign = 0.0
        collapse_delay = 0.0
        collapse_len = 48.0
        sink_start = 64.0
        twist_mult = 0.22
        smoke_drift = 0.0
        deck_mode = "implode"
    else:                   # RIGHT: delayed twisting break-up to the right
        fall_sign = 1.0
        collapse_delay = 10.0
        collapse_len = 92.0
        sink_start = 105.0
        twist_mult = 1.45
        smoke_drift = 1.0
        deck_mode = "twist"
    buckle = min(1.0, t / 34.0)
    collapse = min(1.0, max(0.0, (t - collapse_delay) / collapse_len))
    sink = min(1.0, max(0.0, (t - sink_start) / 165.0))
    burn = max(0.34, 1.0 - t / 520.0)
    pulse = 0.72 + 0.28 * math.sin(t * 0.105 + ix * 0.01 + style)
    # ── Oil slick and fiery reflections ───────────────────────────────
    slick_w = int((105 + min(210, t * (1.15 + 0.22 * style))) * s)
    slick_h = int((34 + min(46, t * (0.24 + 0.03 * style))) * s)
    oil = pygame.Surface((slick_w, slick_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.ellipse(oil, (4, 6, 9, min(180, int(95 + t * 0.42))), oil.get_rect())
    if style == 1:
        inner = pygame.Rect(int(slick_w * 0.22), int(slick_h * 0.18), int(slick_w * 0.56), int(slick_h * 0.50))
    elif style == 0:
        inner = pygame.Rect(int(slick_w * 0.08), int(slick_h * 0.26), int(slick_w * 0.68), int(slick_h * 0.34))
    else:
        inner = pygame.Rect(int(slick_w * 0.25), int(slick_h * 0.23), int(slick_w * 0.67), int(slick_h * 0.36))
    pygame.draw.ellipse(oil, (44, 25, 15, int(54 * burn)), inner)
    for i in range(6):
        y = int(slick_h * (0.35 + i * 0.075))
        wob = int(math.sin(t * 0.04 + i + style) * 7 * s)
        side_pull = int(smoke_drift * i * 4 * s)
        pygame.draw.line(
            oil,
            (255, 102, 30, int(24 * burn)),
            (slick_w // 2 - int(42 * s) + wob + side_pull, y),
            (slick_w // 2 + int(42 * s) + wob + side_pull, y),
            1,
        )
    surface.blit(oil, (int(ix - slick_w // 2), int(waterline - 4 * s)))
    # ── Different water hits/ripples ──────────────────────────────────
    if t < 155:
        ripple = pygame.Surface((int(280 * s), int(96 * s)), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        rw, rh = ripple.get_size()
        for i in range(4):
            age = max(0.0, t - i * (12.0 if style == 1 else 16.0))
            if age <= 0:
                continue
            alpha = int(100 * max(0.0, 1.0 - age / 155.0))
            if style == 1:
                wmul, hmul, offx = 2.5, 0.42, 0
            elif style == 0:
                wmul, hmul, offx = 2.25, 0.34, -i * 12 * s
            else:
                wmul, hmul, offx = 2.8, 0.48, i * 13 * s
            rect = pygame.Rect(0, 0, int((58 + age * wmul) * s), int((10 + age * hmul) * s))
            rect.center = (rw // 2 + int(offx), rh // 2 + int(10 * s))
            pygame.draw.ellipse(ripple, (126, 170, 205, alpha), rect, 1)
        surface.blit(ripple, (int(ix - rw // 2), int(waterline - rh // 2 + 16 * s)))
    deck_start_y = waterline - 104 * s
    # ── Collapsing legs and deck ──────────────────────────────────────
    if collapse < 0.985:
        if deck_mode == "implode":
            # Center rig: legs fold inward and deck drops almost vertically.
            deck_y = deck_start_y + (waterline - 16 * s - deck_start_y) * (collapse ** 0.78)
            deck_w = (150 - 74 * collapse) * s
            deck_d = (30 + 14 * collapse) * s
            twist = math.sin(t * 0.16) * 7 * s
            leg_roots = [ix - 62 * s, ix - 24 * s, ix + 22 * s, ix + 62 * s]
            for li, root_x in enumerate(leg_roots):
                side = -1 if root_x < ix else 1
                top_x = ix + (-56 + li * 37) * s * (1.0 - 0.42 * collapse)
                top_y = deck_y + (12 + li * 1.4) * s
                knee_x = ix + side * (18 + li % 2 * 10) * s * (1.0 - 0.55 * buckle)
                knee_y = waterline - (50 - li * 5) * s + collapse * 62 * s
                pygame.draw.line(surface, (31, 34, 41), (int(top_x), int(top_y)), (int(knee_x), int(knee_y)), max(1, int(4 * s)))
                pygame.draw.line(surface, (25, 28, 35), (int(knee_x), int(knee_y)), (int(root_x), int(waterline + 20 * s)), max(1, int(3 * s)))
            # Two deck halves collapsing toward the middle, with a visible crack.
            for half, side in enumerate((-1, 1)):
                half_w = deck_w * 0.52
                center_x = ix + side * deck_w * 0.20 * (1.0 - collapse)
                dip = (12 + 28 * collapse) * s
                pts = [
                    (center_x - side * half_w * 0.78, deck_y - deck_d * 0.22),
                    (ix + side * 6 * s + twist, deck_y - deck_d * 0.48 + dip),
                    (ix + side * 12 * s + twist, deck_y + deck_d * 0.35 + dip),
                    (center_x - side * half_w * 0.25, deck_y + deck_d * 0.48),
                ]
                pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (27, 30, 37), pts)
                pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (78, 80, 86), pts, 1)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (255, 126, 40, int(120 * burn)), (int(ix), int(deck_y - 20 * s)), (int(ix), int(deck_y + 36 * s)), max(1, int(2 * s)))
            # Mast crumples downward into the hole.
            mast_base = (ix + twist, deck_y - 8 * s)
            mast_tip = (ix + math.sin(t * 0.08) * 18 * s, deck_y - (82 - 72 * collapse) * s)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (44, 48, 56), (int(mast_base[0]), int(mast_base[1])), (int(mast_tip[0]), int(mast_tip[1])), max(1, int(5 * s)))
        else:
            # Left/right rigs: side topples, but right twists harder and tears apart.
            deck_y = deck_start_y + (waterline - 18 * s - deck_start_y) * (collapse ** (1.22 if style == 0 else 1.04))
            deck_w = (148 - (24 if style == 0 else 42) * collapse) * s
            deck_d = (30 - 4 * collapse) * s
            twist = fall_sign * (10 + (72 if style == 0 else 108) * collapse + math.sin(t * 0.09) * 12) * s * twist_mult
            split_gap = (0 if style == 0 else 28 * collapse * s)
            leg_roots = [ix - 62 * s, ix - 25 * s, ix + 18 * s, ix + 58 * s]
            for li, root_x in enumerate(leg_roots):
                top_x = ix + (-56 + li * 35) * s + twist * (0.16 + li * 0.09)
                top_y = deck_y + (12 + li * 1.4) * s
                knee_x = (top_x + root_x) * 0.5 + fall_sign * (12 + li * 6) * buckle * s
