                knee_y = waterline - (42 - li * 4) * s + collapse * (52 if style == 0 else 44) * s
                col = (32, 35, 42) if li % 2 else (25, 28, 35)
                pygame.draw.line(surface, col, (int(top_x), int(top_y)), (int(knee_x), int(knee_y)), max(1, int(4 * s)))
                pygame.draw.line(surface, col, (int(knee_x), int(knee_y)), (int(root_x), int(waterline + 20 * s)), max(1, int(3 * s)))
                if 12 < t < 50 and li in (1, 2):
                    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (255, 166, 58), (int(knee_x), int(knee_y)), max(2, int(3 * s)))
            if style == 2:
                # Right rig: two separate slabs rip apart.
                for chunk, ch_side in enumerate((-1, 1)):
                    cx2 = ix + ch_side * split_gap + twist * (0.20 + 0.18 * chunk)
                    pts = [
                        (cx2 - deck_w * (0.42 if ch_side < 0 else 0.08), deck_y - deck_d * 0.20 + chunk * 5 * s),
                        (cx2 + deck_w * (0.06 if ch_side < 0 else 0.44), deck_y - deck_d * 0.56 - chunk * 3 * s),
                        (cx2 + deck_w * (0.16 if ch_side < 0 else 0.54), deck_y + deck_d * 0.20 + collapse * 18 * s),
                        (cx2 - deck_w * (0.36 if ch_side < 0 else 0.02), deck_y + deck_d * 0.46 + collapse * 10 * s),
                    ]
                    pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (27, 30, 37), pts)
                    pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (80, 82, 88), pts, 1)
            else:
                # Left rig: one heavy deck slab slams sideways.
                top_pts = [
                    (ix - deck_w * 0.55, deck_y - deck_d * 0.15),
                    (ix + deck_w * 0.18 - twist * 0.30, deck_y - deck_d * 0.55),
                    (ix + deck_w * 0.55 + twist, deck_y - deck_d * 0.05 + collapse * 12 * s),
                    (ix - deck_w * 0.10 + twist * 0.35, deck_y + deck_d * 0.42 + collapse * 8 * s),
                ]
                side_pts = [top_pts[3], top_pts[2], (top_pts[2][0], top_pts[2][1] + (14 + 10 * collapse) * s), (top_pts[3][0], top_pts[3][1] + (14 + 10 * collapse) * s)]
                pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (17, 19, 24), side_pts)
                pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (29, 33, 40), top_pts)
                pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (80, 82, 88), top_pts, 1)
            # Mast: left rig falls like a lever; right rig whips and snaps.
            mast_base = (ix + twist * 0.42, deck_y - 10 * s)
            mast_angle = -math.pi / 2 + fall_sign * ((0.35 if style == 0 else 0.10) + collapse * (1.28 if style == 0 else 1.78))
            mast_len = (90 - 18 * collapse) * s
            mast_tip = (mast_base[0] + math.cos(mast_angle) * mast_len, mast_base[1] + math.sin(mast_angle) * mast_len)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (42, 47, 56), (int(mast_base[0]), int(mast_base[1])), (int(mast_tip[0]), int(mast_tip[1])), max(1, int(5 * s)))
            if style == 2 and collapse > 0.34:
                snap_base = (mast_base[0] + (mast_tip[0] - mast_base[0]) * 0.52, mast_base[1] + (mast_tip[1] - mast_base[1]) * 0.52)
                snap_tip = (snap_base[0] + fall_sign * (44 + collapse * 28) * s, snap_base[1] + (12 + collapse * 42) * s)
                pygame.draw.line(surface, (37, 41, 49), (int(snap_base[0]), int(snap_base[1])), (int(snap_tip[0]), int(snap_tip[1])), max(1, int(4 * s)))
            for bi in range(4):
                k = bi / 3.0
                bx = mast_base[0] + (mast_tip[0] - mast_base[0]) * k
                byy = mast_base[1] + (mast_tip[1] - mast_base[1]) * k
                off = (10 - bi * 1.8) * s
                pygame.draw.line(surface, (56, 61, 72), (int(bx - off), int(byy + off * 0.35)), (int(bx + off), int(byy - off * 0.35)), max(1, int(2 * s)))
    # ── Settled wreck. Each style leaves a different silhouette. ─────────
    wreck_y = waterline - (8 - 2 * min(1.0, t / 90.0) + 18 * sink) * s
    if style == 1:
        hull_pts = [
            (ix - 42 * s, wreck_y + 12 * s), (ix - 22 * s, wreck_y - 4 * s),
            (ix, wreck_y + 4 * s), (ix + 22 * s, wreck_y - 4 * s),
            (ix + 42 * s, wreck_y + 12 * s), (ix + 16 * s, wreck_y + 20 * s),
            (ix - 18 * s, wreck_y + 20 * s),
        ]
    elif style == 0:
        hull_pts = [
            (ix - 68 * s, wreck_y + 6 * s), (ix - 46 * s, wreck_y - 9 * s),
            (ix - 12 * s, wreck_y - 2 * s), (ix + 30 * s, wreck_y + 8 * s),
            (ix + 38 * s, wreck_y + 17 * s), (ix - 45 * s, wreck_y + 19 * s),
        ]
    else:
        hull_pts = [
            (ix - 42 * s, wreck_y + 9 * s), (ix - 10 * s, wreck_y - 2 * s),
            (ix + 26 * s, wreck_y - 12 * s), (ix + 70 * s, wreck_y + 5 * s),
            (ix + 58 * s, wreck_y + 18 * s), (ix - 26 * s, wreck_y + 17 * s),
        ]
    pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (10, 12, 16), hull_pts)
    pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (36, 40, 46), hull_pts, 1)
    # Fallen slabs: center pile, left long slab, right two torn chunks.
    if style == 1:
        slabs = [
            [(ix - 36 * s, wreck_y + 0), (ix - 5 * s, wreck_y - 12 * s), (ix + 6 * s, wreck_y + 2 * s), (ix - 28 * s, wreck_y + 13 * s)],
            [(ix + 5 * s, wreck_y + 1 * s), (ix + 36 * s, wreck_y - 10 * s), (ix + 44 * s, wreck_y + 7 * s), (ix + 10 * s, wreck_y + 13 * s)],
        ]
    elif style == 0:
        slabs = [[
            (ix - 72 * s, wreck_y - 10 * s), (ix - 8 * s, wreck_y - 22 * s),
            (ix + 10 * s, wreck_y - 5 * s), (ix - 54 * s, wreck_y + 9 * s),
        ]]
    else:
        slabs = [
            [(ix + 6 * s, wreck_y + 0), (ix + 55 * s, wreck_y - 22 * s), (ix + 74 * s, wreck_y - 6 * s), (ix + 20 * s, wreck_y + 11 * s)],
            [(ix - 26 * s, wreck_y + 4 * s), (ix + 18 * s, wreck_y - 7 * s), (ix + 32 * s, wreck_y + 5 * s), (ix - 12 * s, wreck_y + 15 * s)],
        ]
    for slab in slabs:
        pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (18, 21, 27), slab)
        pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (62, 66, 72), slab, 1)
    # Low beams sticking out of the water.
    for off in (-38, -15, 12, 38):
        side = 0 if style == 1 else fall_sign
        x0 = ix + off * s
        x1 = x0 + (side * (18 + abs(off) * 0.18) + (off * -0.15 if style == 1 else 0)) * s
        y0 = wreck_y + (4 + (off % 3) * 3) * s
        y1 = wreck_y - (18 + abs(off) * 0.16) * s
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (24, 27, 33), (int(x0), int(y0)), (int(x1), int(y1)), max(1, int(3 * s)))
    # ── Debris trajectories differ by rig style. ───────────────────────
    rng = random.Random(int(ix * 9 + style * 997 + scale * 100))
    for di in range(34):
        delay = rng.randint(0, 48)
        age = t - delay
        if age < 0 or age > 125:
            continue
        if style == 1:
            vx = (rng.random() - 0.5) * 1.25 * s
            vy = (-3.4 - rng.random() * 2.8) * s
        elif style == 0:
            vx = (-0.75 - rng.random() * 2.2) * s
            vy = (-2.5 - rng.random() * 2.0) * s
        else:
            spin = math.sin(age * 0.08 + di) * 1.3
            vx = (0.65 + rng.random() * 2.35 + spin) * s
            vy = (-2.8 - rng.random() * 2.3) * s
        px = ix + (rng.random() - 0.5) * 54 * s + vx * age
        py = deck_start_y + rng.random() * 42 * s + vy * age + 0.062 * age * age * s
        if py > waterline + 28 * s:
            continue
        hot = max(0.0, 1.0 - age / 80.0)
        if di % 3:
            col = (255, int(120 + 80 * hot), 45, int(190 * hot))
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, col, (int(px), int(py)), max(1, int(2.1 * s * max(0.45, hot))))
        else:
            rect = pygame.Rect(int(px), int(py), max(2, int((4 + rng.random() * 4) * s)), max(2, int((3 + rng.random() * 3) * s)))
            pygame.draw.rect(surface, (64, 64, 68, int(180 * max(0.25, hot))), rect)
    # ── Fire pockets: layout differs per wreck. ────────────────────────
    fire = pygame.Surface((int(205 * s), int(180 * s)), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    fw, fh = fire.get_size()
    if style == 1:
        pockets = (-24, 0, 24)
    elif style == 0:
        pockets = (-58, -33, -8, 20)
    else:
        pockets = (-20, 12, 44, 68)
    for pocket, offx in enumerate(pockets):
        pocket_phase = t * (0.12 + pocket * 0.013) + pocket * 1.7 + style
        for i in range(8):
            frac = i / 7.0
            ox = offx * s + math.sin(pocket_phase + i * 0.8) * (5 + frac * 7) * s
            oy = (frac * (34 + 14 * pulse) + math.sin(pocket_phase * 0.7 + i) * 3) * s
            rr = max(2, int((8 - frac * 3 + pulse * 2) * s))
            alpha = int((178 - frac * 70) * burn)
            pygame.draw.circle(fire, (255, int(198 - frac * 105), int(66 - frac * 35), alpha), (int(fw // 2 + ox), int(fh - 28 * s - oy)), rr)
            if frac < 0.35:
                pygame.draw.circle(fire, (255, 240, 190, int(92 * burn)), (int(fw // 2 + ox), int(fh - 28 * s - oy)), max(1, rr // 2))
    surface.blit(fire, (int(ix - fw // 2), int(wreck_y - fh + 44 * s)))
    # ── Smoke: left leans left, center towers straight up, right spirals right. ──
    smoke = pygame.Surface((int(330 * s), int(350 * s)), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    sw, sh = smoke.get_size()
    for i in range(30):
        frac = i / 29.0
        rise = (t * (0.38 + frac * 0.18) + i * 19) % max(85, int(310 * s))
        if style == 1:
            sway = math.sin(t * 0.018 + i * 0.6) * (7 + i * 1.0) * s
        elif style == 0:
            sway = -frac * 54 * s + math.sin(t * 0.021 + i * 0.54) * (10 + i * 1.4) * s
        else:
            sway = frac * 58 * s + math.sin(t * 0.027 + i * 0.72) * (12 + i * 1.8) * s
        sr = max(8, int((16 + frac * 31 + min(1.0, t / 180.0) * 9) * s))
        shade = int(28 + frac * 24)
        sa = max(0, int((100 - frac * 54) * (0.72 + 0.28 * burn)))
        pygame.draw.circle(smoke, (shade, shade, shade + 2, sa), (int(sw * 0.50 + sway), int(sh - 24 * s - rise)), sr)
    surface.blit(smoke, (int(ix - sw // 2), int(wreck_y - sh + 34 * s)))
    return True
def _draw_attack_terminal_glitches(surface, elapsed, hud_rect):
    """Terminal interference bursts around targeting and impact moments."""
    hx, hy, hw, hh = hud_rect
    burst = 0
    for frame in (ATK_TARGET_CENTER, ATK_MISSILE_1_LAUNCH, ATK_IMPACT_CENTER,
                  ATK_TARGET_LEFT, ATK_MISSILE_2_LAUNCH, ATK_IMPACT_LEFT,
                  ATK_TARGET_RIGHT, ATK_MISSILE_3_LAUNCH, ATK_IMPACT_RIGHT):
        dt = elapsed - frame
        if 0 <= dt < 24:
            burst = max(burst, 24 - dt)
    if burst <= 0:
        return
    rng_g = random.Random(9000 + elapsed // 2)
    for _ in range(14 + burst // 2):
        gy = hy + rng_g.randint(-8, hh + 10)
        gh = rng_g.randint(1, 5)
        gx = hx + rng_g.randint(-18, 18)
        gw = hw + rng_g.randint(-25, 45)
        strip = pygame.Surface((max(20, gw), gh), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        col = rng_g.choice([(80, 180, 240), (255, 130, 45), (210, 60, 45)])
        strip.fill((*col, rng_g.randint(70, 150)))
        surface.blit(strip, (gx, gy))
    if burst > 8:
        warn = font_terminal_small.render("SIGNAL BREAKUP", True, (255, 120, 60))
        warn_bg = pygame.Surface((warn.get_width() + 12, warn.get_height() + 4), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        warn_bg.fill((0, 0, 0, 145))
        surface.blit(warn_bg, (hx + hw - warn_bg.get_width() - 8, hy + hh + 6))
        surface.blit(warn, (hx + hw - warn_bg.get_width() - 2, hy + hh + 8))
def _draw_attack_impact_overlays(surface, elapsed):
    """Very visible impact/collapse labels and signal tearing."""
    events = [
        (ATK_MISSILE_1_LAUNCH, "MISSILE LAUNCH", (255, 170, 60)),
        (ATK_IMPACT_CENTER, "CENTER RIG IMPACT", (255, 95, 45)),
        (ATK_IMPACT_CENTER + 48, "METAL COLLAPSE DETECTED", (255, 150, 70)),
        (ATK_MISSILE_2_LAUNCH, "MISSILE LAUNCH", (255, 170, 60)),
        (ATK_IMPACT_LEFT, "LEFT RIG IMPACT", (255, 95, 45)),
        (ATK_IMPACT_LEFT + 48, "METAL COLLAPSE DETECTED", (255, 150, 70)),
        (ATK_MISSILE_3_LAUNCH, "MISSILE LAUNCH", (255, 170, 60)),
        (ATK_IMPACT_RIGHT, "RIGHT RIG IMPACT", (255, 95, 45)),
        (ATK_IMPACT_RIGHT + 48, "METAL COLLAPSE DETECTED", (255, 150, 70)),
    ]
    active = None
    for frame, text, col in events:
        dt = elapsed - frame
        if 0 <= dt < 34:
            active = (dt, text, col)
    if not active:
        return
    dt, text, col = active
    a = int(235 * (1.0 - dt / 34.0))
    # Wide signal tear lines, impossible to miss.
    rng_g = random.Random(44000 + elapsed)
    for _ in range(10):
        gy = rng_g.randint(LETTER_H + 12, HEIGHT - LETTER_H - 20)
        gh = rng_g.randint(2, 9)
        tear = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, gh), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        tear.fill((*col, rng_g.randint(20, 70)))
        surface.blit(tear, (rng_g.randint(-28, 28), gy))
    # Big event label.
    label_font = pygame.font.SysFont("Consolas", 26, bold=True)
    label = label_font.render(text, True, col)
    label.set_alpha(a)
    bg = pygame.Surface((label.get_width() + 44, label.get_height() + 18), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    bg.fill((0, 0, 0, min(190, a)))
    pygame.draw.rect(bg, (*col, min(160, a)), bg.get_rect(), 1)
    pygame.draw.rect(bg, (*col, min(210, a)), (0, 0, 5, bg.get_height()))
    bx = WIDTH // 2 - bg.get_width() // 2
    by = LETTER_H + 18
    surface.blit(bg, (bx, by))
    surface.blit(label, (WIDTH // 2 - label.get_width() // 2, by + 8))
def _draw_walk_return_overlay(surface):
    """Soft signal-loss overlay after the oil-rig feed ends, easing back into gameplay."""
    if walk_return_timer <= 0:
        return
    progress = 1.0 - (walk_return_timer / 240.0)
    alpha = int(210 * (1.0 - progress))
    overlay = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    overlay.fill((0, 0, 0, max(0, min(230, alpha + 25))))
    surface.blit(overlay, (0, 0))
    if walk_return_timer > 95:
        msg_a = min(255, alpha + 35)
        panel = pygame.Surface((560, 104), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        panel.fill((4, 9, 14, min(240, msg_a + 25)))
        pygame.draw.rect(panel, (45, 88, 126, min(170, msg_a)), panel.get_rect(), 1)
        pygame.draw.rect(panel, C_KRAP_BLUE, (0, 0, 4, panel.get_height()))
        surface.blit(panel, (WIDTH // 2 - 280, HEIGHT // 2 - 62))
        title = font_terminal.render("KRAP OBSERVATION FEED LOST", True, (145, 186, 216))
        sub = font_terminal_small.render("holding last frame / reacquiring local view...", True, (185, 145, 95))
        title.set_alpha(msg_a)
        sub.set_alpha(msg_a)
        surface.blit(title, (WIDTH // 2 - title.get_width() // 2, HEIGHT // 2 - 38))
        surface.blit(sub, (WIDTH // 2 - sub.get_width() // 2, HEIGHT // 2 + 2))
    elif walk_return_timer > 0:
        msg_a = min(220, int(220 * (walk_return_timer / 95.0)))
        restore = font_terminal_small.render("LOCAL CONTROL RETURNING", True, (110, 175, 210))
        restore.set_alpha(msg_a)
        bg = pygame.Surface((restore.get_width() + 26, restore.get_height() + 10), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        bg.fill((0, 0, 0, min(145, msg_a)))
        pygame.draw.rect(bg, (45, 88, 126, min(150, msg_a)), bg.get_rect(), 1)
        bx = WIDTH // 2 - bg.get_width() // 2
        by = LETTER_H + 18
        surface.blit(bg, (bx, by))
        surface.blit(restore, (WIDTH // 2 - restore.get_width() // 2, by + 5))
def _draw_walk_attack_overlook(surface, force=False):
    """Pseudo-3D overlook panorama for the rig strike sequence.
    force=True keeps drawing the final observation feed during the return
    transition, preventing an abrupt snap back to top-down gameplay.
    """
    if not force and not (STATE.walk_attack_active or STATE.walk_terminal_active):
        return
    elapsed = STATE.walk_attack_timer
    w, h = surface.get_size()
    horizon_y = HORIZON_Y_OVERLOOK
    # Hard-clear the destination first so nothing from the walking-sim scene can
    # ever bleed through this cinematic layer.
    surface.fill((0, 0, 0))
    intro_alpha = 0
    if elapsed < 60:
        intro_alpha = int(255 * (1 - max(0, elapsed) / 60))
    shake_mag = max(0, int(STATE.screen_shake))
    if STATE.screen_shake > 0:
        STATE.screen_shake -= 1
    # Do not offset the entire rendered frame; that was causing visible bands.
    shake_x = 0
    shake_y = 0
    # Pre-fill an alpha scene with opaque black so nothing from the walking-sim
    # map can bleed through, while still letting us use per-pixel alpha effects.
    scene = pygame.Surface((w, h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    scene.fill((0, 0, 0, 255))
    sky_top = (12, 10, 24)
    sky_bot = (56, 38, 72)
    for y in range(0, horizon_y):
        p = y / max(1, horizon_y)
        r = int(sky_top[0] + (sky_bot[0] - sky_top[0]) * p)
        g = int(sky_top[1] + (sky_bot[1] - sky_top[1]) * p)
        b = int(sky_top[2] + (sky_bot[2] - sky_top[2]) * p)
        pygame.draw.line(scene, (r, g, b), (0, y), (w, y))
    rng_stars = random.Random(42)
    for _ in range(38):
        sx = rng_stars.randint(0, w)
        sy = rng_stars.randint(0, horizon_y // 2)
        sa = rng_stars.randint(70, 170)
        sr = rng_stars.randint(1, 2)
        pygame.draw.circle(scene, (190, 205, 235, sa), (sx, sy), sr)
    # Very subtle upper-sky haze only; avoid any strong horizontal banding.
    haze = pygame.Surface((w, 70), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    for i, (cx, cy, rx, ry, alpha) in enumerate([
        (w * 0.28, 8, 120, 34, 8),
        (w * 0.62, 4, 145, 40, 7),
        (w * 0.84, 10, 110, 30, 6),
    ]):
        drift = math.sin(elapsed * 0.006 + i * 1.4) * 6
        pygame.draw.ellipse(
            haze,
            (18, 20, 34, alpha),
            pygame.Rect(int(cx - rx + drift), cy, rx * 2, ry * 2),
        )
    scene.blit(haze, (0, 0))
    _draw_attack_water(scene, elapsed, horizon_y)
    rng_sheen = random.Random(37)
    for _ in range(8):
        sx = (rng_sheen.randint(0, w) + int(elapsed * 0.35)) % w
        sy = horizon_y + rng_sheen.randint(42, 240)
        sw = rng_sheen.randint(24, 54)
        sheen = pygame.Surface((sw, 8), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        sc = rng_sheen.choice([(180, 80, 60), (170, 120, 90), (135, 95, 165)])
        pygame.draw.ellipse(sheen, (*sc, rng_sheen.randint(10, 28)), sheen.get_rect())
        scene.blit(sheen, (int(sx), int(sy)))
    rig_draw_data = [_attack_rig_pose(i, elapsed) for i in range(3)]
    destroyed_count = sum(1 for s in STATE.rig_status if s == "DESTROYED")
    if destroyed_count > 0:
        glow_layer = pygame.Surface((w, 120), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        glow_intensity = min(1.0, destroyed_count / 3.0)
        pulse = 0.78 + 0.22 * math.sin(elapsed * 0.06)
        for gy in range(120):
            alpha = int(40 * glow_intensity * pulse * (1 - gy / 120))
            pygame.draw.line(glow_layer, (255, 105, 30, alpha), (0, gy), (w, gy), 1)
        scene.blit(glow_layer, (0, horizon_y - 46))
        # Long oil-fire reflections under destroyed rigs. These persist even after the blast sprite fades.
        for idx, rig in enumerate(rig_draw_data):
            if STATE.rig_status[idx] != "DESTROYED":
                continue
            since = max(0, elapsed - RIG_IMPACT_FRAMES[idx])
            refl = pygame.Surface((int(190 * rig["scale"]), int(150 * rig["scale"])), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            rw, rh = refl.get_size()
            heat = max(0.25, 1.0 - since / 760.0)
            for ry in range(0, rh, 3):
                frac = ry / max(1, rh)
                alpha = int(72 * heat * (1.0 - frac) * (0.72 + 0.28 * math.sin(elapsed * 0.06 + ry)))
                if alpha <= 0:
                    continue
                half = int((58 * rig["scale"]) * (1.0 - frac * 0.72))
                wob = int(math.sin(elapsed * 0.045 + ry * 0.15 + idx) * (7 + frac * 10))
                pygame.draw.line(refl, (255, 104, 32, alpha), (rw // 2 - half + wob, ry), (rw // 2 + half + wob, ry), 1)
            scene.blit(refl, (int(rig["x"] - rw // 2), int(rig["water_y"] + 10)))
    draw_order = sorted(range(3), key=lambda idx: rig_draw_data[idx]["depth"], reverse=True)
    for idx in draw_order:
        rig = rig_draw_data[idx]
        if STATE.rig_status[idx] == "DESTROYED":
            _draw_destroyed_rig(
                scene,
                rig["x"],
                rig["water_y"],
                rig["scale"],
                elapsed - RIG_IMPACT_FRAMES[idx],
                tilt=rig["tilt"],
                rig_index=idx,
            )
        else:
            _draw_attack_perspective_rig(
                scene,
                rig["x"],
                rig["water_y"],
                rig["scale"],
                elapsed,
                state=STATE.rig_status[idx],
                tilt=rig["tilt"],
            )
    # Waterline occlusion to seat the structures into the ocean a bit better.
    for rig in rig_draw_data:
        occ = pygame.Surface((int(150 * rig["scale"]), int(28 * rig["scale"])), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.ellipse(occ, (12, 22, 36, 95), occ.get_rect())
        pygame.draw.line(
            occ,
            (76, 108, 142, 55),
            (8, int(occ.get_height() * 0.38)),
            (occ.get_width() - 8, int(occ.get_height() * 0.38)),
            1,
        )
        scene.blit(occ, (int(rig["x"] - occ.get_width() // 2), int(rig["water_y"] + 2)))
    if STATE.walk_boat_active:
        boat_pose = _attack_boat_pose_from_world_x(STATE.walk_boat_x + 38, elapsed)
        _draw_attack_boat(
            scene,
            boat_pose["x"],
            boat_pose["y"],
            boat_pose["scale"],
            boat_pose["heading"],
            elapsed,
        )
    current_target = None
    for idx, rig_state in enumerate(STATE.rig_status):
        if rig_state == "TARGETED":
            current_target = idx
            break
    if current_target is not None and STATE.walk_boat_active:
        boat_pose = _attack_boat_pose_from_world_x(STATE.walk_boat_x + 44, elapsed)
        rig = rig_draw_data[current_target]
        _draw_attack_tracer(
            scene,
            int(boat_pose["x"] + 32 * boat_pose["scale"]),
            int(boat_pose["y"] + 4 * boat_pose["scale"]),
            int(rig["x"]),
            int(rig["deck_y"] - 8 * rig["scale"]),
            105,
        )
    for m in STATE.walk_missiles:
        rig = rig_draw_data[m["rig_index"]]
        start_pose = _attack_boat_pose_from_world_x(m["start_x"], elapsed)
        sx = start_pose["x"] + 34 * start_pose["scale"]
        sy = start_pose["y"] + 6 * start_pose["scale"]
        tx = rig["x"] + rig["tilt"] * 10 * rig["scale"]
        ty = rig["deck_y"] - 6 * rig["scale"]
        cx = (sx + tx) * 0.5 - 42
        cy = min(sy, ty) - (78 + abs(tx - sx) * 0.10)
        u = m["t"]
        inv = 1.0 - u
        cur_x = inv * inv * sx + 2 * inv * u * cx + u * u * tx
        cur_y = inv * inv * sy + 2 * inv * u * cy + u * u * ty
        prev_u = max(0.0, u - 0.07)
        prev_inv = 1.0 - prev_u
        prev_x = prev_inv * prev_inv * sx + 2 * prev_inv * prev_u * cx + prev_u * prev_u * tx
        prev_y = prev_inv * prev_inv * sy + 2 * prev_inv * prev_u * cy + prev_u * prev_u * ty
        dx = 2 * inv * (cx - sx) + 2 * u * (tx - cx)
        dy = 2 * inv * (cy - sy) + 2 * u * (ty - cy)
        angle = math.atan2(dy, dx)
        trail = pygame.Surface((w, h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.line(
            trail,
            (255, 150, 60, 55),
            (int(prev_x), int(prev_y)),
            (int(cur_x), int(cur_y)),
            2,
        )
        scene.blit(trail, (0, 0))
        _draw_attack_rocket(
            scene,
            cur_x,
            cur_y,
            angle,
            0.90 + (1.0 - rig["depth"]) * 0.35,
            glow_phase=elapsed * 0.06,
        )
    for ex in STATE.walk_explosions:
        rig = rig_draw_data[ex["rig_index"]]
        t_since = ex["max_life"] - ex["life"]
        blast_drop = min(22 * rig["scale"], t_since * 0.70 * rig["scale"])
        base_scale = 0.90 + (1.0 - rig["depth"]) * 0.34
        _draw_attack_explosion(
            scene,
            rig["x"],
            rig["deck_y"] - 4 * rig["scale"] + blast_drop,
            t_since,
            scale=base_scale,
        )
        if t_since < 16:
            side_alpha_t = max(0, t_since - 3)
            _draw_attack_explosion(
                scene,
                rig["x"] - 16 * rig["scale"],
                rig["deck_y"] + 6 * rig["scale"] + side_alpha_t * 0.45,
                side_alpha_t,
                scale=base_scale * 0.62,
            )
            _draw_attack_explosion(
                scene,
                rig["x"] + 22 * rig["scale"],
                rig["deck_y"] + 2 * rig["scale"] + side_alpha_t * 0.52,
                max(0, t_since - 5),
                scale=base_scale * 0.54,
            )
        # Burning debris rain for the first moments after impact
        if t_since < 28:
            for di in range(10):
                frac = di / 9.0
                dx = (-28 + di * 7) * rig["scale"]
                dy = (6 + t_since * (0.9 + frac * 0.35) + frac * frac * 14) * rig["scale"]
                ember_col = (255, 170 - int(frac * 50), 70, max(0, 180 - t_since * 5 - di * 8))
                pygame.draw.circle(
                    scene,
                    ember_col,
                    (int(rig["x"] + dx), int(rig["deck_y"] - 10 * rig["scale"] + dy)),
                    max(1, int((2.8 - frac) * rig["scale"])),
                )
        refl = pygame.Surface((120, 78), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        for ry in range(44):
            alpha = int(34 * max(0.0, 1.0 - ry / 44.0))
            wobble = int(math.sin(elapsed * 0.05 + ry * 0.11 + ex["rig_index"]) * 5)
            half_w = max(4, 22 - ry // 3)
            pygame.draw.line(
                refl,
                (255, 98, 26, alpha),
                (60 - half_w + wobble, ry),
                (60 + half_w + wobble, ry),
                1,
            )
        scene.blit(refl, (int(rig["x"] - 60), int(rig["water_y"] + 6)))
    _draw_attack_helicopter(scene, elapsed)
    rail_y = h - 36
    pygame.draw.rect(scene, (9, 12, 18), (0, rail_y, w, h - rail_y))
    pygame.draw.line(scene, (18, 22, 28), (0, rail_y + 7), (w, rail_y + 7), 2)
    for px in range(-30, w + 30, 46):
        top_x = px + int((px - ATTACK_VANISH_X) * -0.12)
        pygame.draw.line(scene, (14, 18, 22), (top_x, rail_y), (px + 10, rail_y + 24), 3)
        pygame.draw.circle(scene, (20, 24, 30), (top_x, rail_y + 4), 3)
    for i in range(62):
        alpha = int(144 * (1.0 - i / 62.0))
        pygame.draw.line(scene, (8, 12, 20, alpha), (0, rail_y - i), (w, rail_y - i), 1)
    _draw_attack_soft_rain(scene, elapsed)
    # Removed the heavy dark fade that used to start at SCENE_TOP.
    # That overlay created the ugly black horizontal band directly under the
    # KRAP OBSERVATION FEED terminal during the rig strike cutscene.
    surface.blit(scene, (shake_x, shake_y))
    # Brief screen-wide heat flash/concussion for each rig impact.
    for _impact_frame in RIG_IMPACT_FRAMES:
        _dt = elapsed - _impact_frame
        if 0 <= _dt < 18:
            _flash = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            _a = int(120 * (1.0 - _dt / 18.0))
            _flash.fill((255, 176, 86, _a))
            surface.blit(_flash, (0, 0))
            break
    _draw_attack_impact_overlays(surface, elapsed)
    active_attack = force or not (walk_after_firefight or STATE.walk_after_firefight)
    if not active_attack:
        return
    all_destroyed = all(s == "DESTROYED" for s in STATE.rig_status)
    hud_w, hud_h = 470, 62
    hud = pygame.Surface((hud_w, hud_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    hud.fill((0, 0, 0, 118 if all_destroyed else 172))
    pygame.draw.rect(hud, (42, 72, 104, 150), hud.get_rect(), 1)
    pygame.draw.rect(hud, C_KRAP_BLUE, (0, 0, 3, hud_h))
    status = STATE.walk_terminal_status or "STANDBY"
    title = font_terminal.render("KRAP OBSERVATION FEED", True, (145, 186, 216))
    time_txt = font_terminal_small.render(
        f"T+{elapsed // 60:02d}.{elapsed % 60:02d}", True, (120, 140, 160)
    )
    body_color = (184, 86, 54) if all_destroyed and elapsed > ATK_IMPACT_RIGHT + 60 else C_ORANGE
    body = font_prompt.render(status, True, body_color)
    state_short = {"ONLINE": "ON", "TARGETED": "TGT", "DESTROYED": "DOWN"}
    parts = []
    colors = []
    for i, short_name in enumerate(("L", "C", "R")):
        rs = STATE.rig_status[i]
        parts.append(f"{short_name}:{state_short.get(rs, rs[:2])}")
        if rs == "ONLINE":
            colors.append((80, 150, 190))
        elif rs == "TARGETED":
            colors.append((255, 200, 60))
        else:
            colors.append((180, 55, 45))
    hud.blit(title, (14, 7))
    hud.blit(time_txt, (hud_w - time_txt.get_width() - 14, 11))
    body_y = 35
    hud.blit(body, (14, body_y))
    summary_surfs = [font_terminal_small.render(part, True, color) for part, color in zip(parts, colors)]
    summary_width = sum(s.get_width() for s in summary_surfs) + max(0, (len(summary_surfs) - 1) * 12)
    body_right = 14 + body.get_width()
    can_show_summary = (not all_destroyed) and (body_right < hud_w - summary_width - 20)
    if can_show_summary:
        sx = hud_w - 14
        for surf in reversed(summary_surfs):
            sx -= surf.get_width()
            hud.blit(surf, (sx, body_y + 1))
            sx -= 12
    hud_alpha = min(255, max(0, (elapsed - 30) * 8)) if elapsed > 30 else 0
    hud.set_alpha(hud_alpha)
    surface.blit(hud, (18, 18))
    if hud_alpha > 0:
        _draw_attack_terminal_glitches(surface, elapsed, (18, 18, hud_w, hud_h))
    # Minimal per-rig overlays: only show the active target, plus a brief HIT marker after impact.
    for i, rig in enumerate(rig_draw_data):
        rig_state = STATE.rig_status[i]
        label_y = rig["deck_y"] - 24 * rig["scale"]
        if rig_state == "TARGETED":
            color = (255, 200, 60)
            radius = max(12, int(14 * rig["scale"]))
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, (*color, 74), (int(rig["x"]), int(label_y)), radius, 1)
            for dx1, dy1, dx2, dy2 in [
                (-radius - 7, 0, -radius + 1, 0),
                (radius - 1, 0, radius + 7, 0),
                (0, -radius - 7, 0, -radius + 1),
                (0, radius - 1, 0, radius + 7),
            ]:
                pygame.draw.line(
                    surface,
                    (*color, 70),
                    (rig["x"] + dx1, label_y + dy1),
                    (rig["x"] + dx2, label_y + dy2),
                    1,
                )
            txt = font_terminal_small.render(f"{RIG_NAMES[i]} LOCK", True, color)
            bg = pygame.Surface((txt.get_width() + 8, txt.get_height() + 3), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            bg.fill((0, 0, 0, 110))
            surface.blit(bg, (int(rig["x"] - bg.get_width() // 2), int(label_y - 30)))
            surface.blit(txt, (int(rig["x"] - txt.get_width() // 2), int(label_y - 29)))
        elif rig_state == "DESTROYED":
            since = elapsed - RIG_IMPACT_FRAMES[i]
            if 0 <= since < 34:
                alpha = max(0, 150 - since * 4)
                txt = font_terminal_small.render("HIT", True, (190, 70, 52))
                bg = pygame.Surface((txt.get_width() + 8, txt.get_height() + 3), pygame.SRCALPHA)
                bg.fill((0, 0, 0, min(110, alpha)))
                bg.set_alpha(alpha)
                txt.set_alpha(alpha)
                surface.blit(bg, (int(rig["x"] - bg.get_width() // 2), int(label_y - 24)))
                surface.blit(txt, (int(rig["x"] - txt.get_width() // 2), int(label_y - 23)))
    alive_indices = [i for i, state in enumerate(STATE.rig_status) if state != "DESTROYED"]
    if alive_indices:
        focus_idx = current_target if current_target is not None else alive_indices[min(1, len(alive_indices) - 1)]
        focus_rig = rig_draw_data[focus_idx]
        scope_col = (88, 120, 145, 52)
        pygame.draw.line(
            surface,
            scope_col,
            (int(focus_rig["x"] - 60), int(focus_rig["water_y"] + 8)),
            (int(focus_rig["x"] + 60), int(focus_rig["water_y"] + 8)),
            1,
        )
        pygame.draw.line(
            surface,
            scope_col,
            (int(focus_rig["x"]), int(focus_rig["water_y"] - 42)),
            (int(focus_rig["x"]), int(focus_rig["water_y"] + 50)),
            1,
        )
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, scope_col, (int(focus_rig["x"]), int(focus_rig["water_y"] + 8)), 44, 1)
    if intro_alpha > 0:
        fade = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        fade.fill((0, 0, 0, intro_alpha))
        surface.blit(fade, (0, 0))
def _draw_walk_krap_van(surface, x, y, t):
    """Top-down van, still simple/pixel-art, but readable."""
    x = int(x)
    y = int(y)
    # Ground shadow
    shadow = pygame.Surface((142, 72), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.ellipse(shadow, (0, 0, 0, 90), (0, 10, 142, 48))
    surface.blit(shadow, (x - 12, y + 8))
    # Main body
    body = pygame.Rect(x, y, 120, 54)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (14, 16, 19), body, border_radius=4)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (34, 39, 45), body, 2, border_radius=4)
    # Rear cargo box
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (18, 21, 25), (x + 8, y + 8, 70, 38), border_radius=2)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (38, 43, 50), (x + 8, y + 8, 70, 38), 1, border_radius=2)
    # Cab
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (20, 24, 30), (x + 76, y + 5, 36, 44), border_radius=3)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (48, 56, 66), (x + 76, y + 5, 36, 44), 1, border_radius=3)
    # Windshield / side windows
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (22, 34, 46), (x + 88, y + 10, 17, 15), border_radius=2)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (16, 26, 36), (x + 88, y + 29, 17, 15), border_radius=2)
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (70, 90, 110), (x + 90, y + 12), (x + 102, y + 12), 1)
    # KRAP blue stripe
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, C_KRAP_BLUE, (x + 12, y + 13, 58, 5))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (210, 210, 215), (x + 12, y + 18, 58, 1))
    # Rear door seam
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (45, 50, 55), (x + 8, y + 27), (x + 78, y + 27), 1)
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (8, 10, 12), (x + 43, y + 8), (x + 43, y + 46), 1)
    # Tail light / headlight
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (170, 35, 30), (x - 3, y + 17, 5, 8))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (170, 35, 30), (x - 3, y + 31, 5, 8))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (210, 200, 130), (x + 116, y + 12, 5, 7))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (210, 200, 130), (x + 116, y + 35, 5, 7))
    # Wheels
    for wx in (x + 20, x + 86):
        pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, (5, 6, 8), (wx - 8, y - 5, 18, 10))
        pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, (5, 6, 8), (wx - 8, y + 49, 18, 10))
        pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, (35, 38, 42), (wx - 4, y - 2, 10, 5))
        pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, (35, 38, 42), (wx - 4, y + 52, 10, 5))
    # Rain highlight on roof
    if (t // 180) % 2 == 0:
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (75, 85, 95), (x + 18, y + 9), (x + 65, y + 9), 1)
    # Exhaust puff
    puff_phase = (t // 80) % 4
    if puff_phase < 3:
        puff = pygame.Surface((28, 18), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.ellipse(puff, (65, 70, 75, 45 - puff_phase * 10), (0, 0, 24, 12))
        surface.blit(puff, (x - 26 - puff_phase * 4, y + 24 - puff_phase))
def draw_walking_sim():
    if walk_player is None:
        return
    # Nuclear option: while the overlook attack is active, render ONLY the
    # cinematic scene and its dialogue. Do not draw the walking-sim map at all.
    if STATE.walk_attack_active or STATE.walk_terminal_active:
        _draw_walk_attack_overlook(screen)
        if STATE.walk_dialogue_active and STATE.walk_dialogue_text:
            draw_dialogue_box(
                screen,
                STATE.walk_dialogue_speaker,
                STATE.walk_dialogue_text,
                STATE.walk_dialogue_char_idx,
                len(STATE.walk_dialogue_text),
            )
        elif walk_dialogue_active and walk_dialogue_text:
            draw_dialogue_box(
                screen,
                walk_dialogue_speaker,
                walk_dialogue_text,
                walk_dialogue_char_idx,
                len(walk_dialogue_text),
            )
        return
    if walk_return_timer > 120:
        # Hold the final burning-rigs feed under the signal-lost overlay before
        # fading into the playable harbor view. This makes the handoff breathe.
        _draw_walk_attack_overlook(screen, force=True)
        _draw_walk_return_overlay(screen)
        return
    screen.fill((16, 18, 22))
    fcx, fcy = int(walk_camera_x), int(walk_camera_y)
    start_tx = (fcx // FLOOR_TILE_SIZE) * FLOOR_TILE_SIZE
    start_ty = (fcy // FLOOR_TILE_SIZE) * FLOOR_TILE_SIZE
    for tx in range(
        start_tx - FLOOR_TILE_SIZE, fcx + WIDTH + FLOOR_TILE_SIZE * 2, FLOOR_TILE_SIZE
    ):
        for ty in range(
            start_ty - FLOOR_TILE_SIZE,
            fcy + HEIGHT + FLOOR_TILE_SIZE * 2,
            FLOOR_TILE_SIZE,
        ):
            screen.blit(SLUM_FLOOR, (tx - fcx, ty - fcy))
    _draw_walk_props_pools(fcx, fcy)
    container_tiles = [
        CONTAINER_TILE_RED,
        CONTAINER_TILE_BLUE,
        CONTAINER_TILE_RUST,
        CONTAINER_TILE_GREY,
    ]
    for wall in walk_walls:
        dr = pygame.Rect(wall.x - fcx, wall.y - fcy, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE)
        if -TILE_SIZE < dr.x < WIDTH and -TILE_SIZE < dr.y < HEIGHT:
            screen.blit(wall_shadow, (dr.x - 4, dr.y - 4))
            tile_idx = (wall.x // TILE_SIZE + wall.y // TILE_SIZE) % 4
            screen.blit(container_tiles[tile_idx], (dr.x, dr.y))
    _draw_walk_props(fcx, fcy)
    for sx, sy, key, label, text in walk_story_points:
        if -100 < sx - fcx < WIDTH + 100 and -100 < sy - fcy < HEIGHT + 100:
            drawer = WALK_SPRITE_DRAWERS.get(key)
            if drawer:
                drawer(screen, int(sx - fcx), int(sy - fcy))
            if walk_active_hover and walk_active_hover[0] == label:
                pulse = 0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(pygame.time.get_ticks() * 0.005)
                glow = pygame.Surface((80, 80), pygame.SRCALPHA)
                pygame.draw.circle(glow, (255, 220, 100, int(35 * pulse)), (40, 40), 30)
                screen.blit(glow, (int(sx - fcx) - 40, int(sy - fcy) - 40))
    if walk_after_firefight:
        van_screen_x = walk_van_trigger_x - fcx + 100
        van_screen_y = walk_van_trigger_y - fcy + 30
        if -200 < van_screen_x < WIDTH + 200:
            _draw_walk_krap_van(screen, van_screen_x, van_screen_y, pygame.time.get_ticks())
    if walk_player:
        walk_player.draw(screen, fcx, fcy, walls=(), no_gun=True, is_moving=_walk_player_moving)
    if walk_active_hover and walk_player and not walk_dialogue_active:
        label, desc = walk_active_hover
        px = walk_player.rect.centerx - fcx
        py = walk_player.rect.centery - fcy
        label_surf = font_btn.render(label, True, C_ORANGE)
        bg_w = label_surf.get_width() + 16
        bg_h = label_surf.get_height() + 6
        bg = pygame.Surface((bg_w, bg_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.rect(bg, (0, 0, 0, 170), (0, 0, bg_w, bg_h))
        screen.blit(bg, (px - bg_w // 2, py - 56))
        screen.blit(label_surf, (px - label_surf.get_width() // 2, py - 54))
    for stash in walk_stashes:
        sx = stash["x"] - fcx
        sy = stash["y"] - fcy
        if -100 < sx < WIDTH + 100 and -100 < sy < HEIGHT + 100:
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (75, 55, 35), (sx - 16, sy - 12, 32, 24))
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (45, 30, 15), (sx - 16, sy - 12, 32, 24), 2)
            pygame.draw.line(screen, (50, 35, 20), (sx - 16, sy), (sx + 16, sy), 2)
            pygame.draw.circle(screen, (180, 150, 80), (sx, sy), 3)
            dist = math.hypot(
                walk_player.rect.centerx - stash["x"],
                walk_player.rect.centery - stash["y"],
            )
            if dist < 80 and not walk_dialogue_active:
                prompt = font_terminal_small.render(
                    f"[E] Search {stash['label']}", True, C_ORANGE
                )
                screen.blit(prompt, (sx - prompt.get_width() // 2, sy - 36))
    for npc in walk_npcs:
        nx = npc["x"] - fcx
        ny = npc["y"] - fcy
        if -100 < nx < WIDTH + 100 and -100 < ny < HEIGHT + 100:
            shadow = pygame.Surface((38, 16), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            pygame.draw.ellipse(shadow, (0, 0, 0, 80), (0, 0, 38, 16))
            screen.blit(shadow, (nx - 19, ny + 8))
            if npc.get("sprite") == "old_woman":
                # ── Dress / robe ──
                pygame.draw.polygon(
                    screen,
                    (78, 64, 52),
                    [
                        (nx, ny - 8),
                        (nx - 18, ny + 18),
                        (nx + 18, ny + 18),
                    ],
                )
                pygame.draw.polygon(
                    screen,
                    (50, 42, 34),
                    [
                        (nx, ny - 8),
                        (nx - 18, ny + 18),
                        (nx + 18, ny + 18),
                    ],
                    1,
                )
                # Subtle dress folds
                pygame.draw.line(screen, (60, 50, 40), (nx - 6, ny - 2), (nx - 10, ny + 16), 1)
                pygame.draw.line(screen, (60, 50, 40), (nx + 6, ny - 2), (nx + 10, ny + 16), 1)
                # Apron
                pygame.draw.rect(screen, (110, 100, 88), (nx - 7, ny + 2, 14, 12))
                pygame.draw.rect(screen, (75, 65, 55), (nx - 7, ny + 2, 14, 12), 1)
                # ── Head ──
                pygame.draw.circle(screen, (185, 155, 128), (nx, ny - 14), 8)
                # ── Headscarf ──
                pygame.draw.arc(
                    screen,
                    (60, 55, 50),
                    (nx - 10, ny - 24, 20, 18),
                    0,
                    math.pi,
                    3,
                )
                pygame.draw.line(screen, (60, 55, 50), (nx - 8, ny - 14), (nx - 11, ny - 8), 2)
                pygame.draw.line(screen, (60, 55, 50), (nx + 8, ny - 14), (nx + 11, ny - 8), 2)
                # ── Face details ──
                pygame.draw.circle(screen, (40, 30, 25), (nx - 2, ny - 14), 1)
                pygame.draw.circle(screen, (40, 30, 25), (nx + 2, ny - 14), 1)
                # Wrinkle / frown line
                pygame.draw.line(screen, (130, 105, 85), (nx - 2, ny - 11), (nx + 2, ny - 11), 1)
                # ── Walking cane ──
                pygame.draw.line(
                    screen,
                    (70, 55, 35),
                    (nx + 14, ny - 6),
                    (nx + 18, ny + 18),
                    2,
                )
                pygame.draw.circle(screen, (90, 70, 45), (nx + 14, ny - 6), 2)
            else:
                pygame.draw.circle(screen, npc["color"], (nx, ny), 12)
                pygame.draw.circle(screen, C_PLAYER_SKIN, (nx, ny), 7)
            dist = math.hypot(
                walk_player.rect.centerx - npc["x"], walk_player.rect.centery - npc["y"]
            )
            if dist < 80 and not walk_dialogue_active:
                prompt = font_terminal_small.render(
                    f"[E] Speak to {npc['name']}", True, C_ORANGE
                )
                screen.blit(prompt, (nx - prompt.get_width() // 2, ny - 36))
    walk_progress = min(
        1.0, max(0.0, walk_player.rect.centerx / (len(walk_map[0]) * TILE_SIZE))
    )
    fog_r = int(18 - walk_progress * 8)
    fog_g = int(22 - walk_progress * 14)
    fog_b = int(30 - walk_progress * 16)
    fog_a = int(55 + walk_progress * 85)
    fog = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    fog.fill((fog_r, fog_g, fog_b, fog_a))
    screen.blit(fog, (0, 0))
    top_fog = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT // 2), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    for i in range(HEIGHT // 2):
        a = int(70 * (1 - i / (HEIGHT // 2)))
        pygame.draw.line(top_fog, (20, 25, 35, a), (0, i), (WIDTH, i))
    screen.blit(top_fog, (0, LETTER_H))
    observing_attack = walk_firefight_triggered and not (walk_after_firefight or STATE.walk_after_firefight)
    if not observing_attack:
        draw_rain(screen, rain_drops, speed_mul=1.0)
    draw_vignette(screen, strength=180)
    draw_scanlines(screen)
    draw_letterbox(screen)
    _draw_walk_return_overlay(screen)
    if walk_fp_cutscene:
        _draw_walk_fp_cutscene(screen)
        return
    # Dialogue — attack cutscene or NPC
    if STATE.walk_dialogue_active and STATE.walk_dialogue_text:
        draw_dialogue_box(
            screen,
            STATE.walk_dialogue_speaker,
            STATE.walk_dialogue_text,
            STATE.walk_dialogue_char_idx,
            len(STATE.walk_dialogue_text),
        )
    elif walk_dialogue_active and walk_dialogue_text:
        draw_dialogue_box(
            screen,
            walk_dialogue_speaker,
            walk_dialogue_text,
            walk_dialogue_char_idx,
            len(walk_dialogue_text),
        )
    if STATE.show_minimap and minimap and not observing_attack:
        minimap.draw(screen, walk_player, [], walk_camera_x, walk_camera_y)
    if not observing_attack:
        obj_text = "OBJECTIVE: Walk east toward the harbor."
        if walk_firefight_triggered and not (walk_after_firefight or STATE.walk_after_firefight):
            obj_text = "OBJECTIVE: Observe..."
        elif walk_after_firefight or STATE.walk_after_firefight:
            obj_text = "OBJECTIVE: Approach the van."
        obj_bg = pygame.Surface((400, 30), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.rect(obj_bg, (0, 0, 0, 150), (0, 0, 400, 30))
        pygame.draw.line(obj_bg, C_KRAP_BLUE, (0, 0), (0, 30), 3)
        screen.blit(obj_bg, (20, LETTER_H + 10))
        obj_surf = font_prompt.render(obj_text, True, C_WHITE)
        screen.blit(obj_surf, (30, LETTER_H + 18))
        rat_surf = font_terminal_small.render(
            f"RATIONS [E]: {player_rations}   Kevlar Plate: +{warehouse_hp_bonus} HP   Extra Mags: +{warehouse_mag_bonus}",
            True,
            (200, 180, 140),
        )
        screen.blit(rat_surf, (30, LETTER_H + 48))
        ctrl_surf = font_terminal_small.render(
            "WASD — walk    MOUSE — look", True, (130, 130, 140)
        )
        screen.blit(ctrl_surf, (WIDTH - ctrl_surf.get_width() - 20, HEIGHT - LETTER_H + 10))
        
    # Draw standard tactical crosshair in Walking Simulator
    ch_x, ch_y = pygame.mouse.get_pos()
    pygame.draw.circle(screen, C_ORANGE, (ch_x, ch_y), 14, 1)
    pygame.draw.circle(screen, C_ORANGE, (ch_x, ch_y), 2)
    gap = 3
    for x1, y1, x2, y2 in [
        (ch_x - 12, ch_y, ch_x - gap, ch_y),
        (ch_x + gap, ch_y, ch_x + 12, ch_y),
        (ch_x, ch_y - 12, ch_x, ch_y - gap),
        (ch_x, ch_y + gap, ch_x, ch_y + 12),
    ]:
        pygame.draw.line(screen, C_ORANGE, (x1, y1), (x2, y2), 1)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# effects.py
# ------------------------------------------------------------

"""Visual effects: particles, casings, breach markers, blood decals."""
import math
import random
import pygame

from config import (
    TILE_SIZE, WIDTH, HEIGHT, LETTER_H,
    SCENE_TOP, SCENE_BOT, C_BLOOD, Particle, Casing,
)
from state import STATE


_wall_shadow = pygame.Surface((TILE_SIZE + 8, TILE_SIZE + 8), pygame.SRCALPHA)
pygame.draw.rect(_wall_shadow, (0, 0, 0, 50), (4, 4, TILE_SIZE + 4, TILE_SIZE + 4))

_bullet_glow = pygame.Surface((16, 16), pygame.SRCALPHA)
pygame.draw.circle(_bullet_glow, (255, 200, 100, 60), (8, 8), 8)

_bay_door_shadow = pygame.Surface(
    (TILE_SIZE * 3 + 8, TILE_SIZE + 8), pygame.SRCALPHA
)
pygame.draw.rect(
    _bay_door_shadow, (0, 0, 0, 50),
    (4, 4, TILE_SIZE * 3 + 4, TILE_SIZE + 4),
)


def add_blood(x, y, life=120, size_range=(8, 16)):
    STATE.blood_decals.append([x, y, life, random.randint(*size_range)])


def spawn_particles(x, y, color, count=8):
    for _ in range(count):
        STATE.particles.append(Particle(x, y, color))


def update_particles():
    for p in STATE.particles[:]:
        p.update()
        if p.life <= 0:
            STATE.particles.remove(p)


def update_casings(solid_walls):
    for c in STATE.casings[:]:
        c.update(solid_walls)
        if c.life <= 0:
            STATE.casings.remove(c)


def update_floating_texts():
    for ft in STATE.floating_texts[:]:
        if len(ft) < 6 or ft[5] != "pin":
            ft[1] -= 1.0
        ft[4] -= 1
        if ft[4] <= 0:
            STATE.floating_texts.remove(ft)


def update_breach_markers():
    for marker in STATE.breach_markers[:]:
        marker[2] -= 1
        if marker[2] <= 0:
            STATE.breach_markers.remove(marker)


def shake(amount):
    if amount > STATE.screen_shake:
        STATE.screen_shake = amount


def flash(frames=15):
    STATE.gc_flash_timer = frames

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# ui_hud.py
# ------------------------------------------------------------

"""HUD overlays: minimap, safe keypad, notebook inspection, desk notes."""
import math
import random
import pygame
_last_note_tick_key = None
from config import (
    WIDTH, HEIGHT, TILE_SIZE, FLOOR_TILE_SIZE,
    C_WHITE, C_ORANGE, C_TERMINAL, C_KRAP_BLUE, C_GREY,
    C_BG_GAME, LETTER_H, SCENE_TOP, SCENE_BOT,
    font_terminal, font_terminal_small, font_speaker,
    font_btn, font_prompt, font_dialogue, font_title, screen,
    CONTAINER_TILE_RED, CONTAINER_TILE_BLUE, CONTAINER_TILE_RUST, CONTAINER_TILE_GREY,
    audio,
)
from state import STATE
class MiniMap:
    def __init__(self, map_data, tile_size=64):
        self.tile_size = tile_size
        self.map_w = len(map_data[0])
        self.map_h = len(map_data)
        self.world_w = self.map_w * tile_size
        self.world_h = self.map_h * tile_size
        self.minimap_w = 160
        self.minimap_h = 160
        self.scale_x = self.minimap_w / self.world_w
        self.scale_y = self.minimap_h / self.world_h
        self.margin = 10
        self.x = WIDTH - self.minimap_w - self.margin
        self.y = self.margin
        self.static_surface = self._build_static_surface(map_data)
    def _build_static_surface(self, map_data):
        surf = pygame.Surface((self.minimap_w, self.minimap_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        surf.fill((10, 10, 12, 220))
        wall_color = (60, 55, 50)
        door_color = (80, 70, 60)
        for row_idx, row in enumerate(map_data):
            for col_idx, tile in enumerate(row):
                if tile in ("C", "W"):
                    px = int(col_idx * self.tile_size * self.scale_x)
                    py = int(row_idx * self.tile_size * self.scale_y)
                    pw = max(1, int(self.tile_size * self.scale_x))
                    ph = max(1, int(self.tile_size * self.scale_y))
                    c = door_color if tile == "D" else wall_color
                    pygame.draw.rect(surf, c, (px, py, pw, ph))
        return surf
    def draw(
        self,
        surface,
        player,
        enemies,
        camera_x,
        camera_y,
        notebook_pos=None,
        exfil_pos=None,
        volkov_pos=None,
        commander_pos=None,
    ):
        if player is None:
            return
        panel = self.static_surface.copy()
        px = int(player.rect.centerx * self.scale_x)
        py = int(player.rect.centery * self.scale_y)
        pygame.draw.circle(panel, (80, 220, 100), (px, py), 3)
        angle = player.angle
        dir_len = 6
        dx = int(math.cos(angle) * dir_len)
        dy = int(math.sin(angle) * dir_len)
        pygame.draw.line(panel, (80, 220, 100), (px, py), (px + dx, py + dy), 2)
        for e in enemies:
            ex = int(e.rect.centerx * self.scale_x)
            ey = int(e.rect.centery * self.scale_y)
            pygame.draw.circle(panel, (220, 60, 60), (ex, ey), 2)
        if notebook_pos:
            nx = int(notebook_pos[0] * self.scale_x)
            ny = int(notebook_pos[1] * self.scale_y)
            pygame.draw.rect(panel, (255, 220, 100), (nx - 3, ny - 3, 6, 6))
            pygame.draw.rect(panel, (255, 255, 255), (nx - 3, ny - 3, 6, 6), 1)
        if exfil_pos:
            ex = int(exfil_pos[0] * self.scale_x)
            ey = int(exfil_pos[1] * self.scale_y)
            pygame.draw.circle(panel, (80, 220, 100), (ex, ey), 4, 1)
        if volkov_pos:
            vx = int(volkov_pos[0] * self.scale_x)
            vy = int(volkov_pos[1] * self.scale_y)
            pygame.draw.circle(panel, (255, 160, 60), (vx, vy), 3)
        if commander_pos:
            cx = int(commander_pos[0] * self.scale_x)
            cy = int(commander_pos[1] * self.scale_y)
            pygame.draw.circle(panel, (255, 160, 60), (cx, cy), 3)
        pygame.draw.rect(panel, (90, 75, 45), (0, 0, self.minimap_w, self.minimap_h), 2)
        pygame.draw.rect(
            panel, (55, 45, 28), (1, 1, self.minimap_w - 2, self.minimap_h - 2), 1
        )
        surface.blit(panel, (self.x, self.y))
def _draw_dirty_hand_text(surface, text, x, y, font, color, seed=0, alpha=120, jitter=2, angle=0):
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    cx = x
    for i, ch in enumerate(text):
        if ch == " ":
            cx += font.size(" ")[0] + rng.randint(2, 6)
            continue
        ch_surf = font.render(ch, True, color).convert_alpha()
        ch_surf.set_alpha(max(0, min(255, alpha + rng.randint(-45, 25))))
        rot = pygame.transform.rotate(ch_surf, angle + rng.uniform(-9, 9))
        rx = cx + rng.randint(-jitter, jitter)
        ry = y + rng.randint(-jitter, jitter)
        surface.blit(rot, (rx, ry))
        if rng.random() < 0.65:
            ghost = font.render(ch, True, color).convert_alpha()
            ghost.set_alpha(max(0, alpha // 3))
            ghost = pygame.transform.rotate(ghost, angle + rng.uniform(-14, 14))
            surface.blit(ghost, (rx + rng.randint(-3, 3), ry + rng.randint(-2, 3)))
        cx += font.size(ch)[0] + rng.randint(-2, 3)
def _draw_crumpled_safe_scrap():
    overlay = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    overlay.fill((0, 0, 0, 215))
    screen.blit(overlay, (0, 0))
    rng = random.Random(742)
    paper_w, paper_h = 460, 330
    paper = pygame.Surface((paper_w, paper_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pts = [
        (28, 18), (90, 5), (158, 16), (236, 8), (315, 20), (404, 10), (444, 38),
        (433, 92), (452, 148), (430, 205), (445, 286), (388, 317), (316, 306),

        (10, 96),
    ]
    shadow = pygame.Surface((paper_w + 40, paper_h + 40), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.polygon(shadow, (0, 0, 0, 120), [(x + 20, y + 22) for x, y in pts])
    shadow_rot = pygame.transform.rotate(shadow, -4)
    shadow_rect = shadow_rot.get_rect(center=(WIDTH // 2 + 10, HEIGHT // 2 + 12))
    screen.blit(shadow_rot, shadow_rect)
    pygame.draw.polygon(paper, (177, 160, 118), pts)
    pygame.draw.polygon(paper, (92, 73, 45), pts, 2)
    for _ in range(900):
        x = rng.randint(10, paper_w - 12)
        y = rng.randint(8, paper_h - 10)
        if rng.random() < 0.75:
            col = rng.choice([
                (120, 96, 58, 35), (70, 55, 35, 28),
                (210, 196, 150, 18), (35, 28, 20, 20),
            ])
            paper.set_at((x, y), col)
    for _ in range(12):
        sx = rng.randint(45, paper_w - 80)
        sy = rng.randint(35, paper_h - 70)
        sw = rng.randint(30, 110)
        sh = rng.randint(18, 70)
        stain = pygame.Surface((sw, sh), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.ellipse(stain, rng.choice([
            (45, 32, 20, 45), (78, 51, 22, 38), (20, 20, 18, 35),
        ]), stain.get_rect())
        paper.blit(stain, (sx, sy))
    facets = [
        [(40, 30), (180, 42), (95, 150)],
        [(190, 20), (315, 45), (250, 145)],
        [(320, 38), (430, 70), (365, 180)],
        [(32, 175), (120, 150), (75, 285)],
        [(150, 170), (265, 150), (220, 310)],
        [(275, 165), (420, 210), (335, 300)],
    ]
    for i, fpts in enumerate(facets):
        col = (255, 240, 180, 18) if i % 2 == 0 else (20, 12, 8, 35)
        pygame.draw.polygon(paper, col, fpts)
    fold_lines = [
        ((35, 76), (430, 58), 38), ((54, 145), (432, 132), 62),
        ((34, 220), (428, 205), 45), ((112, 20), (122, 310), 50),
        ((248, 18), (230, 320), 65), ((365, 35), (318, 304), 55),
        ((38, 285), (440, 40), 32), ((30, 44), (420, 292), 30),
    ]
    for (a, b, alpha) in fold_lines:
        pygame.draw.line(paper, (42, 31, 19, alpha), a, b, 4)
        pygame.draw.line(paper, (225, 210, 160, max(15, alpha // 2)),
                         (a[0] + 2, a[1] - 2), (b[0] + 2, b[1] - 2), 1)
    hand_font_big = pygame.font.SysFont("Comic Sans MS", 35, bold=True)
    hand_font_mid = pygame.font.SysFont("Comic Sans MS", 25, bold=True)
    hand_font_small = pygame.font.SysFont("Comic Sans MS", 18, bold=True)
    ink = (37, 31, 25)
    _draw_dirty_hand_text(paper, "dont forget", 72, 72, hand_font_small, ink,
                          seed=12, alpha=55, jitter=3, angle=-6)
    _draw_dirty_hand_text(paper, "7", 122, 132, hand_font_big, ink,
                          seed=1, alpha=95, jitter=5, angle=-10)
    _draw_dirty_hand_text(paper, "4", 210, 126, hand_font_big, ink,
                          seed=2, alpha=62, jitter=7, angle=7)
    _draw_dirty_hand_text(paper, "2", 306, 138, hand_font_big, ink,
                          seed=3, alpha=78, jitter=6, angle=-4)
    _draw_dirty_hand_text(paper, "7  ?  2", 120, 182, hand_font_mid, ink,
                          seed=4, alpha=48, jitter=7, angle=3)
    _draw_dirty_hand_text(paper, "last three", 80, 244, hand_font_small, ink,
                          seed=5, alpha=45, jitter=3, angle=-3)
    for _ in range(34):
        x1 = rng.randint(90, 360)
        y1 = rng.randint(115, 210)
        x2 = x1 + rng.randint(-55, 80)
        y2 = y1 + rng.randint(-10, 18)
        pygame.draw.line(paper, rng.choice([
            (20, 14, 8, 65), (70, 45, 20, 55), (120, 96, 58, 45),
        ]), (x1, y1), (x2, y2), rng.randint(2, 6))
    fold = pygame.Surface((paper_w, paper_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.polygon(fold, (18, 12, 8, 80),
                        [(155, 105), (292, 92), (278, 230), (140, 246)])
    pygame.draw.line(fold, (235, 215, 160, 45), (160, 105), (145, 245), 2)
    paper.blit(fold, (0, 0))
    for _ in range(18):
        hx = rng.randint(35, paper_w - 40)
        hy = rng.randint(30, paper_h - 30)
        if rng.random() < 0.45:
            pygame.draw.line(paper, (65, 45, 28, 90),
                             (hx, hy), (hx + rng.randint(-20, 25), hy + rng.randint(-7, 9)),
                             rng.randint(1, 3))
    for _ in range(85):
        edge = rng.choice(["top", "bottom", "left", "right"])
        if edge == "top":
            x, y = rng.randint(25, paper_w - 25), rng.randint(8, 35)
        elif edge == "bottom":
            x, y = rng.randint(25, paper_w - 25), rng.randint(paper_h - 38, paper_h - 8)
        elif edge == "left":
            x, y = rng.randint(8, 38), rng.randint(25, paper_h - 25)
        else:

        pygame.draw.circle(paper, (35, 24, 15, rng.randint(25, 80)), (x, y), rng.randint(1, 5))
    paper_rot = pygame.transform.rotate(paper, -4)
    paper_rect = paper_rot.get_rect(center=(WIDTH // 2, HEIGHT // 2 - 12))
    screen.blit(paper_rot, paper_rect)
    if (pygame.time.get_ticks() // 600) % 2 == 0:

    lbl = font_terminal_small.render("crumpled scrap", True, (95, 82, 58))
    screen.blit(lbl, (WIDTH // 2 - lbl.get_width() // 2, HEIGHT // 2 - 218))
def _schedule_text_ticks(text, start_delay=0, step=2, volume=0.08, max_ticks=42):
    tick_i = 0
    for ch in text:
        if ch and not ch.isspace():
            if tick_i < max_ticks and (tick_i % 2 == 0 or ch in ".!?"):
                audio.schedule(start_delay + tick_i * step, "type_tick", volume=volume)
            tick_i += 1
def _play_ui_type_tick(volume=0.095):
    try:
        audio.play("type_tick", volume=volume)
    except Exception:
        pass
def draw_desk_note():
    global _last_note_tick_key
    if not STATE.active_read_note:
        _last_note_tick_key = None
        return
    if _last_note_tick_key != STATE.active_read_note:
        _last_note_tick_key = STATE.active_read_note
        _schedule_text_ticks(str(STATE.active_read_note).replace("__CRUMPLED_SAFE_SCRAP__", "crumpled scrap"), start_delay=0, step=2, volume=0.075, max_ticks=38)
    if STATE.active_read_note == "__CRUMPLED_SAFE_SCRAP__":
        _draw_crumpled_safe_scrap()
        return
    overlay = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    overlay.fill((0, 0, 0, 200))
    screen.blit(overlay, (0, 0))
    panel_w, panel_h = 500, 280
    panel_x = (WIDTH - panel_w) // 2
    panel_y = (HEIGHT - panel_h) // 2
    paper = pygame.Surface((panel_w, panel_h))
    paper.fill((32, 28, 22))
    for row in range(0, panel_h, 3):
        shade = random.randint(-3, 3)
        c = max(0, min(255, 32 + shade))
        pygame.draw.line(paper, (c, c - 2, c - 4), (0, row), (panel_w, row))
    screen.blit(paper, (panel_x, panel_y))
    pygame.draw.rect(screen, (80, 70, 50), (panel_x, panel_y, panel_w, panel_h), 2)
    header = font_speaker.render("FIELD NOTE", True, C_ORANGE)
    screen.blit(header, (panel_x + 16, panel_y + 12))
    pygame.draw.line(
        screen,
        (80, 65, 35),
        (panel_x + 16, panel_y + 42),
        (panel_x + panel_w - 16, panel_y + 42),
        1,
    )
    lines = STATE.active_read_note.split("\n")
    y = panel_y + 56
    for line in lines:
        surf = font_terminal.render(line, True, C_WHITE)
        screen.blit(surf, (panel_x + 20, y))
        y += 28
    if (pygame.time.get_ticks() // 600) % 2 == 0:
        prompt = font_prompt.render("[ SPACE ] close", True, (160, 145, 100))
        screen.blit(
            prompt,
            (panel_x + panel_w // 2 - prompt.get_width() // 2, panel_y + panel_h - 28),
        )
_notebook_inspect_timer = 0
def reset_notebook_inspect():
    global _notebook_inspect_timer
    _notebook_inspect_timer = 0
def draw_notebook_inspection():
    global _notebook_inspect_timer
    _notebook_inspect_timer += 1
    # ── Dark overlay fades in ──
    overlay_alpha = min(210, _notebook_inspect_timer * 8)
    overlay = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    overlay.fill((0, 0, 0, overlay_alpha))
    screen.blit(overlay, (0, 0))
    # ── Notebook slides/zooms in ──
    slide_progress = min(1.0, _notebook_inspect_timer / 20.0)
    ease = 1.0 - (1.0 - slide_progress) ** 2
    # ── Bounce settle at end ──
    if slide_progress >= 0.85:
        b_t = (slide_progress - 0.85) / 0.15
        bounce_os = -math.sin(b_t * math.pi * 2) * 5 * (1.0 - b_t)
    else:
        bounce_os = 0
    slide_offset = int((1.0 - ease) * 60 + bounce_os)
    panel_w, panel_h = 780, 520
    panel_x = (WIDTH - panel_w) // 2
    panel_y = (HEIGHT - panel_h) // 2
    # ── Panel rendered with slide offset ──
    paper = pygame.Surface((panel_w, panel_h))
    paper.fill((28, 24, 18))
    for row in range(0, panel_h, 3):
        shade = random.randint(-3, 3)
        c = max(0, min(255, 28 + shade))
        pygame.draw.line(paper, (c, c - 2, c - 4), (0, row), (panel_w, row))
    screen.blit(paper, (panel_x, panel_y + slide_offset))
    pygame.draw.rect(screen, (90, 75, 45), (panel_x, panel_y + slide_offset, panel_w, panel_h), 2)
    pygame.draw.rect(
        screen, (55, 45, 28), (panel_x + 5, panel_y + 5 + slide_offset, panel_w - 10, panel_h - 10), 1
    )
    # ── Classified stamp appears after delay ──
    stamp_alpha = min(255, max(0, _notebook_inspect_timer - 30) * 12)
    stamp_surf = pygame.Surface((180, 60), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    stamp_txt = font_speaker.render("CLASSIFIED", True, (180, 30, 30, stamp_alpha))
    stamp_surf.blit(stamp_txt, (0, 10))
    pygame.draw.rect(
        stamp_surf,
        (180, 30, 30, stamp_alpha),
        (0, 8, stamp_txt.get_width() + 4, stamp_txt.get_height() + 4),
        2,
    )
    rotated = pygame.transform.rotate(stamp_surf, -18)
    screen.blit(rotated, (panel_x + panel_w - 190, panel_y + 8 + slide_offset))
    pygame.draw.rect(screen, (20, 16, 10), (panel_x, panel_y, panel_w, 44))
    pygame.draw.line(
        screen,
        (90, 75, 45),
        (panel_x, panel_y + 44),
        (panel_x + panel_w, panel_y + 44),
        1,
    )
    header_lbl = font_terminal_small.render(
        "OPERATION BLACK NOTEBOOK // FIELD INTELLIGENCE DOSSIER // EYES ONLY",
        True,
        (120, 100, 60),
    )
    screen.blit(header_lbl, (panel_x + 16, panel_y + 14))
    col_div = panel_x + panel_w // 2 - 10
    pygame.draw.line(
        screen,
        (55, 45, 30),
        (col_div, panel_y + 52),
        (col_div, panel_y + panel_h - 40),
        1,
    )
    lx = panel_x + 20
    ly = panel_y + 58
    map_hdr = font_terminal_small.render("[ OPERATIONAL MAP ]", True, (160, 130, 60))
    screen.blit(map_hdr, (lx, ly))
    pygame.draw.line(screen, (80, 65, 35), (lx, ly + 16), (col_div - 20, ly + 16), 1)
    ly += 24
    map_lines = [
        ("IPA safehouses", "7 marked", (200, 80, 80)),

        ("MAG supply routes", "sewers", (80, 160, 80)),

    screen.blit(tgt_hdr, (lx, ly))
    pygame.draw.line(screen, (80, 65, 35), (lx, ly + 16), (col_div - 20, ly + 16), 1)
    ly += 24
    photo_w, photo_h = 90, 110

def draw_safe_keypad():
    if not STATE.safe_keypad_active:
        return
    ticks = pygame.time.get_ticks()
    overlay = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    overlay.fill((0, 0, 0, 200))
    screen.blit(overlay, (0, 0))
    panel_w, panel_h = 340, 360
    panel_x = (WIDTH - panel_w) // 2
    panel_y = (HEIGHT - panel_h) // 2
    paper = pygame.Surface((panel_w, panel_h))
    paper.fill((20, 22, 26))
    for row in range(0, panel_h, 4):
        pygame.draw.line(paper, (14, 16, 18), (0, row), (panel_w, row), 1)
    screen.blit(paper, (panel_x, panel_y))
    pygame.draw.rect(screen, C_ORANGE, (panel_x, panel_y, panel_w, panel_h), 2)
    pygame.draw.rect(
        screen, (35, 40, 45), (panel_x + 3, panel_y + 3, panel_w - 6, panel_h - 6), 1
    )
    # Silver Corner Screws with small dark grooves (mechanical detailing)
    for sx, sy in [
        (panel_x + 12, panel_y + 12),
        (panel_x + panel_w - 12, panel_y + 12),
        (panel_x + 12, panel_y + panel_h - 12),
        (panel_x + panel_w - 12, panel_y + panel_h - 12),
    ]:
        pygame.draw.circle(screen, (90, 95, 100), (sx, sy), 5)
        pygame.draw.circle(screen, (50, 52, 55), (sx, sy), 5, 1)
        pygame.draw.line(screen, (40, 42, 45), (sx - 3, sy - 1), (sx + 3, sy + 1), 1)
        
    # Blinking status LED (Green for power/ready)
    led_color = (50, 255, 100) if (ticks // 400) % 2 == 0 else (15, 80, 30)
    pygame.draw.circle(screen, led_color, (panel_x + panel_w - 32, panel_y + 16), 4)
    # LED label
    led_lbl = font_terminal_small.render("PWR", True, (110, 115, 120))
    screen.blit(led_lbl, (panel_x + panel_w - 62, panel_y + 10))
    
    status_lbl = font_terminal_small.render(
        "SECURE CONSOLE - ACCESS LEVEL 1", True, (110, 115, 120)
    )
    screen.blit(status_lbl, (panel_x + 16, panel_y + 8))
    pygame.draw.line(
        screen,
        (35, 40, 45),
        (panel_x + 10, panel_y + 24),
        (panel_x + panel_w - 10, panel_y + 24),
        1,
    )
    header = font_speaker.render("SAFE KEYPAD", True, C_ORANGE)
    screen.blit(
        header, (panel_x + panel_w // 2 - header.get_width() // 2, panel_y + 28)
    )
    display_x = panel_x + 30
    display_y = panel_y + 64
    display_w = panel_w - 60
    display_h = 42
    # CRT Green background screen
    pygame.draw.rect(screen, (5, 24, 10), (display_x, display_y, display_w, display_h))
    pygame.draw.rect(
        screen, (50, 180, 100), (display_x, display_y, display_w, display_h), 1
    )
    # Draw scanlines specifically on the keypad LCD screen
    for sy in range(2, display_h, 3):
        line_y = display_y + sy
        pygame.draw.line(screen, (10, 38, 15), (display_x + 1, line_y), (display_x + display_w - 2, line_y), 1)
        
    # Pulsing cursor "_" in terminal text
    blink_cursor = "_" if (ticks // 350) % 2 == 0 else " "
    code_display = (
        STATE.safe_keypad_code + blink_cursor if len(STATE.safe_keypad_code) < 6 else STATE.safe_keypad_code
    )
    code_surf = font_terminal.render(code_display, True, (65, 255, 160))
    text_x = display_x + (display_w - code_surf.get_width()) // 2
    text_y = display_y + (display_h - code_surf.get_height()) // 2
    screen.blit(code_surf, (text_x, text_y))
    btn_w, btn_h = 64, 44
    gap = 10
    start_x = panel_x + (panel_w - (3 * btn_w + 2 * gap)) // 2
    btn_y_start = display_y + display_h + 16
    _keypad_buttons_ref = []
    keys_7_9 = [("7", 0), ("8", 1), ("9", 2)]
    keys_4_6 = [("4", 0), ("5", 1), ("6", 2)]
    keys_1_3 = [("1", 0), ("2", 1), ("3", 2)]
    keys_bksp_0_ent = [("DEL", 0), ("0", 1), ("ENT", 2)]
    mouse_pos = pygame.mouse.get_pos()
    rows = [keys_7_9, keys_4_6, keys_1_3, keys_bksp_0_ent]
    for ri, row in enumerate(rows):
        for label, col in row:
            bx = start_x + col * (btn_w + gap)
            by = btn_y_start + ri * (btn_h + gap)
            rect = pygame.Rect(bx, by, btn_w, btn_h)
            hover = rect.collidepoint(mouse_pos)
            bg_color = (48, 52, 58) if hover else (26, 28, 32)
            outline_color = C_ORANGE if hover else (70, 75, 80)
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, bg_color, rect, border_radius=3)
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, outline_color, rect, 1, border_radius=3)
            text_color = C_WHITE if hover else (180, 190, 200)
            lbl = (
                font_terminal_small.render(label, True, text_color)
                if len(label) > 1
                else font_terminal.render(label, True, text_color)
            )
            lbl_x = bx + (btn_w - lbl.get_width()) // 2
            lbl_y = by + (btn_h - lbl.get_height()) // 2
            screen.blit(lbl, (lbl_x, lbl_y))
            _keypad_buttons_ref.append((rect, label))
    handle_keypad_click._buttons = _keypad_buttons_ref
    hint = font_terminal_small.render("[ESC] cancel", True, (120, 110, 90))
    screen.blit(
        hint, (panel_x + panel_w // 2 - hint.get_width() // 2, panel_y + panel_h - 22)
    )
def handle_keypad_click(pos):
    if not STATE.safe_keypad_active:
        return
    buttons = getattr(handle_keypad_click, "_buttons", [])
    for rect, label in buttons:
        if rect.collidepoint(pos):
            if label == "ENT":
                _play_ui_type_tick(0.09)
                if STATE.safe_keypad_callback:
                    STATE.safe_keypad_callback(STATE.safe_keypad_code == STATE.safe_keypad_target)
                STATE.safe_keypad_active = False
                pygame.mouse.set_visible(False)
            elif label == "DEL":
                if len(STATE.safe_keypad_code) > 0:
                    STATE.safe_keypad_code = STATE.safe_keypad_code[:-1]
                    _play_ui_type_tick(0.09)
            elif len(STATE.safe_keypad_code) < 6:
                STATE.safe_keypad_code += label
                _play_ui_type_tick(0.10)
def handle_keypad_keypress(key):
    if not STATE.safe_keypad_active:
        return
    if key == pygame.K_RETURN or key == pygame.K_KP_ENTER:
        _play_ui_type_tick(0.09)
        if STATE.safe_keypad_callback:
            STATE.safe_keypad_callback(STATE.safe_keypad_code == STATE.safe_keypad_target)
        STATE.safe_keypad_active = False
        pygame.mouse.set_visible(False)
    elif key == pygame.K_BACKSPACE:
        if len(STATE.safe_keypad_code) > 0:
            STATE.safe_keypad_code = STATE.safe_keypad_code[:-1]
            _play_ui_type_tick(0.09)
    elif pygame.K_0 <= key <= pygame.K_9:
        if len(STATE.safe_keypad_code) < 6:
            STATE.safe_keypad_code += chr(key)
            _play_ui_type_tick(0.10)
    elif pygame.K_KP0 <= key <= pygame.K_KP9:
        if len(STATE.safe_keypad_code) < 6:
            STATE.safe_keypad_code += chr(key - pygame.K_KP0 + ord("0"))
            _play_ui_type_tick(0.10)
