CHAT_PANEL_Y = SERVER_PANEL_Y + 184
CHAT_PANEL_W = 240
CHAT_PANEL_H = SERVER_PANEL_H - 240
_chat_messages = []
_chat_next_spawn = 0
_server_animation_timer = 0
_refresh_active = False
_refresh_progress = 0.0
_scroll_offset = 0
refresh_btn_rect = pygame.Rect(SERVER_PANEL_X + SERVER_PANEL_W - 130, SERVER_PANEL_Y + 80, 82, 24)
kick_dismiss_btn_rect = pygame.Rect(0, 0, 160, 30)
def _server_status(cur, max_p, ping):
    if cur >= max_p:
        return "FULL", (220, 120, 45)
    if ping >= 180:
        return "UNSTABLE", (220, 80, 70)
    return "OPEN", C_TERMINAL
def _draw_server_badge(text, color, x, y, w=78):
    badge = pygame.Surface((w, 22), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    badge.fill((8, 12, 16, 210))
    pygame.draw.rect(badge, color, badge.get_rect(), 1)
    screen.blit(badge, (x, y))
    label = font_terminal_small.render(text, True, color)
    screen.blit(label, (x + w // 2 - label.get_width() // 2, y + 4))
def _fit_text(text, font, max_width):
    if font.size(text)[0] <= max_width:
        return text
    ellipsis = "..."
    while text and font.size(text + ellipsis)[0] > max_width:
        text = text[:-1]
    return text + ellipsis
def update_server_browser_animation():
    """Called once per frame while on multiplayer screen."""
    global _server_animation_timer, _chat_next_spawn, _chat_messages
    global _refresh_active, _refresh_progress
    _server_animation_timer += 1
    # Animate pings + player counts every ~90 frames (1.5s)
    if _server_animation_timer % 90 == 0 and not _refresh_active:
        for i, (name, cur, max_p, ping) in enumerate(server_list):
            delta = random.choice([-1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1])
            new_cur = max(0, min(max_p, cur + delta))
            new_ping = max(8, min(400, ping + random.randint(-6, 6)))
            server_list[i] = (name, new_cur, max_p, new_ping)
    # Refresh progress
    if _refresh_active:
        _refresh_progress += 0.025
        if _refresh_progress >= 1.0:
            _refresh_active = False
            _refresh_progress = 0.0
            for i, (name, cur, max_p, ping) in enumerate(server_list):
                new_cur = max(0, min(max_p, cur + random.randint(-3, 3)))
                new_ping = max(8, min(400, ping + random.randint(-25, 25)))
                server_list[i] = (name, new_cur, max_p, new_ping)
    # Spawn chat messages
    _chat_next_spawn -= 1
    if _chat_next_spawn <= 0:
        msg = random.choice(CHAT_POOL)
        _chat_messages.append({
            "user": msg[0],
            "text": msg[1],
            "color": msg[2],
            "age": 0,
        })
        _chat_next_spawn = random.randint(60, 130)
        if random.random() < 0.18:
            msg2 = random.choice(CHAT_POOL)
            _chat_messages.append({
                "user": msg2[0],
                "text": msg2[1],
                "color": msg2[2],
                "age": 0,
            })
        if len(_chat_messages) > 50:
            _chat_messages.pop(0)
    # Age messages
    for m in _chat_messages:
        m["age"] += 1
def switch_mp_tab(tab):
    global _mp_tab
    _mp_tab = tab
    audio.play("menu_click", volume=0.3)
def open_dm(user_idx):
    global _dm_open_user, _dm_input
    _dm_open_user = user_idx
    _dm_input = ""
    if user_idx not in _dm_history:
        _dm_history[user_idx] = []
    audio.play("menu_click", volume=0.3)
def close_dm():
    global _dm_open_user, _dm_input
    _dm_open_user = None
    _dm_input = ""
def send_dm_message():
    global _dm_input, _dm_response_delay, _dm_pending_reply
    if _dm_open_user is None or not _dm_input.strip():
        return
    _dm_history[_dm_open_user].append({
        "from": "you",
        "text": _dm_input.strip(),
        "age": 0,
    })
    _dm_input = ""
    _dm_response_delay = random.randint(45, 110)
    _dm_pending_reply = _dm_open_user
    audio.play("menu_click", volume=0.2)
def update_dm_state():
    global _dm_response_delay, _dm_pending_reply
    if _dm_open_user is not None and _dm_open_user in _dm_history:
        for m in _dm_history[_dm_open_user]:
            m["age"] += 1
    if _dm_response_delay > 0:
        _dm_response_delay -= 1
        if _dm_response_delay == 0 and _dm_pending_reply is not None:
            reply = random.choice(CONNECTIONS[_dm_pending_reply]["replies"])
            _dm_history[_dm_pending_reply].append({
                "from": "them",
                "text": reply,
                "age": 0,
            })
            _dm_pending_reply = None
            audio.play("menu_hover", volume=0.3)
def _status_color(status):
    if "Online" in status or status == "always":
        return (80, 220, 100)
    if "Away" in status or "Meeting" in status:
        return (220, 180, 60)
    if "Game" in status:
        return (100, 160, 220)
    if "Offline" in status or "Deleted" in status or "never" in status:
        return (110, 110, 110)
    if "\u2588" in status:
        return (140, 60, 60)
    return (180, 180, 180)
def draw_connections_panel():
    """Draws the friends list + active DM window."""
    mouse_pos = get_scaled_mouse()
    list_x = SERVER_PANEL_X + 28
    list_y = SERVER_LIST_TOP - 4
    list_w = 340
    row_h = 38
    list_header = font_terminal_small.render("CONTACTS", True, (80, 130, 160))
    screen.blit(list_header, (list_x + 6, list_y - 20))
    online_count = sum(1 for c in CONNECTIONS if "Online" in c["status"])
    online_lbl = font_terminal_small.render(
        f"{online_count} ONLINE  \u00b7  {len(CONNECTIONS)} TOTAL",
        True, (90, 130, 100),
    )
    screen.blit(online_lbl, (list_x + list_w - online_lbl.get_width() - 6, list_y - 20))
    list_bottom_limit = SERVER_PANEL_Y + SERVER_PANEL_H - 90
    visible_friends = (list_bottom_limit - list_y) // row_h
    visible_friends = min(visible_friends, len(CONNECTIONS))
    for vi in range(visible_friends):
        i = vi + _dm_scroll_offset
        if i >= len(CONNECTIONS):
            break
        conn = CONNECTIONS[i]
        ry = list_y + vi * row_h
        row_rect = pygame.Rect(list_x, ry, list_w, row_h - 4)
        hover = row_rect.collidepoint(mouse_pos)
        is_open = _dm_open_user == i
        row_surf = pygame.Surface((list_w, row_h - 4), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        if is_open:
            row_surf.fill((28, 36, 42, 235))
            pygame.draw.rect(row_surf, C_ORANGE, row_surf.get_rect(), 1)
            pygame.draw.rect(row_surf, (255, 120, 0, 130), (0, 0, 3, row_h - 4))
        elif hover:
            row_surf.fill((18, 28, 34, 225))
            pygame.draw.rect(row_surf, (80, 120, 140), row_surf.get_rect(), 1)
        else:
            row_surf.fill((9, 14, 18, 210))
            pygame.draw.rect(row_surf, (28, 44, 56), row_surf.get_rect(), 1)
        screen.blit(row_surf, row_rect)
        s_col = _status_color(conn["status"])
        pygame.draw.circle(screen, s_col, (list_x + 14, ry + 17), 4)
        name_col = C_WHITE if (hover or is_open) else conn["color"]
        name_surf = font_terminal.render(conn["name"], True, name_col)
        screen.blit(name_surf, (list_x + 28, ry + 4))
        status_text = f"{conn['status']}  \u00b7  {conn['last_seen']}"
        if len(status_text) > 38:
            status_text = status_text[:35] + "..."
        st_surf = font_terminal_small.render(status_text, True, (120, 130, 140))
        screen.blit(st_surf, (list_x + 28, ry + 22))
    # ── Scroll indicators ──
    remaining_below = len(CONNECTIONS) - (_dm_scroll_offset + visible_friends)
    remaining_above = _dm_scroll_offset
    more_y = list_y + visible_friends * row_h + 4
    if remaining_below > 0 and remaining_above > 0:
        more_lbl = font_terminal_small.render(
            f"\u25b2 {remaining_above} above  \u00b7  \u25bc {remaining_below} below  \u00b7  scroll wheel",
            True, (90, 110, 130),
        )
        screen.blit(more_lbl, (list_x + 6, more_y))
    elif remaining_below > 0:
        more_lbl = font_terminal_small.render(
            f"\u25bc {remaining_below} more contacts  \u00b7  scroll wheel",
            True, (90, 110, 130),
        )
        screen.blit(more_lbl, (list_x + 6, more_y))
    elif remaining_above > 0:
        more_lbl = font_terminal_small.render(
            f"\u25b2 {remaining_above} above  \u00b7  scroll wheel",
            True, (90, 110, 130),
        )
        screen.blit(more_lbl, (list_x + 6, more_y))
    # ── Scrollbar for contacts ──
    if len(CONNECTIONS) > visible_friends:
        sb_x = list_x + list_w + 4
        sb_y = list_y
        sb_h = visible_friends * row_h - 4
        sb_w = 5
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, (15, 22, 28), (sb_x, sb_y, sb_w, sb_h))
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, (28, 44, 56), (sb_x, sb_y, sb_w, sb_h), 1)
        thumb_ratio = visible_friends / len(CONNECTIONS)
        thumb_h = max(20, int(sb_h * thumb_ratio))
        max_scroll = max(1, len(CONNECTIONS) - visible_friends)
        thumb_y = sb_y + int((sb_h - thumb_h) * (_dm_scroll_offset / max_scroll))
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, C_ORANGE, (sb_x, thumb_y, sb_w, thumb_h))
    # DM window (right column)
    dm_x = list_x + list_w + 16
    dm_y = list_y - 4
    dm_w = SERVER_PANEL_X + SERVER_PANEL_W - dm_x - 28
    dm_h = SERVER_PANEL_H - 220
    dm_bg = pygame.Surface((dm_w, dm_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    dm_bg.fill((6, 11, 16, 230))
    pygame.draw.rect(dm_bg, (30, 50, 60), dm_bg.get_rect(), 1)
    pygame.draw.rect(dm_bg, (40, 60, 70, 100), dm_bg.get_rect(), 1)
    screen.blit(dm_bg, (dm_x, dm_y))
    if _dm_open_user is None:
        empty_lbl = font_terminal.render("// SELECT A CONTACT //", True, (60, 90, 110))
        screen.blit(
            empty_lbl,
            (dm_x + dm_w // 2 - empty_lbl.get_width() // 2,
             dm_y + dm_h // 2 - 20),
        )
        hint = font_terminal_small.render(
            "Click a name on the left to start a conversation",
            True, (50, 70, 85),
        )
        screen.blit(
            hint,
            (dm_x + dm_w // 2 - hint.get_width() // 2,
             dm_y + dm_h // 2 + 8),
        )
    else:
        conn = CONNECTIONS[_dm_open_user]
        s_col = _status_color(conn["status"])
        pygame.draw.circle(screen, s_col, (dm_x + 14, dm_y + 14), 4)
        hdr = font_terminal.render(conn["name"], True, conn["color"])
        screen.blit(hdr, (dm_x + 26, dm_y + 4))
        st = font_terminal_small.render(
            conn["status"], True, _status_color(conn["status"]),
        )
        screen.blit(st, (dm_x + 26, dm_y + 22))
        pygame.draw.line(
            screen, (30, 50, 60),
            (dm_x + 8, dm_y + 40),
            (dm_x + dm_w - 8, dm_y + 40), 1,
        )
        msg_x = dm_x + 12
        msg_y = dm_y + 48
        msg_w = dm_w - 24
        msg_area_h = dm_h - 100
        history = _dm_history.get(_dm_open_user, [])
        rendered = []
        for m in history:
            text = m["text"]
            is_you = m["from"] == "you"
            col = (220, 220, 220) if is_you else conn["color"]
            words = text.split(" ")
            line = ""
            wrapped = []
            for w in words:
                test = (line + " " + w).strip() if line else w
                if font_terminal_small.size(test)[0] <= msg_w - 60:
                    line = test
                else:
                    if line:
                        wrapped.append(line)
                    line = w
            if line:
                wrapped.append(line)
            for li, ln in enumerate(wrapped):
                prefix = ""
                if li == 0:
                    prefix = "you: " if is_you else f"{conn['name']}: "
                rendered.append((prefix, ln, col, is_you, m["age"]))
        line_h = 16
        max_lines = msg_area_h // line_h
        visible = rendered[-max_lines:]
        for li, (prefix, ln, col, is_you, age) in enumerate(visible):
            ly = msg_y + li * line_h
            alpha = min(255, age * 15)
            if prefix:
                p_col = (140, 140, 145) if is_you else col
                ps = font_terminal_small.render(prefix, True, p_col)
                ps.set_alpha(alpha)
                screen.blit(ps, (msg_x, ly))
                ts = font_terminal_small.render(ln, True, (220, 220, 220) if is_you else (210, 210, 215))
                ts.set_alpha(alpha)
                screen.blit(ts, (msg_x + ps.get_width(), ly))
            else:
                ts = font_terminal_small.render("  " + ln, True, (210, 210, 215))
                ts.set_alpha(alpha)
                screen.blit(ts, (msg_x, ly))
        if _dm_response_delay > 0 and _dm_pending_reply == _dm_open_user:
            dots = "." * (((pygame.time.get_ticks() // 300) % 3) + 1)
            typing = font_terminal_small.render(
                f"{conn['name']} is typing{dots}", True, (100, 130, 150),
            )
            screen.blit(typing, (msg_x, dm_y + dm_h - 64))
        input_box_y = dm_y + dm_h - 42
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, (15, 22, 28), (dm_x + 8, input_box_y, dm_w - 16, 32))
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, (50, 80, 100), (dm_x + 8, input_box_y, dm_w - 16, 32), 1)
        cursor = "_" if (pygame.time.get_ticks() // 500) % 2 == 0 else " "
        input_display = _dm_input + cursor
        if not _dm_input:
            input_display = "Type a message..."
            in_col = (90, 100, 110)
        else:
            in_col = C_WHITE
        ins = font_terminal_small.render(input_display, True, in_col)
        screen.blit(ins, (dm_x + 16, input_box_y + 10))
        send_hint = font_terminal_small.render(
            "[ENTER] send  \u00b7  [ESC] close DM",
            True, (90, 110, 120),
        )
        screen.blit(send_hint, (dm_x + 12, dm_y + dm_h + 4))
def scroll_server_list(direction):
    """direction: +1 scrolls down, -1 scrolls up."""
    global _scroll_offset
    max_offset = max(0, len(server_list) - 1)
    _scroll_offset = max(0, min(max_offset, _scroll_offset + direction))
def scroll_dm_list(direction):
    global _dm_scroll_offset
    list_bottom_limit = SERVER_PANEL_Y + SERVER_PANEL_H - 90
    list_y = SERVER_LIST_TOP - 4
    row_h = 38
    visible_friends = (list_bottom_limit - list_y) // row_h
    max_offset = max(0, len(CONNECTIONS) - visible_friends)
    _dm_scroll_offset = max(0, min(max_offset, _dm_scroll_offset + direction))
def trigger_server_refresh():
    global _refresh_active, _refresh_progress
    if not _refresh_active:
        _refresh_active = True
        _refresh_progress = 0.0
        audio.play("menu_click", volume=0.4)
def get_kick_message_for_server(server_name):
    if server_name in KICK_MESSAGES_BY_SERVER:
        return random.choice(KICK_MESSAGES_BY_SERVER[server_name])
    return random.choice(KICK_MESSAGES)
_server_shade = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
_server_shade.fill((0, 0, 0, 115))
_server_panel = pygame.Surface((SERVER_PANEL_W, SERVER_PANEL_H), pygame.SRCALPHA)
_server_panel.fill((7, 10, 13, 236))
pygame.draw.rect(_server_panel, (38, 58, 70, 170), _server_panel.get_rect(), 1)
pygame.draw.rect(_server_panel, (255, 120, 0, 90), (0, 0, SERVER_PANEL_W, 3))
def _wrap_server_name(name, font, max_width, max_lines=2):
    """Wrap a server name into up to max_lines lines.
    Returns a list of rendered strings (1 or 2 elements)."""
    if font.size(name)[0] <= max_width:
        return [name]
    words = name.split(" ")
    if len(words) > 1:
        best_split = 1
        for i in range(1, len(words)):
            first = " ".join(words[:i])
            if font.size(first)[0] <= max_width:
                best_split = i
            else:
                break
        line1 = " ".join(words[:best_split])
        line2 = " ".join(words[best_split:])
        if font.size(line2)[0] > max_width:
            ellipsis = "..."
            while line2 and font.size(line2 + ellipsis)[0] > max_width:
                line2 = line2[:-1]
            line2 = line2 + ellipsis
        return [line1, line2]
    mid = len(name) // 2
    while mid > 1 and font.size(name[:mid])[0] > max_width:
        mid -= 1
    line1 = name[:mid]
    line2 = name[mid:]
    if font.size(line2)[0] > max_width:
        ellipsis = "..."
        while line2 and font.size(line2 + ellipsis)[0] > max_width:
            line2 = line2[:-1]
        line2 = line2 + ellipsis
    return [line1, line2]
def draw_server_browser(selected_idx, connecting, kicked_msg, connect_progress=0.0):
    draw_menu_background()
    screen.blit(_server_shade, (0, 0))
    screen.blit(_server_panel, (SERVER_PANEL_X, SERVER_PANEL_Y))
    # ── Faint horizontal grid behind list ──
    for gy in range(SERVER_LIST_TOP + 50, SERVER_PANEL_Y + SERVER_PANEL_H - 80, 50):
        pygame.draw.line(
            screen, (15, 27, 34),
            (SERVER_ROW_X, gy),
            (SERVER_ROW_X + SERVER_ROW_W, gy), 1,
        )
    # ── Title ──
    screen.blit(_server_title_font.render("SERVER BROWSER", True, (0, 0, 0)), (SERVER_PANEL_X + 30, SERVER_PANEL_Y + 25))
    screen.blit(_server_title_font.render("SERVER BROWSER", True, C_WHITE), (SERVER_PANEL_X + 28, SERVER_PANEL_Y + 22))
    subtitle = font_terminal_small.render(
        f"KRAPNET regional lobbies  ·  {len(server_list)} servers online",
        True, (95, 125, 145),
    )
    screen.blit(subtitle, (SERVER_PANEL_X + 31, SERVER_PANEL_Y + 79))
    # ── Tabs (SERVERS / CONNECTIONS) ──
    tab_y = SERVER_PANEL_Y + 120
    servers_tab_rect.x = SERVER_PANEL_X + 28
    servers_tab_rect.y = tab_y
    connections_tab_rect.x = SERVER_PANEL_X + 28 + servers_tab_rect.w + 4
    connections_tab_rect.y = tab_y
    for tab_name, tab_rect in [("SERVERS", servers_tab_rect), ("CONNECTIONS", connections_tab_rect)]:
        is_active = _mp_tab == tab_name
        hover = tab_rect.collidepoint(get_scaled_mouse())
        if is_active:
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (28, 38, 48), tab_rect)
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, C_ORANGE, tab_rect, 1)
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, C_ORANGE, (tab_rect.x, tab_rect.bottom - 2, tab_rect.w, 2))
        else:
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (10, 16, 22), tab_rect)
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (50, 70, 85) if hover else (28, 44, 56), tab_rect, 1)
        label_text = tab_name
        if tab_name == "CONNECTIONS":
            online = sum(1 for c in CONNECTIONS if "Online" in c["status"])
            label_text = f"CONNECTIONS  ({online})"
        ts = font_terminal_small.render(label_text, True, C_WHITE if (is_active or hover) else (150, 160, 170))
        screen.blit(ts, (tab_rect.centerx - ts.get_width() // 2, tab_rect.centery - ts.get_height() // 2))
    # ── Refresh button ──
    refresh_hover = refresh_btn_rect.collidepoint(get_scaled_mouse())
    refresh_bg = (40, 50, 60, 220) if refresh_hover else (20, 30, 40, 200)
    btn_surf = pygame.Surface((refresh_btn_rect.w, refresh_btn_rect.h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    btn_surf.fill(refresh_bg)
    pygame.draw.rect(btn_surf, C_ORANGE if refresh_hover else (80, 100, 120), btn_surf.get_rect(), 1)
    screen.blit(btn_surf, refresh_btn_rect)
    if _refresh_active:
        fw = int(refresh_btn_rect.w * _refresh_progress)
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, (255, 120, 0, 100), (refresh_btn_rect.x, refresh_btn_rect.y, fw, refresh_btn_rect.h))
        rt = font_terminal_small.render("SCANNING...", True, C_ORANGE)
    else:
        rt = font_terminal_small.render("[REFRESH]", True, C_ORANGE if refresh_hover else (180, 180, 190))
    screen.blit(rt, (refresh_btn_rect.centerx - rt.get_width() // 2, refresh_btn_rect.centery - rt.get_height() // 2))
    mouse_pos = get_scaled_mouse()
    if _mp_tab == "SERVERS":
        # ── Divider under header ──
        pygame.draw.line(
            screen, C_ORANGE,
            (SERVER_PANEL_X + 28, SERVER_PANEL_Y + 156),
            (SERVER_PANEL_X + SERVER_PANEL_W - 28, SERVER_PANEL_Y + 156), 1,
        )
        # ── Column headers (above list) ──
        header_y = SERVER_LIST_TOP - 22
        status_x = SERVER_ROW_X + 320
        players_x = SERVER_ROW_X + 402
        ping_x = SERVER_ROW_X + 468
        headers = [
            ("SERVER", SERVER_ROW_X + 14),
            ("STATUS", status_x + 5),
            ("PLAYERS", players_x),
            ("PING", ping_x),
        ]
        for label, hx in headers:
            screen.blit(font_terminal_small.render(label, True, (80, 130, 160)), (hx, header_y))
        # ── Server list (left side) — rows can be 1 or 2 lines tall ──
        visible_count = 5
        hovered_server_idx = -1
        list_top = SERVER_LIST_TOP
        SERVER_ROW_H_SINGLE = 50
        SERVER_ROW_H_DOUBLE = 64
        ROW_GAP = 5
        name_max_width = (status_x - SERVER_ROW_X) - 24
        row_layouts = []
        cur_y = list_top
        for vi in range(visible_count):
            i = vi + _scroll_offset
            if i >= len(server_list):
                break
            name = server_list[i][0]
            name_lines = _wrap_server_name(name, font_terminal, name_max_width)
            row_h = SERVER_ROW_H_DOUBLE if len(name_lines) > 1 else SERVER_ROW_H_SINGLE
            row_layouts.append((i, name_lines, row_h, cur_y))
            cur_y += row_h + ROW_GAP
        list_bottom = cur_y
        for i, name_lines, row_h, ry in row_layouts:
            name, cur, max_p, ping = server_list[i]
            row_rect = pygame.Rect(SERVER_ROW_X, ry, SERVER_ROW_W, row_h - 4)
            hover = row_rect.collidepoint(mouse_pos) and not _refresh_active and not connecting and not kicked_msg
            if hover:
                hovered_server_idx = i
            status, status_color = _server_status(cur, max_p, ping)
            row_surf = pygame.Surface((SERVER_ROW_W, row_h - 4), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            if i == selected_idx:
                row_surf.fill((28, 36, 42, 235))
                pygame.draw.rect(row_surf, C_ORANGE, row_surf.get_rect(), 1)
                pygame.draw.rect(row_surf, (255, 120, 0, 130), (0, 0, 4, row_h - 4))
            elif hover:
                row_surf.fill((18, 28, 34, 225))
                pygame.draw.rect(row_surf, (80, 120, 140), row_surf.get_rect(), 1)
            else:
                row_surf.fill((9, 14, 18, 210))
                pygame.draw.rect(row_surf, (28, 44, 56), row_surf.get_rect(), 1)
            if _refresh_active:
                fade = pygame.Surface((SERVER_ROW_W, row_h - 4), pygame.SRCALPHA)
                fade.fill((0, 0, 0, 140))
                row_surf.blit(fade, (0, 0))
            screen.blit(row_surf, row_rect)
            name_color = C_WHITE if (hover or i == selected_idx) and not _refresh_active else (195, 205, 210)
            for li, line in enumerate(name_lines):
                line_surf = font_terminal.render(line, True, name_color)
                screen.blit(line_surf, (SERVER_ROW_X + 12, ry + 6 + li * 20))
            region_y_base = ry + 6 + len(name_lines) * 20
            region_idx = (sum(ord(c) for c in name) + i) % len(SERVER_REGIONS)
            region = font_terminal_small.render(SERVER_REGIONS[region_idx], True, (80, 95, 105))
            screen.blit(region, (SERVER_ROW_X + 13, region_y_base + 2))
            center_y = ry + (row_h - 4) // 2 - 11
            _draw_server_badge(status, status_color, status_x, center_y)
            pc = font_terminal.render(f"{cur:02d}/{max_p:02d}", True, C_ORANGE if cur >= max_p else C_TERMINAL)
            ping_c = (100, 200, 100) if ping < 60 else ((200, 200, 100) if ping < 150 else (200, 100, 100))
            ping_t = font_terminal.render(f"{ping}ms", True, ping_c)
            screen.blit(pc, (players_x, center_y + 1))
            screen.blit(ping_t, (ping_x, center_y + 1))
            bar_x = ping_x
            bar_y = center_y + 22
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (25, 28, 30), (bar_x, bar_y, 42, 4))
            fill_w = max(6, min(42, int(42 * (1 - min(ping, 240) / 240))))
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, ping_c, (bar_x, bar_y, fill_w, 4))
        if len(server_list) > visible_count:
            sb_x = SERVER_ROW_X + SERVER_ROW_W + 8
            sb_y = list_top
            sb_h = list_bottom - list_top - ROW_GAP
            sb_w = 5
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (15, 22, 28), (sb_x, sb_y, sb_w, sb_h))
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (28, 44, 56), (sb_x, sb_y, sb_w, sb_h), 1)
            thumb_ratio = visible_count / len(server_list)
            thumb_h = max(20, int(sb_h * thumb_ratio))
            max_scroll = max(1, len(server_list) - visible_count)
            thumb_y = sb_y + int((sb_h - thumb_h) * (_scroll_offset / max_scroll))
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, C_ORANGE, (sb_x, thumb_y, sb_w, thumb_h))
        # ── Scroll indicators (bottom of list, right-aligned to avoid BACK button) ──
        rendered_count = len(row_layouts)
        remaining_below = len(server_list) - (_scroll_offset + rendered_count)
        remaining_above = _scroll_offset
        if remaining_below > 0 or remaining_above > 0:
            if remaining_below > 0 and remaining_above > 0:
                hint_text = f"\u25b2 {remaining_above} above  \u00b7  \u25bc {remaining_below} below  \u00b7  scroll wheel"
            elif remaining_below > 0:
                hint_text = f"\u25bc {remaining_below} more servers  \u00b7  scroll wheel"
            else:
                hint_text = f"\u25b2 {remaining_above} above  \u00b7  scroll wheel"
            more_hint = font_terminal_small.render(hint_text, True, (90, 110, 130))
            screen.blit(
                more_hint,
                (
                    SERVER_ROW_X + SERVER_ROW_W - more_hint.get_width() - 4,
                    list_bottom + 4,
                ),
            )
        # ── Vertical Chat Panel (right side) ──
        chat_bg = pygame.Surface((CHAT_PANEL_W, CHAT_PANEL_H), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        chat_bg.fill((4, 8, 12, 215))
        pygame.draw.rect(chat_bg, (30, 50, 60), chat_bg.get_rect(), 1)
        pygame.draw.rect(chat_bg, (80, 130, 160, 90), (0, 0, CHAT_PANEL_W, 2))
        screen.blit(chat_bg, (CHAT_PANEL_X, CHAT_PANEL_Y))
        chat_lbl = font_terminal_small.render("// GLOBAL CHAT //", True, (80, 130, 160))
        screen.blit(chat_lbl, (CHAT_PANEL_X + 10, CHAT_PANEL_Y + 8))
        online_dot_pulse = (pygame.time.get_ticks() // 500) % 2
        dot_col = (80, 220, 100) if online_dot_pulse else (40, 140, 70)
        pygame.draw.circle(screen, dot_col, (CHAT_PANEL_X + CHAT_PANEL_W - 22, CHAT_PANEL_Y + 14), 3)
        online_lbl = font_terminal_small.render("LIVE", True, (80, 130, 100))
        screen.blit(online_lbl, (CHAT_PANEL_X + CHAT_PANEL_W - 60, CHAT_PANEL_Y + 8))
        pygame.draw.line(
            screen, (30, 50, 60),
            (CHAT_PANEL_X + 8, CHAT_PANEL_Y + 28),
            (CHAT_PANEL_X + CHAT_PANEL_W - 8, CHAT_PANEL_Y + 28), 1,
        )
        msg_area_x = CHAT_PANEL_X + 10
        msg_area_y = CHAT_PANEL_Y + 36
        msg_area_w = CHAT_PANEL_W - 20
        msg_area_h = CHAT_PANEL_H - 60
        rendered_lines = []
        for msg in _chat_messages:
            alpha = min(255, msg["age"] * 13)
            user_text = f"[{msg['user']}]"
            body_text = msg["text"]
            user_surf = font_terminal_small.render(user_text, True, msg["color"])
            user_surf.set_alpha(alpha)
            rendered_lines.append([(user_surf, msg_area_x, alpha)])
            words = body_text.split(" ")
            line = ""
            for w in words:
                test = (line + " " + w).strip() if line else w
                if font_terminal_small.size(test)[0] <= msg_area_w - 12:
                    line = test
                else:
                    if line:
                        s = font_terminal_small.render(line, True, (200, 200, 205))
                        s.set_alpha(alpha)
                        rendered_lines.append([(s, msg_area_x + 12, alpha)])
                    line = w
            if line:
                s = font_terminal_small.render(line, True, (200, 200, 205))
                s.set_alpha(alpha)
                rendered_lines.append([(s, msg_area_x + 12, alpha)])
            rendered_lines.append([])
        line_h = 14
        max_lines = msg_area_h // line_h
        visible_lines = rendered_lines[-max_lines:]
        for li, line_entries in enumerate(visible_lines):
            ly = msg_area_y + li * line_h
            for surf, x, _ in line_entries:
                screen.blit(surf, (x, ly))
        typing_y = CHAT_PANEL_Y + CHAT_PANEL_H - 18
        pygame.draw.line(
            screen, (30, 50, 60),
            (CHAT_PANEL_X + 8, typing_y - 4),
            (CHAT_PANEL_X + CHAT_PANEL_W - 8, typing_y - 4), 1,
        )
        typing_dots = "." * ((pygame.time.get_ticks() // 400) % 4)
        typing_users = [
            "Volkov_Real", "admin", "noob123", "MaruWatches", "Sebi_PM",
            "VasileFan2007", "xX_KRAP_Xx", "Cigarette_Enjoyer", "MOD_Krapov",
            "OldFisherman", "ConspiracyGuy", "MAGRecruiter", "Cmdr_VodkaTime",
            "RushB_Acaristan", "EX_KRAP_OPERATOR", "CapsLock_Stuck",
        ]
        typing_user = typing_users[(pygame.time.get_ticks() // 4000) % len(typing_users)]
        typing_lbl = font_terminal_small.render(
            f"{typing_user} is typing{typing_dots}", True, (90, 110, 120),
        )
        screen.blit(typing_lbl, (CHAT_PANEL_X + 10, typing_y))
        if hovered_server_idx >= 0 and not _refresh_active and not connecting and not kicked_msg:
            name = server_list[hovered_server_idx][0]
            rules = SERVER_RULES.get(name)
            if rules:
                tip_w = 360
                tip_h = 22 + len(rules) * 16 + 12
                tip_x = min(mouse_pos[0] + 16, WIDTH - tip_w - 10)
                tip_y = min(mouse_pos[1] + 16, HEIGHT - tip_h - 10)
                tip_surf = pygame.Surface((tip_w, tip_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
                tip_surf.fill((6, 10, 14, 245))
                pygame.draw.rect(tip_surf, C_ORANGE, tip_surf.get_rect(), 1)
                pygame.draw.rect(tip_surf, (255, 120, 0, 100), (0, 0, tip_w, 3))
                screen.blit(tip_surf, (tip_x, tip_y))
                tlbl = font_terminal_small.render("SERVER INFO", True, C_ORANGE)
                screen.blit(tlbl, (tip_x + 10, tip_y + 6))
                for li, line in enumerate(rules):
                    col = (200, 200, 210) if not line.startswith(" ") else (140, 140, 150)
                    ls = font_terminal_small.render(line, True, col)
                    screen.blit(ls, (tip_x + 10, tip_y + 24 + li * 16))
    else:
        draw_connections_panel()
    # ── Back button ──
    server_back_btn.x = SERVER_PANEL_X + 28
    server_back_btn.y = SERVER_PANEL_Y + SERVER_PANEL_H - 62
    server_back_btn.width = 150
    server_back_btn.rect.x = server_back_btn.x
    server_back_btn.rect.y = server_back_btn.y
    server_back_btn.rect.width = server_back_btn.width
    server_back_btn.check_hover(get_scaled_mouse())
    server_back_btn.draw(screen)
    hint = font_terminal_small.render("ESC returns to menu", True, (95, 110, 120))
    screen.blit(hint, (SERVER_PANEL_X + SERVER_PANEL_W - hint.get_width() - 28, SERVER_PANEL_Y + SERVER_PANEL_H - 48))
    # ── Connecting popup ──
    if connecting:
        popup = pygame.Surface((460, 220), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        popup.fill((8, 11, 16, 248))
        popup_rect = popup.get_rect(center=(WIDTH // 2, HEIGHT // 2))
        pygame.draw.rect(popup, C_ORANGE, popup.get_rect(), 1)
        pygame.draw.rect(popup, (255, 120, 0, 120), (0, 0, 460, 3))
        screen.blit(popup, popup_rect)
        progress = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(connect_progress or 0.0)))
        dots = "." * ((pygame.time.get_ticks() // 300) % 4)
        t = font_speaker.render(f"CONNECTING{dots}", True, C_ORANGE)
        screen.blit(t, (popup_rect.x + 28, popup_rect.y + 18))
        pct = font_terminal_small.render(f"{int(progress * 100):02d}%", True, (120, 160, 175))
        screen.blit(pct, (popup_rect.right - pct.get_width() - 30, popup_rect.y + 28))
        log_lines = [
            (0.00, "Resolving krapnet.acaristan.gov..."),
            (0.16, "Handshake established"),
            (0.32, "Verifying loyalty token..."),
            (0.50, "Bypassing IPA firewall..."),
            (0.68, "Downloading server modpack (4.7 GB)..."),
            (0.88, "Almost there..."),
        ]
        ly = popup_rect.y + 60
        for threshold, line in log_lines:
            active = progress >= threshold
            col = (140, 200, 140) if active else (55, 75, 75)
            prefix = ">" if active else "·"
            ls = font_terminal_small.render(f"{prefix} {line}", True, col)
            screen.blit(ls, (popup_rect.x + 30, ly))
            ly += 18
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, (24, 28, 32), (popup_rect.x + 30, popup_rect.y + 180, 400, 10))
        fill_w = int(400 * progress)
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, C_ORANGE, (popup_rect.x + 30, popup_rect.y + 180, fill_w, 10))
        # Small moving scan highlight inside the filled portion; does not affect progress.
        if fill_w > 12:
            scan_x = popup_rect.x + 30 + int((pygame.time.get_ticks() * 0.12) % max(1, fill_w))
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (255, 190, 80), (scan_x, popup_rect.y + 180, min(18, fill_w), 10))
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, (80, 70, 55), (popup_rect.x + 30, popup_rect.y + 180, 400, 10), 1)
    elif kicked_msg:
        popup_w, popup_h = 460, 220
        popup = pygame.Surface((popup_w, popup_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        popup.fill((15, 10, 10, 245))
        popup_rect = popup.get_rect(center=(WIDTH // 2, HEIGHT // 2))
        pygame.draw.rect(popup, (200, 50, 50), popup.get_rect(), 1)
        pygame.draw.rect(popup, (220, 60, 60, 120), (0, 0, popup_w, 3))
        screen.blit(popup, popup_rect)
        t = font_speaker.render("KICKED", True, (220, 60, 60))
        screen.blit(t, (popup_rect.x + 28, popup_rect.y + 18))
        pygame.draw.line(
            screen, (90, 30, 30),
            (popup_rect.x + 20, popup_rect.y + 52),
            (popup_rect.right - 20, popup_rect.y + 52), 1,
        )
        reason_lbl = font_terminal_small.render("REASON:", True, (140, 90, 90))
        screen.blit(reason_lbl, (popup_rect.x + 28, popup_rect.y + 64))
        words = kicked_msg.split(" ")
        wrap_w = popup_w - 60
        lines = []
        line = ""
        for w in words:
            test = (line + " " + w).strip() if line else w
            if font_terminal.size(test)[0] <= wrap_w:
                line = test
            else:
                if line:
                    lines.append(line)
                line = w
        if line:
            lines.append(line)
        for j, ln in enumerate(lines):
            lt = font_terminal.render(ln, True, (220, 200, 200))
            screen.blit(lt, (popup_rect.x + 30, popup_rect.y + 88 + j * 24))
        kick_dismiss_btn_rect.x = popup_rect.centerx - kick_dismiss_btn_rect.w // 2
        kick_dismiss_btn_rect.y = popup_rect.bottom - 46
        btn_hover = kick_dismiss_btn_rect.collidepoint(mouse_pos)
        bg_col = (60, 25, 25) if btn_hover else (40, 18, 18)
        outline_col = (220, 80, 80) if btn_hover else (140, 50, 50)
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, bg_col, kick_dismiss_btn_rect, border_radius=3)
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, outline_col, kick_dismiss_btn_rect, 1, border_radius=3)
        ok = font_btn.render("DISMISS", True, C_WHITE if btn_hover else (200, 180, 180))
        screen.blit(
            ok,
            (
                kick_dismiss_btn_rect.centerx - ok.get_width() // 2,
                kick_dismiss_btn_rect.centery - ok.get_height() // 2,
            ),
        )
        hint2 = font_terminal_small.render("[ESC] also dismisses", True, (110, 70, 70))
        screen.blit(
            hint2,
            (
                popup_rect.centerx - hint2.get_width() // 2,
                kick_dismiss_btn_rect.bottom + 4,
            ),
        )
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# SETTINGS (tabbed: Display / Audio / Accessibility)
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
SETTINGS_RESOLUTIONS = ["1920x1080", "1600x900", "1280x720"]
DISPLAY_MODES = ["Windowed", "Fullscreen"]
SETTINGS_TABS = ["DISPLAY", "AUDIO", "ACCESSIBILITY"]

SETTINGS_PANEL_W = 640
SETTINGS_PANEL_H = 520
SETTINGS_PANEL_X = WIDTH // 2 - SETTINGS_PANEL_W // 2
SETTINGS_PANEL_Y = 70

# Tab bar
TAB_BAR_Y = SETTINGS_PANEL_Y + 80
TAB_W = SETTINGS_PANEL_W // len(SETTINGS_TABS)
settings_tab_rects = [
    pygame.Rect(SETTINGS_PANEL_X + i * TAB_W, TAB_BAR_Y, TAB_W, 36)
    for i in range(len(SETTINGS_TABS))
]

# Content area
CONTENT_Y = TAB_BAR_Y + 60
LABEL_X = SETTINGS_PANEL_X + 50
CONTROL_X = SETTINGS_PANEL_X + 340

# ── DISPLAY tab rows ──
ROW_RES_Y = CONTENT_Y + 20
ROW_DISPLAY_Y = CONTENT_Y + 80
settings_res_left_rect    = pygame.Rect(CONTROL_X, ROW_RES_Y - 4, 32, 32)
settings_res_right_rect   = pygame.Rect(CONTROL_X + 188, ROW_RES_Y - 4, 32, 32)
settings_display_left_rect  = pygame.Rect(CONTROL_X, ROW_DISPLAY_Y - 4, 32, 32)
settings_display_right_rect = pygame.Rect(CONTROL_X + 188, ROW_DISPLAY_Y - 4, 32, 32)

# ── AUDIO tab rows (three sliders) ──
VOL_BAR_W = 200
VOL_BAR_X = CONTROL_X + 42
ROW_MASTER_Y = CONTENT_Y + 20
ROW_MUSIC_Y  = CONTENT_Y + 80
ROW_SFX_Y    = CONTENT_Y + 140
settings_master_left_rect  = pygame.Rect(CONTROL_X, ROW_MASTER_Y - 4, 32, 32)
settings_master_right_rect = pygame.Rect(VOL_BAR_X + VOL_BAR_W + 10, ROW_MASTER_Y - 4, 32, 32)
settings_master_bar_rect   = pygame.Rect(VOL_BAR_X, ROW_MASTER_Y + 8, VOL_BAR_W, 16)
settings_music_left_rect   = pygame.Rect(CONTROL_X, ROW_MUSIC_Y - 4, 32, 32)
settings_music_right_rect  = pygame.Rect(VOL_BAR_X + VOL_BAR_W + 10, ROW_MUSIC_Y - 4, 32, 32)
settings_music_bar_rect    = pygame.Rect(VOL_BAR_X, ROW_MUSIC_Y + 8, VOL_BAR_W, 16)
settings_sfx_left_rect     = pygame.Rect(CONTROL_X, ROW_SFX_Y - 4, 32, 32)
settings_sfx_right_rect    = pygame.Rect(VOL_BAR_X + VOL_BAR_W + 10, ROW_SFX_Y - 4, 32, 32)
settings_sfx_bar_rect      = pygame.Rect(VOL_BAR_X, ROW_SFX_Y + 8, VOL_BAR_W, 16)

# ── ACCESSIBILITY tab rows ──
ROW_REDUCED_MOTION_Y = CONTENT_Y + 20
settings_reduced_motion_rect = pygame.Rect(CONTROL_X, ROW_REDUCED_MOTION_Y - 2, 60, 28)

# Pre-rendered volume bar gradient
_VOL_GRADIENT = pygame.Surface((VOL_BAR_W, 16))
for vx in range(VOL_BAR_W):
    t = vx / VOL_BAR_W
    _VOL_GRADIENT.set_at((vx, 0), (int(200 - t * 40), int(150 - t * 60), 50))

# Back buttons (distinct instances for settings and server browser)
settings_back_btn = ModernButton(
    WIDTH // 2 - 150,
    SETTINGS_PANEL_Y + SETTINGS_PANEL_H - 60,
    "BACK",
)
server_back_btn = ModernButton(0, 0, "BACK")


def _draw_arrow_btn(rect, sym):
    hover = rect.collidepoint(get_scaled_mouse())
    bg = (90, 92, 88) if hover else (50, 52, 48)
    pygame.draw.rect(screen, bg, rect)
    pygame.draw.rect(screen, C_ORANGE if hover else (90, 90, 90), rect, 1)
    c = font_btn.render(sym, True, C_WHITE)
    screen.blit(c, (rect.centerx - c.get_width() // 2, rect.centery - c.get_height() // 2))


def _draw_volume_slider(label, bar_rect, left_rect, right_rect, value, row_y):
    screen.blit(font_terminal_small.render(label, True, C_GREY), (LABEL_X, row_y + 3))
    _draw_arrow_btn(left_rect, "<")
    pygame.draw.rect(screen, (25, 25, 28), bar_rect)
    fill_w = int(bar_rect.width * value / 100)
    if fill_w > 0:
        screen.blit(_VOL_GRADIENT, bar_rect.topleft, (0, 0, fill_w, bar_rect.height))
    pygame.draw.rect(screen, (80, 80, 85), bar_rect, 1)
    _draw_arrow_btn(right_rect, ">")
    pct = font_terminal.render(f"{value:>3}%", True, C_WHITE)
    screen.blit(pct, (right_rect.right + 12, row_y + 2))


def _draw_toggle(label, rect, value, row_y):
    screen.blit(font_terminal_small.render(label, True, C_GREY), (LABEL_X, row_y + 3))
    hover = rect.collidepoint(get_scaled_mouse())
    on_col = (200, 140, 50) if value else (45, 45, 48)
    off_col = (90, 90, 90) if hover else (60, 60, 60)
    pygame.draw.rect(screen, on_col, rect)
    pygame.draw.rect(screen, C_ORANGE if hover else off_col, rect, 1)
    # knob
    knob_x = rect.right - 22 if value else rect.x + 4
    pygame.draw.rect(screen, C_WHITE, (knob_x, rect.y + 4, 18, rect.height - 8))
    state_label = "ON" if value else "OFF"
    s = font_terminal_small.render(state_label, True, C_WHITE)
    screen.blit(s, (rect.right + 12, row_y + 4))


_settings_overlay = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
_settings_overlay.fill((0, 0, 0, 180))
_settings_panel = pygame.Surface((SETTINGS_PANEL_W, SETTINGS_PANEL_H), pygame.SRCALPHA)
_settings_panel.fill((10, 12, 16, 245))
pygame.draw.rect(_settings_panel, (60, 60, 65), (0, 0, SETTINGS_PANEL_W, SETTINGS_PANEL_H), 1)


def draw_settings(active_tab, display_mode, res_idx,
                  master_volume, music_volume, sfx_volume,
                  reduced_motion):
    """Render the settings overlay.

    active_tab: 0 = DISPLAY, 1 = AUDIO, 2 = ACCESSIBILITY
    """
    screen.blit(_settings_overlay, (0, 0))
    screen.blit(_settings_panel, (SETTINGS_PANEL_X, SETTINGS_PANEL_Y))

    # Title
    title_surf = font_speaker.render("SETTINGS", True, C_WHITE)
    title_x = SETTINGS_PANEL_X + SETTINGS_PANEL_W // 2 - title_surf.get_width() // 2
    screen.blit(font_speaker.render("SETTINGS", True, (0, 0, 0)), (title_x + 2, SETTINGS_PANEL_Y + 27))
    screen.blit(title_surf, (title_x, SETTINGS_PANEL_Y + 25))
    pygame.draw.line(screen, C_ORANGE,
                     (SETTINGS_PANEL_X + 80, SETTINGS_PANEL_Y + 68),
                     (SETTINGS_PANEL_X + SETTINGS_PANEL_W - 80, SETTINGS_PANEL_Y + 68), 1)

    # Tab bar
    mouse = get_scaled_mouse()
    for i, (rect, label) in enumerate(zip(settings_tab_rects, SETTINGS_TABS)):
        is_active = (i == active_tab)
        hover = rect.collidepoint(mouse)
        bg = (30, 22, 12) if is_active else ((22, 22, 26) if hover else (14, 14, 18))
        pygame.draw.rect(screen, bg, rect)
        # underline for active tab
        if is_active:
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, C_ORANGE, (rect.x, rect.bottom - 3, rect.width, 3))
        else:
            pygame.draw.line(screen, (50, 50, 55), (rect.x, rect.bottom), (rect.right, rect.bottom), 1)
        col = C_WHITE if is_active else (C_GREY if not hover else (210, 210, 210))
        ts = font_btn.render(label, True, col)
        screen.blit(ts, (rect.centerx - ts.get_width() // 2, rect.centery - ts.get_height() // 2))

    # Tab content
    if active_tab == 0:
        # DISPLAY
        screen.blit(font_terminal_small.render("Resolution", True, C_GREY),
                    (LABEL_X, ROW_RES_Y + 3))
        _draw_arrow_btn(settings_res_left_rect, "<")
        res_str = SETTINGS_RESOLUTIONS[res_idx] if res_idx < len(SETTINGS_RESOLUTIONS) else "N/A"
        res_surf = font_terminal.render(res_str, True, C_WHITE)
        res_box_cx = (settings_res_left_rect.right + settings_res_right_rect.left) // 2
        screen.blit(res_surf, (res_box_cx - res_surf.get_width() // 2, ROW_RES_Y))
        _draw_arrow_btn(settings_res_right_rect, ">")

        screen.blit(font_terminal_small.render("Display Mode", True, C_GREY),
                    (LABEL_X, ROW_DISPLAY_Y + 3))
        _draw_arrow_btn(settings_display_left_rect, "<")
        mode_str = DISPLAY_MODES[display_mode] if display_mode < len(DISPLAY_MODES) else "N/A"
        mode_surf = font_terminal.render(mode_str, True, C_WHITE)
        mode_box_cx = (settings_display_left_rect.right + settings_display_right_rect.left) // 2
        screen.blit(mode_surf, (mode_box_cx - mode_surf.get_width() // 2, ROW_DISPLAY_Y))
        _draw_arrow_btn(settings_display_right_rect, ">")

    elif active_tab == 1:
        # AUDIO
        _draw_volume_slider("Master Volume", settings_master_bar_rect,
                            settings_master_left_rect, settings_master_right_rect,
                            master_volume, ROW_MASTER_Y)
        _draw_volume_slider("Music Volume", settings_music_bar_rect,
                            settings_music_left_rect, settings_music_right_rect,
                            music_volume, ROW_MUSIC_Y)
        _draw_volume_slider("SFX Volume", settings_sfx_bar_rect,
                            settings_sfx_left_rect, settings_sfx_right_rect,
                            sfx_volume, ROW_SFX_Y)

    else:
        # ACCESSIBILITY
        _draw_toggle("Reduced Motion", settings_reduced_motion_rect,
                     reduced_motion, ROW_REDUCED_MOTION_Y)
        # Helper text under the toggle
        hint = font_terminal_small.render(
            "Disables screen shake and bright full-screen flashes.",
            True, (140, 140, 140))
        screen.blit(hint, (LABEL_X, ROW_REDUCED_MOTION_Y + 44))

    # Back button
    settings_back_btn.x = WIDTH // 2 - 150
    settings_back_btn.y = SETTINGS_PANEL_Y + SETTINGS_PANEL_H - 60
    settings_back_btn.width = 300
    settings_back_btn.rect = pygame.Rect(settings_back_btn.x, settings_back_btn.y, 300, 40)
    settings_back_btn.check_hover(get_scaled_mouse())
    settings_back_btn.draw(screen)
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# PAUSE / DEATH
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
pause_buttons = [
    ModernButton(WIDTH // 2 - 150, 280, "RESUME"),
    ModernButton(WIDTH // 2 - 150, 340, "RESTART MISSION"),
    ModernButton(WIDTH // 2 - 150, 400, "QUIT TO MENU"),
]
death_buttons = [
    ModernButton(WIDTH // 2 - 150, 330, "RESTART MISSION"),
    ModernButton(WIDTH // 2 - 150, 390, "QUIT TO MENU"),
]
_pause_overlay = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
_pause_overlay.fill((0, 0, 0, 180))
def draw_pause_menu():
    screen.blit(_pause_overlay, (0, 0))
    txt = font_speaker.render("PAUSED", True, C_WHITE)
    screen.blit(txt, (WIDTH // 2 - txt.get_width() // 2, 180))
    for btn in pause_buttons:
        btn.check_hover(get_scaled_mouse())
        btn.draw(screen)
_death_animation_start = 0
_death_particles = []
_death_font = pygame.font.SysFont("Arial Black", 70)
_death_sub_font = pygame.font.SysFont("Consolas", 22, bold=True)
_death_flavor_font = pygame.font.SysFont("Consolas", 16)
_server_title_font = pygame.font.SysFont("Impact", 46)
def reset_death_animation():
    global _death_animation_start, _death_particles
    _death_animation_start = 0
    _death_particles = []
def draw_death_screen():
    global _death_animation_start, _death_particles
    if _death_animation_start == 0:
        _death_animation_start = pygame.time.get_ticks()
        for _ in range(150):
            _death_particles.append({
                'x': random.randint(0, WIDTH),
                'y': random.randint(0, HEIGHT),
                'r': random.randint(2, 7),
                'a': random.randint(60, 160),
                'vy': random.uniform(0.1, 0.4)
            })
    elapsed = pygame.time.get_ticks() - _death_animation_start
    dark_alpha = min(180, int(elapsed * 0.15))
    overlay = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    overlay.fill((10, 5, 5, dark_alpha))
    screen.blit(overlay, (0, 0))
    beat_cycle = elapsed % 1200
    if beat_cycle < 200:
        beat_intensity = 1.0 - (beat_cycle / 200.0)
    else:
        beat_intensity = 0.0
    vig_alpha = int(100 * beat_intensity) + 30
    vig = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    for i in range(0, WIDTH, 3):
        a = max(0, vig_alpha - int(vig_alpha * i / WIDTH * 1.2))
        if a > 0:
            pygame.draw.rect(vig, (120, 0, 0, a), (i, i, WIDTH - i * 2, HEIGHT - i * 2), 3)
    screen.blit(vig, (0, 0))
    for p in _death_particles:
        p['y'] += p['vy']
        pygame.draw.circle(screen, (80, 5, 5, p['a']), (int(p['x']), int(p['y'])), p['r'])
    if elapsed < 400:
        flash_alpha = max(0, 255 - int(elapsed * 1.2))
        flash = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        if elapsed < 100:
            flash_color = (255, 220, 220, flash_alpha)
        else:
            flash_color = (150, 10, 10, flash_alpha)
        flash.fill(flash_color)
        screen.blit(flash, (0, 0))
    if elapsed > 600:
        text_alpha = min(255, (elapsed - 600) * 2)
        offset_x, offset_y = 0, 0
        if elapsed < 700:
            offset_x = random.randint(-8, 8)
            offset_y = random.randint(-4, 4)
        shadow_surf = _death_font.render("MISSION FAILED", True, (0, 0, 0))
        shadow_surf.set_alpha(text_alpha)
        title_y = HEIGHT // 2 - 140
        screen.blit(shadow_surf, (WIDTH // 2 - shadow_surf.get_width() // 2 + 4 + offset_x, title_y + 4 + offset_y))
        text_surf = _death_font.render("MISSION FAILED", True, (210, 30, 30))
        text_surf.set_alpha(text_alpha)
        screen.blit(text_surf, (WIDTH // 2 - text_surf.get_width() // 2 + offset_x, title_y + offset_y))
    if elapsed > 1000:
        sub_alpha = min(255, (elapsed - 1000) * 2)
        if getattr(STATE, "active_mission", "MISSION1") == "MISSION2":
            sub_text = "OPERATION QUIET ON ROUTE 12 COMPROMISED"
            flavor_text = "VASILE LINK: LOST. IPA CLEANUP TEAMS CLOSING ON ROUTE 12."
        else:
            sub_text = "OPERATION BLACK NOTEBOOK COMPROMISED"
            flavor_text = "KRAP COMMAND: SIGNAL LOST. DISPATCHING CLEANUP CREW."
        sub = _death_sub_font.render(sub_text, True, (180, 180, 180))
        sub.set_alpha(sub_alpha)
        screen.blit(sub, (WIDTH // 2 - sub.get_width() // 2, HEIGHT // 2 - 60))
        flavor = _death_flavor_font.render(flavor_text, True, (120, 120, 120))
        flavor.set_alpha(sub_alpha)
        screen.blit(flavor, (WIDTH // 2 - flavor.get_width() // 2, HEIGHT // 2 - 30))
    if elapsed > 1150:
        panel_alpha = min(230, int((elapsed - 1150) * 0.35))
        try:
            import walking_sim
            civilians_helped = sum(1 for n in walking_sim.walk_npcs if n.get("has_given"))
            rations_used = max(0, 2 - int(walking_sim.player_rations))
        except Exception:
            civilians_helped = 0
            rations_used = 0
        end_t = STATE.mission_end_time or pygame.time.get_ticks()
        start_t = STATE.mission_start_time or end_t
        elapsed_sec = max(0, (end_t - start_t) // 1000)
        accuracy = int((STATE.shots_hit / STATE.shots_fired) * 100) if STATE.shots_fired > 0 else 0
        if getattr(STATE, "active_mission", "MISSION1") == "MISSION2":
            evidence_found = 0
            try:
                evidence_found = sum(1 for item in getattr(STATE, "mission2_evidence", []) if item.get("done"))
            except Exception:
                evidence_found = 0
            stats = [
                ("KILLS", str(STATE.hostiles_killed)),
                ("SHOTS FIRED", str(STATE.shots_fired)),
                ("ACCURACY", f"{accuracy}%"),
                ("TOTAL TIME", f"{elapsed_sec // 60:02d}:{elapsed_sec % 60:02d}"),
                ("EVIDENCE FOUND", f"{evidence_found}/3"),
                ("COMMS", "OFFLINE" if getattr(STATE, "mission2_comms_cut", False) else "VASILE"),
            ]
        else:
            stats = [
                ("KILLS", str(STATE.hostiles_killed)),
                ("SHOTS FIRED", str(STATE.shots_fired)),
                ("ACCURACY", f"{accuracy}%"),
                ("TOTAL TIME", f"{elapsed_sec // 60:02d}:{elapsed_sec % 60:02d}"),
                ("CIVILIANS HELPED", str(civilians_helped)),
                ("RATIONS USED", str(rations_used)),
            ]
        panel_w, panel_h = 520, 104
        panel_x = WIDTH // 2 - panel_w // 2
        panel_y = HEIGHT // 2 + 16
        panel = pygame.Surface((panel_w, panel_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        panel.fill((8, 8, 12, panel_alpha))
        pygame.draw.rect(panel, (120, 35, 35, min(160, panel_alpha)), panel.get_rect(), 1)
        pygame.draw.rect(panel, (170, 35, 35, min(200, panel_alpha)), (0, 0, 4, panel_h))
        screen.blit(panel, (panel_x, panel_y))
        for i, (label, value) in enumerate(stats):
            col = i % 3
            row = i // 3
            sx = panel_x + 24 + col * 165
            sy = panel_y + 16 + row * 42
            lbl = font_terminal_small.render(label, True, (135, 80, 80))
            val = _death_sub_font.render(value, True, (220, 210, 205))
            lbl.set_alpha(panel_alpha)
            val.set_alpha(panel_alpha)
            screen.blit(lbl, (sx, sy))
            screen.blit(val, (sx, sy + 15))
    if elapsed > 600 and random.random() > 0.92:
        glitch_y = random.randint(0, HEIGHT)
        glitch_h = random.randint(2, 8)
        glitch_surf = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, glitch_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        glitch_surf.fill((255, 255, 255, 40))
        screen.blit(glitch_surf, (random.randint(-10, 10), glitch_y))
    if elapsed > 1500:
        btn_progress = min(1.0, (elapsed - 1500) / 600.0)
        ease = 1 - (1 - btn_progress) ** 3
        for i, btn in enumerate(death_buttons):
            target_y = HEIGHT // 2 + 150 + i * 60
            start_y = target_y + 40
            current_y = start_y + (target_y - start_y) * ease
            btn.y = int(current_y)
            btn.rect.y = btn.y
            btn.check_hover(get_scaled_mouse())
            btn.draw(screen)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# audio.py

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# audio.py
# ------------------------------------------------------------

import pygame
import math
import random
import struct
import io
import array
import time

SAMPLE_RATE = 22050
MAX_AMP = 32767

def _arr(n):
    return array.array("h", [0]) * n

def _wav(samples):
    n = len(samples)
    data_size = n * 2
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    buf.write(b"RIFF")
    buf.write(struct.pack("<I", 36 + data_size))
    buf.write(b"WAVE")
    buf.write(b"fmt ")
    buf.write(struct.pack("<I", 16))
    buf.write(struct.pack("<H", 1))
    buf.write(struct.pack("<H", 1))
    buf.write(struct.pack("<I", SAMPLE_RATE))
    buf.write(struct.pack("<I", SAMPLE_RATE * 2))
    buf.write(struct.pack("<H", 2))
    buf.write(struct.pack("<H", 16))
    buf.write(b"data")
    buf.write(struct.pack("<I", data_size))
    buf.write(samples.tobytes())
    return buf.getvalue()

def _load(wav_bytes):
    return pygame.mixer.Sound(io.BytesIO(wav_bytes))

def _write(buf, samples, amp=1.0):
    for i in range(len(buf)):
        v = int(samples[i] * amp)
        buf[i] = max(-MAX_AMP, min(MAX_AMP, buf[i] + v))

def _sin(n, freq, amp=1.0, phase=0.0):
    a = _arr(n)
    step = 2 * math.pi * freq / SAMPLE_RATE
    sin = math.sin
    max_amp_amp = MAX_AMP * amp
    for i in range(n):
        a[i] = int(max_amp_amp * sin(phase))
        phase += step
    return a

def _noise(n, amp=1.0):
    a = _arr(n)
    max_amp_amp = MAX_AMP * amp
    for i in range(n):
        a[i] = int(max_amp_amp * (random.random() * 2 - 1))
    return a

def _env_linear(buf, start=1.0, end=0.0):
    n = len(buf)
    for i in range(n):
        t = start + (end - start) * (i / n)
        buf[i] = int(buf[i] * t)

def _env_exp(buf, start=1.0, end=0.001, rate=5.0):
    n = len(buf)
    for i in range(n):
        t = start * math.exp(-rate * i / n)
        if t < end:
            t = 0
        buf[i] = int(buf[i] * t)

def _env_adsr(buf, attack=0.1, decay=0.2, sustain=0.5, release=0.2, sus_level=0.6):
    n = len(buf)
    a_i = int(n * attack)
    d_i = int(n * decay)
    s_i = int(n * (1 - release))
    for i in range(n):
        if i < a_i:
            t = i / a_i
        elif i < a_i + d_i:
            t = 1.0 - (1.0 - sus_level) * ((i - a_i) / d_i)
        elif i < s_i:
            t = sus_level
        else:
            t = sus_level * (1.0 - (i - s_i) / (n - s_i))
        buf[i] = int(buf[i] * t)

def _mix(base, overlay, overlay_amp=1.0):
    for i in range(min(len(base), len(overlay))):
        v = int(overlay[i] * overlay_amp)
        base[i] = max(-MAX_AMP, min(MAX_AMP, base[i] + v))

def _sine_sweep(n, f_start, f_end, amp=1.0):
    a = _arr(n)
    phase = 0.0
    sin = math.sin
    max_amp_amp = MAX_AMP * amp
    for i in range(n):
        t = i / n
        freq = f_start + (f_end - f_start) * t
        a[i] = int(max_amp_amp * sin(phase))
        phase += 2 * math.pi * freq / SAMPLE_RATE
    return a

def _lowpass(buf, strength=0.3):
    prev = 0
    for i in range(len(buf)):
        prev = prev * (1 - strength) + buf[i] * strength
        buf[i] = int(prev)

def _harmonics(n, base_freq, amps, amp=1.0):
    a = _arr(n)
    for i, h_amp in enumerate(amps):
        if h_amp:
            h = _sin(n, base_freq * (i + 1), amp * h_amp)
            _mix(a, h)
    return a

# ── New Powerful DSP Helpers for 16-Bit DOS Style Sound Design ──

def _resonant_filter(buf, cutoff_hz, resonance=0.5, filter_type="low"):
    """
    Chamberlin State Variable Filter (SVF) for beautiful analog-sounding sweeps.
    resonance: damping Q factor from 0.0 (flat) to 0.95 (ringing self-oscillation)
    """
    n = len(buf)
    out = _arr(n)
    cutoff_hz = max(12, min(9500, cutoff_hz))
    
    # SVF coefficients
    f = 2.0 * math.sin(math.pi * cutoff_hz / SAMPLE_RATE)
    q = 2.0 * (1.0 - max(0.0, min(0.95, resonance)))
    
    low = 0.0
    band = 0.0
    
    for i in range(n):
        input_val = float(buf[i])
        high = input_val - low - q * band
        band += f * high
        low += f * band
        
        if filter_type == "low":
            val = low
        elif filter_type == "high":
            val = high
        elif filter_type == "band":
            val = band
        else: # notch
            val = high + low
            
        out[i] = int(max(-MAX_AMP, min(MAX_AMP, val)))
        
    for i in range(n):
        buf[i] = out[i]

def _fm(n, carrier_hz, carrier_amp, mod_hz, mod_index, decay_rate=0.0):
    """
    Two-Operator FM Synthesizer.
    Generates rich metallic, mechanical, brassy, or chest-pounding sounds.
    """
    buf = _arr(n)
    phase_c = 0.0
    phase_m = 0.0
    step_c = 2.0 * math.pi * carrier_hz / SAMPLE_RATE
    step_m = 2.0 * math.pi * mod_hz / SAMPLE_RATE
    
    sin = math.sin
    max_amp_camp = MAX_AMP * carrier_amp
    
    for i in range(n):
        mod_env = math.exp(-decay_rate * i / n) if decay_rate > 0 else 1.0
        current_mod_index = mod_index * mod_env
        
        # Modulator output
        mod_val = sin(phase_m)
        phase_m += step_m
        
        # Frequency modulation
        inst_freq_mod = current_mod_index * step_m * mod_val
        
        buf[i] = int(max_amp_camp * sin(phase_c))
        phase_c += step_c + inst_freq_mod
        
    return buf

def _apply_delay(buf, delay_ms, feedback=0.4, wet=0.3):
    """
    Feedback delay line to simulate space, echo, and acoustic room depth.
    """
    n = len(buf)
    delay_samples = int(SAMPLE_RATE * (delay_ms / 1000.0))
    if delay_samples <= 0 or delay_samples >= n:
        return
    
    history = [0.0] * delay_samples
    ptr = 0
    
    for i in range(n):
        dry = float(buf[i])
        delayed = history[ptr]
        
        # Calculate new delay state
        new_history = dry + feedback * delayed
        history[ptr] = max(-MAX_AMP, min(MAX_AMP, new_history))
        ptr = (ptr + 1) % delay_samples
        
        # Mix dry and wet signals
        mixed = dry * (1.0 - wet) + delayed * wet
        buf[i] = int(max(-MAX_AMP, min(MAX_AMP, mixed)))

def _soft_clip(buf, drive=1.0):
    """
    Cubic saturation model to add warmth, weight, and prevent harsh digital clipping.
    """
    for i in range(len(buf)):
        x = (buf[i] / MAX_AMP) * drive
        # Cubic soft clipper
        if x > 1.25:
            y = 1.0
        elif x < -1.25:
            y = -1.0
        else:
            y = x - (x**3) / 3.0
            y = y * 0.95
        buf[i] = int(max(-1.0, min(1.0, y)) * MAX_AMP)

def _offset_mix(base, overlay, offset=0, amp=1.0):
    for i in range(min(len(overlay), len(base) - offset)):
        if offset + i < 0:
            continue
        v = int(overlay[i] * amp)
        base[offset + i] = max(-MAX_AMP, min(MAX_AMP, base[offset + i] + v))

# ── Upgraded DOS/16-Bit Sound Generators ──

def _gen_gunshot_core(volume=1.0, lowpass_strength=0.0):
    """
    Layered gunshot: sharp transient crack → resonant muzzle blast → chest-hitting FM thump → metallic FM casing drop → simulated echo chamber delay.
    """
    dur = 0.85
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    # Layer 1: Ultra-sharp initial transient (1ms of noise)
    crack_n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.001)
    crack = _noise(crack_n, 1.0 * volume)
    _mix(buf, crack)
    
    # Layer 2: Muzzle blast - mid-range noise, resonant lowpass filter
    blast_n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.1)
    blast = _noise(blast_n, 0.78 * volume)
    _resonant_filter(blast, 800, 0.35, "low")
    _env_exp(blast, 1.0, 0.001, 24.0)
    _offset_mix(buf, blast, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.001))
    
    # Layer 3: Chest-hitting FM sub thump (Simulates raw kinetic pressure wave)
    thump = _fm(int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.35), 55, 0.9 * volume, 110, 1.8, decay_rate=7.0)
    _env_exp(thump, 1.0, 0.001, 6.0)
    _offset_mix(buf, thump, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.003))
    
    # Layer 4: Upper-mid body crackle
    mid_n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.15)
    mid = _noise(mid_n, 0.52 * volume)
    _resonant_filter(mid, 2200, 0.2, "low")
    _env_exp(mid, 1.0, 0.001, 15.0)
    _offset_mix(buf, mid, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.002))
    
    # Layer 5: Meaty body resonance (FM carrier 200Hz, modulated by 100Hz)
    body_n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.18)
    body = _fm(body_n, 200, 0.36 * volume, 100, 0.8, decay_rate=12.0)
    _env_exp(body, 1.0, 0.001, 14.0)
    _offset_mix(buf, body, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.002))
    
    # Layer 6: Brass shell casing hitting floor (Realistic metallic ring via FM)
    casing_delay = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.4)
    casing_n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.15)
    casing = _fm(casing_n, 5200, 0.18 * volume, 8200, 3.5, decay_rate=18.0)
    _env_exp(casing, 1.0, 0.001, 14.0)
    _offset_mix(buf, casing, casing_delay)
    
    # Feedback echo simulating tight indoor warehouse walls
    _apply_delay(buf, 120, feedback=0.35, wet=0.25)
    _apply_delay(buf, 45, feedback=0.2, wet=0.15)
    
    # Cubic saturation and limiting
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.45)
    _env_exp(buf, 1.0, 0.001, 1.4)
    
    if lowpass_strength > 0:
        _resonant_filter(buf, int(750 * (1.0 - lowpass_strength)), 0.1)
        
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_gunshot():
    return _gen_gunshot_core(volume=1.0, lowpass_strength=0.0)

def _gen_gunshot_distant():
    """Muffled distant version: high-frequencies stripped, bass thuds enhanced."""
    return _gen_gunshot_core(volume=0.5, lowpass_strength=0.075)


def _gen_sniper_crack():
    """High-powered distant sniper rifle: supersonic snap + delayed heavy report."""
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 1.15)
    buf = _arr(n)

    # Instant supersonic crack.
    snap = _noise(int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.004), 0.98)
    _resonant_filter(snap, 4200, 0.18, "high")
    _env_exp(snap, 1.0, 0.001, 80.0)
    _mix(buf, snap)

    # Rifle report arrives a hair later.
    blast = _noise(int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.16), 0.62)
    _resonant_filter(blast, 620, 0.38, "low")
    _env_exp(blast, 1.0, 0.001, 16.0)
    _offset_mix(buf, blast, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.035))

    # Distant valley/building slap echo.
    echo = _noise(int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.34), 0.24)
    _resonant_filter(echo, 280, 0.35, "low")
    _env_exp(echo, 1.0, 0.001, 6.2)
    _offset_mix(buf, echo, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.19))

    # Low pressure bloom.
    sub = _fm(int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.55), 45, 0.34, 22, 0.7, decay_rate=4.8)
    _env_exp(sub, 1.0, 0.001, 5.0)
    _offset_mix(buf, sub, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.045))

    _apply_delay(buf, 170, feedback=0.26, wet=0.18)
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.45)
    _env_exp(buf, 1.0, 0.001, 1.25)
    return _wav(buf)


def _gen_bullet_snap():
    """Terrifying near-miss whip/snap passing the listener."""
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.22)
    buf = _arr(n)
    zip1 = _sine_sweep(int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.07), 6200, 1700, 0.42)
    _env_exp(zip1, 1.0, 0.001, 18.0)
    _mix(buf, zip1)
    grit = _noise(int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.035), 0.34)
    _resonant_filter(grit, 5200, 0.2, "high")
    _env_exp(grit, 1.0, 0.001, 55.0)
    _offset_mix(buf, grit, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.006))
    tail = _sine_sweep(int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.12), 1800, 520, 0.16)
    _env_exp(tail, 1.0, 0.001, 13.0)
    _offset_mix(buf, tail, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.035))
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.2)
    return _wav(buf)


def _gen_concrete_impact():
    """Heavy concrete bullet strike with debris scatter."""
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.32)
    buf = _arr(n)
    hit = _noise(int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.028), 0.92)
    _resonant_filter(hit, 760, 0.42, "low")
    _env_exp(hit, 1.0, 0.001, 28.0)
    _mix(buf, hit)
    crunch = _noise(int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.18), 0.38)
    _resonant_filter(crunch, 420, 0.55)
    _env_exp(crunch, 1.0, 0.001, 9.5)
    _offset_mix(buf, crunch, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.012))
    for off_ms in (38, 74, 112):
        chip = _noise(int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.018), 0.22)
        _resonant_filter(chip, 2400, 0.25, "high")
        _env_exp(chip, 1.0, 0.001, 36.0)
        _offset_mix(buf, chip, int(SAMPLE_RATE * off_ms / 1000))
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.35)
    return _wav(buf)


def _gen_tinnitus():
    """Post-near-miss ear ringing with slow fade."""
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 2.1)
    buf = _arr(n)
    tone = _sin(n, 3180, 0.24)
    wobble = _sin(n, 7.0, 0.06)
    for i in range(n):
        # Amplitude shimmer, intentionally uncomfortable but not too loud.
        amp = 0.72 + 0.28 * math.sin(i * 0.004)
        buf[i] = int(tone[i] * amp + wobble[i] * 0.2)
    _env_adsr(buf, 0.02, 0.18, 0.42, 0.58, 0.72)
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.05)
    return _wav(buf)


def _gen_rifle_handle():
    """Small metallic rifle handling tick for origin reveal tension."""
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.14)
    buf = _arr(n)
    for off_ms, freq in [(0, 1800), (38, 2600), (82, 1200)]:
        click = _fm(int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.025), freq, 0.22, freq * 1.6, 2.4, decay_rate=35.0)
        _env_exp(click, 1.0, 0.001, 42.0)
        _offset_mix(buf, click, int(SAMPLE_RATE * off_ms / 1000))
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.1)
    return _wav(buf)


def _gen_footstep_variant(pitch_mul=1.0, amp=0.3, dur=0.045):
    """Foley style walk steps: punchy, organic, and clean of boxiness."""
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    # Heel strike: FM low-frequency thump (removes paper-thin sound)
    thump = _fm(n, int(82 * pitch_mul), amp, int(41 * pitch_mul), 0.5, decay_rate=20.0)
    _env_exp(thump, 1.0, 0.001, 28.0)
    
    # Friction texture: brief filtered noise
    grit = _noise(n, amp * 0.32)
    _resonant_filter(grit, 450, 0.2)
    _env_exp(grit, 1.0, 0.001, 32.0)
    
    # Toe-off tap transient
    click_n = min(n, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.008))
    click = _noise(click_n, amp * 0.45)
    _resonant_filter(click, 1800, 0.15)
    _env_exp(click, 1.0, 0.001, 60.0)
    
    _mix(buf, thump)
    _mix(buf, grit)
    _offset_mix(buf, click, 0)
    
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.1)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_footstep():
    return _gen_footstep_variant(pitch_mul=1.05, amp=0.34, dur=0.042)

def _gen_footstep_b():
    """Heavier boot step: deeper FM thump, wider gravel scrape."""
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.055)
    buf = _arr(n)
    thump = _fm(n, 70, 0.38, 35, 0.7, decay_rate=15.0)
    _env_exp(thump, 1.0, 0.001, 22.0)
    
    slap = _noise(n, 0.32)
    _resonant_filter(slap, 320, 0.15)
    _env_exp(slap, 1.0, 0.001, 26.0)
    
    _mix(buf, thump)
    _mix(buf, slap)
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.15)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_footstep_c():
    """Light quick sneaker tap."""
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.038)
    buf = _arr(n)
    thump = _fm(n, 105, 0.3, 52, 0.4, decay_rate=25.0)
    _env_exp(thump, 1.0, 0.001, 32.0)
    
    click = _noise(n, 0.38)
    _resonant_filter(click, 1400, 0.3)
    _env_exp(click, 1.0, 0.001, 45.0)
    
    _mix(buf, thump)
    _mix(buf, click)
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.1)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_footstep_walk_variant(pitch_mul=1.0, amp=0.22, dur=0.060):
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    thump = _fm(n, int(76 * pitch_mul), amp * 0.6, int(38 * pitch_mul), 0.3, decay_rate=18.0)
    _env_exp(thump, 1.0, 0.001, 18.0)
    
    scruff = _noise(n, amp * 0.42)
    _resonant_filter(scruff, 380, 0.1)
    _env_exp(scruff, 1.0, 0.001, 20.0)
    
    _mix(buf, thump)
    _mix(buf, scruff)
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.0)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_footstep_walk():
    return _gen_footstep_walk_variant(pitch_mul=1.0, amp=0.22, dur=0.060)

def _gen_footstep_walk_b():

def _gen_melee_hit():
    """Fleshy blunt trauma: solid FM thump + wood/bone shatter + resonant low slap."""
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.18)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    # Crack transient
    crack_n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.003)
    crack = _noise(crack_n, 0.85)
    _mix(buf, crack)
    
    # Low body punch (FM synthesis carrier 60Hz modulated by 120Hz)
    punch = _fm(n, 60, 0.65, 120, 1.6, decay_rate=14.0)
    _env_exp(punch, 1.0, 0.001, 12.0)
    _mix(buf, punch)
    
    # Bone/flesh resonance harmonics
    thump = _harmonics(n, 160, [0.6, 0.3, 0.1], 0.42)
    _env_exp(thump, 1.0, 0.001, 10.0)
    _mix(buf, thump)
    
    # Ringing knuckles/skull (metallic ping)
    ring_n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.06)
    ring = _fm(ring_n, 1200, 0.15, 2400, 2.5, decay_rate=30.0)
    _env_exp(ring, 1.0, 0.001, 25.0)
    _offset_mix(buf, ring, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.005))
    
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.35)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_bullet_impact():
    """Concrete ricochet: hard punch, debris lowpass hiss, and dual FM metallic whine."""
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.08)

    tick_n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.0015)

    _mix(buf, tick)

    body = _noise(n, 0.48)
    _resonant_filter(body, 650, 0.4, "low")
    _env_exp(body, 1.0, 0.001, 35.0)
    _mix(buf, body)
    
    # Ricochet whine (FM sine sweep: gorgeous laser-ricochet ring!)
    whine_n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.035)

    _env_exp(whine, 1.0, 0.001, 40.0)
    _offset_mix(buf, whine, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.002))
    
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.25)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_door_creak():
    """Wooden friction creak: slow pitch FM modulator sweep + resonant low groan."""
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.6)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    # Swept FM friction squeak
    squeak = _fm(n, 340, 0.28, 48, 1.8)
    for i in range(n):
        t = i / n
        pitch_mod = 1.0 + 0.55 * math.sin(t * math.pi * 3.5)
        squeak[i] = int(squeak[i] * pitch_mod)
    _env_adsr(squeak, 0.12, 0.28, 0.45, 0.15, 0.5)
    
    # Wood body groan noise (bandpassed)
    groan = _noise(n, 0.24)
    _resonant_filter(groan, 220, 0.65, "low")
    _env_exp(groan, 1.0, 0.001, 4.0)
    
    _mix(buf, squeak)
    _mix(buf, groan)
    _apply_delay(buf, 40, feedback=0.25, wet=0.2)
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.25)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_door_rattle():
    """Heavy steel-reinforced door punch: massive sub-thump + dual-phase FM metallic rattle."""
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.35)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    thud = _noise(n, 0.6)
    _resonant_filter(thud, 180, 0.5, "low")
    _env_exp(thud, 1.0, 0.001, 14.0)
    _mix(buf, thud)
    
    sub = _fm(n, 48, 0.62, 24, 1.0, decay_rate=12.0)
    _env_exp(sub, 1.0, 0.001, 10.0)
    _mix(buf, sub)
    
    # Scattered metallic rattles
    for delay_sec, amp in ((0.04, 0.38), (0.10, 0.26), (0.17, 0.14)):
        off = int(SAMPLE_RATE * delay_sec)
        rattle_n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.06)
        r = _fm(rattle_n, 850, amp, 1400, 3.2, decay_rate=22.0)
        _env_exp(r, 1.0, 0.001, 30.0)
        _offset_mix(buf, r, off)
        
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.35)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_door_open():
    """Deadbolt mechanics: crisp latch snap + metal handle friction sweep."""

    buf = _arr(n)

    click_n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.02)

    _env_exp(click, 1.0, 0.001, 40.0)
    _offset_mix(buf, click, 0)
    
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.2)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_reload():
    """Tactical reloading: Mag Out (crisp metallic friction) → Mag In (deep mechanical slam) → Slide Release (sliding metal click)."""
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.6)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    # Mag Out (high metallic scrape, 0.05s)
    m1_n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.04)
    mag_out = _fm(m1_n, 1200, 0.58, 1900, 3.5, decay_rate=25.0)
    _env_exp(mag_out, 1.0, 0.001, 35.0)
    _offset_mix(buf, mag_out, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.05))
    
    # Mag In (Heavy chamber latch slam, 0.25s)
    m2_n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.05)
    mag_in_fm = _fm(m2_n, 780, 0.65, 1200, 2.8, decay_rate=20.0)
    mag_in_noise = _noise(m2_n, 0.25)
    _resonant_filter(mag_in_noise, 1100, 0.3)
    _mix(mag_in_fm, mag_in_noise)
    _env_exp(mag_in_fm, 1.0, 0.001, 25.0)
    _offset_mix(buf, mag_in_fm, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.25))
    
    # Slide Release (Friction-slide recoil metal, 0.4s)
    s_n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.18)
    slide = _fm(s_n, 160, 0.48, 620, 4.2, decay_rate=6.0)
    slide_noise = _noise(s_n, 0.18)
    _resonant_filter(slide_noise, 2800, 0.45)
    _mix(slide, slide_noise)
    _env_adsr(slide, 0.02, 0.05, 0.55, 0.1, 0.65)
    _offset_mix(buf, slide, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.4))
    
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.35)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_ambient_drone():
    """Deep industrial machine hum: Tri-operator FM drone with perfect 5th harmonics + LFO swept resonant lowpass filter."""
    dur = 4.0
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    # Stacking FM drone operators (sub, mid, fifth)
    drone1 = _fm(n, 55, 0.30, 11, 2.0)
    drone2 = _fm(n, 82.5, 0.18, 16.5, 1.5)  # Perfect fifth
    drone3 = _fm(n, 110, 0.12, 5.5, 1.0)
    _mix(buf, drone1)
    _mix(buf, drone2)
    _mix(buf, drone3)
    
    # Moving resonant lowpass filter on noise bed (Slow sweep over 4 seconds)
    noise = _noise(n, 0.14)
    out_noise = _arr(n)
    low = 0.0
    band = 0.0
    for i in range(n):
        # LFO modulating cutoff from 130Hz to 230Hz
        cutoff = 180.0 + 50.0 * math.sin(i * 0.0003)
        f = 2.0 * math.sin(math.pi * cutoff / SAMPLE_RATE)
        high = float(noise[i]) - low - 0.45 * band
        band += f * high
        low += f * band
        out_noise[i] = int(max(-MAX_AMP, min(MAX_AMP, band)))
    _mix(buf, out_noise)
    
    # Soft global loop boundaries fade
    fade = int(n * 0.05)
    for i in range(fade):
        t = i / fade
        buf[i] = int(buf[i] * t)
        buf[n - 1 - i] = int(buf[n - 1 - i] * t)
        
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.1)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_rain():
    """Moody weather: heavily filtered cloudpour noise + random scattered droplet splashes filtered through a high-resonance peak."""
    dur = 3.0
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    # Overlapping rain roar
    noise = _noise(n, 0.28)
    _resonant_filter(noise, 450, 0.1, "low")
    _mix(buf, noise)
    
    # Procedural droplet drops
    for _ in range(25):
        tick_pos = random.randint(0, n - int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.015))
        tick_len = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.012)
        tick = _noise(tick_len, 0.35)
        _resonant_filter(tick, 2200, 0.5)
        _env_exp(tick, 1.0, 0.001, 45.0)
        _offset_mix(buf, tick, tick_pos)
        
    fade = int(n * 0.03)
    for i in range(fade):
        t = i / fade
        buf[i] = int(buf[i] * t)
        buf[n - 1 - i] = int(buf[n - 1 - i] * t)
        
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.1)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_wind():
    """Swirling storm gusts: slow swept resonant filter on wind noise + sub bass rumble."""
    dur = 4.0
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    noise = _noise(n, 0.15)
    # Slow LFO frequency sweep to emulate rushing wind gusts
    low = 0.0
    band = 0.0
    q = 0.32
    for i in range(n):
        t = i / SAMPLE_RATE
        cutoff = 380.0 + 160.0 * math.sin(t * 0.8) + 60.0 * math.cos(t * 1.6)
        f = 2.0 * math.sin(math.pi * cutoff / SAMPLE_RATE)
        high = float(noise[i]) - low - q * band
        band += f * high
        low += f * band
        buf[i] = int(max(-MAX_AMP, min(MAX_AMP, low)))
        
    # Deep physical sub roar (modulated volume)
    sub = _sin(n, 42, 0.06)
    for i in range(n):
        t = i / SAMPLE_RATE
        sub[i] = int(sub[i] * (0.35 + 0.65 * math.sin(t * 0.5)))
    _mix(buf, sub)
    
    fade = int(n * 0.05)
    for i in range(fade):
        t = i / fade
        buf[i] = int(buf[i] * t)
        buf[n - 1 - i] = int(buf[n - 1 - i] * t)
        
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.15)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_explosion():
    """Muffled devastating explosion: thick resonant low-pass roar + deep FM sub thuds + falling concrete crackles + room space echoes."""
    dur = 1.5
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    # 1. Main rumble
    noise = _noise(n, 0.82)
    _resonant_filter(noise, 220, 0.4, "low")
    _env_adsr(noise, 0.04, 0.28, 0.5, 0.15, 0.7)
    _mix(buf, noise)
    
    # 2. Huge dual FM chest thumps
    thump = _fm(n, 38, 0.8, 76, 1.8, decay_rate=5.0)
    _env_adsr(thump, 0.02, 0.4, 0.5, 0.05, 0.9)
    _mix(buf, thump)
    
    # 3. High-freq cracking debris
    for _ in range(8):
        off = random.randint(0, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.3))
        ln = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.08)
        crack = _noise(ln, 0.35)
        _resonant_filter(crack, 1500, 0.3)
        _env_exp(crack, 1.0, 0.001, 15.0)
        _offset_mix(buf, crack, off)
        
    # Global delay echo
    _apply_delay(buf, 180, feedback=0.45, wet=0.35)
    _apply_delay(buf, 75, feedback=0.25, wet=0.2)
    
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.5)
    _env_exp(buf, 1.0, 0.001, 2.5)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_heartbeat():
    """Deep chest pulse: dual-thump FM sweep for heavy pressure wave."""
    dur = 1.8
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    beat_n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.16)
    
    for offset in [int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.1), int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.42)]:
        # Dual-thump FM: carrier 45Hz modulated by 22Hz (very heavy sub bass)
        beat = _fm(beat_n, 45, 0.72, 22, 1.2, decay_rate=14.0)
        _env_exp(beat, 1.0, 0.001, 10.0)
        _offset_mix(buf, beat, offset)
        
    floor = _noise(n, 0.01)
    _mix(buf, floor)
    
    fade = int(n * 0.02)
    for i in range(fade):
        t = i / fade
        buf[i] = int(buf[i] * t)
        buf[n - 1 - i] = int(buf[n - 1 - i] * t)
    return _wav(buf)
