def _gen_radio_static():
    dur = 1.5
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    # Scattered static noise bursts
    for _ in range(30):
        burst_start = random.randint(0, n - int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.05))
        burst_len = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.02 + random.random() * 0.03)
        burst = _noise(burst_len, 0.45 + random.random() * 0.4)
        _resonant_filter(burst, 1200, 0.2)
        _env_exp(burst, 1.0, 0.001, 30.0)
        _offset_mix(buf, burst, burst_start)
        
    carrier = _noise(n, 0.08)
    _resonant_filter(carrier, 300, 0.1)
    _mix(buf, carrier)
    
    fade = int(n * 0.02)
    for i in range(fade):
        t = i / fade
        buf[i] = int(buf[i] * t)
        buf[n - 1 - i] = int(buf[n - 1 - i] * t)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_radio_chatter():
    """Garbled vocal transmission: ring modulator + swept bandpass on noise (classic military radio speaker feel)."""
    dur = 2.0
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    noise = _noise(n, 0.22)
    # Garble with Ring Modulation (multiplied by a 400Hz voice pitch)
    for i in range(n):
        mod = 0.35 + 0.65 * (0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * 380 * i / SAMPLE_RATE))
        buf[i] = int(noise[i] * mod)
        
    # Bandpass voice filter to isolate nasal speaking frequencies
    _resonant_filter(buf, 950, 0.6, "band")
    _env_adsr(buf, 0.08, 0.18, 0.5, 0.2, 0.5)
    
    # Intermittent squelch bursts
    for _ in range(8):
        burst_start = random.randint(0, n - int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.02))
        burst = _noise(int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.015), 0.32)
        _resonant_filter(burst, 3500, 0.3, "high")
        _env_exp(burst, 1.0, 0.001, 40.0)
        _offset_mix(buf, burst, burst_start)
        
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.35)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_water_drip():
    """Dripping cavern: swept sine bubble plop + resonant bandpass splash + delay echo."""
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.22)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    # Fast pitch slide (recreates water surface tension bubble snap)
    tone = _sine_sweep(n, 650, 1150, 0.45)
    _env_exp(tone, 1.0, 0.001, 14.0)
    _mix(buf, tone)
    
    # Debris splash
    splash = _noise(n, 0.14)
    _resonant_filter(splash, 2200, 0.4)
    _env_exp(splash, 1.0, 0.001, 28.0)
    _mix(buf, splash)
    
    _apply_delay(buf, 35, feedback=0.3, wet=0.25)
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.1)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_mission_complete():
    """16-bit arpeggiated victory chord: stacked major triads with FM synthesizer brass envelopes."""
    dur = 1.4
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    # Stacked major triad chord notes (C3, E3, G3, C4)
    freqs = [261.6, 329.6, 392.0, 523.3]
    note_dur = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.8)
    
    for idx, f in enumerate(freqs):
        # Cascaded starting delays (arpeggio style)
        delay = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.12 * idx)
        # FM brass operator (freq modulated by 1.5x ratio)
        tone = _fm(note_dur, f, 0.32, f * 1.5, 1.2, decay_rate=2.2)
        _env_adsr(tone, 0.08, 0.18, 0.6, 0.35, 0.7)
        _offset_mix(buf, tone, delay)
        
    _apply_delay(buf, 150, feedback=0.32, wet=0.22)
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.25)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_breach():
    """Wall breaching explosion: heavy plaster/wooden door splintering crash, massive low rumble, no sci-fi beeps."""
    dur = 0.85
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    # 1. Main splintering/crushing noise
    noise = _noise(n, 0.8)
    _resonant_filter(noise, 340, 0.45, "low")
    _env_adsr(noise, 0.01, 0.22, 0.48, 0.25, 0.6)
    _mix(buf, noise)
    
    # 2. Massive low-frequency structural thump (FM sub thud)
    thump = _fm(n, 75, 0.72, 38, 1.5, decay_rate=12.0)
    _env_adsr(thump, 0.0, 0.18, 0.5, 0.32, 0.7)
    _mix(buf, thump)
    
    # 3. Falling plaster and wooden debris crumble (noise swept from high-mid to low)
    debris_n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.45)
    debris = _noise(debris_n, 0.52)
    low = 0.0
    band = 0.0
    q = 0.35
    for i in range(debris_n):
        t = i / debris_n
        cutoff = 1800.0 - 1500.0 * (t ** 0.5) # sweep down from 1800Hz to 300Hz
        f = 2.0 * math.sin(math.pi * cutoff / SAMPLE_RATE)
        high = float(debris[i]) - low - q * band
        band += f * high
        low += f * band
        debris[i] = int(max(-MAX_AMP, min(MAX_AMP, band)))
    _env_exp(debris, 1.0, 0.001, 8.0)
    _offset_mix(buf, debris, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.01))
    
    _apply_delay(buf, 90, feedback=0.4, wet=0.28)
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.45)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_molotov_throw():
    """Whoosh of a burning rag bottle being thrown: soft attack, sweeping wind-like noise."""
    dur = 0.35
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    # Whoosh noise swept from low-mid
    noise = _noise(n, 0.45)
    low = 0.0
    band = 0.0
    q = 0.25
    for i in range(n):
        t = i / n
        cutoff = 180.0 + 320.0 * math.sin(t * math.pi)
        f = 2.0 * math.sin(math.pi * cutoff / SAMPLE_RATE)
        high = float(noise[i]) - low - q * band
        band += f * high
        low += f * band
        buf[i] = int(max(-MAX_AMP, min(MAX_AMP, low)))
        
    _env_adsr(buf, 0.12, 0.18, 0.6, 0.1, 0.7)
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.25)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_molotov_explode():
    """Molotov ignition burst: sharp bottle smash + roaring liquid fire burst."""
    dur = 1.3
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    # 1. Glass bottle smash (scattered high-pitch FM ringing)
    for _ in range(6):
        shard_start = random.randint(0, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.04))
        shard_len = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.05 + random.random() * 0.05)
        freq = 3400 + random.random() * 2500
        shard = _fm(shard_len, freq, 0.35, freq * 1.4, 3.5, decay_rate=22.0)
        _env_exp(shard, 1.0, 0.001, 35.0)
        _offset_mix(buf, shard, shard_start)
        
    # 2. Liquid fire burst whoosh (noise swept via resonant bandpass filter)
    whoosh_n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.8)
    whoosh = _noise(whoosh_n, 0.65)
    low = 0.0
    band = 0.0
    q = 0.45 # high resonance
    for i in range(whoosh_n):
        t = i / whoosh_n
        cutoff = 220.0 + 440.0 * math.sin(t * math.pi)
        f = 2.0 * math.sin(math.pi * cutoff / SAMPLE_RATE)
        high = float(whoosh[i]) - low - q * band
        band += f * high
        low += f * band
        whoosh[i] = int(max(-MAX_AMP, min(MAX_AMP, band)))
        
    _env_adsr(whoosh, 0.02, 0.15, 0.6, 0.25, 0.7)
    _offset_mix(buf, whoosh, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.005))
    
    # 3. Flame crackles (high frequency noise spikes)
    for _ in range(15):
        off = random.randint(int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.05), int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.65))
        crackle = _noise(int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.015), 0.28)
        _resonant_filter(crackle, 3500, 0.3, "high")
        _env_exp(crackle, 1.0, 0.001, 60.0)
        _offset_mix(buf, crackle, off)
        
    _apply_delay(buf, 65, feedback=0.25, wet=0.18)
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.35)
    _env_exp(buf, 1.0, 0.001, 2.2)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_pickup():
    """Satisfying ascending arcade chime: fast stacked FM laser sweep."""
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.15)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    # Rapid ascending sweep
    tone = _sine_sweep(n, 380, 1150, 0.32)
    _env_exp(tone, 1.0, 0.001, 9.0)
    _mix(buf, tone)
    
    # Stacked high harmonic tap
    harm = _sine_sweep(n, 760, 2300, 0.15)
    _env_exp(harm, 1.0, 0.001, 14.0)
    _mix(buf, harm)
    
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.1)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_alarm():
    """Industrial security siren: swept FM alarm warbling over LFO + simulated warehouse acoustic echo."""
    dur = 1.0
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    # LFO sweeping pitch back and forth between 600Hz and 880Hz at 6.2Hz rate
    for i in range(n):
        t = i / SAMPLE_RATE
        freq = 740.0 + 140.0 * math.sin(2.0 * math.pi * 6.2 * t)
        buf[i] = int(MAX_AMP * 0.38 * math.sin(2.0 * math.pi * freq * i / SAMPLE_RATE))
        
    _env_adsr(buf, 0.02, 0.08, 0.82, 0.08, 0.9)
    _apply_delay(buf, 85, feedback=0.42, wet=0.32)
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.25)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_empty_click():
    """Pistol dry firing pin: quick dry FM metallic spring snap."""
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.03)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    click = _fm(n, 1800, 0.45, 3400, 5.0, decay_rate=65.0)
    _env_exp(click, 1.0, 0.001, 50.0)
    _mix(buf, click)
    
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_glass_shatter():
    """Falling glass pane: multiple scattered FM metallic shattering pings + high frequency noise shards."""
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.4)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    # Scattered shard rings
    for _ in range(12):
        shard_start = random.randint(0, n - int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.05))
        shard_len = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.02 + random.random() * 0.03)
        freq = 3100 + random.random() * 4500
        
        # High crisp metallic FM shard ping
        shard = _fm(shard_len, freq, 0.22, freq * 1.5, 4.2, decay_rate=35.0)
        _env_exp(shard, 1.0, 0.001, 40.0)
        
        noise_part = _noise(shard_len, 0.16)
        _resonant_filter(noise_part, 4200, 0.3, "high")
        _env_exp(noise_part, 1.0, 0.001, 50.0)
        
        # Mix shard and noise part
        _mix(shard, noise_part)
        _offset_mix(buf, shard, shard_start)
        
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.2)
    return _wav(buf)

# ── Cinematic procedural sounds ──

def _gen_terminal_glitch():
    """Digital terminal data transfer: rapid FM data chirps and radio packet breakups."""
    rng = random.Random(301)
    dur = 0.34
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    bed = _noise(n, 0.18)
    _resonant_filter(bed, 650, 0.2, "low")
    _env_exp(bed, 1.0, 0.001, 5.0)
    _mix(buf, bed)
    
    # FM digital burst spikes
    for _ in range(11):
        ln = rng.randint(int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.006), int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.026))
        off = rng.randint(0, max(1, n - ln))
        
        # Digital data chirp via FM (1800Hz modulated with high index)
        burst = _fm(ln, rng.uniform(1100, 2600), rng.uniform(0.3, 0.55), 3200, 5.0, decay_rate=25.0)
        _env_exp(burst, 1.0, 0.001, rng.uniform(18.0, 45.0))
        _offset_mix(buf, burst, off)
        
    carrier = _sin(n, 95, 0.055)
    _env_exp(carrier, 1.0, 0.001, 3.5)
    _mix(buf, carrier)
    
    _resonant_filter(buf, 1800, 0.1)
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.3)
    _env_linear(buf, 1.0, 0.0)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_missile_launch():
    """Rocket motor launch: heavy dirty ignition cough + low engine pressure roar."""
    rng = random.Random(410)
    dur = 0.78
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    # Ignition blast
    cough_n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.12)
    cough = _noise(cough_n, 0.72)
    _resonant_filter(cough, 180, 0.45, "low")
    _env_exp(cough, 1.0, 0.001, 13.0)
    _mix(buf, cough)
    
    # Engine roar (noise, resonant bandpass)
    roar = _noise(n, 0.3)
    _resonant_filter(roar, 140, 0.5, "low")
    for i in range(n):
        wobble = 0.72 + 0.18 * math.sin(i * 0.006) + 0.10 * math.sin(i * 0.019)
        roar[i] = int(roar[i] * wobble)
    _env_adsr(roar, 0.03, 0.20, 0.45, 0.32, 0.48)
    _mix(buf, roar)
    
    # Sub-bass rocket pressure (FM thud)
    sub = _fm(n, 46, 0.3, 23, 0.5, decay_rate=3.0)
    _env_exp(sub, 1.0, 0.001, 3.0)
    _mix(buf, sub)
    
    _apply_delay(buf, 60, feedback=0.3, wet=0.2)
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.4)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_missile_flyby():
    """Rushing air doppler effect: swept-cutoff wind roar past listener."""
    dur = 0.92
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    air = _noise(n, 0.36)
    _resonant_filter(air, 450, 0.1, "low")
    
    for i in range(n):
        x = i / n
        whoosh = math.exp(-((x - 0.42) ** 2) / 0.055)
        flutter = 0.86 + 0.14 * math.sin(i * 0.032)
        air[i] = int(air[i] * (0.18 + 1.05 * whoosh) * flutter)
    _mix(buf, air)
    
    low_air = _noise(n, 0.14)
    _resonant_filter(low_air, 120, 0.1, "low")
    _env_adsr(low_air, 0.14, 0.20, 0.38, 0.28, 0.50)
    _mix(buf, low_air)
    
    _env_adsr(buf, 0.10, 0.18, 0.46, 0.26, 0.60)
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.15)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_rig_impact_crack():
    """Distant structural collapse crack: giant filtered crunch."""
    dur = 0.55
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    crack_n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.045)
    crack = _noise(crack_n, 0.9)
    _resonant_filter(crack, 380, 0.4, "low")
    _env_exp(crack, 1.0, 0.001, 28.0)
    _mix(buf, crack)
    
    slap = _noise(int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.20), 0.45)
    _resonant_filter(slap, 180, 0.3)
    _env_exp(slap, 1.0, 0.001, 8.0)
    _offset_mix(buf, slap, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.012))
    
    sub = _fm(int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.36), 52, 0.4, 26, 0.5, decay_rate=5.5)
    _env_exp(sub, 1.0, 0.001, 5.5)
    _offset_mix(buf, sub, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.006))
    
    _apply_delay(buf, 140, feedback=0.4, wet=0.3)
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.8)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_rig_low_boom():
    """The main dark pressure wave: heavy sub rumble + multi-operator low-end FM."""
    dur = 2.65
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    # Overlapping low FM sub layers
    for off_ms, freq, amp, rate in [(0, 36, 0.62, 2.0), (70, 48, 0.48, 2.4), (160, 68, 0.28, 3.1)]:
        ln = n - int(SAMPLE_RATE * off_ms / 1000)
        tone = _fm(max(1, ln), freq, amp, freq // 2, 0.5, decay_rate=rate)
        _offset_mix(buf, tone, int(SAMPLE_RATE * off_ms / 1000))
        
    rumble = _noise(n, 0.36)
    _resonant_filter(rumble, 80, 0.5, "low")
    for i in range(n):
        rumble[i] = int(rumble[i] * (0.8 + 0.2 * math.sin(i * 0.0017)))
    _env_exp(rumble, 1.0, 0.001, 1.75)
    _mix(buf, rumble)
    
    _apply_delay(buf, 200, feedback=0.35, wet=0.25)
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.35)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_rig_secondary_explosion():
    """Muffled fuel tank eruptions: low thuds and deep rumbles."""
    rng = random.Random(512)
    dur = 1.35
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    for start_ms, amp, lp in [(0, 0.48, 120), (130, 0.34, 100), (310, 0.24, 80)]:
        ln = int(SAMPLE_RATE * rng.uniform(0.24, 0.44))
        blast = _noise(ln, amp)
        _resonant_filter(blast, lp, 0.4, "low")
        _env_exp(blast, 1.0, 0.001, rng.uniform(6.0, 9.0))
        _offset_mix(buf, blast, int(SAMPLE_RATE * start_ms / 1000))
        
    sub = _fm(n, 58, 0.22, 29, 0.5, decay_rate=3.4)
    _env_exp(sub, 1.0, 0.001, 3.4)
    _mix(buf, sub)
    
    _apply_delay(buf, 110, feedback=0.3, wet=0.2)
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.4)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_rig_metal_collapse():
    """Collapsing metal beams: grinding FM wood/metal groans + heavy thuds."""
    rng = random.Random(777)
    dur = 2.8
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    for off_ms, start_f, end_f, amp in [(0, 130, 72, 0.2), (260, 96, 54, 0.18), (620, 155, 82, 0.12)]:
        ln = n - int(SAMPLE_RATE * off_ms / 1000)
        groan = _sine_sweep(max(1, ln), start_f, end_f, amp)
        grit = _noise(len(groan), amp * 0.9)
        _resonant_filter(grit, 140, 0.6)
        _mix(groan, grit)
        _env_adsr(groan, 0.08, 0.22, 0.48, 0.22, 0.55)
        _offset_mix(buf, groan, int(SAMPLE_RATE * off_ms / 1000))
        
    # Steel frame snap hits
    for _ in range(15):
        off = rng.randint(int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.18), n - int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.12))
        ln = rng.randint(int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.07), int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.20))
        hit = _noise(ln, rng.uniform(0.14, 0.32))
        _resonant_filter(hit, rng.randint(80, 240), 0.4)
        _env_exp(hit, 1.0, 0.001, rng.uniform(10, 24))
        _offset_mix(buf, hit, off)
        
    _apply_delay(buf, 150, feedback=0.35, wet=0.25)
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.45)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_rig_water_splash():
    """Massive ocean splash: low mass surge + steam spray noise."""
    dur = 1.9
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    slap = _noise(int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.20), 0.58)
    _resonant_filter(slap, 180, 0.3)
    _env_exp(slap, 1.0, 0.001, 10.5)
    _mix(buf, slap)
    
    surge = _noise(n, 0.24)
    _resonant_filter(surge, 90, 0.1, "low")
    _env_exp(surge, 1.0, 0.001, 2.2)
    _mix(buf, surge)
    
    steam = _noise(n, 0.14)
    _resonant_filter(steam, 1100, 0.2)
    _env_adsr(steam, 0.04, 0.26, 0.44, 0.26, 0.52)
    _offset_mix(buf, steam, int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.11))
    
    wave = _sin(n, 39, 0.12)
    _env_exp(wave, 1.0, 0.001, 2.1)
    _mix(buf, wave)
    
    _apply_delay(buf, 120, feedback=0.3, wet=0.2)
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.25)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_rig_fire_burst():
    """Fireball whoosh: dark low whoomp + burning roar crackles."""
    dur = 2.35
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    whoomp = _noise(int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.62), 0.38)
    _resonant_filter(whoomp, 120, 0.4, "low")
    _env_exp(whoomp, 1.0, 0.001, 4.2)
    _mix(buf, whoomp)
    
    burn = _noise(n, 0.13)
    _resonant_filter(burn, 380, 0.1)
    for i in range(n):
        burn[i] = int(burn[i] * (0.75 + 0.25 * math.sin(i * 0.004)))
    _env_adsr(burn, 0.14, 0.24, 0.44, 0.18, 0.45)
    _mix(buf, burn)
    
    sub = _fm(n, 45, 0.1, 22, 0.3, decay_rate=2.6)
    _env_exp(sub, 1.0, 0.001, 2.6)
    _mix(buf, sub)
    
    _apply_delay(buf, 100, feedback=0.25, wet=0.15)
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.2)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_type_tick_variant(freq=1350, amp=0.16, dur=0.042):
    """
    Simulates a highly satisfying mechanical typewriter key strike (tactile mechanical click + hollow platen impact + spring ring).
    """
    n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * dur)
    buf = _arr(n)
    
    # 1. High-frequency tactile switch transient (metal key striking paper/platen)
    snap_n = int(SAMPLE_RATE * 0.005)
    snap = _noise(snap_n, amp * 0.85)
    _resonant_filter(snap, 4500, 0.4, "high")
    _env_exp(snap, 1.0, 0.001, 45.0)
    _mix(buf, snap)
    
    # 2. Hollow mechanical body thud (key mechanism hitting platen roller ~280Hz)
    body = _fm(n, freq * 0.2, amp * 0.45, freq * 0.1, 1.5, decay_rate=18.0)
    _resonant_filter(body, 350, 0.5, "low")
    _env_exp(body, 1.0, 0.001, 25.0)
    _mix(buf, body)
    
    # 3. Tiny ringing steel spring ring (gives it authentic typewriter lever resonance)
    spring = _fm(n, freq * 3.5, amp * 0.28, freq * 4.5, 3.2, decay_rate=30.0)
    _env_exp(spring, 1.0, 0.001, 35.0)
    _mix(buf, spring)
    
    # Apply a tight desk-reflection slapback echo
    _apply_delay(buf, 15, feedback=0.15, wet=0.1)
    
    _soft_clip(buf, 1.2)
    return _wav(buf)

def _gen_type_tick():
    return _gen_type_tick_variant(1320, 0.75, 0.040)

def _gen_type_tick_b():
    return _gen_type_tick_variant(1560, 0.70, 0.038)

def _gen_type_tick_c():
    return _gen_type_tick_variant(1180, 0.72, 0.044)

# ── Audio Manager ──

_channels = {}

def _ch(ch_id):
    if ch_id not in _channels:
        idx = min(len(_channels), 31)
        _channels[ch_id] = pygame.mixer.Channel(idx)
    return _channels[ch_id]

class AudioManager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.sounds = {}
        self.master_volume = 0.8
        self.scheduled = []
        self._loop_channels = {}
        self._loop_base_volumes = {}
        self._duck_frames = 0
        self._duck_factor = 1.0
        self._ambience_names = {"rain", "wind", "ambient_drone"}
        self._init_mixer()
        self._generate_all()
        
    def _pick_footstep(self, prefix, count):
        key = f"{prefix}_{random.randint(1, count)}"
        return self.sounds.get(key, self.sounds.get(prefix))
        
    def _init_mixer(self):
        try:
            if not pygame.mixer.get_init():
                pygame.mixer.init(frequency=SAMPLE_RATE, size=-16, channels=2)
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            pygame.mixer.set_num_channels(32)
        except Exception:
            pass
            
    def _generate_all(self):
        gens = [
            ("gunshot", _gen_gunshot),
            ("gunshot_distant", _gen_gunshot_distant),
            ("sniper_crack", _gen_sniper_crack),
            ("bullet_snap", _gen_bullet_snap),
            ("concrete_impact", _gen_concrete_impact),
            ("tinnitus", _gen_tinnitus),
            ("rifle_handle", _gen_rifle_handle),
            ("footstep", _gen_footstep),
            ("footstep_1", _gen_footstep),
            ("footstep_2", _gen_footstep_b),
            ("footstep_3", _gen_footstep_c),
            ("footstep_walk", _gen_footstep_walk),
            ("footstep_walk_1", _gen_footstep_walk),
            ("footstep_walk_2", _gen_footstep_walk_b),
            ("footstep_walk_3", _gen_footstep_walk_c),
            ("melee_hit", _gen_melee_hit),
            ("bullet_impact", _gen_bullet_impact),
            ("bullet_impact_enemy", _gen_bullet_impact_enemy),
            ("door_creak", _gen_door_creak),
            ("door_rattle", _gen_door_rattle),
            ("door_open", _gen_door_open),
            ("reload", _gen_reload),
            ("ambient_drone", _gen_ambient_drone),
            ("rain", _gen_rain),
            ("wind", _gen_wind),
            ("explosion", _gen_explosion),
            ("terminal_glitch", _gen_terminal_glitch),
            ("missile_launch", _gen_missile_launch),
            ("missile_flyby", _gen_missile_flyby),
            ("rig_impact_crack", _gen_rig_impact_crack),
            ("rig_low_boom", _gen_rig_low_boom),
            ("rig_secondary_explosion", _gen_rig_secondary_explosion),
            ("rig_metal_collapse", _gen_rig_metal_collapse),
            ("rig_water_splash", _gen_rig_water_splash),
            ("rig_fire_burst", _gen_rig_fire_burst),
            ("heartbeat", _gen_heartbeat),
            ("menu_click", _gen_menu_click),
            ("menu_hover", _gen_menu_hover),
            ("type_tick", _gen_type_tick),
            ("type_tick_1", _gen_type_tick),
            ("type_tick_2", _gen_type_tick_b),
            ("type_tick_3", _gen_type_tick_c),
            ("radio_static", _gen_radio_static),
            ("radio_chatter", _gen_radio_chatter),
            ("water_drip", _gen_water_drip),
            ("mission_complete", _gen_mission_complete),
            ("breach", _gen_breach),
            ("molotov_throw", _gen_molotov_throw),
            ("molotov_explode", _gen_molotov_explode),
            ("pickup", _gen_pickup),
            ("alarm", _gen_alarm),
            ("empty_click", _gen_empty_click),
            ("glass_shatter", _gen_glass_shatter),
        ]
        for name, gen in gens:
            try:
                self.sounds[name] = _load(gen())
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Audio gen failed: {name} — {e}")
                
    def _effective_volume(self, name, volume):
        duck = self._duck_factor if name in self._ambience_names and self._duck_frames > 0 else 1.0
        if name == "wind":
            volume *= 0.40
        elif name == "rain":
            volume *= 0.55
        return max(0.0, min(1.0, self.master_volume * volume * duck))
        
    def play(self, name, volume=1.0, loops=0, channel=None):
        if name in ("footstep", "footstep_walk", "type_tick"):
            variant = f"{name}_{random.randint(1, 3)}"
            s = self.sounds.get(variant, self.sounds.get(name))
            if name == "type_tick":
                volume = min(1.0, volume * 4.5)
        else:
            s = self.sounds.get(name)
        if s is None:
            return None
        vol = self._effective_volume(name, volume)
        s.set_volume(vol)
        if channel is not None:
            c = _ch(channel)
            c.play(s, loops=loops)
        else:
            c = s.play(loops=loops)
        if c and loops != 0:
            self._loop_channels[name] = c
            self._loop_base_volumes[name] = volume
        return c
        
    def play_random(self, prefix, count=3, volume=1.0, loops=0, channel=None):
        return self.play(f"{prefix}_{random.randint(1, count)}", volume=volume, loops=loops, channel=channel)
        
    def schedule(self, delay_frames, name, volume=1.0, loops=0, channel=None):
        self.scheduled.append({
            "delay": max(0, int(delay_frames)),
            "name": name,
            "volume": volume,
            "loops": loops,
            "channel": channel,
        })
        
    def update(self):
        for item in self.scheduled[:]:
            item["delay"] -= 1
            if item["delay"] <= 0:
                self.play(item["name"], item["volume"], item["loops"], item["channel"])
                self.scheduled.remove(item)
        if self._duck_frames > 0:
            self._duck_frames -= 1
            if self._duck_frames <= 0:
                self._duck_factor = 1.0
            for name, ch in list(self._loop_channels.items()):
                if ch is None:
                    continue
                base = self._loop0_base_volumes.get(name, 1.0) if hasattr(self, '_loop0_base_volumes') else self._loop_base_volumes.get(name, 1.0)
                try:
                    ch.set_volume(self._effective_volume(name, base))
                except Exception:
                    pass
                    
    def duck_ambience(self, frames=90, factor=0.35):
        self._duck_frames = max(self._duck_frames, int(frames))
        self._duck_factor = min(self._duck_factor, max(0.0, min(1.0, factor)))
        
    def play_3d(self, name, source_pos, listener_pos, max_dist=800, base_volume=1.0):
        s = self.sounds.get(name)
        if s is None:
            return None
        dx = source_pos[0] - listener_pos[0]
        dy = source_pos[1] - listener_pos[1]
        dist = math.hypot(dx, dy)
        if dist >= max_dist:
            return None
        attenuation = max(0.0, 1.0 - (dist / max_dist))
        final_vol = base_volume * self.master_volume * attenuation
        pan = dx / max_dist
        pan = max(-1.0, min(1.0, pan))
        left_vol = final_vol * min(1.0, 1.0 - pan)
        right_vol = final_vol * min(1.0, 1.0 + pan)
        channel = s.play()
        if channel:
            channel.set_volume(left_vol, right_vol)
        return channel
        
    def stop(self, name=None, channel=None):
        if channel is not None and channel in _channels:
            _channels[channel].stop()
        elif name and name in self.sounds:
            self.sounds[name].stop()
            self._loop_channels.pop(name, None)
            self._loop_base_volumes.pop(name, None)
            
    def stop_all(self):
        for ch in _channels.values():
            ch.stop()
        for name in self.sounds:
            try:
                self.sounds[name].stop()
            except Exception:
                pass
        self._loop_channels.clear()
        self._loop_base_volumes.clear()
        self.scheduled.clear()
        
    def set_master_volume(self, vol_0_to_100):
        self.master_volume = max(0.0, min(1.0, vol_0_to_100 / 100.0))
        try:
            pygame.mixer.music.set_volume(self.master_volume * getattr(self, "_current_music_vol", 0.5))
        except Exception:
            pass
        
    def play_music(self, filename, volume=0.5):
        """Loads and streams an MP3 music track in the background."""
        import os
        filepath = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), filename)
        if not os.path.exists(filepath):
            filepath = filename
        if not os.path.exists(filepath):
            print(f"Music file not found: {filename}")
            return False
        try:
            pygame.mixer.music.load(filepath)
            self._current_music_vol = volume
            pygame.mixer.music.set_volume(self.master_volume * volume)
            pygame.mixer.music.play(loops=-1)
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Failed to play music: {e}")
            return False

    def stop_music(self):
        """Stops the streaming background music."""
        try:
            pygame.mixer.music.stop()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def set_music_volume(self, volume):
        """Adjusts the streaming music volume dynamically."""
        self._current_music_vol = volume
        try:
            pygame.mixer.music.set_volume(self.master_volume * volume)
        except Exception:
            pass
        
    def get_sound(self, name):
        return self.sounds.get(name)
        
    def is_playing(self, channel):
        if channel in _channels:
            return _channels[channel].get_busy()
        return False
        
    def fadeout(self, channel, fade_ms=500):
        if channel in _channels:
            _channels[channel].fadeout(fade_ms)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# smoke_test.py
# ------------------------------------------------------------

import os

# Must be set before pygame/config import.
os.environ.setdefault("SDL_VIDEODRIVER", "dummy")
os.environ.setdefault("SDL_AUDIODRIVER", "dummy")
os.environ.setdefault("PYGAME_HIDE_SUPPORT_PROMPT", "1")

import importlib
import importlib.util
import traceback

import pygame

import config
import game_core as gc
import menu


# Set this to True after you finish splitting game_core.py into modules.
# If False, missing split modules are reported as SKIP instead of FAIL.
REQUIRE_SPLIT_MODULES = True

VERBOSE_TRACEBACKS = True

SPLIT_MODULES = [
    "state",
    "player",
    "weapons",
    "mission",
    "cutscenes",
    "walking_sim",
    "effects",
    "ui_hud",
    "interactions",
]


class EmptyKeys:
    """Fake pygame.key.get_pressed() object.

    Any key lookup returns False.
    """
    def __getitem__(self, key):
        return False


def run_check(name, fn, required=True):
    try:
        fn()
        pygame.event.pump()

        if pygame.display.get_surface() is not None:
            pygame.display.flip()

    except Exception as e:
        status = "FAIL" if required else "SKIP"
        print(f"{name}: {status} {e!r}")

        if required and VERBOSE_TRACEBACKS:
            traceback.print_exc()

        return not required

    print(f"{name}: OK")
    return True


def run_import_check(module_name):
    spec = importlib.util.find_spec(module_name)

    if spec is None:
        if REQUIRE_SPLIT_MODULES:
            print(f"import {module_name}: FAIL module not found")
            return False

        print(f"import {module_name}: SKIP module not found")
        return True

    return run_check(
        f"import {module_name}",
        lambda: importlib.import_module(module_name),
        required=True,
    )


def clear_screen():
    config.screen.fill((0, 0, 0))


def check_menu_draw():
    clear_screen()
    menu.draw_menu()


def check_settings_draw():
    clear_screen()
    menu.draw_menu_background()
    menu.draw_settings(0, 0, 0, 80, 80, 100, False)


def check_server_browser_servers_draw():
    clear_screen()
    if hasattr(menu, "switch_mp_tab"):
        menu.switch_mp_tab("SERVERS")
    menu.draw_menu_background()
    menu.draw_server_browser(-1, False, "")


def check_server_browser_connections_draw():
    clear_screen()
    if hasattr(menu, "switch_mp_tab"):
        menu.switch_mp_tab("CONNECTIONS")
    menu.draw_menu_background()
    menu.draw_server_browser(-1, False, "")

    # Restore default tab.
    if hasattr(menu, "switch_mp_tab"):
        menu.switch_mp_tab("SERVERS")


def check_notebook_inspection_draw():
    clear_screen()
    gc.draw_notebook_inspection()


def check_safe_keypad_draw():
    clear_screen()

    setattr(gc, "safe_keypad_active", True)
    setattr(gc, "safe_keypad_code", "")
    setattr(gc, "safe_keypad_target", "742")
    setattr(gc, "safe_keypad_callback", None)

    gc.draw_safe_keypad()

    setattr(gc, "safe_keypad_active", False)


def check_desk_note_draw():
    clear_screen()

    setattr(
        gc,
        "active_read_note",
        "TEST NOTE:\nThis is a smoke-test note.\nPress SPACE to close.",
    )

    gc.draw_desk_note()

    setattr(gc, "active_read_note", None)


def check_cutscene_draw():
    clear_screen()

    if not hasattr(gc, "cutscene1_shots"):
        raise RuntimeError("gc.cutscene1_shots missing")

    shot = gc.cutscene1_shots[0]

    gc.draw_cutscene_scene(config.screen, shot, 0)
    gc.draw_dialogue_box(
        config.screen,
        shot.get("speaker", "NARRATOR"),
        shot.get("text", ""),
        min(20, len(shot.get("text", ""))),
        len(shot.get("text", "")),
    )


def check_start_mission():
    gc.start_mission()

    if getattr(gc, "player", None) is None:
        raise RuntimeError("gc.start_mission() did not create player")


def check_game_update():
    gc.game_update(EmptyKeys())


def check_game_draw():
    clear_screen()

    fcx = getattr(gc, "camera_x", 0)
    fcy = getattr(gc, "camera_y", 0)

    gc.game_draw(fcx, fcy)


def check_walking_sim_start_update_draw():
    clear_screen()

    gc.start_walking_sim()
    gc.update_walking_sim(EmptyKeys())
    gc.draw_walking_sim()


def check_pause_draw():
    clear_screen()
    menu.draw_pause_menu()


def check_death_draw():
    clear_screen()

    if hasattr(menu, "reset_death_animation"):
        menu.reset_death_animation()

    menu.draw_death_screen()


def check_molotov_update_path():
    """Catches missing _spawn_molotov_fire import in mission.py."""
    gc.start_mission()
    setattr(gc, "player_bottles", 1)
    gc.handle_throw_bottle()

    for _ in range(45):
        gc.game_update(EmptyKeys())


def check_mission_draw_story_characters():
    """Catches missing draw_volkov / draw_commander imports."""
    gc.start_mission()

    if getattr(gc, "volkov_pos", None) is None:
        raise RuntimeError("volkov_pos missing")

    vx, vy = gc.volkov_pos
    gc.game_draw(vx - config.WIDTH // 2, vy - config.HEIGHT // 2)


def check_walking_dialogue_draw():
    """Catches missing draw_dialogue_box import in walking_sim.py."""
    gc.start_walking_sim()

    setattr(gc, "walk_dialogue_active", True)
    setattr(gc, "walk_dialogue_speaker", "SYSTEM")
    setattr(gc, "walk_dialogue_text", "Smoke test walking dialogue.")
    setattr(gc, "walk_dialogue_char_idx", 10)

    gc.draw_walking_sim()

    setattr(gc, "walk_dialogue_active", False)


def check_game_core_facade_state():
    """Checks that game_core still exposes old global-style names.

    This is especially useful if you use the proxy facade approach.
    """
    setattr(gc, "show_minimap", True)

    if getattr(gc, "show_minimap") is not True:
        raise RuntimeError("gc.show_minimap did not round-trip correctly")

    setattr(gc, "show_minimap", False)

    if getattr(gc, "show_minimap") is not False:
        raise RuntimeError("gc.show_minimap write failed")


def main():
    assert config.screen is not None, "config.screen was not initialized"

    ok = True

    print("\n=== Import checks ===")
    for module_name in SPLIT_MODULES:
        ok = run_import_check(module_name) and ok

    print("\n=== UI draw checks ===")
    ui_checks = [
        ("draw_menu", check_menu_draw),
        ("draw_settings", check_settings_draw),
        ("draw_server_browser_servers", check_server_browser_servers_draw),
        ("draw_server_browser_connections", check_server_browser_connections_draw),
        ("draw_notebook_inspection", check_notebook_inspection_draw),
        ("draw_safe_keypad", check_safe_keypad_draw),
        ("draw_desk_note", check_desk_note_draw),
        ("draw_cutscene", check_cutscene_draw),
        ("draw_pause_menu", check_pause_draw),
        ("draw_death_screen", check_death_draw),
    ]

    for name, fn in ui_checks:
        ok = run_check(name, fn) and ok

    print("\n=== Mission checks ===")
    mission_checks = [
        ("game_core_facade_state", check_game_core_facade_state),
        ("start_mission", check_start_mission),
        ("game_update", check_game_update),
        ("game_draw", check_game_draw),
    ]

    for name, fn in mission_checks:
        ok = run_check(name, fn) and ok

    print("\n=== Walking sim checks ===")
    walking_checks = [
        ("walking_sim_start_update_draw", check_walking_sim_start_update_draw),
    ]

    for name, fn in walking_checks:
        ok = run_check(name, fn) and ok

    print("\n=== Hidden branch checks ===")
    hidden_checks = [
        ("molotov_update_path", check_molotov_update_path),
        ("mission_draw_story_characters", check_mission_draw_story_characters),
        ("walking_dialogue_draw", check_walking_dialogue_draw),
    ]

    for name, fn in hidden_checks:
        ok = run_check(name, fn) and ok

    pygame.quit()

    if ok:
        print("\nSMOKE TEST: OK")
        raise SystemExit(0)

    print("\nSMOKE TEST: FAILED")
    raise SystemExit(1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

def _tc(col, row):
    return col * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE // 2, row * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE // 2


def _tile_rect(col, row, w=1, h=1):
    return pygame.Rect(col * TILE_SIZE, row * TILE_SIZE, w * TILE_SIZE, h * TILE_SIZE)


def _zone_world_rect(zone_name):
    tr = ZONE_DEFS[zone_name]["tile_rect"]
    return _tile_rect(tr.x, tr.y, tr.w, tr.h)


ZONE_WORLD_RECTS = {name: _zone_world_rect(name) for name in ZONE_DEFS}


def get_zone_for_world_pos(pos):
    """Return the authored zone name for a world-space point, or None."""
    for name, rect in ZONE_WORLD_RECTS.items():
        if rect.collidepoint(pos):
            return name
    return None

# Reordered cutscene per the 12-point rework.
#
# - "wrong_scan": squad looks the WRONG way (west cars / south ditch).  This
#   widens the gap between what the audience knows and what the squad knows.
# - "gabi_shot" is now SHORT and has NO laser telegraph for the first kill —
#   she dies mid-sentence to maximize shock.
# - "dead_air" is a held silent beat after Gabi falls (no music, ducked ambience,
#   tinnitus, dripping radio).  The squad is frozen in disbelief.
# - "origin_reveal" now happens AFTER the first death: muzzle flash, brief
#   silhouette of the shooter on the gantry, then he hides again.
# - Seby's death is long and physical (he drags Gabi, shouts at the gantry).
# - Darius's death is mid-radio-call: he reaches for the field radio handset
#   and dies whispering a mayday into it.
# - "biribel_near_miss" freezes player input, slams camera to impact, etc.
# - "handoff": single hard black frame at the very end before gameplay.
SNIPER_PHASES = [
    {"name": "regroup",        "duration": 280},
    {"name": "movement_one",   "duration": 760},
    {"name": "movement_two",   "duration": 720},
    {"name": "movement_three", "duration": 360},
]

SNIPER_BEAT_CLOCK = {
    "regroup": {
        "spread_line": 80,
    },
    "movement_one": {
        "regroup_line":   70,
        "scan_west":      170,
        "gabi_crack":     300,
        "silence_start":  302,
        "silence_frames": 110,
        "vasile_down":    320,
        "origin_flash":   440,
        "muzzle_flash":   440,
        "gantry_hold":    460,
        "seby_react":     660,
    },
    "movement_two": {
        "seby_breaks":             70,
        "seby_arrives":            190,
        "seby_drag_to_cover_end":  460,
        "seby_crack":              430,
        "vasile_cover":            100,
        "vasile_hold":             450,
        "darius_reaches":          500,
        "darius_crack":            570,
    },
    "movement_three": {
        "near_miss": 110,
        "hard_black": 300,
    },
}


def _beat(phase, event, default=0):
    return SNIPER_BEAT_CLOCK.get(phase, {}).get(event, default)

def _beat_now(phase, pt, event):
    f = SNIPER_BEAT_CLOCK.get(phase, {}).get(event)
    return f is not None and pt == f


def _set_tile(g, col, row, tile):
    if 0 <= col < MAP_W and 0 <= row < MAP_H:
        g[row][col] = tile


def _paint_tiles(g, rect, tile):
    for row in range(rect.y, rect.y + rect.h):
        for col in range(rect.x, rect.x + rect.w):
            _set_tile(g, col, row, tile)


def _outline_building(g, col, row, w, h, interior="O", door_tiles=()):
    for yy in range(row, row + h):
        for xx in range(col, col + w):
            border = xx in (col, col + w - 1) or yy in (row, row + h - 1)
            _set_tile(g, xx, yy, "C" if border else interior)
    for dx, dy in door_tiles:
        _set_tile(g, col + dx, row + dy, interior)


def _generate_map():
    g = [["g" for _ in range(MAP_W)] for _ in range(MAP_H)]

    # Paint authored regions first.  Collidable structure is added after.
    for zone in ZONE_DEFS.values():
        _paint_tiles(g, zone["tile_rect"], zone["floor"])

    # Continuous highway spine and shoulders.  These broad open sightlines are
    # intentional: the sniper ambush later weaponizes this road geometry.
    _paint_tiles(g, pygame.Rect(17, 23, 37, 55), ".")
    _paint_tiles(g, pygame.Rect(12, 54, 42, 13), ".")
    _paint_tiles(g, pygame.Rect(7, 70, 25, 8), ".")
    _paint_tiles(g, pygame.Rect(4, 47, 23, 15), "d")
    _paint_tiles(g, pygame.Rect(45, 38, 23, 15), "s")
    _paint_tiles(g, pygame.Rect(39, 12, 28, 16), "O")

    # World border.
    for x in range(MAP_W):
        g[0][x] = g[MAP_H - 1][x] = "C"
    for y in range(MAP_H):
        g[y][0] = g[y][MAP_W - 1] = "C"

    # Control building and offices.
    _outline_building(g, 51, 9, 17, 17, interior="O", door_tiles=((6, 16), (7, 16), (0, 9)))
    # Smaller security hut / checkpoint utility room.
    _outline_building(g, 41, 18, 9, 8, interior="O", door_tiles=((4, 7),))

    # Toll booths and gantry supports.
    for bx, by in [(23, 32), (30, 32), (44, 32), (51, 32), (24, 37), (45, 37)]:
        for yy in range(by, by + 2):
            for xx in range(bx, bx + 2):
                _set_tile(g, xx, yy, "C")
    for sx, sy in [(56, 25), (59, 25), (56, 27), (59, 27)]:
        _set_tile(g, sx, sy, "C")

    # Barriers / cover.  These break up travel without deleting the long sniper
    # sightline through the center lane.
    barriers = [
        (12, 72), (13, 72), (21, 69), (22, 69),
        (18, 61), (19, 61), (27, 60), (28, 60), (37, 58),
        (10, 51), (11, 51), (20, 47), (21, 47),
        (29, 40), (30, 40), (34, 39), (35, 39), (40, 40),
        (47, 45), (48, 45), (62, 47), (63, 47),
        (44, 27), (45, 27), (49, 28), (50, 28),
        (57, 21), (58, 21), (62, 18), (63, 18),
    ]
    for col, row in barriers:
        _set_tile(g, col, row, "B")

    # Final-build density pass: guardrails, broken dividers, service-road clutter,
    # and ditch obstructions.  These are intentionally staggered so the map has
    # shape without blocking the central sniper sightline.
    for row in range(25, 76, 4):
        if row not in (32, 33, 34, 35, 36, 37, 38, 39):
            _set_tile(g, 16, row, "B")
            _set_tile(g, 54, row + (row // 4) % 2, "B")
    for row in range(55, 66, 3):
        for col in (14, 41, 46, 51):
            _set_tile(g, col, row, "B")
    for col in range(6, 26, 4):
        _set_tile(g, col, 46, "B")
        _set_tile(g, col + 1, 59, "B")
    for col in range(46, 66, 4):
        _set_tile(g, col, 40, "B")
        _set_tile(g, col + 1, 51, "B")
    for col in range(21, 53, 7):
        _set_tile(g, col, 30, "B")
        _set_tile(g, col + 2, 42, "B")
    # Interior partitions in the control building so it no longer reads as one
    # empty rectangle.  Door gaps keep all evidence reachable.
    for col in range(53, 67):
        if col not in (57, 62):
            _set_tile(g, col, 17, "C")
    for row in range(11, 25):
        if row not in (15, 21):
            _set_tile(g, 60, row, "C")

    # Tanker footprint.  The actual collision uses mission2_tanker_rect.
    for row in range(36, 39):
        for col in range(43, 48):
            _set_tile(g, col, row, "T")

    _set_tile(g, 8, 74, "P")
    _set_tile(g, 64, 12, "X")
    return ["".join(row) for row in g]


MISSION2_MAP = _generate_map()

# Module-level draw/update scratch.
_last_keys = None
_objective = "Inspect staged evidence markers with [E]"
_radio_marks = set()

# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# Utility helpers
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
def _dist(a, b):
    return math.hypot(a[0] - b[0], a[1] - b[1])


def _player_pos():
    if not STATE.player:
        return (0, 0)
    return STATE.player.rect.centerx, STATE.player.rect.centery


def _near(pos, radius=80):
    return STATE.player and _dist(_player_pos(), pos) <= radius


def _camera_offset_for_target(target):
    tx, ty = target
    max_x = max(0, MAP_W * TILE_SIZE - WIDTH)
    max_y = max(0, MAP_H * TILE_SIZE - HEIGHT)
    cam_x = tx - WIDTH // 2
    cam_y = ty - HEIGHT // 2
    cam_x = max(0, min(max_x, cam_x))
    cam_y = max(0, min(max_y, cam_y))
    return cam_x, cam_y


def _snap_camera_to(target=None):
    if target is None:
        target = _player_pos()
    cam_x, cam_y = _camera_offset_for_target(target)
    STATE.camera_x = cam_x
    STATE.camera_y = cam_y
    STATE.cam_kick_x = 0.0
    STATE.cam_kick_y = 0.0
    STATE.screen_shake = 0


def _solid_walls():
    return (
        list(STATE.walls)
        + list(getattr(STATE, "mission2_vehicle_walls", []))
        + list(getattr(STATE, "mission2_prop_walls", []))
    )


def _set_objective(text):
    global _objective
    _objective = text


def _radio(text, color=C_VASILE, duration=300, mark=None, allow_when_cut=False):
    """Small radio helper. Used sparingly; cards do most story work."""
    if mark and mark in _radio_marks:
        return
    if mark:
        _radio_marks.add(mark)

    # Sniper subtitles need reading time (~4 seconds minimum).
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_stage", "") == STAGE_SNIPER:
        duration = max(duration, 240)

    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_comms_cut", False) and not allow_when_cut:
        text = "RADIO: <static>"
        color = (125, 125, 135)
        duration = min(duration, 140)
    # Muffled radio after the near-miss: drop letters, insert ellipses, fade
    # color so it visually reads as "underwater" without needing a real
    # low-pass filter.
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_radio_muffle_timer", 0) > 0 and not text.startswith("RADIO:"):
        text = _muffle_radio_text(text)
        color = tuple(max(70, int(c * 0.62)) for c in color)
        if getattr(STATE, "mission2_stage", "") == STAGE_SNIPER:
            duration = max(duration, 240)
        else:
            duration = max(160, int(duration * 0.75))
    trigger_radio_chatter(text, color=color, duration=duration)


def _muffle_radio_text(text):
    """Drop syllables and insert ellipses so a radio line reads as ear-ringing.

    Ramps the muffle severity based on mission2_radio_muffle_timer so the
    effect fades out gradually as the player's hearing returns.
    """
    timer = getattr(STATE, "mission2_radio_muffle_timer", 0)
    if timer <= 0:
        return text
    if ":" in text:
        speaker, _, spoken = text.partition(":")
        spoken = spoken.strip().strip('"')
        words = spoken.split()
        if len(words) <= 2:
            return text
        intensity = min(0.6, timer / 180.0)
        keep = []
        for i, w in enumerate(words):
            if i == 0 or random.random() > intensity:
                keep.append(w)
        return f'{speaker}: "{" . . . ".join(keep)}"'
    return text


def _burst(x, y, color, count=20, speed_mul=1.0):
    for _ in range(count):
        p = Particle(x + random.randint(-8, 8), y + random.randint(-8, 8), color)
        p.dx *= speed_mul
        p.dy *= speed_mul
        p.life = random.randint(18, 50)
        STATE.particles.append(p)


def _open_card(title, lines, color=C_TERMINAL, after=None, kind="document"):
    """Open a readable inspection/intel card. It stays until the player presses E."""
    if isinstance(lines, str):
        lines = [lines]
    STATE.mission2_card = {
        "title": title,
        "lines": list(lines),
        "color": color,
        "age": 0,
        "after": after,
        "kind": kind,
    }
    audio.play("terminal_glitch", volume=0.24)


def _close_card():
    card = getattr(STATE, "mission2_card", None)
    if not card:
        return
    after = card.get("after")
    STATE.mission2_card = None
    audio.play("menu_click", volume=0.24)
    if after == "truck_done":
        STATE.mission2_stage = STAGE_TANKER
        STATE.mission_phase = "m2_tanker"
        _set_objective("Approach the leaking fuel tanker")
        _radio('VASILE: "Balaceanu logistics. Not MAG. Move to the tanker — slow."', color=(255, 170, 80), mark="truck_done")
    elif after == "blast_done":
        STATE.mission2_stage = STAGE_EVIDENCE
        STATE.mission_phase = "m2_evidence"
        _set_objective("Enter the control building and inspect green evidence terminals")
        _spawn_control_cleanup()
    elif after == "evidence_ready":
        _set_objective("Hold [E] at the upload terminal")
    elif after == "upload_done":
        STATE.mission2_stage = STAGE_EXFIL
        STATE.mission_phase = "m2_exfil"
        STATE.mission2_camera_focus = None
        _set_objective("Reach the green exfil marker")
        _radio('SYSTEM: Evidence transmitted. Move to extraction.', color=C_TERMINAL, mark="exfil", allow_when_cut=True)


def _spawn_enemy(col, row, hp=115, speed=2.0, sniper=False):
    x, y = _tc(col, row)
    e = Enemy(x - 16, y - 16)
    e.hp = hp
    e.speed = speed
    e.alerted = True
    e.state = "CHASE"
    e.last_seen_pos = _player_pos()
    e.fire_cooldown = random.randint(18, 75)
    e.is_mission2_ipa = True
    e.is_mission2_sniper = sniper
    if sniper:
        e.speed = 1.1
        e.hp = 90
    STATE.enemies.append(e)
    return e


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# Initialization
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
def _build_world():
    STATE.walls = []
    STATE.doors = []
    STATE.enemies = []
    STATE.pickups = []
    STATE.blood_decals = []
    STATE.bullets = []
    STATE.casings = []
    STATE.particles = []
    STATE.thrown_bottles = []
    STATE.molotov_fires = []
    STATE.fire_zones = []
    STATE.floating_texts = []
    STATE.mission2_barriers = []
    STATE.mission2_vehicle_walls = []
    STATE.mission2_prop_walls = []
    STATE.player = None

    for row_idx, row in enumerate(MISSION2_MAP):
        for col_idx, tile in enumerate(row):
            x, y = col_idx * TILE_SIZE, row_idx * TILE_SIZE
            if tile == "C":
                STATE.walls.append(pygame.Rect(x, y, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE))
            elif tile == "B":
                r = pygame.Rect(x + 4, y + 12, TILE_SIZE - 8, TILE_SIZE - 24)
                STATE.walls.append(r)
                STATE.mission2_barriers.append(r)
            elif tile == "P":
                STATE.player = Player(x + 16, y + 16, max_hp=115)

    if STATE.player is None:
        STATE.player = Player(PLAYER_START[0] - 16, PLAYER_START[1] - 16, max_hp=115)
    STATE.player.speed = 3.35

    STATE.mission2_tanker_rect = pygame.Rect(43 * TILE_SIZE + 6, 36 * TILE_SIZE + 8, TILE_SIZE * 5 - 12, TILE_SIZE * 3 - 16)
    STATE.walls.append(STATE.mission2_tanker_rect)

    # Seal the gantry so the player can't walk into the sniper's elevated nest.
    STATE.walls.append(pygame.Rect(56 * TILE_SIZE, 25 * TILE_SIZE, 4 * TILE_SIZE, 3 * TILE_SIZE))

    # Vehicles are visual props and collision/LOS blockers, but are not drawn as
    # wall tiles.  Vehicle collision is returned by _solid_walls().
    STATE.mission2_vehicles = [
        {"kind": "civilian", "rect": pygame.Rect(9 * TILE_SIZE + 8, 70 * TILE_SIZE + 14, 118, 44)},
        {"kind": "burned", "rect": pygame.Rect(18 * TILE_SIZE + 8, 60 * TILE_SIZE + 10, 126, 48)},
        {"kind": "civilian", "rect": pygame.Rect(31 * TILE_SIZE + 5, 57 * TILE_SIZE + 16, 110, 42)},
        {"kind": "burned", "rect": pygame.Rect(12 * TILE_SIZE + 8, 48 * TILE_SIZE + 14, 112, 44)},
        {"kind": "civilian", "rect": pygame.Rect(49 * TILE_SIZE + 5, 45 * TILE_SIZE + 12, 116, 44)},
        {"kind": "ipa_truck", "rect": pygame.Rect(46 * TILE_SIZE + 8, 31 * TILE_SIZE + 12, 160, 54)},
        {"kind": "burned", "rect": pygame.Rect(41 * TILE_SIZE + 6, 25 * TILE_SIZE + 12, 126, 46)},
    ]
    for v in STATE.mission2_vehicles:
        STATE.mission2_vehicle_walls.append(v["rect"].inflate(-12, -10))

    STATE.pickups.extend([
        [12 * TILE_SIZE + 32, 68 * TILE_SIZE + 32, "bandage"],
        [24 * TILE_SIZE + 32, 57 * TILE_SIZE + 32, "mag"],
        [54 * TILE_SIZE + 32, 43 * TILE_SIZE + 32, "bandage"],
        [57 * TILE_SIZE + 32, 24 * TILE_SIZE + 32, "mag"],
    ])


def _spawn_mission2_clues():
    """Create mutable clue state from the static clue definitions."""
    STATE.mission2_clues = []
    STATE.mission2_clues_found = set()
    for definition in MISSION2_CLUE_DEFS:
        clue = dict(definition)
        clue["done"] = False
        clue["world_zone"] = definition.get("zone")
        STATE.mission2_clues.append(clue)


def _setup_travel_triggers():
    """Build one-shot environmental story triggers for the larger map."""
    STATE.mission2_triggers = []
    STATE.mission2_story_props = []
    for definition in MISSION2_TRIGGER_DEFS:
        trig = dict(definition)
        trig["triggered"] = False
        STATE.mission2_triggers.append(trig)


# Only props that visually imply mass should block movement/line-of-sight.  Flares,
# soot, puddles, and glints stay decorative; cones/barrels/signs/electrical boxes
# get compact collision so they feel grounded without making navigation annoying.
STORY_PROP_COLLISION_SIZES = {
    "cone": (22, 18),
    "barrel": (30, 30),
    "sign": (34, 18),
    "spark_box": (30, 32),
    "field_radio": (24, 18),
    "broken_camera": (28, 18),
}


def _story_prop_collision_rect(prop):
    size = STORY_PROP_COLLISION_SIZES.get(prop.get("kind"))
    if not size:
        return None
    x, y = prop.get("pos", (0, 0))
    w, h = size
    return pygame.Rect(int(x - w // 2), int(y - h // 2), w, h)


def _sync_story_prop_collisions():
    STATE.mission2_prop_walls = []
    for prop in getattr(STATE, "mission2_story_props", []):
        rect = _story_prop_collision_rect(prop)
        if rect is not None:
            STATE.mission2_prop_walls.append(rect)


def _init_scripted_props():
    _spawn_mission2_clues()
    _setup_travel_triggers()
    # Baseline non-interactive set dressing.  Trigger props still add more
    # moment-to-moment story beats, but these make the larger map feel occupied
    # immediately instead of waiting for trigger activation.
    STATE.mission2_story_props.extend([
        {"kind": "flare", "pos": _tc(9, 72), "life": -1, "age": 0},
        {"kind": "cone", "pos": _tc(15, 69), "life": -1, "age": 0},
        {"kind": "cone", "pos": _tc(24, 66), "life": -1, "age": 0},
        {"kind": "soot", "pos": _tc(19, 60), "life": -1, "age": 0},
        {"kind": "soot", "pos": _tc(32, 58), "life": -1, "age": 0},
        {"kind": "barrel", "pos": _tc(27, 55), "life": -1, "age": 0},
        {"kind": "puddle", "pos": _tc(10, 53), "life": -1, "age": 0},
        {"kind": "puddle", "pos": _tc(21, 49), "life": -1, "age": 0},
        {"kind": "sign", "pos": _tc(34, 43), "life": -1, "age": 0},
        {"kind": "field_radio", "pos": FIELD_RADIO_POS, "life": -1, "age": 0},
        {"kind": "cone", "pos": _tc(40, 35), "life": -1, "age": 0},
        {"kind": "barrel", "pos": _tc(49, 42), "life": -1, "age": 0},
        {"kind": "puddle", "pos": _tc(58, 48), "life": -1, "age": 0},
        {"kind": "spark_box", "pos": _tc(61, 43), "life": -1, "age": 0},
        {"kind": "sign", "pos": _tc(55, 29), "life": -1, "age": 0},
        {"kind": "barrel", "pos": _tc(56, 22), "life": -1, "age": 0},
        {"kind": "barrel", "pos": _tc(64, 20), "life": -1, "age": 0},
        {"kind": "puddle", "pos": _tc(59, 15), "life": -1, "age": 0},
    ])
    # Repeating low-cost dressing across long travel lanes.  These are small
    # enough not to distract from clues, but frequent enough to break up empty
    # asphalt/shoulder screens.
    for col, row, kind in [
        (18, 72, "cone"), (27, 72, "puddle"), (33, 70, "barrel"),
        (18, 65, "puddle"), (36, 64, "cone"), (44, 62, "barrel"),
        (16, 57, "cone"), (39, 55, "puddle"), (49, 56, "sign"),
        (8, 56, "barrel"), (23, 54, "puddle"), (6, 49, "cone"),
        (52, 50, "cone"), (65, 50, "barrel"), (47, 47, "puddle"),
        (23, 43, "sign"), (30, 42, "cone"), (50, 39, "barrel"),
        (22, 34, "puddle"), (42, 34, "cone"), (53, 34, "sign"),
        (43, 24, "barrel"), (48, 23, "cone"), (65, 24, "puddle"),
        (54, 13, "cone"), (66, 14, "barrel"), (53, 19, "puddle"),
    ]:
        STATE.mission2_story_props.append({"kind": kind, "pos": _tc(col, row), "life": -1, "age": 0})
    _sync_story_prop_collisions()

    STATE.mission2_truck_manifest = {
        "kind": "manifest",
        "name": "IPA MANIFEST",

    # Scripted actors. They do not pathfind and cannot phase through walls.
    # `reaction` drives draw posture during the sniper sequence:
    #   standing  - default upright
    #   breaking  - leaning toward a run, the veteran response (sideways escape)

    STATE.mission2_squad = [
        {"name": "GABI",   "pos": list(_tc(31, 41)), "alive": True, "activity": "regrouping", "reaction": "standing"},
        {"name": "SEBY",   "pos": list(_tc(30, 42)), "alive": True, "activity": "regrouping", "reaction": "standing"},

    ]

# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# Story scripting / interactions

def _clue_count():
    return sum(1 for c in STATE.mission2_clues if c.get("done"))


def _evidence_count():
    return sum(1 for e in STATE.mission2_evidence if e.get("done"))


def _activate_travel_trigger(trigger):
    trigger["triggered"] = True
    sound = trigger.get("sound")
    if sound:
        audio.play(sound, volume=trigger.get("volume", 0.2))
    dialogue = trigger.get("dialogue")
    if dialogue:
        _radio(dialogue, color=trigger.get("color", C_VASILE), duration=trigger.get("duration", 260), mark=trigger["id"])
    for prop in trigger.get("props", []):
        story_prop = dict(prop)
        story_prop.setdefault("life", -1)
        story_prop.setdefault("age", 0)
        story_prop.setdefault("source", trigger["id"])
        STATE.mission2_story_props.append(story_prop)
    _sync_story_prop_collisions()


def _update_travel_triggers():
    """One-shot environmental story beats between clue zones."""
    if (not STATE.player or getattr(STATE, "mission2_stage", "") != STAGE_INVESTIGATE
            or getattr(STATE, "mission2_card", None) is not None):
        return
    player_pos = _player_pos()
    STATE.mission2_current_zone = get_zone_for_world_pos(player_pos)
    for trigger in getattr(STATE, "mission2_triggers", []):
        if trigger.get("triggered"):
            continue
        if trigger["rect"].collidepoint(player_pos):
            _activate_travel_trigger(trigger)


def _actor_collision_rect(x, y):
    return pygame.Rect(int(x - 12), int(y - 12), 24, 24)


def _actor_rect_collides(rect):
    for wall in _solid_walls():
        if rect.colliderect(wall):
            return True
    return False


def _try_actor_step(actor, dx, dy):
    if abs(dx) < 0.001 and abs(dy) < 0.001:
        return False
    moved = False
    x, y = actor["pos"]
    # X axis first.
    if abs(dx) > 0.001:
        nr = _actor_collision_rect(x + dx, y)
        if not _actor_rect_collides(nr):
            actor["pos"][0] = x + dx
            x = actor["pos"][0]
            moved = True
    # Y axis second.
    if abs(dy) > 0.001:
        nr = _actor_collision_rect(x, y + dy)
        if not _actor_rect_collides(nr):
            actor["pos"][1] = y + dy
            moved = True
    return moved


def _move_actor_towards(actor, target, rate=0.055, max_step=2.7):
    """Move scripted squad actors while respecting walls/vehicles/solid props.

    This is deliberately not full combat pathfinding.  It is a lightweight
    cinematic/ambient mover with axis collision and a small sidestep fallback so
    actors stop sliding through barriers but can still work around minor clutter.
    """
    if not actor.get("alive", True):
        return
    x, y = actor["pos"]
    dx = target[0] - x
    dy = target[1] - y
    dist = math.hypot(dx, dy)
    if dist < 1:
        return
    step = min(max_step, dist * rate)
    nx = dx / dist * step
    ny = dy / dist * step
    moved = _try_actor_step(actor, nx, ny)
    if not moved and dist > 8:
        # Try sliding around small props/barriers.  Alternating side preference
        # stops all three squadmates choosing the exact same detour.
        side = -1 if (hash(actor.get("name", "")) % 2) else 1
        px = -ny * side
        py = nx * side
        if not _try_actor_step(actor, px, py):
            _try_actor_step(actor, -px, -py)
    if not moved and dist > 8:
        # Broader escape — try multiple angles when stuck on adjacent barriers.
        for slide_angle in (0.5, -0.5, 1.0, -1.0, 1.5, -1.5):
            cs = math.cos(slide_angle)
            sn = math.sin(slide_angle)
            sx = nx * cs - ny * sn
            sy = nx * sn + ny * cs
            if _try_actor_step(actor, sx, sy):
                break


def _nearest_unfound_clue(max_dist=360):
    if not STATE.player:
        return None
    px, py = _player_pos()
    best = None
    best_d = max_dist
    for clue in getattr(STATE, "mission2_clues", []):
        if clue.get("done"):
            continue
        d = math.hypot(clue["pos"][0] - px, clue["pos"][1] - py)
        if d < best_d:
            best = clue
            best_d = d
    return best


def _update_squad_ambient():
    """Zone-post squad behavior: active, but not player-following.

    The team occupies authored posts in the same broad investigation zone instead
    of orbiting Biribel.  They still move, scan, and peel toward relevant clues,
    but they do not form a follow-the-player escort blob.
    """
    if not STATE.player or getattr(STATE, "mission2_stage", "") != STAGE_INVESTIGATE:
        return

    px, py = _player_pos()
    zone = getattr(STATE, "mission2_current_zone", None) or get_zone_for_world_pos((px, py)) or "approach"
    tick = pygame.time.get_ticks()
    patrol_step = (tick // 3600) % 2

    zone_posts = {
        "approach": [
            [(_tc(10, 72), _tc(14, 70)), (_tc(16, 74), _tc(21, 72)), (_tc(11, 75), _tc(18, 76))],
            ["scanning road", "covering rear", "radio check"],
            [SNIPER_NEST_POS, _tc(8, 74), _tc(12, 70)],
        ],
        "blast_scene": [
            [(_tc(20, 59), _tc(25, 58)), (_tc(31, 61), _tc(38, 60)), (_tc(28, 55), _tc(33, 56))],
            ["checking markings", "covering road", "checking timer"],
            [_tc(20, 59), SNIPER_NEST_POS, _tc(33, 40)],
        ],
        "south_ditch": [
            [(_tc(12, 50), _tc(18, 49)), (_tc(20, 47), _tc(23, 53)), (_tc(13, 50), _tc(17, 52))],
            ["watching bank", "covering ditch", "tracking signal"],
            [_tc(20, 47), _tc(6, 49), _tc(13, 50)],
        ],
        "north_service": [
            [(_tc(51, 43), _tc(58, 42)), (_tc(55, 45), _tc(62, 47)), (_tc(61, 43), _tc(56, 48))],
            ["checking gate", "checking tread", "comms diag"],
            [SNIPER_NEST_POS, _tc(55, 45), _tc(61, 43)],
        ],
        "toll_plaza": [
            [(_tc(38, 33), _tc(35, 34)), (_tc(34, 37), _tc(40, 35)), (_tc(39, 36), _tc(37, 37))],
            ["watching gantry", "covering lane", "checking radio"],
            [SNIPER_NEST_POS, SNIPER_NEST_POS, FIELD_RADIO_POS],
        ],
        "east_control": [
            [(_tc(55, 23), _tc(58, 20)), (_tc(63, 22), _tc(64, 18)), (_tc(61, 16), _tc(59, 15))],
            ["covering door", "watching hall", "terminal check"],
            [_tc(51, 25), _tc(67, 9), UPLOAD_TERMINAL_POS],
        ],
    }
    posts, activities, lookats = zone_posts.get(zone, zone_posts["approach"])

    # If Biribel is close to an unfound clue, the role-relevant teammate moves
    # to that clue, not to Biribel.
    clue = _nearest_unfound_clue(max_dist=380)
    chosen_posts = [post_pair[int(patrol_step)] for post_pair in posts]
    chosen_activities = list(activities)
    chosen_lookats = list(lookats)
    if clue:
        cx, cy = clue["pos"]
        kind = clue.get("kind")
        if kind in ("mag_paint", "range_card"):
            chosen_posts[0] = (cx - 38, cy + 26)
            chosen_activities[0] = "reading marks"
            chosen_lookats[0] = clue["pos"]
        elif kind in ("bootprints", "timer_fragment"):
            chosen_posts[1] = (cx + 38, cy + 22)
            chosen_activities[1] = "inspecting scene"
            chosen_lookats[1] = clue["pos"]
        elif kind == "radio":
            chosen_posts[2] = (cx + 34, cy + 18)
            chosen_activities[2] = "checking static"
            chosen_lookats[2] = clue["pos"]

    for actor, target, activity, look_at in zip(STATE.mission2_squad, chosen_posts, chosen_activities, chosen_lookats):
        actor["activity"] = activity
        actor["look_at"] = look_at
        _move_actor_towards(actor, target, rate=0.035, max_step=1.85)


def _sniper_phase_activity():
    return


def _update_squad_actor_positions():
    squad = getattr(STATE, "mission2_squad", None)
    if not squad or len(squad) < 3:
        return

    gabi, seby, darius = squad[0], squad[1], squad[2]
    seby_activity = (seby.get("activity") or "").lower()
    darius_activity = (darius.get("activity") or "").lower()
    pt = int(getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_phase_timer", 0))

    if "sprint" in seby_activity or "drag" in seby_activity or "kneel" in seby_activity:
        if "_sprint_origin" not in seby:
            seby["_sprint_origin"] = tuple(seby["pos"])
        if "_corpse_origin" not in gabi:
            gabi["_corpse_origin"] = tuple(gabi["pos"])

        s_ox, s_oy = seby["_sprint_origin"]
        g_ox, g_oy = gabi["_corpse_origin"]

        kneel_x, kneel_y = g_ox - 22, g_oy - 4
        cover_x, cover_y = g_ox - 70, g_oy + 18

        if "sprint" in seby_activity:
            start = _beat("movement_two", "seby_breaks", 80)
            end = _beat("movement_two", "seby_arrives", 180)
            t = max(0.0, min(1.0, (pt - start) / max(1, end - start)))
            t = 1.0 - (1.0 - t) ** 2
            seby["pos"] = [s_ox + (kneel_x - s_ox) * t,
                           s_oy + (kneel_y - s_oy) * t]

        elif "drag" in seby_activity or "kneel" in seby_activity:
            # Use cover-lerp during the actual dragging phase.
            # _drag_lerp was only used for the earlier sprint-to-Gabi phase.
            t = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(seby.get("_cover_lerp", seby.get("_drag_lerp", 0.0)))))
            start = _beat("movement_two", "seby_arrives", 180)
            end = _beat("movement_two", "seby_drag_to_cover_end", 340)
            t = max(0.0, min(1.0, (pt - start) / max(1, end - start)))
            t = t * t * (3.0 - 2.0 * t)
            seby["pos"] = [kneel_x + (cover_x + 18 - kneel_x) * t,
                           kneel_y + (cover_y - 4 - kneel_y) * t]
            gabi["pos"] = [g_ox + (cover_x - g_ox) * t,
                           g_oy + (cover_y - g_oy) * t]

            if pt % 5 == 0:
                STATE.blood_decals.append([
                    gabi["pos"][0] + random.randint(-4, 4),
                    gabi["pos"][1] + random.randint(-2, 2),
                    90,
                    random.randint(8, 14),
                ])
    else:
        seby.pop("_sprint_origin", None)
        gabi.pop("_corpse_origin", None)

    if "reach" in darius_activity:
        if "_reach_origin" not in darius:
            darius["_reach_origin"] = tuple(darius["pos"])
        ox, oy = darius["_reach_origin"]
        darius["pos"] = [ox + 18, oy - 8]
    elif "_reach_origin" in darius and "down" not in darius_activity:
        ox, oy = darius["_reach_origin"]
        darius["pos"] = [ox, oy]


SNIPER_PHASE_NAMES = ["regroup", "movement_one", "movement_two", "movement_three"]


def _sniper_phase_index():
    name = getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_phase", "regroup")
    return SNIPER_PHASE_NAMES.index(name) if name in SNIPER_PHASE_NAMES else 0

def _sniper_phase():
    return SNIPER_PHASES[_sniper_phase_index()]

def _sniper_phase_name():
    return getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_phase", "")

def _sniper_phase_event(name):
    events = getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_events_fired", None)
    if events is None:
        events = set()
        STATE.mission2_sniper_events_fired = events
    if name in events:
        return False
    events.add(name)
    return True


def _sniper_actor_pos(index):
    try:
        return tuple(STATE.mission2_squad[index]["pos"])
    except Exception:
        return KILL_ZONE


def _midpoint(a, b, t=0.5):
    return (
        a[0] + (b[0] - a[0]) * t,
        a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * t,
    )


def _avg_points(points):
    if not points:
        return KILL_ZONE
    return (
        sum(p[0] for p in points) / len(points),
        sum(p[1] for p in points) / len(points),
    )


def _squad_focus(include_dead=True):
    pts = []
    for actor in getattr(STATE, "mission2_squad", []):
        if include_dead or actor.get("alive", True):
            pts.append(tuple(actor["pos"]))
    return _avg_points(pts) if pts else KILL_ZONE


def _sniper_camera_target(t, phase):
    """Anchor-based camera target — the camera holds fixed compositions."""
    if phase == "movement_one":
        if t < _beat("movement_one", "gabi_crack") - 25:
            return CAM_SQUAD_WIDE
        if t < _beat("movement_one", "gantry_hold"):
            return CAM_GABI_BODY          # hold completely still on the body
        if t < _beat("movement_one", "seby_react"):
            return CAM_GANTRY_REVEAL      # hold for ~200 frames on the gantry
        return CAM_SEBY_DRAG              # settle on Seby for the transition

    elif phase == "movement_two":
        if t < _beat("movement_two", "seby_crack") + 60:
            return CAM_SEBY_DRAG          # hold during the entire drag
        if t < _beat("movement_two", "darius_crack") + 40:
            return CAM_DARIUS_RADIO       # hold on Darius
        return CAM_DARIUS_RADIO

    elif phase == "movement_three":
        if STATE.player:
            return (STATE.player.rect.centerx, STATE.player.rect.centery - 20)
        return KILL_ZONE

    return KILL_ZONE


def _advance_sniper_phase():
    idx = _sniper_phase_index() + 1
    if idx >= len(SNIPER_PHASE_NAMES):
        _finish_sniper_sequence()
        return
    STATE.mission2_sniper_phase = SNIPER_PHASE_NAMES[idx]
    STATE.mission2_sniper_phase_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_sniper_events_fired = set()


def _start_sniper_aftermath():
    STATE.mission2_stage = STAGE_COMBAT
    STATE.mission_phase = "m2_killbox_get_cover"
    STATE.mission2_post_sniper_phase = "get_cover"
    STATE.mission2_post_sniper_timer = 0

    STATE.mission2_grace_timer = SNIPER_GRACE_FRAMES

    STATE.mission2_sniper_pressure_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_sniper_pending_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_sniper_target = None
    STATE.mission2_sniper_suppressed = False

    STATE.mission2_sniper_lock = 0
    STATE.mission2_sniper_lock_max = SNIPER_LOCK_FRAMES
    STATE.mission2_sniper_warned = set()
    STATE.mission2_sniper_lethal_target = None
    STATE.mission2_sniper_kill_flash = 0
    STATE.mission2_cover_hold = 0

    STATE.mission2_input_freeze = 18
    STATE.mission2_camera_focus = None
    STATE.mission2_camera_focus_timer = 0

    _spawn_initial_ipa_team()

    _set_objective("GET TO COVER — sniper has the lane")


def _finish_sniper_sequence():
    _start_sniper_aftermath()

def _nearest_killbox_cover():
    if not STATE.player:
        return KILLBOX_COVER_POSITIONS[0]
    px, py = _player_pos()
    return min(KILLBOX_COVER_POSITIONS, key=lambda p: math.hypot(p[0] - px, p[1] - py))


def _nearest_flank_marker():
    if not STATE.player:
        return KILLBOX_FLANK_POSITIONS[0]
    px, py = _player_pos()
    return min(KILLBOX_FLANK_POSITIONS, key=lambda p: math.hypot(p[0] - px, p[1] - py))


def _sniper_alive():
    return any(getattr(e, "is_mission2_sniper", False) and e.hp > 0 for e in STATE.enemies)


def _player_exposed_to_sniper():
    if (
        not STATE.player
        or not _sniper_alive()
        or getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_suppressed", False)
        or getattr(STATE, "mission2_stage", "") != STAGE_COMBAT
    ):
        return False

    px, py = _player_pos()
    sx, sy = SNIPER_NEST_POS

    dist = math.hypot(px - sx, py - sy)

    # The sniper should not shoot across the entire giant map,
    # but if you are anywhere near the checkpoint/gantry road and visible,
    # you are in danger.
    if dist > SNIPER_MAX_LOS_RANGE:
        return False

    # If nothing blocks line-of-sight, the sniper sees you.
    # No tiny killbox rectangle. No magic safety just because you walked past it.
    return not _wall_blocks_line(sx, sy, px, py, _solid_walls())


def _player_in_meaningful_cover():
    if not STATE.player:
        return False
    if _player_exposed_to_sniper():
        STATE.mission2_cover_hold = 0
        return False
    STATE.mission2_cover_hold = min(90, getattr(STATE, "mission2_cover_hold", 0) + 1)
    return STATE.mission2_cover_hold >= 30


def _suppress_or_remove_sniper():
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_suppressed", False):
        return
    STATE.mission2_sniper_suppressed = True
    for e in STATE.enemies[:]:
        if getattr(e, "is_mission2_sniper", False):
            e.hp = 0
            _burst(e.rect.centerx, e.rect.centery, (90, 90, 85), 18, speed_mul=1.4)
    STATE.mission2_sniper_lasers = []
    STATE.mission2_sniper_pending_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_sniper_target = None
    audio.play("radio_static", volume=0.24)
    _radio('VASILE: "Sniper is relocating. Clear the cleanup team."', color=C_VASILE, mark="sniper_suppressed")


def _player_too_close_to_active_sniper():
    if not STATE.player or STATE.player_dead:
        return False
    if not _sniper_alive():
        return False
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_suppressed", False):
        return False
    px, py = _player_pos()
    return math.hypot(px - SNIPER_NEST_POS[0], py - SNIPER_NEST_POS[1]) <= SNIPER_CLOSE_KILL_RADIUS


def _sniper_close_execute_player():
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_close_execute_done", False):
        return

    STATE.mission2_sniper_close_execute_done = True

    if not STATE.player:
        return

    px, py = _player_pos()

    _sniper_fire(
        (px, py),
        body=True,
        trace_life=70,
        shake=52,
    )

    _sniper_callout(
        SNIPER_CLOSE_KILL_TEXT,
        (px, py),
        color=(255, 55, 35),
        frames=120,
    )

    _burst(px, py, C_BLOOD, 70, speed_mul=2.0)
    STATE.blood_decals.append([px, py, 220, random.randint(20, 30)])

    STATE.player.hp = 0
    STATE.player_dead = True
    STATE.death_timer = 90
    STATE.screen_shake = max(STATE.screen_shake, 48)
    STATE.mission2_near_miss_flash = max(getattr(STATE, "mission2_near_miss_flash", 0), 50)
    STATE.mission2_tinnitus_timer = max(getattr(STATE, "mission2_tinnitus_timer", 0), 130)

    audio.play("sniper_crack", volume=1.0)
    audio.play("bullet_impact_enemy", volume=0.85)
    audio.schedule(8, "tinnitus", volume=0.55)

    STATE.floating_texts.append([
        px,
        py - 54,
        SNIPER_CLOSE_KILL_TEXT,
        (255, 70, 45),
        100,
    ])


def _update_sniper_pressure():
    if (
        STATE.mission2_stage != STAGE_COMBAT
        or not _sniper_alive()
        or getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_suppressed", False)
        or not STATE.player
        or STATE.player_dead
    ):
        return

    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_grace_timer", 0) > 0:
        STATE.mission2_grace_timer -= 1
        return

    px, py = _player_pos()
    sx, sy = SNIPER_NEST_POS
    dist_to_sniper = math.hypot(px - sx, py - sy)
    exposed = _player_exposed_to_sniper()

    # Already aiming/firing
    if STATE.mission2_sniper_pending_timer > 0:
        STATE.mission2_sniper_pending_timer -= 1

        # Keep laser snapping to the player's current position
        if exposed and STATE.mission2_sniper_pending_timer in (28, 18, 8, 4):
            tx, ty = _player_pos()
            STATE.mission2_sniper_target = (
                tx + random.randint(-10, 10),
                ty + random.randint(-10, 10),
            )
            _add_sniper_laser(STATE.mission2_sniper_target, life=24)

        if STATE.mission2_sniper_pending_timer <= 0:
            target = STATE.mission2_sniper_target or _player_pos()

            if exposed:
                too_close = dist_to_sniper <= SNIPER_POINT_BLANK_RANGE

                if too_close:
                    damage = SNIPER_EXECUTION_DAMAGE
                    msg = "SNIPER EXECUTION"
                else:
                    damage = SNIPER_DAMAGE
                    msg = "SNIPER HIT"

                STATE.player.hp -= damage
                _burst(STATE.player.rect.centerx, STATE.player.rect.centery, C_BLOOD, 18, speed_mul=1.4)

                STATE.floating_texts.append([
                    STATE.player.rect.centerx,
                    STATE.player.rect.centery - 48,
                    msg,
                    (255, 55, 35),
                    80,
                ])

                _sniper_fire_at(_player_pos(), body=True, trace_life=38, shake=34 if too_close else 26)

            else:
                # Player reached cover before the shot landed
                cover = _nearest_killbox_cover()
                _sniper_fire_at(cover, concrete=True, trace_life=32, shake=18)

            STATE.mission2_sniper_pressure_timer = 120
            STATE.mission2_sniper_target = None
            return

    # Not currently aiming
    if exposed:
        STATE.mission2_sniper_pressure_timer -= 1

        # If the player walks close to the sniper, near-instant kill.
        if dist_to_sniper <= SNIPER_POINT_BLANK_RANGE:
            STATE.mission2_sniper_pending_timer = SNIPER_CLOSE_AIM_FRAMES
            STATE.mission2_sniper_target = _player_pos()
            _add_sniper_laser(STATE.mission2_sniper_target, life=18)
            audio.play("rifle_handle", volume=0.35)
            STATE.floating_texts.append([
                px,
                py - 58,
                "TOO CLOSE — SNIPER HAS YOU",
                (255, 60, 40),
                60,
            ])
            return

        # Normal long-range sniper acquisition
        if STATE.mission2_sniper_pressure_timer <= 0:
            STATE.mission2_sniper_pending_timer = SNIPER_AIM_FRAMES
            STATE.mission2_sniper_target = (
                px + random.randint(-12, 12),
                py + random.randint(-12, 12),
            )
            _add_sniper_laser(STATE.mission2_sniper_target, life=44)
            audio.play("rifle_handle", volume=0.30)
            STATE.floating_texts.append([
                px,
                py - 58,
                "SNIPER LINE!",
                (255, 110, 60),
                58,
            ])

    else:
        # If covered, keep pressure timer from instantly firing the moment player peeks.
        STATE.mission2_sniper_pressure_timer = max(45, STATE.mission2_sniper_pressure_timer)


def _update_post_sniper_gameplay():
    if STATE.mission2_stage != STAGE_COMBAT:
        return
    phase = getattr(STATE, "mission2_post_sniper_phase", "")
    if not phase:
        return
    STATE.mission2_post_sniper_timer += 1
    _update_sniper_pressure()

    if phase == "get_cover":
        STATE.mission_phase = "m2_get_cover"
        if STATE.mission2_post_sniper_timer == 1:
            _spawn_initial_ipa_team()
        if STATE.mission2_post_sniper_timer == 15:
            _set_objective("GET TO COVER — break the sniper line")
            _radio('VASILE: "Concrete blocks! Left side! Move now!"', color=(255, 170, 80), mark="post_cover")
        if _player_in_meaningful_cover() and STATE.mission2_post_sniper_timer > 45:
            STATE.mission2_post_sniper_phase = "survive"
            STATE.mission2_post_sniper_timer = 0
            STATE.mission2_sniper_pressure_timer = 70
