            _radio('VASILE: "Hold there. Watch the glint. Move only between shots."', color=C_VASILE, mark="survive_killbox")
            # Player committed to cover -> IPA pours in from the flanks to
            # punish the static position.  First wave is immediate and a bit
            # bigger; follow-up waves are scheduled below in the survive phase.
            if not getattr(STATE, "mission2_reinforcement_triggered", False):
                STATE.mission2_reinforcement_triggered = True
                _spawn_reinforcement_wave(count=4, prefer_far=True)
                STATE.mission2_reinforcement_waves = 1
                STATE.mission2_reinforcement_timer = 360
                _radio('VASILE: "They saw you stop! Contacts moving in from both flanks!"',
                       color=(255, 130, 75), mark="reinforce_warn")
        elif STATE.mission2_post_sniper_timer > 360:
            # Fail-soft: if the player is confused but alive, advance to survival
            # instead of trapping the mission in a tutorial objective.
            STATE.mission2_post_sniper_phase = "survive"
            STATE.mission2_post_sniper_timer = 0
    elif phase == "survive":
        STATE.mission_phase = "m2_survive_killbox"
        _set_objective("SURVIVE THE KILL-BOX — stay behind cover")
        # Keep IPA pressure up while the player holds cover.  Waves taper
        # off in size, and stop entirely once a cap is reached so the fight
        # can actually be cleared.
        if getattr(STATE, "mission2_reinforcement_triggered", False):
            STATE.mission2_reinforcement_timer = max(0, STATE.mission2_reinforcement_timer - 1)
            if (STATE.mission2_reinforcement_timer <= 0
                    and STATE.mission2_reinforcement_waves < 3
                    and len(STATE.enemies) < 7):
                STATE.mission2_reinforcement_waves += 1
                wave_size = 3 if STATE.mission2_reinforcement_waves == 2 else 2
                _spawn_reinforcement_wave(count=wave_size, prefer_far=False)
                STATE.mission2_reinforcement_timer = 420
        if not getattr(STATE, "mission2_late_reveal_played", False) and STATE.mission2_post_sniper_timer > 480:
            STATE.mission2_late_reveal_played = True
            _radio('VASILE: "Biribel — the tanker manifest — it lists a second cargo..."', color=C_VASILE, mark="late_reveal")
        if STATE.mission2_post_sniper_timer > 520 or not _sniper_alive():
            STATE.mission2_post_sniper_phase = "flank"
            STATE.mission2_post_sniper_timer = 0
            _radio('VASILE: "Center lane is death. Flank through the ditch or service road."', color=(255, 170, 80), mark="flank_sniper")
    elif phase == "flank":
        STATE.mission_phase = "m2_flank_sniper"
        _set_objective("FLANK THE SNIPER LINE — reach side cover")
        px, py = _player_pos()
        reached_flank = any(math.hypot(px - f[0], py - f[1]) < 105 for f in KILLBOX_FLANK_POSITIONS)
        reached_nest_angle = math.hypot(px - SNIPER_FLANK_POS[0], py - SNIPER_FLANK_POS[1]) < 160
        if reached_flank or reached_nest_angle or not _sniper_alive():
            _suppress_or_remove_sniper()
            STATE.mission2_post_sniper_phase = "clear"
            STATE.mission2_post_sniper_timer = 0
    elif phase == "clear":
        STATE.mission_phase = "m2_clear_ipa"
        _set_objective(f"CLEAR REMAINING IPA CONTACTS ({len(STATE.enemies)} left)")


def _smoothstep(t):
    return t * t * (3 - 2 * t) if 0 < t < 1 else max(0, min(1, t))

def _ease_out_cubic_m2(t):
    return 1 - pow(1 - max(0, min(1, t)), 3)

def _pulse01(t, period=60):
    """Sawtooth 0→1 over `period` frames, resets at every multiple."""
    return max(0, min(1, (t % period) / period))

def _sniper_set_focus(pos, frames=90, snap=False):
    STATE.mission2_camera_focus = (pos[0], pos[1])
    STATE.mission2_camera_focus_timer = frames
    if snap:
        _snap_camera_to(pos)


def _sniper_callout(label, pos, color=(255, 70, 45), frames=105):
    STATE.mission2_death_callout = {
        "label": label, "pos": (pos[0], pos[1]),
        "color": color, "life": frames, "max_life": frames,
    }


def _sniper_fire(target_pos, body=False, concrete=False, near_miss=False,
                 trace_life=44, shake=30, dead_air=False):
    kind = "near_miss" if near_miss else "concrete" if concrete else "body"
    STATE.mission2_sniper_traces.append({
        "from": SNIPER_NEST_POS, "to": target_pos, "kind": kind,
        "life": trace_life, "max_life": trace_life,
    })
    STATE.mission2_sniper_muzzle_flashes.append({
        "pos": SNIPER_NEST_POS, "life": 16, "max_life": 16,
    })
    STATE.mission2_sniper_flash = max(getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_flash", 0),
                                      24 if near_miss else 16)
    STATE.screen_shake = max(STATE.screen_shake, shake)
    audio.play("bullet_snap", volume=0.9 if near_miss else 0.6)
    audio.schedule(2, "sniper_crack", volume=1.0)
    audio.play("rig_low_boom", volume=0.2)
    if body:
        audio.schedule(5, "bullet_impact_enemy", volume=0.86)
    if concrete or near_miss:
        audio.schedule(5, "concrete_impact", volume=0.95 if near_miss else 0.72)
    _burst(target_pos[0], target_pos[1], (220, 196, 128), 34 if near_miss else 22, speed_mul=2.3)
    _burst(target_pos[0], target_pos[1], (120, 118, 108), 26 if near_miss else 14, speed_mul=1.7)
    audio.schedule(12, "radio_static", volume=0.34 if near_miss else 0.20)
    if near_miss:
        STATE.mission2_near_miss_flash = max(getattr(STATE, "mission2_near_miss_flash", 0), 64)
        STATE.mission2_tinnitus_timer = max(getattr(STATE, "mission2_tinnitus_timer", 0), 150)
        audio.schedule(8, "tinnitus", volume=0.62)
    try:
        if dead_air:
            audio.duck_ambience(frames=_beat("movement_one", "silence_frames", 110), factor=0.0)
        else:
            audio.duck_ambience(frames=55 if not near_miss else 130,
                                factor=0.16 if not near_miss else 0.05)
    except Exception:
        pass


def _sniper_concrete_impact(pos, trace_life=32, shake=20):
    _sniper_fire(pos, concrete=True, trace_life=trace_life, shake=shake)


def _sniper_fire_at(target_pos, body=False, concrete=False, trace_life=44, shake=30):
    _sniper_fire(target_pos, body=body, concrete=concrete, trace_life=trace_life, shake=shake)


def _draw_sniper_callout(surface, fcx, fcy):
    callout = getattr(STATE, "mission2_death_callout", None)
    if not callout:
        return
    sx, sy = _world(callout["pos"], fcx, fcy)
    life = callout.get("life", 0)
    max_life = max(1, callout.get("max_life", 1))
    t = max(0.0, min(1.0, life / max_life))
    color = callout.get("color", (255, 70, 45))
    alpha = int(255 * t)
    r = int(30 + (1.0 - t) * 34)
    m = pygame.Surface((190, 190), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    cx = cy = 95
    pygame.draw.circle(m, (*color, alpha), (cx, cy), r, 3)
    pygame.draw.circle(m, (*color, alpha // 2), (cx, cy), r + 10, 1)
    for ang in (0, 90, 180, 270):
        a = math.radians(ang)
        pygame.draw.line(m, (*color, alpha),
                         (cx + math.cos(a) * (r - 6), cy + math.sin(a) * (r - 6)),
                         (cx + math.cos(a) * (r + 14), cy + math.sin(a) * (r + 14)), 2)
    surface.blit(m, (sx - cx, sy - cy))
    lbl = font_terminal.render(callout["label"], True, color)
    lbl.set_alpha(alpha)
    bg = pygame.Surface((lbl.get_width() + 18, lbl.get_height() + 8), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    bg.fill((0, 0, 0, min(190, alpha)))
    pygame.draw.rect(bg, (*color, min(200, alpha)), bg.get_rect(), 1)
    surface.blit(bg, (sx - bg.get_width() // 2, sy - r - 52))
    surface.blit(lbl, (sx - lbl.get_width() // 2, sy - r - 48))


def _sniper_phase_activity():
    for a in STATE.mission2_squad:
        if not a.get("alive", True):
            a["activity"] = "down"


def _m0_regroup(pt):
    all_set = True
    for actor, target in zip(STATE.mission2_squad, REGROUP_TARGET_POSITIONS):
        actor["look_at"] = KILL_ZONE
        d = math.hypot(target[0] - actor["pos"][0], target[1] - actor["pos"][1])
        if d > 6:
            all_set = False
            actor["activity"] = "regrouping"
            _move_actor_towards(actor, target, rate=0.06, max_step=2.4)
        else:
            actor["pos"][0], actor["pos"][1] = target[0], target[1]
            actor["activity"] = "covering"
    if _beat_now("regroup", pt, "spread_line") and _sniper_phase_event("m0_spread"):
        _radio('VASILE: "Form up at the barrier. Eyes up."', mark="m0_spread", duration=120)


def _m1_regroup_wrong_scan(pt):
    """SHOCK — Gabi dies mid-sentence, no warning."""
    gabi, seby, darius = STATE.mission2_squad
    for actor, target in zip(STATE.mission2_squad, REGROUP_TARGET_POSITIONS):
        if actor.get("alive", True):
            d = math.hypot(target[0] - actor["pos"][0],
                           target[1] - actor["pos"][1])
            if d > 3:
                _move_actor_towards(actor, target, rate=0.10, max_step=2.0)
    if gabi.get("alive"):
        gabi["activity"] = "watching lane"; gabi["look_at"] = KILL_ZONE
    if seby.get("alive"):
        seby["activity"] = "covering west"; seby["look_at"] = _tc(18, 60)
    if darius.get("alive"):
        darius["activity"] = "holding radio"; darius["look_at"] = KILL_ZONE

    if _beat_now("movement_one", pt, "regroup_line") and _sniper_phase_event("m1_gabi1"):
        _radio('GABI: "Nobody likes this lane."', color=(220, 220, 205), mark="m1_gabi1", duration=120)
    if _beat_now("movement_one", pt, "scan_west") and _sniper_phase_event("m1_scan"):
        _radio("SEBY: \"West side's clear. Just burned-out cars.\"", color=(255, 210, 180), mark="m1_scan", duration=120)
    if _beat_now("movement_one", pt, "gabi_crack") and _sniper_phase_event("m1_crack"):
        _radio('GABI: "Vasile, I have—"', color=(220, 220, 205), mark="m1_last", duration=55)
        _kill_actor(0, SNIPER_NEST_POS, dead_air=True)
    if _beat_now("movement_one", pt, "vasile_down") and _sniper_phase_event("m1_vasile"):
        _radio('VASILE: "Everybody DOWN. Do not move."',
               color=C_VASILE, mark="m1_vasile", duration=140)
    if _beat_now("movement_one", pt, "origin_flash") and _sniper_phase_event("m1_origin"):
        STATE.mission2_sniper_origin_revealed = True
        STATE.mission2_sniper_flash = 12
        STATE.mission2_sniper_silhouette_flash = 14
        STATE.mission2_sniper_muzzle_flashes.append({"pos": SNIPER_NEST_POS, "life": 16, "max_life": 16})
        _sniper_set_focus(SNIPER_NEST_POS, frames=55)
        audio.play("sniper_crack", volume=0.5)
    if _beat_now("movement_one", pt, "gantry_hold") and _sniper_phase_event("m1_silhouette2"):
        STATE.mission2_sniper_silhouette_flash = 20
        STATE.mission2_sniper_muzzle_flashes.append({"pos": SNIPER_NEST_POS, "life": 16, "max_life": 16})
        audio.play("sniper_crack", volume=0.35)
    if _beat_now("movement_one", pt, "seby_react") and _sniper_phase_event("m1_react"):
        _radio('SEBY: "GABI! NO!"', color=(255, 210, 180), mark="m1_react", duration=120)
        if seby.get("alive"):
            seby["activity"] = "breaking"; seby["look_at"] = gabi["pos"]


def _m2_seby_darius_die(pt):
    """DESPERATION — Seby drags Gabi, both he and Darius die."""
    gabi, seby, darius = STATE.mission2_squad
    gabi["alive"] = False
    gabi["activity"] = "down"

    seby_breaks   = _beat("movement_two", "seby_breaks", 70)
    seby_arrives  = _beat("movement_two", "seby_arrives", 190)
    drag_end      = _beat("movement_two", "seby_drag_to_cover_end", 380)
    seby_shot     = _beat("movement_two", "seby_crack", 430)

    if pt < seby_breaks:
        if seby.get("alive"):   seby["activity"]   = "frozen shock"
        if darius.get("alive"): darius["activity"] = "frozen"
    elif pt < seby_arrives:
        if seby.get("alive"):   seby["activity"]   = "sprinting_to_gabi"
        if darius.get("alive"): darius["activity"] = "frozen"
    elif pt < drag_end:
        if seby.get("alive"):   seby["activity"]   = "dragging_body"
        if darius.get("alive"): darius["activity"] = "turning to radio"

    if _beat_now("movement_two", pt, "vasile_cover") and _sniper_phase_event("m2_vasile1"):
        _radio('VASILE: "Seby — stay low — do NOT go out there."',
               color=C_VASILE, mark="m2_vasile1", duration=120)
    if _beat_now("movement_two", pt, "vasile_hold") and _sniper_phase_event("m2_vasile2"):
        _radio('VASILE: "Biribel. Stay behind the barrier. Do not look up."',
               color=C_VASILE, mark="m2_vasile2", duration=160)
    if _beat_now("movement_two", pt, "seby_breaks") and _sniper_phase_event("m2_break"):
        _radio('SEBY: "Hold on — I got you — I GOT YOU—"', color=(255, 210, 180), mark="m2_break", duration=130)
    if _beat_now("movement_two", pt, "seby_crack") and _sniper_phase_event("m2_seby"):
        _kill_actor(1, SNIPER_NEST_POS)
    if pt >= seby_shot and darius.get("alive"):
        darius["activity"] = "reaching for radio"
    if _beat_now("movement_two", pt, "darius_reaches") and _sniper_phase_event("m2_dar_reach"):
        _radio('DARIUS: "Mayday, mayday, this is—"', color=(255, 190, 130), mark="m2_mayday", duration=90)
        _sniper_set_focus(_sniper_actor_pos(2), frames=130)
    if _beat_now("movement_two", pt, "darius_crack") and _sniper_phase_event("m2_dar"):
        _kill_actor(2, SNIPER_NEST_POS)


def _m3_near_miss_handoff(pt):
    """YOU'RE NEXT — near-miss on the player, then hard cut."""
    for actor in STATE.mission2_squad:
        actor["alive"] = False
        actor["activity"] = "down"
    if _beat_now("movement_three", pt, "near_miss") and _sniper_phase_event("m3_miss"):
        target = ((STATE.player.rect.centerx + 40, STATE.player.rect.centery - 24)
                  if STATE.player else KILL_ZONE)
        _sniper_set_focus(target, frames=120, snap=True)
        _sniper_fire(target, concrete=True, near_miss=True, trace_life=60, shake=42)
        _sniper_callout("MISSED — YOU'RE NEXT", target, color=(255, 190, 90), frames=120)
        STATE.mission2_chromatic_timer = 90
        STATE.mission2_radio_muffle_timer = 200
        STATE.mission2_heartbeat_active = True
        STATE.mission2_heartbeat_timer = 0
        _radio('VASILE: "Biribel — MOVE!"', mark="m3_move", duration=120)
    if _beat_now("movement_three", pt, "hard_black") and _sniper_phase_event("m3_black"):
        STATE.mission2_hard_black = 6
        _finish_sniper_sequence()


def _update_sniper_sequence():
    pt = int(getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_phase_timer", 0))
    phase = _sniper_phase_name()
    new_target = _sniper_camera_target(pt, phase)
    existing = STATE.mission2_camera_focus
    if existing is None:
        STATE.mission2_camera_focus = new_target
    else:
        cx, cy = existing
        nx, ny = new_target
        if math.hypot(nx - cx, ny - cy) > 4:
            STATE.mission2_camera_focus = new_target
    _sniper_phase_activity()
    if phase == "regroup":           _m0_regroup(pt)
    elif phase == "movement_one":    _m1_regroup_wrong_scan(pt)
    elif phase == "movement_two":    _m2_seby_darius_die(pt)
    elif phase == "movement_three":  _m3_near_miss_handoff(pt)
    _update_squad_actor_positions()
    STATE.mission2_sniper_phase_timer = pt + 1
    if STATE.mission2_sniper_phase_timer >= _sniper_phase().get("duration", 120):
        _advance_sniper_phase()


def _start_sniper_sequence():
    STATE.mission2_stage = STAGE_SNIPER
    STATE.mission_phase = "m2_sniper"
    STATE.mission2_sniper_phase = "regroup"
    STATE.mission2_sniper_phase_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_sniper_events_fired = set()
    STATE.mission2_sniper_origin_revealed = False
    STATE.mission2_sniper_silhouette_flash = 0
    STATE.mission2_sniper_flash = 0
    STATE.mission2_death_callout = None
    STATE.mission2_camera_focus = SNIPER_ARENA_CAM
    STATE.mission2_camera_focus_timer = 0
    for actor, entry in zip(STATE.mission2_squad, REGROUP_ENTRY_POSITIONS):
        actor["alive"] = True
        actor["pos"] = list(entry)
        actor["activity"] = "regrouping"
        actor.pop("_sprint_origin", None)
        actor.pop("_corpse_origin", None)
        actor.pop("_reach_origin", None)
        actor.pop("_cover_lerp", None)
        actor.pop("_drag_lerp", None)
    _snap_camera_to(SNIPER_ARENA_CAM)
    _set_objective("")
    audio.play("radio_static", volume=0.2)


def _kill_actor(index, origin_pos, dead_air=False):
    actor = STATE.mission2_squad[index]
    if not actor.get("alive", True):
        return
    actor["alive"] = False
    actor["activity"] = "down"
    pos = tuple(actor.get("pos", (0, 0)))
    name = actor.get("name", "SQUAD")
    _sniper_callout(f"{name} DOWN", pos, color=(255, 70, 45), frames=110)
    _sniper_fire(pos, body=True, trace_life=60, shake=22, dead_air=dead_air)
    _burst(pos[0], pos[1], C_BLOOD, 46, speed_mul=1.2)
    STATE.blood_decals.append([pos[0], pos[1], 190, random.randint(16, 24)])
    STATE.mission2_chromatic_timer = max(getattr(STATE, "mission2_chromatic_timer", 0), 28)


def _add_sniper_laser(target_pos, life=55):
    STATE.mission2_sniper_lasers.append({"from": SNIPER_NEST_POS, "to": target_pos, "life": life})


def _spawn_initial_ipa_team():
    if "initial_ipa_team" in STATE.mission2_events:
        return
    STATE.mission2_events.add("initial_ipa_team")
    spawned = [
        _spawn_enemy(58, 26, hp=90, speed=0.0, sniper=True),
        _spawn_enemy(47, 35, hp=115, speed=1.85),
        _spawn_enemy(52, 41, hp=115, speed=1.85),
        _spawn_enemy(42, 31, hp=105, speed=1.75),
    ]
    for e in spawned:
        e.fire_cooldown = 150 + random.randint(0, 80)
    _radio('VASILE: "IPA rifles from the gantry. Not MAG. Clear the lanes."', color=(255, 170, 80), mark="ipa_reveal")


def _spawn_reinforcement_wave(count=3, prefer_far=True):
    """Pour in IPA reinforcements from kill-box perimeter spawn anchors.

    Triggered when the player commits to cover during the kill-box.  The wave
    picks spawn anchors farther from the player so it reads as enemies closing
    in from the flanks rather than materializing on top of them.  Each spawn
    gets a small dust/spark burst so it doesn't pop in silently.
    """
    if not STATE.player:
        return
    px, py = _player_pos()
    anchors = list(KILLBOX_REINFORCEMENT_SPAWNS)
    # Sort by distance; pick from the far half if prefer_far, else random.
    anchors.sort(key=lambda a: math.hypot(_tc(*a)[0] - px, _tc(*a)[1] - py),
                 reverse=prefer_far)
    pool = anchors[: max(count + 2, 4)]
    random.shuffle(pool)
    spawned = []
    for col, row in pool[:count]:
        # Small tile jitter so successive waves don't stack identically.
        jc = col + random.randint(-1, 1)
        jr = row + random.randint(-1, 1)
        e = _spawn_enemy(jc, jr, hp=100, speed=2.05)
        e.fire_cooldown = 70 + random.randint(0, 50)
        spawned.append(e)
        # Visual cue: dust kick + spark so the spawn reads as "running in."
        ex, ey = e.rect.center
        _burst(ex, ey, (120, 110, 95), 14, speed_mul=1.6)
        _burst(ex, ey, (255, 200, 110), 4, speed_mul=2.1)
    if spawned:
        audio.play("radio_static", volume=0.18)
        audio.schedule(8, "footstep", volume=0.35)
    return spawned


def _spawn_control_cleanup():
    # Small authored team; do not overwhelm the evidence phase.
    if "control_cleanup" in STATE.mission2_events:
        return
    STATE.mission2_events.add("control_cleanup")
    _spawn_enemy(54, 20, hp=105, speed=2.0)
    _spawn_enemy(64, 22, hp=105, speed=2.0)
    _radio('SYSTEM: IPA cleanup team in control sector.', color=(255, 130, 75), mark="cleanup", allow_when_cut=True)


def _start_tanker_run():
    STATE.mission2_stage = STAGE_RUN
    STATE.mission_phase = "m2_tanker_run"
    STATE.mission2_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_countdown = 420
    STATE.mission2_camera_focus = None
    _set_objective("RUN to the blue shelter marker before the tanker detonates")
    _radio('VASILE: "Hidden detonator! Run to the concrete shelter!"', color=(255, 150, 70), mark="run")
    audio.play("alarm", volume=0.45)


def _explode_tanker():
    if STATE.mission2_tanker_exploded:
        return
    STATE.mission2_tanker_exploded = True
    STATE.mission2_stage = STAGE_BLACKOUT
    STATE.mission_phase = "m2_blackout"
    STATE.mission2_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_countdown = 0
    STATE.mission2_comms_cut = True
    STATE.mission2_camera_focus = TANKER_POS
    _set_objective("Comms lost")
    audio.play("explosion", volume=1.0)
    audio.play("rig_low_boom", volume=0.48)
    STATE.screen_shake = 62
    STATE.gc_flash_timer = 25
    tanker = STATE.mission2_tanker_rect
    for _ in range(160):
        col = random.choice([(255, 95, 20), (255, 190, 60), (95, 88, 76), (42, 37, 33)])
        p = Particle(tanker.centerx + random.randint(-110, 110), tanker.centery + random.randint(-80, 80), col)
        p.dx *= 3.2
        p.dy *= 3.2
        p.life = random.randint(35, 95)
        STATE.particles.append(p)
    for ox, oy in [(-80, -40), (0, 0), (86, 44)]:
        _spawn_molotov_fire(tanker.centerx + ox, tanker.centery + oy, _solid_walls())
    if STATE.player:
        d = _dist(_player_pos(), TANKER_POS)
        if d < 310:
            dmg = max(10, int(90 * (1.0 - d / 310)))
            STATE.player.hp -= dmg
            STATE.floating_texts.append([STATE.player.rect.centerx, STATE.player.rect.centery - 48, f"-{dmg} HP BLAST", (255, 75, 45), 110])


def _complete_mission2():
    if getattr(STATE, "request_mission2_ending", False):
        return
    STATE.mission2_stage = STAGE_DONE
    STATE.mission2_evidence_uploaded = True
    STATE.mission_end_time = pygame.time.get_ticks()
    STATE.request_mission2_ending = True
    audio.play("terminal_glitch", volume=0.55)


def handle_interact():
    """Mission-specific [E] interactions. Called through game_core."""
    if not STATE.player or STATE.player_dead:
        return
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_card", None):
        if STATE.mission2_card.get("age", 0) >= 18:
            _close_card()
        return

    stage = STATE.mission2_stage
    if stage == STAGE_SNIPER:
        gabi_pos = _sniper_actor_pos(0)
        if _dist(_player_pos(), gabi_pos) < 90:
            # Point 10: Player can try to save Gabi during the 40-frame window
            # Input is read but does nothing; acknowledged later in Vasile line
            window_start = _beat("movement_one", "tried_to_save_window_start", 340)
            window_end = _beat("movement_one", "tried_to_save_window_end", 380)
            pt = int(getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_phase_timer", 0))
            if window_start <= pt < window_end:
                if not getattr(STATE, "mission2_tried_to_save", False):
                    STATE.mission2_tried_to_save = True
                    # Store that player tried - will be acknowledged later
                    STATE.mission2_tried_to_save_acknowledged = False
            return
    if stage == STAGE_INVESTIGATE:
        for clue in STATE.mission2_clues:
            if not clue["done"] and _near(clue["pos"], 86):
                clue["done"] = True
                STATE.mission2_clues_found.add(clue["id"])
                _open_card(clue["title"], clue["lines"], color=(245, 190, 65), kind=clue.get("kind", "document"))
                if _clue_count() >= REQUIRED_INVESTIGATION_CLUES:
                    _set_objective("Regroup at the blue toll-barrier marker")
                return
    elif stage == STAGE_TRUCK:
        truck = STATE.mission2_truck_manifest
        if not truck["done"] and _near(truck["pos"], 100):
            truck["done"] = True
            _open_card(truck["title"], truck["lines"], color=(255, 190, 90), after="truck_done", kind=truck.get("kind", "manifest"))
            return
    elif stage == STAGE_EVIDENCE:
        if STATE.enemies:
            if any(_near(ev["pos"], 82) for ev in STATE.mission2_evidence if not ev["done"]):
                STATE.floating_texts.append([STATE.player.rect.centerx, STATE.player.rect.centery - 42, "CLEAR IPA CONTACTS FIRST", (255, 120, 75), 90])
            return
        for ev in STATE.mission2_evidence:
            if not ev["done"] and _near(ev["pos"], 82):
                ev["done"] = True
                after = "evidence_ready" if _evidence_count() >= 3 else None
                _open_card(ev["title"], ev["lines"], color=C_TERMINAL, after=after, kind=ev.get("kind", "terminal"))
                return
        if _evidence_count() >= 3 and _near(UPLOAD_TERMINAL_POS, 95):
            _set_objective("Hold [E] at the upload terminal")


def blocks_player_actions():
    """True when the main input router should suppress weapons/items."""
    return (
        getattr(STATE, "active_mission", "") == MISSION2_KEY
        and (
            getattr(STATE, "mission2_card", None) is not None
            or getattr(STATE, "mission2_stage", "") in (STAGE_SNIPER, STAGE_BLACKOUT, STAGE_DONE)
        )
    )


def debug_jump_to_evidence():
    if not STATE.player:
        return
    STATE.mission2_stage = STAGE_EVIDENCE
    STATE.mission_phase = "m2_debug_evidence"
    STATE.mission2_comms_cut = True
    STATE.mission2_tanker_exploded = True
    STATE.mission2_camera_focus = None
    STATE.enemies.clear()
    STATE.player.rect.center = CONTROL_ROOM
    _set_objective("Debug: inspect green evidence terminals")


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# Stage update
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
def _movement_locked():
    return (
        STATE.player_dead
        or STATE.mission2_stage in (STAGE_SNIPER, STAGE_BLACKOUT, STAGE_DONE)
        or getattr(STATE, "mission2_card", None) is not None
    )


def _update_stage(keys):
    stage = STATE.mission2_stage
    STATE.mission2_timer += 1
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_card", None):
        STATE.mission2_card["age"] += 1
        return

    if stage == STAGE_INVESTIGATE:
        count = _clue_count()
        total = len(STATE.mission2_clues)
        if count < REQUIRED_INVESTIGATION_CLUES:
            _set_objective(f"Inspect staged evidence markers with [E] ({count}/{REQUIRED_INVESTIGATION_CLUES})")
        else:
            _set_objective("Regroup at the blue toll-barrier marker")
            if count >= total and "all_clues_warning" not in STATE.mission2_events:
                STATE.mission2_events.add("all_clues_warning")
                _radio('VASILE: "Range marks, timing relay, clean boots... this scene is a trap."', color=(255, 170, 80), mark="all_clues")
            if _near(KILL_ZONE, 120):
                _start_sniper_sequence()

    elif stage == STAGE_SNIPER:
        _update_sniper_sequence()

    elif stage == STAGE_COMBAT:
        _update_post_sniper_gameplay()
        post_phase = getattr(STATE, "mission2_post_sniper_phase", "")
        if STATE.enemies:
            if post_phase in ("", "clear"):
                _set_objective(f"CLEAR REMAINING IPA CONTACTS ({len(STATE.enemies)} left)")
        else:
            STATE.mission2_post_sniper_phase = ""
            STATE.mission2_stage = STAGE_TRUCK
            STATE.mission_phase = "m2_truck"
            _set_objective("Inspect the IPA truck manifest with [E]")
            _radio('VASILE: "No MAG soldiers. Find out why that IPA truck is here."', mark="truck")
    elif stage == STAGE_TRUCK:
        _set_objective("Inspect the IPA truck manifest with [E]")
    elif stage == STAGE_TANKER:
        if _near(TANKER_POS, 170):
            _start_tanker_run()
        else:
            _set_objective("Approach the leaking fuel tanker")
    elif stage == STAGE_RUN:
        if _near(SAFE_ZONE, 105):
            STATE.mission2_countdown = min(STATE.mission2_countdown, 90)
            _set_objective("Stay in cover — brace for the tanker blast")
        else:
            _set_objective("RUN to the blue shelter marker before the tanker detonates")
        STATE.mission2_countdown -= 1
        if STATE.mission2_countdown <= 0:
            _explode_tanker()
    elif stage == STAGE_BLACKOUT:
        if STATE.mission2_timer >= 210:
            _open_card(
                "COMMS LOST",
                [
                    "Vasile cuts out mid-sentence.",
                    "The checkpoint loudspeakers are dead.",
                    "Only the control building still has power.",
                ],
                color=(165, 170, 180),
                after="blast_done",
                kind="static",
            )
    elif stage == STAGE_EVIDENCE:
        found = _evidence_count()
        if STATE.enemies:
            _set_objective(f"Clear IPA cleanup team ({len(STATE.enemies)} left)")
        elif found < 3:
            _set_objective(f"Inspect green evidence terminals with [E] ({found}/3)")
        else:
            _set_objective("Hold [E] at the upload terminal")
            if _near(UPLOAD_TERMINAL_POS, 95) and keys[pygame.K_e]:
                STATE.mission2_stage = STAGE_UPLOAD
                STATE.mission_phase = "m2_upload"
                STATE.mission2_timer = 0
    elif stage == STAGE_UPLOAD:
        if not _near(UPLOAD_TERMINAL_POS, 110):
            _set_objective("Return to the upload terminal")
            STATE.mission2_upload_progress = max(0.0, STATE.mission2_upload_progress - 1 / 360)
        else:
            _set_objective("Hold [E] to upload Route 12 evidence")
            if keys[pygame.K_e]:
                if not STATE.mission2_upload_started:
                    STATE.mission2_upload_started = True
                    _radio('SYSTEM: TRANSMITTING ROUTE 12 EVIDENCE PACKAGE', color=C_TERMINAL, allow_when_cut=True, mark="upload")
                STATE.mission2_upload_progress = min(1.0, STATE.mission2_upload_progress + 1 / 240)
                if STATE.mission2_upload_progress >= 1.0:
                    _open_card(
                        "TRANSMISSION SENT",
                        [
                            "The Route 12 evidence package is out.",
                            "IPA corrupted part of the archive, but enough survived.",
                            "Move to extraction before the state broadcast lands.",
                        ],
                        color=C_TERMINAL,
                        after="upload_done",
                        kind="terminal",
                    )
            else:
                STATE.mission2_upload_progress = max(0.0, STATE.mission2_upload_progress - 1 / 500)
    elif stage == STAGE_EXFIL:
        _set_objective("Reach the green exfil marker")
        if _near(EXFIL_POS, 92):
            _complete_mission2()


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# Combat/update helpers
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
def _update_player(keys, solid_walls):
    if not STATE.player or STATE.player_dead:
        return
    if STATE.mission2_stage == STAGE_INVESTIGATE:
        STATE.player.speed = 3.45
    elif STATE.mission2_stage == STAGE_RUN:
        STATE.player.speed = 4.45
    else:
        STATE.player.speed = 3.6

    mx, my = get_scaled_mouse()
    world_mx = mx + STATE.camera_x
    world_my = my + STATE.camera_y
    STATE.player.angle = math.atan2(world_my - STATE.player.rect.centery, world_mx - STATE.player.rect.centerx)

    dx = dy = 0
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_input_freeze", 0) > 0:
        keys_for_move = {pygame.K_w: False, pygame.K_s: False, pygame.K_a: False, pygame.K_d: False}
    else:
        keys_for_move = _last_keys
    if not _movement_locked():
        if keys_for_move[pygame.K_w]:
            dy -= STATE.player.speed
        if keys_for_move[pygame.K_s]:
            dy += STATE.player.speed
        if keys_for_move[pygame.K_a]:
            dx -= STATE.player.speed
        if keys_for_move[pygame.K_d]:
            dx += STATE.player.speed
    if dx and dy:
        dx *= 0.707
        dy *= 0.707
    STATE.player.move(dx, dy, solid_walls)

    if dx or dy:
        STATE._footstep_timer -= 1
        if STATE._footstep_timer <= 0:
            audio.play("footstep", volume=0.25)
            STATE._footstep_timer = 27 + random.randint(0, 8)


def _update_weapon_timers():
    if STATE.shot_cooldown > 0:
        STATE.shot_cooldown -= 1
    if STATE.is_reloading:
        STATE.reload_timer -= 1
        if STATE.reload_timer <= 0:
            STATE.is_reloading = False
            if STATE.spare_mags > 0:
                STATE.spare_mags -= 1
                STATE.ammo_in_mag = STATE.mag_size
    if STATE.melee_active:
        STATE.melee_timer -= 1
        if STATE.player and not STATE.melee_hit_done and STATE.melee_timer <= 10:
            px, py = _player_pos()
            for e in STATE.enemies:
                if math.hypot(e.rect.centerx - px, e.rect.centery - py) < 55:
                    e.hp -= 75
                    e.on_hit(px, py)
                    STATE.melee_hit_done = True
                    STATE.shots_hit += 1
                    _burst(e.rect.centerx, e.rect.centery, C_BLOOD, 12)
                    break
        if STATE.melee_timer <= 0:
            STATE.melee_active = False
            STATE.melee_hit_done = False


def _update_molotovs(solid_walls):
    player_hit = False
    for b in STATE.thrown_bottles[:]:
        prev_x, prev_y = b["x"], b["y"]
        b["t"] = min(1.0, b["t"] + b["speed"])
        nx = b["start_x"] + (b["tx"] - b["start_x"]) * b["t"]
        ny = b["start_y"] + (b["ty"] - b["start_y"]) * b["t"]
        ix, iy = nx, ny
        hit_wall = False
        for wall in solid_walls:
            clipped = wall.clipline((prev_x, prev_y), (nx, ny))
            if clipped:
                ix, iy = clipped[0]
                hit_wall = True
                break
        if b["t"] >= 1.0 or hit_wall:
            _spawn_molotov_fire(ix, iy, solid_walls)
            STATE.thrown_bottles.remove(b)
        else:
            b["x"], b["y"] = nx, ny

    for fire in STATE.molotov_fires[:]:
        fire["life"] -= 1
        fx, fy = fire["x"], fire["y"]
        if random.random() < 0.55:
            STATE.particles.append(Particle(fx + random.randint(-55, 55), fy + random.randint(-45, 45), random.choice([(255, 95, 25), (255, 160, 45), (70, 65, 60)])))
        for e in STATE.enemies:
            d = math.hypot(e.rect.centerx - fx, e.rect.centery - fy)
            if d < MOLOTOV_RADIUS and not _wall_blocks_line(fx, fy, e.rect.centerx, e.rect.centery, solid_walls):
                e.hp -= MOLOTOV_ENEMY_DAMAGE * (0.5 + (1 - d / MOLOTOV_RADIUS))
                e.alerted = True
                e.state = "CHASE"
        if STATE.player and not STATE.player_dead and not _movement_locked():
            d = math.hypot(STATE.player.rect.centerx - fx, STATE.player.rect.centery - fy)
            if d < MOLOTOV_RADIUS * 0.7 and not _wall_blocks_line(fx, fy, STATE.player.rect.centerx, STATE.player.rect.centery, solid_walls):
                STATE.player.hp -= MOLOTOV_PLAYER_DAMAGE
                player_hit = True
        if fire["life"] <= 0:
            STATE.molotov_fires.remove(fire)

    if STATE.player and not STATE.player_dead:
        STATE.fire_zones[:] = [fz for fz in STATE.fire_zones if fz.update(STATE.enemies, STATE.player.rect, audio)]
    return player_hit


def _update_sniper_enemy(e, solid_walls):
    """Mission 2 sniper: fixed nest, long sightline, deliberate shots."""
    if not STATE.player or STATE.player_dead:
        return
    px, py = _player_pos()
    dx = px - e.rect.centerx
    dy = py - e.rect.centery
    e.angle = math.atan2(dy, dx)
    if e.fire_cooldown > 0:
        e.fire_cooldown -= 1
    if _wall_blocks_line(e.rect.centerx, e.rect.centery, px, py, solid_walls):
        return
    if e.fire_cooldown <= 0:
        dist = max(1.0, math.hypot(dx, dy))
        vx, vy = dx / dist, dy / dist
        STATE.bullets.append({
            "x": e.rect.centerx + vx * 22,
            "y": e.rect.centery + vy * 22,
            "prev_x": e.rect.centerx + vx * 22,
            "prev_y": e.rect.centery + vy * 22,
            "dx": vx * 28,
            "dy": vy * 28,
            "life": 34,
            "owner": "enemy",
            "damage": 26,
        })
        STATE.mission2_sniper_traces.append({"from": (e.rect.centerx, e.rect.centery), "to": (px, py), "life": 10})
        audio.play("gunshot_distant", volume=0.55)
        e.fire_cooldown = 115 + random.randint(0, 45)


def _update_bullets_enemies(solid_walls):
    player_hit = False
    for b in STATE.bullets[:]:
        b["prev_x"], b["prev_y"] = b["x"], b["y"]
        b["x"] += b["dx"]
        b["y"] += b["dy"]
        b["life"] -= 1
        hit = False
        for wall in solid_walls:
            if wall.clipline((b["prev_x"], b["prev_y"]), (b["x"], b["y"])):
                hit = True
                _burst(b["x"], b["y"], (130, 125, 110), 4)
                audio.play("bullet_impact", volume=0.16)
                break
        if not hit and b.get("owner") == "player":
            for e in STATE.enemies:
                if math.hypot(b["x"] - e.rect.centerx, b["y"] - e.rect.centery) < 22:

                    # You cannot pistol-snipe the sniper from the open road.
                    # If your bullet can reach him, he has already seen you.
                    if getattr(e, "is_mission2_sniper", False) and not getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_suppressed", False):
                        hit = True

                        STATE.mission2_sniper_pressure_timer = 0
                        STATE.mission2_sniper_pending_timer = min(
                            getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_pending_timer", 999),
                            SNIPER_CLOSE_AIM_FRAMES,
                        )
                        STATE.mission2_sniper_target = _player_pos()
                        _add_sniper_laser(STATE.mission2_sniper_target, life=20)

                        STATE.floating_texts.append([
                            STATE.player.rect.centerx,
                            STATE.player.rect.centery - 52,
                            "SNIPER HAS YOUR ANGLE",
                            (255, 60, 40),
                            70,
                        ])

                        _burst(b["x"], b["y"], (140, 135, 120), 10, speed_mul=1.4)
                        audio.play("concrete_impact", volume=0.45)
                        break

                    e.hp -= 50
                    e.on_hit(STATE.player.rect.centerx, STATE.player.rect.centery)
                    hit = True
                    STATE.shots_hit += 1
                    STATE.blood_decals.append([b["x"], b["y"], 140, random.randint(8, 16)])
                    _burst(b["x"], b["y"], C_BLOOD, 14)
                    audio.play("bullet_impact_enemy", volume=0.42)
                    break
        if not hit and b.get("owner") == "enemy" and STATE.player and not STATE.player_dead:
            if math.hypot(b["x"] - STATE.player.rect.centerx, b["y"] - STATE.player.rect.centery) < 22:
                if getattr(STATE, "mission2_grace_timer", 0) <= 0:
                    STATE.player.hp -= b.get("damage", ENEMY_DAMAGE)
                    player_hit = True
                hit = True
                STATE.screen_shake = max(STATE.screen_shake, 6)
                _burst(b["x"], b["y"], C_BLOOD, 8)
                audio.play("bullet_impact_enemy", volume=0.5)
        if hit or b["life"] <= 0 or not (-500 < b["x"] < MAP_W * TILE_SIZE + 500 and -500 < b["y"] < MAP_H * TILE_SIZE + 500):
            if b in STATE.bullets:
                STATE.bullets.remove(b)

    for e in STATE.enemies[:]:
        if STATE.player and not STATE.player_dead:
            e.alerted = True
            if getattr(e, "is_mission2_sniper", False):
                # Mission 2's post-ambush sniper is handled by the authored
                # kill-box pressure system, not generic enemy fire. Keep the
                # actor facing the player for drawing/feedback.
                px, py = _player_pos()
                e.angle = math.atan2(py - e.rect.centery, px - e.rect.centerx)
            else:
                if e.state in ("IDLE", "PATROL", "SEARCH"):
                    e.state = "CHASE"
                e.last_seen_pos = _player_pos()
                e.update(STATE.player.rect.centerx, STATE.player.rect.centery, solid_walls, STATE.bullets, STATE.particles, STATE.enemies, STATE.fire_zones)
        if e.hp <= 0:
            STATE.hostiles_killed += 1
            STATE.blood_decals.append([e.rect.centerx, e.rect.centery, 165, random.randint(12, 22)])
            _burst(e.rect.centerx, e.rect.centery, C_BLOOD, 24)
            roll = random.random()
            if roll < 0.28:
                STATE.pickups.append([e.rect.centerx, e.rect.centery, "bandage"])
            elif roll < 0.56 and STATE.spare_mags < 5:
                STATE.pickups.append([e.rect.centerx, e.rect.centery, "mag"])
            STATE.enemies.remove(e)
    return player_hit


def _update_pickups():
    if not STATE.player or STATE.player_dead:
        return
    for pu in STATE.pickups[:]:
        if math.hypot(pu[0] - STATE.player.rect.centerx, pu[1] - STATE.player.rect.centery) > 32:
            continue
        kind = pu[2]
        if kind == "bandage":
            old = STATE.player.hp
            STATE.player.hp = min(STATE.player.max_hp, STATE.player.hp + 35)
            STATE.floating_texts.append([pu[0], pu[1] - 16, f"+{int(STATE.player.hp - old)} HP", (100, 220, 100), 70])
        elif kind == "mag":
            STATE.spare_mags = min(5, STATE.spare_mags + 1)
            STATE.floating_texts.append([pu[0], pu[1] - 16, "+1 MAG", C_TERMINAL, 70])
        audio.play("pickup", volume=0.45)
        STATE.pickups.remove(pu)


def _update_particles_texts(solid_walls):
    for p in STATE.particles[:]:
        p.update()
        if p.life <= 0:
            STATE.particles.remove(p)
    for c in STATE.casings[:]:
        c.update(solid_walls)
        if c.life <= 0:
            STATE.casings.remove(c)
    for ft in STATE.floating_texts[:]:
        if len(ft) < 6 or ft[5] != "pin":
            ft[1] -= 1.0
        ft[4] -= 1
        if ft[4] <= 0:
            STATE.floating_texts.remove(ft)
    for tr in STATE.mission2_sniper_traces[:]:
        tr["life"] -= 1
        if tr["life"] <= 0:
            STATE.mission2_sniper_traces.remove(tr)
    for laser in getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_lasers", [])[:]:
        laser["life"] -= 1
        if laser["life"] <= 0:
            STATE.mission2_sniper_lasers.remove(laser)
    for flash in getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_muzzle_flashes", [])[:]:
        flash["life"] -= 1
        if flash["life"] <= 0:
            STATE.mission2_sniper_muzzle_flashes.remove(flash)
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_tinnitus_timer", 0) > 0:
        STATE.mission2_tinnitus_timer -= 1
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_kill_flash", 0) > 0:
        STATE.mission2_sniper_kill_flash -= 1
    props_changed = False
    for prop in getattr(STATE, "mission2_story_props", [])[:]:
        prop["age"] = prop.get("age", 0) + 1
        if prop.get("life", -1) > 0:
            prop["life"] -= 1
            if prop["life"] <= 0:
                STATE.mission2_story_props.remove(prop)
                props_changed = True
    if props_changed:
        _sync_story_prop_collisions()
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_flash", 0) > 0:
        STATE.mission2_sniper_flash -= 1
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_near_miss_flash", 0) > 0:
        STATE.mission2_near_miss_flash -= 1

    callout = getattr(STATE, "mission2_death_callout", None)
    if callout:
        callout["life"] -= 1
        if callout["life"] <= 0:
            STATE.mission2_death_callout = None


def _update_camera():
    target = STATE.mission2_camera_focus or _player_pos()
    tx, ty = target

    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_camera_snap", None):
        tx, ty = STATE.mission2_camera_snap
        STATE.mission2_camera_snap = None

    sx = sy = 0
    if getattr(STATE, "reduced_motion", False):
        STATE.screen_shake = 0
    elif STATE.screen_shake > 0:
        sx = random.randint(-STATE.screen_shake, STATE.screen_shake)
        sy = random.randint(-STATE.screen_shake, STATE.screen_shake)
        STATE.screen_shake = max(0, STATE.screen_shake - 1)

    STATE.cam_kick_x *= 0.82
    STATE.cam_kick_y *= 0.82

    desired_x, desired_y = _camera_offset_for_target((
        tx + STATE.cam_kick_x + sx,
        ty + STATE.cam_kick_y + sy,
    ))

    if not getattr(STATE, "mission2_camera_initialized", False):
        STATE.camera_x = desired_x
        STATE.camera_y = desired_y
        STATE.mission2_camera_initialized = True
        return STATE.camera_x, STATE.camera_y

    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_stage", "") == STAGE_SNIPER:
        # Smoothstep easing — slow start, slow end, fast middle
        dx = desired_x - STATE.camera_x
        dy = desired_y - STATE.camera_y
        dist = math.hypot(dx, dy)
        if dist < 1.0:
            STATE.camera_x = desired_x
            STATE.camera_y = desired_y
        else:
            factor = min(1.0, 0.08 * (1.0 + dist / 600.0))
            factor = factor * factor * (3.0 - 2.0 * factor)
            STATE.camera_x += dx * factor
            STATE.camera_y += dy * factor
    elif getattr(STATE, "mission2_camera_focus_timer", 0) > 0:
        ease = 0.38
        STATE.camera_x += (desired_x - STATE.camera_x) * ease
        STATE.camera_y += (desired_y - STATE.camera_y) * ease
    else:
        ease = 0.14
        STATE.camera_x += (desired_x - STATE.camera_x) * ease
        STATE.camera_y += (desired_y - STATE.camera_y) * ease

    max_x = max(0, MAP_W * TILE_SIZE - WIDTH)
    max_y = max(0, MAP_H * TILE_SIZE - HEIGHT)
    STATE.camera_x = max(0, min(max_x, STATE.camera_x))
    STATE.camera_y = max(0, min(max_y, STATE.camera_y))

    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_stage", "") == STAGE_SNIPER:
        if not getattr(STATE, "reduced_motion", False):
            timer = getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_phase_timer", 0)
            STATE.camera_x += math.sin(timer * 0.13) * 0.8
            STATE.camera_y += math.cos(timer * 0.17) * 0.6

    return STATE.camera_x, STATE.camera_y


def game_update(keys):
    global _last_keys
    _last_keys = keys
    if STATE.gc_flash_timer > 0:
        STATE.gc_flash_timer -= 1
    if STATE.player and STATE.player.hp <= 0 and not STATE.player_dead:
        STATE.player_dead = True
        STATE.death_timer = 120
        STATE.screen_shake = 22
        _burst(STATE.player.rect.centerx, STATE.player.rect.centery, C_BLOOD, 48, speed_mul=1.6)
    if STATE.player_dead:
        STATE.death_timer -= 1
        if STATE.death_timer <= 0:
            if not STATE.mission_end_time:
                STATE.mission_end_time = pygame.time.get_ticks()
            STATE.request_death_screen = True

    solid_walls = _solid_walls()
    _update_player(keys, solid_walls)
    _update_travel_triggers()
    _update_squad_ambient()
    _update_weapon_timers()
    player_hit = _update_molotovs(solid_walls)
    if STATE.mission2_stage not in (STAGE_SNIPER, STAGE_BLACKOUT, STAGE_DONE) and not getattr(STATE, "mission2_card", None):
        player_hit = _update_bullets_enemies(solid_walls) or player_hit
    if player_hit:
        STATE.screen_shake = max(STATE.screen_shake, 4)
    _update_pickups()
    _update_stage(keys)
    _update_particles_texts(solid_walls)

    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_chromatic_timer", 0) > 0:
        STATE.mission2_chromatic_timer -= 1
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_silhouette_flash", 0) > 0:
        STATE.mission2_sniper_silhouette_flash -= 1
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_radio_muffle_timer", 0) > 0:
        STATE.mission2_radio_muffle_timer -= 1
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_input_freeze", 0) > 0:
        STATE.mission2_input_freeze -= 1

    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_heartbeat_active", False):
        STATE.mission2_heartbeat_timer += 1
        if STATE.mission2_heartbeat_timer % 72 == 0:
            audio.play("heartbeat", volume=0.18)
        # Point 12: Fade heartbeat as player takes first action (moves, shoots, etc.)
        # Also hard cap at ~12 seconds (720 frames)
        player_acting = False
        if STATE.player and not STATE.player_dead:
            keys = _last_keys
            if keys and (keys[pygame.K_w] or keys[pygame.K_s] or keys[pygame.K_a] or keys[pygame.K_d] or keys[pygame.K_e] or keys[pygame.K_SPACE] or keys[pygame.K_r] or keys[pygame.K_g] or keys[pygame.K_v]):
                player_acting = True
        if (STATE.mission2_stage == STAGE_COMBAT and (STATE.mission2_heartbeat_timer > 720 or player_acting)):
            STATE.mission2_heartbeat_active = False

    return _update_camera()


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# Drawing helpers
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# Mission 2 clue/prop draw tuning.  The same _draw_prop_icon() artwork is used
# both on the ground and inside the inspection cards; only the presentation shell
# changes.  Keep world props small, then use halos/markers for readability.
PROP_WORLD_SCALE = 0.62
PROP_CARD_SCALE = 1.05
PROP_WORLD_HALO_RADIUS = 22
MARKER_RADIUS = 22


def _world(pos, fcx, fcy):
    # Snap world-to-screen coordinates to whole pixels.  Mission 2 camera values
    # can be fractional because the player speed is fractional; drawing tiles and
    # clue icons at fractional positions causes shimmering/"glitchy" textures
    # while walking.
    return int(round(pos[0] - fcx)), int(round(pos[1] - fcy))


def _draw_vehicle(surface, rect, kind, fcx, fcy):
    r = pygame.Rect(rect.x - fcx, rect.y - fcy, rect.w, rect.h)
    col = {"ipa_truck": (75, 58, 45), "civilian": (58, 53, 48), "burned": (32, 30, 28)}.get(kind, (58, 53, 48))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (0, 0, 0), r.move(4, 5), border_radius=5)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, col, r, border_radius=5)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (105, 95, 80), r, 2, border_radius=5)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (18, 22, 24), (r.x + r.w - 34, r.y + 8, 24, r.h - 16), border_radius=3)
    for wx in (r.x + 18, r.x + r.w - 22):
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (10, 10, 12), (wx, r.bottom - 2), 8)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (10, 10, 12), (wx, r.y + 2), 8)
    if kind == "ipa_truck":
        label = font_terminal_small.render("IPA", True, (210, 55, 45))
        surface.blit(label, (r.x + 12, r.y + r.h // 2 - 7))


def _draw_tanker(surface, fcx, fcy):
    rect = STATE.mission2_tanker_rect
    x, y = rect.x - fcx, rect.y - fcy
    pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, (0, 0, 0), (x - 15, y + 22, rect.w + 30, rect.h + 42))
    col = (34, 30, 27) if STATE.mission2_tanker_exploded else (80, 74, 64)
    rim = (90, 60, 45) if STATE.mission2_tanker_exploded else (135, 126, 98)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, col, (x, y, rect.w, rect.h), border_radius=22)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, rim, (x, y, rect.w, rect.h), 2, border_radius=22)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (140, 40, 32), (x + 12, y + rect.h // 2 - 9, rect.w - 24, 18))
    for sx in range(int(x + 24), int(x + rect.w - 20), 38):
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (42, 45, 48), (sx, y + 10), (sx, y + rect.h - 10), 3)
    label = font_terminal_small.render("FUEL", True, (225, 205, 160))
    surface.blit(label, (x + rect.w // 2 - label.get_width() // 2, y + rect.h // 2 - 7))
    if not STATE.mission2_tanker_exploded:
        leak = pygame.Surface((100, 36), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.ellipse(leak, (45, 38, 25, 125), (0, 0, 100, 36))
        surface.blit(leak, (x + rect.w - 24, y + rect.h - 5))


def _draw_story_prop(surface, prop, fcx, fcy):
    """Small non-interactive environmental props spawned by trigger zones."""
    sx, sy = _world(prop["pos"], fcx, fcy)
    kind = prop.get("kind", "soot")
    age = prop.get("age", 0)
    pulse = 0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(pygame.time.get_ticks() * 0.008 + sx * 0.01)
    if kind == "flare":
        glow = pygame.Surface((70, 50), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.ellipse(glow, (255, 80, 25, int(45 + 40 * pulse)), (0, 10, 70, 30))
        surface.blit(glow, (sx - 35, sy - 24))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (95, 35, 25), (sx - 10, sy - 3, 20, 6), border_radius=2)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (255, 190, 75), (sx + 9, sy), 4)
    elif kind == "soot":
        patch = pygame.Surface((95, 60), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.ellipse(patch, (8, 7, 6, 120), (0, 0, 95, 60))
        pygame.draw.ellipse(patch, (55, 42, 28, 45), (10, 8, 76, 42), 2)
        surface.blit(patch, (sx - 48, sy - 30))
    elif kind == "broken_camera":
        pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, (0, 0, 0, 65), (sx - 18, sy + 8, 36, 10))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (28, 32, 34), (sx - 16, sy - 9, 32, 18), border_radius=3)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (5, 7, 8), (sx + 8, sy), 7)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (80, 84, 82), (sx - 14, sy - 8), (sx - 28, sy - 24), 2)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (125, 40, 35), (sx - 12, sy + 2), (sx - 25, sy + 12), 2)
    elif kind == "spark_box":
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (35, 38, 34), (sx - 14, sy - 16, 28, 32), border_radius=3)
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (85, 95, 80), (sx - 14, sy - 16, 28, 32), 1, border_radius=3)
        if age % 45 < 10:
            for i in range(5):
                ang = i / 5.0 * math.tau + age * 0.12
                pygame.draw.line(surface, (255, 210, 90), (sx, sy), (sx + math.cos(ang) * 18, sy + math.sin(ang) * 12), 1)
    elif kind == "glint":
        a = int(110 + 90 * pulse)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (255, 255, 210), (sx, sy), 4)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (255, 90, 45, a), (sx, sy), 15, 1)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (255, 235, 190), (sx - 30, sy), (sx + 30, sy), 1)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (255, 235, 190), (sx, sy - 30), (sx, sy + 30), 1)
    elif kind == "field_radio":
        _draw_prop_icon(surface, "radio", sx, sy, scale=0.62, active=True, color=(245, 190, 65))
    elif kind == "cone":
        pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, (0, 0, 0, 55), (sx - 12, sy + 7, 24, 8))
        pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (220, 90, 28), [(sx, sy - 17), (sx - 12, sy + 12), (sx + 12, sy + 12)])
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (245, 210, 130), (sx - 6, sy - 2), (sx + 6, sy - 2), 2)
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (80, 55, 35), (sx - 15, sy + 10, 30, 5), border_radius=1)
    elif kind == "barrel":
        pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, (0, 0, 0, 60), (sx - 16, sy + 12, 32, 10))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (75, 62, 45), (sx - 13, sy - 17, 26, 34), border_radius=5)
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (110, 90, 55), (sx - 13, sy - 17, 26, 34), 2, border_radius=5)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (45, 40, 34), (sx - 12, sy - 5), (sx + 12, sy - 5), 2)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (45, 40, 34), (sx - 12, sy + 7), (sx + 12, sy + 7), 2)
    elif kind == "puddle":
        puddle = pygame.Surface((80, 38), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.ellipse(puddle, (25, 35, 44, 105), (0, 0, 80, 38))
        pygame.draw.ellipse(puddle, (92, 110, 125, 38), (12, 7, 42, 16), 1)
        surface.blit(puddle, (sx - 40, sy - 19))
    elif kind == "sign":
        pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, (0, 0, 0, 55), (sx - 20, sy + 12, 40, 10))
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (80, 80, 72), (sx - 10, sy + 14), (sx + 12, sy - 16), 3)
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (55, 62, 65), (sx - 26, sy - 25, 42, 18))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (150, 150, 125), (sx - 26, sy - 25, 42, 18), 1)
        txt = font_terminal_small.render("12", True, (190, 190, 160))
        surface.blit(txt, (sx - txt.get_width() // 2 - 5, sy - 24))
    else:
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (90, 80, 65), (sx, sy), 8)


def _draw_story_props(surface, fcx, fcy):
    for prop in getattr(STATE, "mission2_story_props", []):
        _draw_story_prop(surface, prop, fcx, fcy)


def _draw_sniper_lasers_world(surface, fcx, fcy):
    for laser in getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_lasers", []):
        sx1, sy1 = _world(laser["from"], fcx, fcy)
        sx2, sy2 = _world(laser["to"], fcx, fcy)
        alpha = max(0, min(190, int(laser.get("life", 0) * 4)))
        beam = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.line(beam, (255, 35, 22, alpha), (sx1, sy1), (sx2, sy2), 3)
        pygame.draw.line(beam, (255, 175, 110, alpha // 2), (sx1, sy1), (sx2, sy2), 1)
        pygame.draw.circle(beam, (255, 45, 30, alpha), (int(sx2), int(sy2)), 8, 1)
        surface.blit(beam, (0, 0))


def _draw_sniper_muzzle_flashes(surface, fcx, fcy):
    for flash in getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_muzzle_flashes", []):
        sx, sy = _world(flash.get("pos", SNIPER_NEST_POS), fcx, fcy)
        life = flash.get("life", 1)
        max_life = max(1, flash.get("max_life", 16))
        t = max(0.0, min(1.0, life / max_life))
        glow = pygame.Surface((150, 120), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        cx, cy = 75, 60
        pygame.draw.circle(glow, (255, 245, 190, int(210 * t)), (cx, cy), int(10 + 20 * t))
        pygame.draw.circle(glow, (255, 110, 35, int(130 * t)), (cx, cy), int(26 + 32 * t), 2)
        pygame.draw.line(glow, (255, 220, 130, int(180 * t)), (cx, cy), (cx - 48, cy + 38), 4)
        surface.blit(glow, (sx - cx, sy - cy))


def _draw_tinnitus_overlay(surface):
    timer = getattr(STATE, "mission2_tinnitus_timer", 0)
    if timer <= 0:
        return
    strength = min(1.0, timer / 120.0)
    gray = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    gray.fill((185, 185, 175, int(46 * strength)))
    surface.blit(gray, (0, 0))
    red = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.rect(red, (120, 0, 0, int(45 * strength)), (0, 0, WIDTH, HEIGHT), 18)
    surface.blit(red, (0, 0))
    # Cheap disorientation: scanline jitter bars.
    for i in range(9):
        y = (pygame.time.get_ticks() // 6 + i * 71) % HEIGHT
        bar = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, 3), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        bar.fill((255, 245, 220, int(22 * strength)))
        surface.blit(bar, (random.randint(-5, 5), y))


def _draw_gantry(surface, fcx, fcy):
    """Clear top-down highway sign gantry / sniper platform landmark."""
    sx, sy = _world(SNIPER_NEST_POS, fcx, fcy)
    # Platform shadow.
    shadow = pygame.Surface((330, 120), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.ellipse(shadow, (0, 0, 0, 70), (8, 62, 314, 36))
    surface.blit(shadow, (sx - 165, sy - 50))
    # Crossbeam and service catwalk.
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (46, 48, 46), (sx - 160, sy - 38, 320, 18), border_radius=2)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (83, 84, 78), (sx - 160, sy - 38, 320, 18), 2, border_radius=2)
    for gx in range(sx - 148, sx + 150, 22):
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (26, 28, 27), (gx, sy - 38), (gx + 18, sy - 20), 2)
    # Support towers.
    for lx in (sx - 142, sx + 142):
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (55, 56, 52), (lx - 8, sy - 72, 16, 90))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (96, 95, 84), (lx - 8, sy - 72, 16, 90), 2)
        for yy in range(sy - 62, sy + 12, 14):
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (25, 25, 24), (lx - 7, yy), (lx + 7, yy + 12), 2)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (25, 25, 24), (lx + 7, yy), (lx - 7, yy + 12), 2)
    # Road signs under the gantry.
    sign_rects = [pygame.Rect(sx - 108, sy - 74, 92, 40), pygame.Rect(sx + 16, sy - 74, 92, 40)]
    for i, rect in enumerate(sign_rects):
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (38, 55, 52), rect, border_radius=4)
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (150, 165, 140), rect, 2, border_radius=4)
        txt = font_terminal_small.render("ROUTE 12" if i == 0 else "CHECKPOINT", True, (205, 220, 190))
        surface.blit(txt, (rect.centerx - txt.get_width() // 2, rect.y + 7))
        txt2 = font_terminal_small.render("NORTH" if i == 0 else "12", True, (160, 185, 160))
        surface.blit(txt2, (rect.centerx - txt2.get_width() // 2, rect.y + 22))
    # Sniper silhouette and rifle, so the origin is physically readable.
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (8, 8, 8), (sx, sy - 15), 7)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (12, 12, 11), (sx - 6, sy - 12, 12, 19), border_radius=3)
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (8, 8, 7), (sx + 3, sy - 10), (sx - 34, sy + 20), 4)
    pygame.draw.circle(surface, (255, 230, 160), (sx - 34, sy + 20), 3)
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_stage", "") == STAGE_SNIPER or getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_origin_revealed", False):
        lab = font_terminal_small.render("NORTHEAST GANTRY", True, (255, 170, 90))
        surface.blit(lab, (sx - lab.get_width() // 2, sy + 28))


def _draw_actor(surface, actor, fcx, fcy):
    """Top-down KRAP squadmate. Pose is driven entirely by activity string."""
    x = actor["pos"][0] - fcx
    y = actor["pos"][1] - fcy
    name  = actor.get("name", "")
    activity = (actor.get("activity") or "standing").lower()

    # alive is ONLY used for label colour and blood — never for pose branch.
    alive = actor.get("alive", True)

    accent = {
        "GABI":   (220, 175,  70),
        "SEBY":   ( 85, 170, 225),
        "DARIUS": (110, 220, 145),
    }.get(name, C_KRAP_BLUE)
    name_col = (150, 185, 230) if alive else (130, 80, 80)

    # --- Pose flags ---------------------------------------------------
    is_down      = "down"   in activity or "dead" in activity
    is_drag_body = "dragging_body" in activity
    is_drag      = ("drag" in activity) and not is_drag_body
    is_reach     = "reach"  in activity or "radio" in activity
    is_frozen    = "frozen" in activity or "shock" in activity
    is_crouch    = is_drag or is_reach or is_frozen or "crouch" in activity or "cover" in activity
    is_shout     = "shout"  in activity or "yell" in activity

    if is_down:
        # ---- DOWNED body ------------------------------------------------
        pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, (0, 0, 0, 110),
                            (x - 28, y + 2, 56, 16))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (36, 44, 52),
                         (x - 22, y - 10, 44, 18), border_radius=6)
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, C_KRAP_BLUE,
                         (x - 12, y - 10, 24, 4), border_radius=2)
        # Rifle dropped beside body
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (16, 16, 15),
                         (x - 26, y + 6), (x + 26, y - 4), 4)
        # Head
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, C_PLAYER_SKIN,
                           (int(x - 20), int(y - 3)), 5)
        # Blood smear
        pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, (90, 14, 14, 190),
                            (x - 14, y + 6, 28, 9))

    else:
        # ---- UPRIGHT / CROUCHED body ------------------------------------
        body_dy = 6 if is_crouch else 0
        cy = y + body_dy

        # Shadow
        sw = 44 if is_crouch else 36
        shad = pygame.Surface((sw, 14), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.ellipse(shad, (0, 0, 0, 70), shad.get_rect())
        surface.blit(shad, (x - sw // 2, cy + 12))

        # Legs
        boot = (10, 10, 12)
        trouser = (24, 28, 34)
        if is_crouch:
            pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, boot,    (x - 15, cy + 11, 14, 8))
            pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, boot,    (x +  1, cy + 11, 14, 8))
            pygame.draw.rect(surface,   trouser,  (x - 14, cy +  4, 10, 10), border_radius=2)
            pygame.draw.rect(surface,   trouser,  (x +  4, cy +  4, 10, 10), border_radius=2)
        else:
            pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, boot,    (x - 13, cy + 17, 12, 7))
            pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, boot,    (x +  1, cy + 17, 12, 7))
            pygame.draw.rect(surface,   trouser,  (x - 10, cy +  3,  8, 15), border_radius=2)
            pygame.draw.rect(surface,   trouser,  (x +  2, cy +  3,  8, 15), border_radius=2)

        # Torso
        torso_pts = [
            (x - 13, cy - 14), (x + 13, cy - 14),
            (x + 11, cy + 10), (x -  11, cy + 10),
        ]
        pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (44, 54, 60), torso_pts)
        pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (22, 28, 32), torso_pts, 2)
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (34, 42, 48),
                         (x - 9, cy - 9, 18, 14), border_radius=3)
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, C_KRAP_BLUE,
                         (x - 11, cy - 12, 22, 4), border_radius=2)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, accent, (int(x + 9), int(cy + 3)), 3)

        # --- Arms & rifle: branch on pose ---
        if is_drag:
            # Both arms extended, pulling a body behind them
            lh = (x - 24, cy + 14)
            rh = (x - 18, cy +  6)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, C_KRAP_UNIFORM,
                             (x - 11, cy - 4), lh, 5)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, C_KRAP_UNIFORM,
                             (x -  9, cy - 6), rh, 5)
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, C_PLAYER_SKIN, lh, 3)
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, C_PLAYER_SKIN, rh, 3)
            # Rifle slung across back
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (16, 16, 15),
                             (x + 6, cy - 4), (x + 22, cy + 12), 3)

        elif is_reach:
            # One arm reaching forward to grab the radio handset
            lh = (x - 12, cy + 2)
            rh = (x + 20, cy - 16)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, C_KRAP_UNIFORM,
                             (x - 11, cy - 4), lh, 5)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, C_KRAP_UNIFORM,
                             (x +  9, cy - 6), rh, 5)
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, C_PLAYER_SKIN, lh, 3)
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, C_PLAYER_SKIN, rh, 3)
            # Radio handset in reaching hand
            pygame.draw.rect(surface, (20, 24, 28),
                             (rh[0] - 3, rh[1] - 5, 6, 9), border_radius=1)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (40, 50, 55),
                             rh, (rh[0] - 6, rh[1] + 10), 1)
            # Rifle dropped across lap
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (16, 16, 15),
                             (x - 14, cy + 8), (x + 14, cy + 8), 3)

        elif is_drag_body:
            # Hunched forward, walking backward, both arms gripping under Gabi's arms.
            arm_thick = 5
            leg_col = (24, 28, 34)
            skin = C_PLAYER_SKIN
            step = (pygame.time.get_ticks() // 140) % 2
            leg_off = 3 if step else -3
            pygame.draw.rect(surface, leg_col,
                             (x - 12 + leg_off, cy + 4, 7, 14), border_radius=2)
            pygame.draw.rect(surface, leg_col,
                             (x + 5 - leg_off, cy + 4, 7, 14), border_radius=2)
            lh = (x - 16, cy + 14)
            rh = (x + 4, cy + 16)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, C_KRAP_UNIFORM,
                             (x - 11, cy - 4), lh, arm_thick + 1)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, C_KRAP_UNIFORM,
                             (x + 9, cy - 6), rh, arm_thick + 1)
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, skin, lh, 4)
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, skin, rh, 4)
            pygame.draw.arc(surface, (60, 72, 84),
                (x - 18, cy - 12, 36, 24),
                math.pi * 0.8, math.pi * 1.8, 3)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (16, 16, 15),
                (x + 8 + step, cy - 10), (x + 26 + step, cy - 2), 3)

        elif is_frozen or is_shout:
            # Rifle lowered in shock, hands gripping low
            lh = (x -  8, cy + 8)
            rh = (x + 10, cy + 10)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (16, 16, 15),
                             lh, (x + 18, cy + 2), 3)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, C_KRAP_UNIFORM,
                             (x - 11, cy - 4), lh, 5)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, C_KRAP_UNIFORM,
                             (x +  9, cy - 6), rh, 5)
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, C_PLAYER_SKIN, lh, 3)
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, C_PLAYER_SKIN, rh, 3)

        else:
            # Standard rifle-raised scanning pose
            stock  = (x - 14, cy - 2)
            muzzle = (x + 26, cy - 8)
            lh     = (x -  4, cy - 4)
            rh     = (x + 12, cy - 6)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (75, 55, 34),
                             (x - 22, cy), stock, 4)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (16, 16, 15), stock, muzzle, 3)
            pygame.draw.rect(surface, (25, 26, 24),
                             (x - 8, cy - 8, 14, 6), border_radius=2)
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, (9, 9, 8), muzzle, 2)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, C_KRAP_UNIFORM,
                             (x - 11, cy - 4), lh, 5)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, C_KRAP_UNIFORM,
                             (x +  9, cy - 6), rh, 5)
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, C_PLAYER_SKIN, lh, 3)
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, C_PLAYER_SKIN, rh, 3)

        # Head
        head_y = cy - (18 if is_crouch else 24)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, ( 7,  8,  9),
                           (int(x), int(head_y)), 10)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (58, 70, 82),
                           (int(x), int(head_y)),  8)
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, C_KRAP_BLUE,
                         (x - 7, head_y - 5, 14, 3), border_radius=2)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, C_PLAYER_SKIN,
                           (int(x + 1), int(head_y + 2)), 5)
        # Eyes — dot pair
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (35, 22, 12),
                           (int(x - 1), int(head_y + 1)), 1)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (35, 22, 12),
                           (int(x + 3), int(head_y + 1)), 1)

    # Eye-line toward look_at target
    if alive and actor.get("look_at"):
        lx = actor["look_at"][0] - fcx
        ly = actor["look_at"][1] - fcy
        dx, dy = lx - x, ly - y
        d = max(1.0, math.hypot(dx, dy))
        ex, ey = x + dx / d * 34, y + dy / d * 34
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (95, 150, 170),
                         (int(x), int(y)), (int(ex), int(ey)), 1)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (95, 150, 170),
                           (int(ex), int(ey)), 2)

    # Name label
    lbl  = font_terminal_small.render(name, True, name_col)
    shad = font_terminal_small.render(name, True, (0, 0, 0))
    label_y = y - 44 if not is_down else y - 26
    surface.blit(shad, (x - lbl.get_width() // 2 + 1, label_y + 1))
    surface.blit(lbl,  (x - lbl.get_width() // 2,     label_y))

    # Activity sub-label removed — was only used by deleted sniper cutscene.

def _draw_prop_icon(surface, kind, cx, cy, scale=1.0, active=True, color=C_TERMINAL):
    """Draw a readable clue/evidence prop icon.

    Used by both the in-world prop and the card preview so the object identity
    stays consistent.  Positions are snapped to pixels to prevent texture shimmer
    when the camera scrolls.
    """
    s = max(0.35, float(scale))
    cx, cy = int(round(cx)), int(round(cy))
    pulse = 0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(pygame.time.get_ticks() * 0.006)
    if kind == "radio" or kind == "static":
        # Smaller broken field radio: cracked case, bent antenna, dead speaker,
        # loose wire, and a failing red indicator.
        w, h = int(34 * s), int(24 * s)
        x, y = cx - w // 2, cy - h // 2
        # Broken shadow / tilted debris plate.
        pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, (0, 0, 0, 65), (x - int(5*s), y + int(16*s), w + int(10*s), int(10*s)))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (10, 13, 14), (x, y, w, h), border_radius=max(2, int(3*s)))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (74, 86, 88), (x, y, w, h), max(1, int(2*s)), border_radius=max(2, int(3*s)))
        # Chipped corner.
        chip = [(x + w - int(8*s), y), (x + w, y), (x + w, y + int(8*s))]
        pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (4, 6, 7), chip)
        # Cracked display.
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (3, 6, 7), (x + int(5*s), y + int(6*s), int(13*s), int(8*s)))
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (55, 75, 76), (x + int(6*s), y + int(7*s)), (x + int(16*s), y + int(13*s)), max(1, int(1*s)))
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (55, 75, 76), (x + int(12*s), y + int(7*s)), (x + int(9*s), y + int(14*s)), max(1, int(1*s)))
        # Speaker/knob, dark and damaged.
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (25, 29, 30), (x + int(25*s), y + int(13*s)), max(4, int(5*s)))
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (8, 10, 11), (x + int(25*s), y + int(13*s)), max(2, int(3*s)))
        # Bent antenna and loose cable.
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (82, 90, 92), (x + int(7*s), y + int(1*s)), (x - int(5*s), y - int(13*s)), max(1, int(2*s)))
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (82, 90, 92), (x - int(5*s), y - int(13*s)), (x - int(2*s), y - int(20*s)), max(1, int(1*s)))
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (45, 48, 42), (x + int(8*s), y + h), (x - int(8*s), y + h + int(8*s)), max(1, int(2*s)))
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (38, 40, 36), (x - int(9*s), y + h + int(8*s)), max(1, int(2*s)))
        led = (255, 45, 30) if active and pulse > 0.58 else (75, 18, 14)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, led, (x + w - int(5*s), y + int(5*s)), max(2, int(2*s)))
    elif kind == "mag_paint":
        tag = font_terminal.render("MAG", True, (22, 22, 20))
        tag = pygame.transform.rotate(tag, -9)
        if scale != 1.0:
            tag = pygame.transform.smoothscale(tag, (max(1, int(tag.get_width()*s)), max(1, int(tag.get_height()*s))))
        tag.set_alpha(190 if active else 120)
        surface.blit(tag, (cx - tag.get_width()//2, cy - tag.get_height()//2))
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (18, 18, 16), (cx - int(28*s), cy + int(18*s)), (cx + int(30*s), cy - int(16*s)), max(2, int(3*s)))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (95, 90, 75), (cx + int(32*s), cy - int(20*s), int(10*s), int(22*s)), border_radius=max(1, int(2*s)))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (25, 25, 25), (cx + int(32*s), cy - int(25*s), int(10*s), int(5*s)))
    elif kind == "timer_fragment":
        # Charred circuit/timing relay with clipped wires.
        w, h = int(42*s), int(28*s)
        x, y = cx - w//2, cy - h//2
        pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, (0, 0, 0, 62), (x - int(5*s), y + int(18*s), w + int(10*s), int(10*s)))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (10, 20, 14), (x, y, w, h), border_radius=max(2, int(3*s)))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (42, 92, 58), (x, y, w, h), max(1, int(2*s)), border_radius=max(2, int(3*s)))
        for wx1, wx2 in [(-int(11*s), -int(2*s)), (w + int(2*s), w + int(12*s))]:
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (120, 45, 35), (x + wx1, y + int(7*s)), (x + wx2, y + int(15*s)), max(1, int(2*s)))
        for i, chip_col in enumerate([(18, 50, 28), (58, 120, 70), (20, 35, 28)]):
            pygame.draw.rect(surface, chip_col, (x + int((7 + i*11)*s), y + int(7*s), int(7*s), int(6*s)))
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (255, 55, 38) if active and pulse > 0.5 else (90, 22, 18), (x + w - int(8*s), y + int(8*s)), max(2, int(3*s)))
        pygame.draw.arc(surface, (25, 25, 20), (x - int(7*s), y - int(6*s), w + int(14*s), h + int(16*s)), 0.2, 2.7, max(1, int(2*s)))
    elif kind == "bootprints":
        # Two clean square-tread government prints crossing the blast soot.
        w, h = int(18*s), int(32*s)
        for i, (ox, oy, rot) in enumerate([(-10, -8, -10), (9, 9, 8)]):
            foot = pygame.Surface((w + 8, h + 8), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            pygame.draw.ellipse(foot, (0, 0, 0, 50), (2, h - int(7*s), w + 4, int(10*s)))
            pygame.draw.rect(foot, (30, 27, 23), (4, 3, w, h), border_radius=max(2, int(4*s)))
            pygame.draw.rect(foot, (12, 12, 10), (5, 4, w-2, h-2), max(1, int(1*s)), border_radius=max(2, int(4*s)))
            for ty in range(7, max(8, h - 4), max(4, int(6*s))):
                pygame.draw.line(foot, (82, 75, 58), (7, ty), (w + 1, ty), max(1, int(1*s)))
            foot = pygame.transform.rotate(foot, rot)
            surface.blit(foot, (cx + int(ox*s) - foot.get_width()//2, cy + int(oy*s) - foot.get_height()//2))
        pygame.draw.arc(surface, (92, 82, 55), (cx - int(28*s), cy - int(20*s), int(56*s), int(42*s)), 3.4, 5.9, max(1, int(1*s)))
    elif kind == "range_card":
        # Scratch marks / folded range card foreshadowing the kill-box.
        w, h = int(46*s), int(32*s)
        x, y = cx - w//2, cy - h//2
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (0, 0, 0), (x + int(3*s), y + int(4*s), w, h), border_radius=max(1, int(2*s)))
        pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (185, 174, 132), [(x, y), (x+w, y+int(3*s)), (x+w-int(4*s), y+h), (x+int(3*s), y+h-int(2*s))])
        pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (80, 65, 42), [(x, y), (x+w, y+int(3*s)), (x+w-int(4*s), y+h), (x+int(3*s), y+h-int(2*s))], max(1, int(1*s)))
        for i, mark in enumerate(("180", "220", "260")):
            yy = y + int((7 + i*8)*s)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (95, 68, 45), (x + int(7*s), yy), (x + int(24*s), yy), max(1, int(1*s)))
            if s >= 0.8:
                txt = font_terminal_small.render(mark, True, (75, 48, 35))
                txt = pygame.transform.smoothscale(txt, (max(1, int(txt.get_width()*s*0.55)), max(1, int(txt.get_height()*s*0.55))))
                surface.blit(txt, (x + int(27*s), yy - int(5*s)))
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (155, 35, 30), (x + int(8*s), y + h - int(7*s)), (x + w - int(8*s), y + int(6*s)), max(1, int(2*s)))
    elif kind in ("manifest", "decree", "packet", "document"):
        w, h = int(38*s), int(48*s)
        x, y = cx - w//2, cy - h//2
        paper_col = (215, 205, 174) if kind != "packet" else (185, 170, 130)
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (0, 0, 0), (x + int(3*s), y + int(4*s), w, h), border_radius=max(1, int(2*s)))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, paper_col, (x, y, w, h), border_radius=max(1, int(2*s)))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (75, 60, 42), (x, y, w, h), max(1, int(1*s)), border_radius=max(1, int(2*s)))
        if kind == "manifest":
            pygame.draw.rect(surface, (70, 55, 35), (x + int(8*s), y - int(5*s), int(22*s), int(8*s)), border_radius=max(1, int(2*s)))
        if kind == "decree":
            pygame.draw.rect(surface, (160, 35, 30), (x + int(5*s), y + int(7*s), w - int(10*s), int(8*s)))
        for i in range(4):
            yy = y + int((18 + i*7)*s)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (95, 82, 62), (x + int(7*s), yy), (x + w - int(7*s), yy), max(1, int(1*s)))
        if kind == "packet":
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, (155, 30, 28), (x + w - int(10*s), y + int(12*s)), max(3, int(5*s)), max(1, int(1*s)))
    else:
        w, h = int(54*s), int(38*s)
        x, y = cx - w//2, cy - h//2
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (4, 9, 10), (x, y, w, h), border_radius=max(2, int(4*s)))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, color, (x, y, w, h), max(1, int(2*s)), border_radius=max(2, int(4*s)))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (7, 22, 18), (x + int(7*s), y + int(7*s), w - int(14*s), h - int(16*s)))
        for i in range(3):
            yy = y + int((12 + i*6)*s)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, color, (x + int(12*s), yy), (x + w - int(12*s), yy), max(1, int(1*s)))
        if active:
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, color, (x + w - int(8*s), y + int(8*s)), max(2, int(3*s)))


def _draw_world_prop(surface, item, fcx, fcy, active=True):
    sx, sy = _world(item["pos"], fcx, fcy)
    kind = item.get("kind", item.get("id", "document"))
    if item in getattr(STATE, "mission2_clues", []):
        prop_col = (245, 190, 65)
    elif kind == "manifest":
        prop_col = (255, 190, 90)
    else:
        prop_col = C_TERMINAL

    # Small object, readable silhouette: visibility comes from a restrained halo
    # instead of oversized clue art.  Completed props stay visible but quieter.
    halo_r = PROP_WORLD_HALO_RADIUS
    halo = pygame.Surface((halo_r * 2 + 8, halo_r * 2 + 8), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pulse = 0.62 + 0.38 * math.sin(pygame.time.get_ticks() * 0.005)
    halo_alpha = int((48 if active else 20) * pulse)
    pygame.draw.circle(halo, (*prop_col, halo_alpha), (halo_r + 4, halo_r + 4), halo_r)
    pygame.draw.circle(halo, (*prop_col, min(120, halo_alpha + 38)), (halo_r + 4, halo_r + 4), halo_r, 1)
    surface.blit(halo, (sx - halo_r - 4, sy - halo_r - 4))

    _draw_prop_icon(surface, kind, sx, sy, scale=PROP_WORLD_SCALE, active=active, color=prop_col)


def _draw_marker(surface, pos, label, col, fcx, fcy, radius=MARKER_RADIUS):
    sx, sy = _world(pos, fcx, fcy)
    pulse = int(80 + math.sin(time.time() * 5) * 35)
    if -60 < sx < WIDTH + 60 and -60 < sy < HEIGHT + 60:
