        ring = pygame.Surface((radius * 3, radius * 3), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.circle(ring, (*col, pulse), (radius * 3 // 2, radius * 3 // 2), radius, 2)
        pygame.draw.circle(ring, (*col, pulse // 2), (radius * 3 // 2, radius * 3 // 2), radius + 5, 1)
        surface.blit(ring, (sx - radius * 3 // 2, sy - radius * 3 // 2))
        txt = font_terminal_small.render(label, True, col)
        surface.blit(txt, (sx - txt.get_width() // 2, sy - radius - 23))
    else:
        # Edge arrow.
        cx = max(32, min(WIDTH - 32, sx))
        cy = max(90, min(HEIGHT - 58, sy))
        ang = math.atan2(sy - HEIGHT / 2, sx - WIDTH / 2)
        pts = [
            (cx + math.cos(ang) * 16, cy + math.sin(ang) * 16),
            (cx + math.cos(ang + 2.45) * 12, cy + math.sin(ang + 2.45) * 12),
            (cx + math.cos(ang - 2.45) * 12, cy + math.sin(ang - 2.45) * 12),
        ]
        pygame.draw.polygon(surface, col, pts)
        txt = font_terminal_small.render(label, True, col)
        surface.blit(txt, (cx - txt.get_width() // 2, cy + 16))


def _draw_interactables(surface, fcx, fcy):
    if STATE.mission2_stage == STAGE_INVESTIGATE:
        for clue in STATE.mission2_clues:
            _draw_world_prop(surface, clue, fcx, fcy, active=not clue["done"])
            if not clue["done"]:
                label = f"{clue['name']} [E]"
                _draw_marker(surface, clue["pos"], label, (245, 190, 65), fcx, fcy)
        if _clue_count() >= REQUIRED_INVESTIGATION_CLUES:
            _draw_marker(surface, KILL_ZONE, "REGROUP", C_VASILE, fcx, fcy)
    elif STATE.mission2_stage == STAGE_COMBAT:
        phase = getattr(STATE, "mission2_post_sniper_phase", "")
        if phase == "get_cover":
            _draw_marker(surface, _nearest_killbox_cover(), "COVER", C_VASILE, fcx, fcy, radius=34)
        elif phase == "survive":
            _draw_marker(surface, _nearest_killbox_cover(), "STAY IN COVER", C_VASILE, fcx, fcy, radius=30)
            _draw_marker(surface, SNIPER_NEST_POS, "SNIPER NEST", (255, 110, 60), fcx, fcy, radius=34)
        elif phase == "flank":
            _draw_marker(surface, _nearest_flank_marker(), "FLANK ROUTE", (255, 170, 80), fcx, fcy, radius=34)
            _draw_marker(surface, SNIPER_NEST_POS, "SNIPER LINE", (255, 110, 60), fcx, fcy, radius=30)
        elif phase == "clear":
            _draw_marker(surface, SNIPER_NEST_POS, "SNIPER SUPPRESSED", (120, 220, 150), fcx, fcy, radius=28)
    elif STATE.mission2_stage == STAGE_TRUCK:
        truck = STATE.mission2_truck_manifest
        _draw_world_prop(surface, truck, fcx, fcy, active=not truck["done"])
        _draw_marker(surface, IPA_TRUCK_POS, "IPA MANIFEST [E]", (255, 190, 90), fcx, fcy)
    elif STATE.mission2_stage == STAGE_TANKER:
        _draw_marker(surface, TANKER_POS, "LEAKING TANKER", (255, 140, 60), fcx, fcy, radius=34)
    elif STATE.mission2_stage == STAGE_RUN:
        _draw_marker(surface, SAFE_ZONE, "RUN HERE", C_VASILE, fcx, fcy, radius=34)
    elif STATE.mission2_stage == STAGE_EVIDENCE:
        for ev in STATE.mission2_evidence:
            _draw_world_prop(surface, ev, fcx, fcy, active=not ev["done"])
            if not ev["done"]:
                _draw_marker(surface, ev["pos"], f"{ev['name']} [E]", C_TERMINAL, fcx, fcy)
        if _evidence_count() >= 3:
            _draw_marker(surface, UPLOAD_TERMINAL_POS, "UPLOAD [E]", C_TERMINAL, fcx, fcy)
    elif STATE.mission2_stage == STAGE_UPLOAD:
        _draw_prop_icon(surface, "terminal", *_world(UPLOAD_TERMINAL_POS, fcx, fcy), scale=0.9, active=True, color=C_TERMINAL)
        _draw_marker(surface, UPLOAD_TERMINAL_POS, "UPLOAD", C_TERMINAL, fcx, fcy)
    elif STATE.mission2_stage == STAGE_EXFIL:
        _draw_marker(surface, EXFIL_POS, "EXFIL", (80, 220, 120), fcx, fcy, radius=34)


def _draw_card(surface):
    card = getattr(STATE, "mission2_card", None)
    if not card:
        return
    age = card.get("age", 0)
    open_t = min(1.0, age / 18.0)
    ease = 1 - (1 - open_t) ** 3
    overlay = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    overlay.fill((0, 0, 0, int(170 * ease)))
    surface.blit(overlay, (0, 0))
    col = card["color"]
    w, h = 720, 330
    x = WIDTH // 2 - w // 2
    y = int(HEIGHT // 2 - h // 2 + (1 - ease) * 80)
    shadow = pygame.Surface((w + 22, h + 22), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    shadow.fill((0, 0, 0, int(105 * ease)))
    surface.blit(shadow, (x + 10, y + 12))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (5, 10, 13), (x, y, w, h), border_radius=6)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, col, (x, y, w, h), 2, border_radius=6)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (2, 5, 8), (x, y, w, 54), border_radius=6)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, col, (x, y, 5, h), border_radius=3)
    title = font_speaker.render(card["title"], True, col)
    surface.blit(title, (x + 28, y + 13))
    preview_w, preview_h = 142, 158
    prop_x, prop_y = x + 102, y + 178
    preview = pygame.Surface((preview_w, preview_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    scan_y = int((age * 2) % preview_h)
    pygame.draw.rect(preview, (0, 0, 0, 70), (0, 0, preview_w, preview_h), border_radius=8)
    pygame.draw.rect(preview, (*col, 110), (0, 0, preview_w, preview_h), 1, border_radius=8)
    _draw_prop_icon(preview, card.get("kind", "document"), preview_w // 2, preview_h // 2, scale=PROP_CARD_SCALE, active=True, color=col)
    pygame.draw.line(preview, (*col, 125), (8, scan_y), (preview_w - 8, scan_y), 1)
    surface.blit(preview, (prop_x - preview_w // 2, prop_y - preview_h // 2))
    yy = y + 82
    text_x = x + 195
    max_w = w - 225
    for line in card["lines"]:
        words = line.split()
        cur = ""
        wrapped = []
        for word in words:
            test = (cur + " " + word).strip()
            if font_dialogue.size(test)[0] > max_w and cur:
                wrapped.append(cur)
                cur = word
            else:
                cur = test
        if cur:
            wrapped.append(cur)
        for part in wrapped:
            surf = font_dialogue.render(part, True, C_WHITE)
            surface.blit(surf, (text_x, yy))
            yy += 32
        yy += 8
    hint = font_terminal.render("PRESS [E] TO CLOSE", True, col)
    if age >= 18 or (pygame.time.get_ticks() // 400) % 2 == 0:
        surface.blit(hint, (x + w - hint.get_width() - 26, y + h - 40))


def _draw_hud(surface):
    if not STATE.player:
        return
    # Exact Mission 1 HUD layout/positioning, with Mission 2 objective text.
    hb_x, hb_y, hb_w, hb_h = 30, HEIGHT - 50, 160, 14
    hp_ratio = max(0.0, STATE.player.hp / STATE.player.max_hp)
    hud_bg = pygame.Surface((hb_w + 20, hb_h + 28), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.rect(hud_bg, (0, 0, 0, 140), (0, 0, hb_w + 20, hb_h + 28))
    surface.blit(hud_bg, (hb_x - 10, hb_y - 16))
    surface.blit(font_terminal_small.render("HP", True, C_TERMINAL), (hb_x, hb_y - 14))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (30, 30, 30), (hb_x, hb_y, hb_w, hb_h))
    if hp_ratio > 0.5:
        bar_col = (int(200 * hp_ratio), int(220 * hp_ratio), 40)
    elif hp_ratio > 0.25:
        bar_col = (220, int(200 * hp_ratio * 2), 40)
    else:
        bar_col = (220, 40, 40)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, bar_col, (hb_x, hb_y, int(hb_w * hp_ratio), hb_h))
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, C_TERMINAL, (hb_x, hb_y, hb_w, hb_h), 1)

    obj_bg = pygame.Surface((620, 30), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.rect(obj_bg, (0, 0, 0, 150), (0, 0, 620, 30))
    pygame.draw.line(obj_bg, C_KRAP_BLUE, (0, 0), (0, 30), 3)
    surface.blit(obj_bg, (20, LETTER_H + 10))
    obj_surf = font_prompt.render("OBJECTIVE: " + _objective, True, C_WHITE)
    surface.blit(obj_surf, (30, LETTER_H + 18))

    ammo_x, ammo_y = 30, hb_y - 96
    bg_w, bg_h = 246, 92
    bg = pygame.Surface((bg_w, bg_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.rect(bg, (0, 0, 0, 140), (0, 0, bg_w, bg_h), border_radius=4)
    pygame.draw.rect(bg, (40, 42, 38), (0, 0, bg_w, bg_h), 1, border_radius=4)
    surface.blit(bg, (ammo_x - 6, ammo_y))
    for i in range(4):
        mx = ammo_x + 8 + i * 36
        my = ammo_y + 8
        is_active = i == 0
        if is_active:
            bullets_fill = min(STATE.ammo_in_mag, 12)
            is_empty = STATE.ammo_in_mag <= 0
        else:
            bullets_fill = 12 if STATE.spare_mags >= i else 0
            is_empty = STATE.spare_mags < i
        top_w, bot_w, h = 24, 20, 46
        taper = (top_w - bot_w) // 2
        body_pts = [(mx, my), (mx + top_w, my), (mx + taper + bot_w, my + h), (mx + taper, my + h)]
        if is_empty:
            base = (45, 42, 38); hl = (55, 52, 48); rim = (55, 50, 45)
        elif is_active:
            base = (90, 86, 78); hl = (115, 110, 100); rim = (70, 66, 58)
        else:
            base = (72, 68, 62); hl = (92, 88, 80); rim = (60, 56, 50)
        pygame.draw.polygon(surface, base, body_pts)
        pygame.draw.polygon(surface, hl, [(mx + 1, my + 2), (mx + 5, my + 2), (mx + taper + 3, my + h - 3), (mx + taper + 1, my + h - 3)])
        outline = C_TERMINAL if is_active else (rim if not is_empty else (55, 50, 45))
        pygame.draw.polygon(surface, outline, body_pts, 1)
        bullet_y = my + 8
        gold = (220, 185, 45)
        dark = (55, 52, 48) if not is_empty else (40, 38, 35)
        for bi in range(12):
            col = gold if bi < bullets_fill else dark
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, col, (mx + top_w // 2, bullet_y + bi * 3), 2)
        if is_empty:
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (90, 55, 55), (mx + 2, my + 2), (mx + top_w - 2, my + h - 2), 1)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (90, 55, 55), (mx + top_w - 2, my + 2), (mx + 2, my + h - 2), 1)
    ammo_text = f"{STATE.ammo_in_mag}/{STATE.mag_size}"
    text_col = C_TERMINAL if STATE.ammo_in_mag > 0 else (220, 60, 60)
    surface.blit(font_terminal_small.render(ammo_text, True, text_col), (ammo_x + 154, ammo_y + 8))
    surface.blit(font_terminal_small.render("9MM", True, (100, 110, 100)), (ammo_x + 154, ammo_y + 26))
    if STATE.is_reloading:
        bar_x, bar_y = ammo_x + 148, ammo_y + 46
        bar_w, bar_h = 80, 8
        progress = 1.0 - STATE.reload_timer / 60.0
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (50, 50, 50), (bar_x, bar_y, bar_w, bar_h), border_radius=2)
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (220, 160, 40), (bar_x, bar_y, int(bar_w * progress), bar_h), border_radius=2)
        reload_lbl = font_terminal_small.render("RELOADING", True, (220, 160, 40))
        surface.blit(reload_lbl, (ammo_x + 150, ammo_y + 58))
    else:
        mag_label = f"MAGS: {STATE.spare_mags}"
        surface.blit(font_terminal_small.render(mag_label, True, (140, 150, 130)), (ammo_x + 154, ammo_y + 44))

    bot_x = ammo_x - 6
    bot_y = ammo_y - 42
    bot_bg_w, bot_bg_h = 246, 36
    bot_bg = pygame.Surface((bot_bg_w, bot_bg_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.rect(bot_bg, (0, 0, 0, 140), (0, 0, bot_bg_w, bot_bg_h), border_radius=4)
    pygame.draw.rect(bot_bg, (40, 42, 38), (0, 0, bot_bg_w, bot_bg_h), 1, border_radius=4)
    surface.blit(bot_bg, (bot_x, bot_y))
    from mission import _draw_molotov_icon as _mission1_molotov_icon
    for bi in range(4):
        icon_x = bot_x + 18 + bi * 26
        icon_y = bot_y + 23
        _mission1_molotov_icon(surface, icon_x, icon_y, bi < STATE.player_bottles)
    bot_lbl = font_terminal_small.render(f"MOLOTOVS  [G]", True, (255, 150, 60) if STATE.player_bottles > 0 else (130, 80, 80))
    surface.blit(bot_lbl, (bot_x + 120, bot_y + 4))
    bot_count = font_terminal_small.render(f"{STATE.player_bottles} / 4", True, C_TERMINAL if STATE.player_bottles > 0 else (180, 60, 60))
    surface.blit(bot_count, (bot_x + 120, bot_y + 18))

    hud_bg2 = pygame.Surface((180, 26), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.rect(hud_bg2, (0, 0, 0, 120), (0, 0, 180, 26))
    surface.blit(hud_bg2, (20, 20))
    surface.blit(font_terminal_small.render(f"HOSTILES: {len(STATE.enemies)}", True, C_TERMINAL), (30, 25))

    comms = "COMMS OFFLINE" if STATE.mission2_comms_cut else "COMMS: VASILE"
    comms_col = (220, 60, 55) if STATE.mission2_comms_cut else C_VASILE
    cs = font_terminal_small.render(comms, True, comms_col)
    surface.blit(cs, (WIDTH - cs.get_width() - 28, HEIGHT - 40))

    if STATE.mission2_stage == STAGE_COMBAT and getattr(STATE, "mission2_post_sniper_phase", "") in ("get_cover", "survive", "flank"):
        phase = getattr(STATE, "mission2_post_sniper_phase", "")
        exposed = _player_exposed_to_sniper()
        lock = getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_lock", 0)
        lock_max = max(1, getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_lock_max", SNIPER_LOCK_FRAMES))
        grace = getattr(STATE, "mission2_grace_timer", 0)

        if grace > 0:
            label = "SNIPER ACQUIRING"
            col = (255, 180, 80)
            ratio = 1.0 - grace / max(1, SNIPER_GRACE_FRAMES)
        elif exposed:
            label = "SNIPER LOCK"
            col = (255, 65, 40)
            ratio = lock / lock_max
        else:
            if phase == "get_cover":
                label = "BEHIND COVER"
            elif phase == "survive":
                label = "HOLD COVER"
            elif phase == "flank":
                label = "FLANK NOW"
            else:
                label = "LINE BROKEN"
            col = (90, 220, 135)
            ratio = lock / lock_max

        is_safe = not exposed and grace <= 0
        show_bar = not (phase == "flank" and is_safe)

        if is_safe:
            panel_w, panel_h = 220, 34
        else:
            panel_w, panel_h = 380, 58

        panel_x = WIDTH // 2 - panel_w // 2
        panel_y = 92

        panel = pygame.Surface((panel_w, panel_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        panel.fill((0, 0, 0, 190))
        pygame.draw.rect(panel, col, panel.get_rect(), 1)
        pygame.draw.rect(panel, (*col, 130), (0, 0, 5, panel_h))

        surface.blit(panel, (panel_x, panel_y))

        warn = font_terminal.render(label, True, col)
        surface.blit(warn, (WIDTH // 2 - warn.get_width() // 2, panel_y + 7))

        if show_bar:
            bar_w = 310 if not is_safe else 180
            bar_h = 9 if not is_safe else 5
            bx = WIDTH // 2 - bar_w // 2
            by = panel_y + (38 if not is_safe else 22)

            pygame.draw.rect(surface, (35, 20, 18), (bx, by, bar_w, bar_h))

            fill_w = int(bar_w * max(0.0, min(1.0, ratio)))

            if grace > 0:
                fill_col = (255, 180, 80)
            elif exposed:
                fill_col = (255, 55, 35)
            else:
                fill_col = (90, 220, 135)

            pygame.draw.rect(surface, fill_col, (bx, by, fill_w, bar_h))
            pygame.draw.rect(surface, col, (bx, by, bar_w, bar_h), 1)

            if exposed and lock > lock_max * 0.75 and (pygame.time.get_ticks() // 160) % 2 == 0:
                urgent = font_terminal_small.render("MOVE OR DIE", True, (255, 210, 190))
                surface.blit(urgent, (WIDTH // 2 - urgent.get_width() // 2, by + 13))

    if STATE.mission2_stage == STAGE_RUN:
        sec = max(0, STATE.mission2_countdown // 60 + 1)
        warn = font_speaker.render(f"RUN — TANKER DETONATION {sec}", True, (255, 75, 45))
        panel = pygame.Surface((warn.get_width() + 34, warn.get_height() + 14), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        panel.fill((0, 0, 0, 195))
        pygame.draw.rect(panel, (220, 60, 40), panel.get_rect(), 2)
        surface.blit(panel, (WIDTH // 2 - panel.get_width() // 2, 96))
        surface.blit(warn, (WIDTH // 2 - warn.get_width() // 2, 103))

    if STATE.mission2_stage == STAGE_UPLOAD:
        pct = int(STATE.mission2_upload_progress * 100)
        bar_w = 360
        bx = WIDTH // 2 - bar_w // 2
        by = HEIGHT - 116
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (0, 0, 0), (bx - 12, by - 28, bar_w + 24, 58))
        surface.blit(font_terminal.render(f"EVIDENCE UPLOAD {pct}%", True, C_TERMINAL), (bx, by - 23))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, (30, 30, 30), (bx, by + 7, bar_w, 10))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, C_TERMINAL, (bx, by + 7, int(bar_w * STATE.mission2_upload_progress), 10))
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, C_TERMINAL, (bx, by + 7, bar_w, 10), 1)


def _draw_radio_text(surface):
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_card", None) or STATE.mission2_stage in (STAGE_SNIPER, STAGE_BLACKOUT):
        return
    pinned = [ft for ft in STATE.floating_texts if len(ft) >= 6 and ft[5] == "pin"]
    for i, ft in enumerate(pinned[-2:]):
        alpha = min(230, ft[4] * 3)
        txt = font_terminal_small.render(ft[2], True, ft[3])
        bg = pygame.Surface((min(520, txt.get_width() + 16), txt.get_height() + 8), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        bg.fill((0, 0, 0, min(150, alpha)))
        pygame.draw.line(bg, (*ft[3], min(200, alpha)), (0, 0), (0, bg.get_height()), 3)
        x = WIDTH - bg.get_width() - 28
        y = LETTER_H + 74 + i * 30
        surface.blit(bg, (x, y))
        surface.blit(txt, (x + 10, y + 4))


def _draw_clues_note(surface):
    if not getattr(STATE, "mission2_all_clues_found", False):
        return
    if STATE.mission2_stage != STAGE_SNIPER:
        return
    phase = _sniper_phase_name()
    if phase != "movement_one":
        return
    txt = font_dialogue.render("The evidence was wrong.", True, C_TERMINAL)
    bg = pygame.Surface((txt.get_width() + 24, txt.get_height() + 12), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    bg.fill((0, 0, 0, 150))
    pygame.draw.rect(bg, C_TERMINAL, bg.get_rect(), 1)
    surface.blit(bg, (WIDTH // 2 - bg.get_width() // 2, HEIGHT - 90))
    surface.blit(txt, (WIDTH // 2 - txt.get_width() // 2, HEIGHT - 86))


def _draw_sniper_closeup_insert(surf, actor, cam_x, cam_y):
    sx, sy = _world(actor["pos"], cam_x, cam_y)
    panel_w, panel_h = 220, 280
    px = WIDTH // 2 - panel_w // 2
    py = 0
    panel = pygame.Surface((panel_w, panel_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    panel.fill((18, 16, 20, 235))
    name = actor.get("name", "???")
    fnt = pygame.font.Font(None, 22)
    name_s = fnt.render(name, True, (200, 195, 180))
    panel.blit(name_s, (panel_w // 2 - name_s.get_width() // 2, 8))
    portrait_rect = pygame.Rect(panel_w // 2 - 48, 40, 96, 120)
    pygame.draw.rect(panel, (35, 30, 28), portrait_rect, border_radius=4)
    pygame.draw.rect(panel, (80, 75, 65), portrait_rect, 1, border_radius=4)
    face_cx = portrait_rect.centerx
    face_cy = portrait_rect.centery + 6
    pygame.draw.circle(panel, (60, 55, 50), (face_cx, face_cy), 28)
    pygame.draw.circle(panel, (85, 78, 70), (face_cx - 8, face_cy - 6), 5)
    pygame.draw.circle(panel, (85, 78, 70), (face_cx + 8, face_cy - 6), 5)
    activity = actor.get("activity", "")
    if activity == "frozen":
        pygame.draw.line(panel, (100, 95, 85), (face_cx - 14, face_cy + 12), (face_cx + 14, face_cy + 12), 2)
    elif activity in ("shouting", "breaking"):
        mouth_pts = [(face_cx - 10, face_cy + 10), (face_cx, face_cy + 16), (face_cx + 10, face_cy + 10)]
        pygame.draw.lines(panel, (140, 130, 115), False, mouth_pts, 2)
    hr_fnt = pygame.font.Font(None, 16)
    hr = hr_fnt.render("HR 112 — SIG ACTIVE", True, (140, 35, 30))
    panel.blit(hr, (panel_w // 2 - hr.get_width() // 2, 168))
    for i in range(8):
        bar_h = 4 + ((i * 7 + getattr(STATE, "mission2_timer", 0) * 3) % 17)
        bar_x = 24 + i * 24
        bar_y = 194 + (22 - bar_h)
        c = (100 + i * 12, 180 + i * 6, 60 + i * 8)
        pygame.draw.rect(panel, c, (bar_x, bar_y, 12, bar_h))
    for i in range(6):
        sy2 = 222 + i * 8
        pygame.draw.line(panel, (55, 65, 50, 60 + i * 20), (16, sy2), (panel_w - 16, sy2), 1)
    pygame.draw.line(panel, (40, 120, 200, 100), (16, 260), (panel_w - 16, 260), 1)
    pygame.draw.line(panel, (40, 120, 200, 100), (16, 264), (panel_w - 16, 264), 1)
    audio_lines = ["V-16  //  MIC-DROP", "BW  4.2kHz  //  VOX"]
    for j, line in enumerate(audio_lines):
        al = hr_fnt.render(line, True, (80, 160, 210))
        panel.blit(al, (panel_w // 2 - al.get_width() // 2, 242 + j * 14))
    surf.blit(panel, (px, py))
    rain_dots = getattr(STATE, "mission2_rain_intensity", 0) > 0.5
    if rain_dots:
        for i in range(14):
            dx = (getattr(STATE, "mission2_timer", 0) * 5 + i * 31) % panel_w
            dy = (getattr(STATE, "mission2_timer", 0) * 7 + i * 53) % panel_h
            a = 40 + (i * 17) % 80
            panel.set_at((int(dx), int(dy)), (180, 195, 210, a))

def _draw_dead_air_overlay(surf):
    overlay = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    overlay.fill((8, 6, 10, 110))
    surf.blit(overlay, (0, 0))


def _draw_sniper_subtitles(surface, bar_h):
    pinned = [ft for ft in STATE.floating_texts if len(ft) >= 6 and ft[5] == "pin"]
    if not pinned:
        return
    newest = pinned[-1]
    for ft in pinned[:-1]:
        if ft[4] > 10:
            ft[4] = 10
    raw = newest[2] or ""
    color = newest[3] if len(newest) > 3 else (220, 220, 220)
    life = newest[4] if len(newest) > 4 else 60
    if ":" in raw:
        speaker, _, spoken = raw.partition(":")
        body_text = f"{speaker.strip()}: {spoken.strip().strip(chr(34))}"
    else:
        body_text = raw.strip()
    if not body_text:
        return
    alpha = 255 if life > 20 else max(0, life * 12)
    line = font_dialogue.render(body_text, True, (238, 235, 226))
    if line.get_width() > WIDTH - 80:
        line = font_terminal.render(body_text, True, (238, 235, 226))
    line.set_alpha(alpha)
    bar_top = HEIGHT - bar_h
    card_h = min(bar_h - 6, line.get_height() + 10)
    card_w = min(WIDTH - 60, line.get_width() + 36)
    x = WIDTH // 2 - card_w // 2
    y = bar_top + (bar_h - card_h) // 2
    bg = pygame.Surface((card_w, card_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    bg.fill((0, 0, 0, min(210, alpha)))
    pygame.draw.rect(bg, (*color, min(200, alpha)), (0, 0, 4, card_h))
    surface.blit(bg, (x, y))
    surface.blit(line, (x + 18, y + (card_h - line.get_height()) // 2))


def _draw_sniper_overlay(surface):
    if STATE.mission2_stage != STAGE_SNIPER:
        return
    phase = _sniper_phase_name()
    pt = int(getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_phase_timer", 0))
    cam_x = int(round(STATE.camera_x))
    cam_y = int(round(STATE.camera_y))

    def clamp(v, lo=0, hi=255):
        return max(lo, min(hi, int(v)))

    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_origin_revealed", False):
        pulse = (math.sin(STATE.mission2_timer * 0.06) + 1) / 2
        sx, sy = _world(SNIPER_NEST_POS, cam_x, cam_y)
        gl = pygame.Surface((60, 60), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        a = 90 + int(80 * pulse)
        pygame.draw.circle(gl, (255, 255, 210, a), (30, 30), 4)
        pygame.draw.circle(gl, (255, 255, 220, a // 2), (30, 30), 9)
        surface.blit(gl, (sx - 30, sy - 30))

    sil = getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_silhouette_flash", 0)
    if sil > 0:
        sx, sy = _world(SNIPER_NEST_POS, cam_x, cam_y)
        a = clamp(sil * 12, 0, 220)
        s = pygame.Surface((46, 58), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.ellipse(s, (10, 8, 10, a), (16, 4, 14, 14))
        pygame.draw.polygon(s, (10, 8, 10, a), [(10, 22), (36, 22), (40, 50), (6, 50)])
        pygame.draw.line(s, (10, 8, 10, a), (24, 26), (46, 18), 3)
        surface.blit(s, (sx - 23, sy - 48))

    flash = getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_flash", 0)
    if flash > 0:
        full = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        full.fill((255, 245, 220, clamp(flash * 12, 0, 200) // 6))
        surface.blit(full, (0, 0))

    bar_h = 64
    bar = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, bar_h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    bar.fill((0, 0, 0, 235))
    surface.blit(bar, (0, 0))
    surface.blit(bar, (0, HEIGHT - bar_h))

    label = {"regroup": "ROUTE 12 \u00b7 CHECKPOINT 12",
             "movement_one": "ROUTE 12 \u00b7 CONTACT",
             "movement_two": "ROUTE 12 \u00b7 THE SQUAD",
             "movement_three": "ROUTE 12 \u00b7 YOU'RE NEXT"}.get(phase, "ROUTE 12")
    stamp = font_terminal_small.render(label, True, (180, 60, 60))
    surface.blit(stamp, (28, bar_h // 2 - stamp.get_height() // 2))
    if (pygame.time.get_ticks() // 500) % 2 == 0:
        rec = font_terminal_small.render("\u25cf REC", True, (220, 50, 50))
        surface.blit(rec, (WIDTH - rec.get_width() - 28, bar_h // 2 - rec.get_height() // 2))

    s_start = _beat("movement_one", "silence_start", 999999)
    s_len = _beat("movement_one", "silence_frames", 110)
    if phase == "movement_one" and s_start <= pt < s_start + s_len:
        _draw_dead_air_overlay(surface)

    _draw_sniper_callout(surface, cam_x, cam_y)
    _draw_sniper_subtitles(surface, bar_h)
    _draw_tinnitus_overlay(surface)
    draw_vignette(surface, strength=210)


def clamp_alpha(v):
    return max(0, min(255, int(v)))


def _wrap_text(text, font, max_width):
    """Simple word-wrap that respects an existing pygame font."""
    words = text.split()
    if not words:
        return [""]
    lines = []
    cur = words[0]
    for w in words[1:]:
        trial = cur + " " + w
        if font.size(trial)[0] <= max_width:
            cur = trial
        else:
            lines.append(cur)
            cur = w
    lines.append(cur)
    return lines

def _draw_blackout_overlay(surface):
    if STATE.mission2_stage != STAGE_BLACKOUT:
        return
    fade = min(220, 220 - max(0, STATE.mission2_timer - 70))
    overlay = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    overlay.fill((0, 0, 0, max(90, fade)))
    surface.blit(overlay, (0, 0))
    if 35 < STATE.mission2_timer < 125:
        txt = font_dialogue.render('VASILE: "Biribel, the file said there was a second--"', True, C_VASILE)
        surface.blit(txt, (WIDTH // 2 - txt.get_width() // 2, HEIGHT // 2 - 52))
    if STATE.mission2_timer > 95:
        txt = font_speaker.render("COMMS LOST", True, (160, 165, 175))
        surface.blit(txt, (WIDTH // 2 - txt.get_width() // 2, HEIGHT // 2 - 10))


def game_draw(fcx: float, fcy: float) -> None:
    screen.fill((25, 26, 26))
    # Render from an integer camera.  This removes the tile/prop seam shimmer
    # that happens when the camera scrolls by fractional pixels.
    cam_x, cam_y = int(round(fcx)), int(round(fcy))

    start_col = max(0, int(cam_x // TILE_SIZE) - 1)
    end_col = min(MAP_W, int((cam_x + WIDTH) // TILE_SIZE) + 2)
    start_row = max(0, int(cam_y // TILE_SIZE) - 1)
    end_row = min(MAP_H, int((cam_y + HEIGHT) // TILE_SIZE) + 2)

    tile_base = {
        ".": (32, 33, 33),   # wet asphalt / highway
        "g": (40, 36, 32),   # muddy shoulder
        "d": (29, 35, 34),   # drainage ditch
        "s": (34, 37, 35),   # service road
        "O": (29, 36, 38),   # interior/control sector
        "X": (29, 38, 32),
        "T": (35, 32, 28),
        "C": (54, 54, 52),
        "B": (82, 82, 76),
    }
    for row in range(start_row, end_row):
        for col in range(start_col, end_col):
            sx, sy = col * TILE_SIZE - cam_x, row * TILE_SIZE - cam_y
            tile = MISSION2_MAP[row][col]
            base = tile_base.get(tile, (31, 32, 32))
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, base, (sx, sy, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE))
            if (row + col) % 3 == 0:
                seam = (25, 26, 26) if tile in ".TOX" else (31, 30, 28)
                pygame.draw.line(screen, seam, (sx, sy + TILE_SIZE - 1), (sx + TILE_SIZE, sy + TILE_SIZE - 1), 1)
            # Deterministic micro-detail keeps the big map from reading as an
            # empty grid while avoiding per-frame random shimmer.
            seed = row * 97 + col * 131
            if tile == ".":
                if seed % 11 == 0:
                    pygame.draw.line(screen, (22, 22, 22), (sx + 10, sy + 18), (sx + 42, sy + 28), 1)
                if seed % 17 == 0:
                    pygame.draw.line(screen, (45, 42, 38), (sx + 4, sy + 50), (sx + 58, sy + 44), 2)
            elif tile == "g":
                if seed % 7 == 0:
                    pygame.draw.circle(screen, (55, 48, 38), (sx + 18, sy + 38), 3)
                if seed % 13 == 0:
                    pygame.draw.line(screen, (48, 58, 42), (sx + 45, sy + 25), (sx + 49, sy + 18), 1)
            elif tile == "d":
                if seed % 5 == 0:
                    pygame.draw.ellipse(screen, (24, 31, 38), (sx + 8, sy + 34, 34, 13))
            elif tile in "sO":
                if seed % 9 == 0:
                    pygame.draw.rect(screen, (23, 27, 28), (sx + 8, sy + 8, 12, 3))

    # Vertical lane stripes for the expanded Route 12 corridor.
    for xcol in (28, 36, 44):
        x = xcol * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE // 2 - cam_x
        for y in range(-int(cam_y) % 150 - 150, HEIGHT + 150, 150):
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (145, 130, 60), (x, y, 5, 72))

    for v in STATE.mission2_vehicles:
        _draw_vehicle(screen, v["rect"], v["kind"], cam_x, cam_y)

    barrier_ids = {id(r) for r in STATE.mission2_barriers}
    for wall in STATE.walls:
        if wall is STATE.mission2_tanker_rect:
            continue
        dr = pygame.Rect(wall.x - cam_x, wall.y - cam_y, wall.w, wall.h)
        if id(wall) in barrier_ids:
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (92, 92, 84), dr, border_radius=4)
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (38, 38, 36), dr, 2, border_radius=4)
            pygame.draw.line(screen, (130, 130, 120), (dr.x + 6, dr.y + 8), (dr.right - 6, dr.y + 8), 1)
        else:
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (63, 61, 58), dr)
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (106, 100, 90), dr, 2)

    _draw_gantry(screen, cam_x, cam_y)

    for decal in STATE.blood_decals[:]:
        surf = pygame.Surface((decal[3] * 2, decal[3] * 2), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.circle(surf, (130, 10, 10, int(decal[2])), (decal[3], decal[3]), decal[3])
        screen.blit(surf, (decal[0] - decal[3] - cam_x, decal[1] - decal[3] - cam_y))
        decal[2] -= 0.25
        if decal[2] <= 0:
            STATE.blood_decals.remove(decal)

    _draw_tanker(screen, cam_x, cam_y)
    for fz in STATE.fire_zones:
        fz.draw(screen, cam_x, cam_y)
    _draw_story_props(screen, cam_x, cam_y)

    _draw_interactables(screen, cam_x, cam_y)

    for b in STATE.bullets:
        pygame.draw.circle(screen, C_BULLET, (int(b["x"] - cam_x), int(b["y"] - cam_y)), 2)
        pygame.draw.line(screen, (255, 220, 140), (b["x"] - cam_x, b["y"] - cam_y), (b.get("prev_x", b["x"] - b["dx"]) - cam_x, b.get("prev_y", b["y"] - b["dy"]) - cam_y), 2)

    for tr in STATE.mission2_sniper_traces:
        sx1, sy1 = _world(tr["from"], cam_x, cam_y)
        sx2, sy2 = _world(tr["to"], cam_x, cam_y)
        life_pct = max(0, min(1, tr["life"] / max(tr.get("max_life", 1), 1)))
        a = max(0, min(255, int(life_pct * 200)))
        kind = tr.get("kind", "body")
        if kind == "near_miss":
            base_c = (255, 220, 180)
        elif kind == "concrete":
            base_c = (200, 185, 160)
        else:
            base_c = (220, 180, 170)
        segments = 9
        seg_dist = 6
        trace_surf = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        for i in range(segments):
            frac = i / segments
            jfrac = (i + 1) / segments
            mx1 = sx1 + (sx2 - sx1) * frac + random.randint(-3, 3)
            my1 = sy1 + (sy2 - sy1) * frac + random.randint(-3, 3)
            mx2 = sx1 + (sx2 - sx1) * jfrac + random.randint(-3, 3)
            my2 = sy1 + (sy2 - sy1) * jfrac + random.randint(-3, 3)
            seg_a = max(0, a - i * 15)
            thickness = max(1, 4 - i // 3)
            pygame.draw.line(trace_surf, (*base_c, seg_a), (mx1, my1), (mx2, my2), thickness)
        screen.blit(trace_surf, (0, 0))
        if life_pct > 0.6:
            spark = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
            end_a = int((life_pct - 0.6) / 0.4 * 180)
            pygame.draw.circle(spark, (255, 200, 150, end_a), (int(sx2), int(sy2)), 4)
            pygame.draw.circle(spark, (255, 240, 220, end_a // 2), (int(sx2), int(sy2)), 8)
            screen.blit(spark, (0, 0))

    _draw_sniper_lasers_world(screen, cam_x, cam_y)
    _draw_sniper_muzzle_flashes(screen, cam_x, cam_y)

    for b in STATE.thrown_bottles:
        t = b["t"]
        bx = b["x"] - cam_x
        by = b["y"] - cam_y - math.sin(t * math.pi) * 40
        pygame.draw.circle(screen, (255, 120, 35), (int(bx), int(by)), 5)
        pygame.draw.line(screen, (90, 55, 25), (bx, by), (bx + 3, by - 12), 3)

    for actor in STATE.mission2_squad:
        _draw_actor(screen, actor, cam_x, cam_y)

    for e in STATE.enemies:
        e.draw(screen, cam_x, cam_y)

    reload_progress = 1.0 - STATE.reload_timer / 60.0 if STATE.is_reloading and STATE.reload_timer > 0 else 0.0
    melee_progress = 1.0 - STATE.melee_timer / 18.0 if STATE.melee_active and STATE.melee_timer > 0 else 0.0
    if STATE.player:
        STATE.player.draw(
            screen,
            cam_x,
            cam_y,
            STATE.walls,
            STATE.is_reloading,
            reload_progress,
            STATE.melee_active,
            melee_progress,
            is_moving=not STATE.player_dead and _last_keys and (_last_keys[pygame.K_w] or _last_keys[pygame.K_s] or _last_keys[pygame.K_a] or _last_keys[pygame.K_d]),
        )

    for p in STATE.particles:
        p.draw(screen, cam_x, cam_y)
    for c in STATE.casings:
        c.draw(screen, cam_x, cam_y)
    for pu in STATE.pickups:
        px = pu[0] - cam_x
        py = pu[1] - cam_y + math.sin(time.time() * 3 + pu[0]) * 3
        if pu[2] == "bandage":
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (240, 240, 240), (px - 10, py - 10, 20, 20), border_radius=3)
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (220, 40, 40), (px - 2, py - 6, 4, 12))
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (220, 40, 40), (px - 6, py - 2, 12, 4))
        else:
            pygame.draw.rect(screen, (95, 90, 82), (px - 7, py - 12, 14, 24))

    # Rain overlay.  Suppressed during the kill-box silence; intensity > 1.0
    # spawns extra drops (rain spike on first kill -- Point 9 of the rework).
    rain_suppressed = getattr(STATE, "mission2_rain_suppressed", False)
    rain_intensity = getattr(STATE, "mission2_rain_intensity", 1.0)
    if not rain_suppressed:
        for drop in STATE.mission2_rain_drops:
            x, y, length = drop
            pygame.draw.line(screen, C_RAIN, (x, y), (x - 4, y + length), 1)
            drop[0] -= 3
            drop[1] += length
            if drop[1] > HEIGHT + 30:
                drop[0] = random.randint(0, WIDTH)
                drop[1] = random.randint(-90, -10)
        # Extra sheets of rain when intensity is boosted (first-kill spike).
        if rain_intensity > 1.05:
            extra = int(len(STATE.mission2_rain_drops) * (rain_intensity - 1.0))
            for _ in range(extra):
                x = random.randint(0, WIDTH)
                y = random.randint(0, HEIGHT)
                length = random.randint(10, 24)
                pygame.draw.line(screen, C_RAIN, (x, y), (x - 4, y + length), 1)
        # Decay intensity back toward 1.0 so the spike fades naturally.
        if rain_intensity > 1.01:
            STATE.mission2_rain_intensity = max(1.0, rain_intensity - 0.004)

    if STATE.muzzle_flash_timer > 0 and STATE.player:
        tip_x, tip_y = STATE.player.barrel_tip()
        screen.blit(MUZZLE_FLASH, (int(tip_x - cam_x - 40), int(tip_y - cam_y - 40)))
        STATE.muzzle_flash_timer -= 1

    ch_x, ch_y = get_scaled_mouse()
    if not getattr(STATE, "mission2_card", None) and STATE.mission2_stage not in (STAGE_SNIPER, STAGE_BLACKOUT):
        pygame.draw.circle(screen, C_ORANGE, (ch_x, ch_y), 14, 1)
        pygame.draw.circle(screen, C_ORANGE, (ch_x, ch_y), 2)
        for x1, y1, x2, y2 in [(ch_x - 12, ch_y, ch_x - 3, ch_y), (ch_x + 3, ch_y, ch_x + 12, ch_y), (ch_x, ch_y - 12, ch_x, ch_y - 3), (ch_x, ch_y + 3, ch_x, ch_y + 12)]:
            pygame.draw.line(screen, C_ORANGE, (x1, y1), (x2, y2), 1)

    if STATE.player_dead:
        overlay = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        overlay.fill((120, 10, 10, 90))
        screen.blit(overlay, (0, 0))
    if STATE.gc_flash_timer > 0 and not getattr(STATE, "reduced_motion", False):
        flash = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        flash.fill((255, 245, 220, min(255, STATE.gc_flash_timer * 18)))
        screen.blit(flash, (0, 0))

    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_kill_flash", 0) > 0:
        a = min(220, STATE.mission2_sniper_kill_flash * 10)
        flash = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        flash.fill((255, 40, 25, a))
        screen.blit(flash, (0, 0))

    draw_vignette(screen, strength=150)
    draw_scanlines(screen)

    if STATE.mission2_stage == STAGE_SNIPER:
        _draw_sniper_overlay(screen)
    elif STATE.mission2_stage == STAGE_BLACKOUT:
        _draw_blackout_overlay(screen)
    else:
        _draw_hud(screen)
        _draw_radio_text(screen)
        if STATE.show_minimap and STATE.minimap:
            STATE.minimap.draw(screen, STATE.player, STATE.enemies, cam_x, cam_y, notebook_pos=UPLOAD_TERMINAL_POS, exfil_pos=EXFIL_POS)
    _draw_card(screen)

    # Hard black cut (Point 11): overrides everything for a few frames at the
    # cutscene -> gameplay handoff.  Decrement here so the counter ticks once
    # per drawn frame regardless of update timing.
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_hard_black", 0) > 0:
        screen.fill((0, 0, 0))
        STATE.mission2_hard_black -= 1


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# Data helper accessors
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
def get_mission2_overview():
    return {
        "id": MISSION2_ID,
        "title": MISSION2_TITLE,
        "location": MISSION2_LOCATION,
        "style": MISSION2_PRIMARY_STYLE,
        "story_flags": MISSION2_STORY_FLAGS.copy(),
    }


def get_opening_cinematic():
    return list(OPENING_CINEMATIC)


def get_squadmate_death_cutscene():
    return list(SQUADMATE_DEATH_CUTSCENE)


def get_mission_phases():
    return list(MISSION_PHASES)


def get_mission2_zones():
    return {
        name: {
            "label": data["label"],
            "tile_rect": (data["tile_rect"].x, data["tile_rect"].y, data["tile_rect"].w, data["tile_rect"].h),
        }
        for name, data in ZONE_DEFS.items()
    }


def get_mission2_clue_defs():
    return [
        {"id": c["id"], "name": c["name"], "zone": c["zone"], "pos": c["pos"]}
        for c in MISSION2_CLUE_DEFS
    ]


def get_ending_cinematic():
    return list(mission2_ending_shots)


def get_all_dialogue_lines():
    out = []
    for group in (mission2_intro_shots, mission2_ending_shots, SQUADMATE_DEATH_CUTSCENE):
        for beat in group:
            if beat.get("speaker") and beat.get("text"):
                out.append({"speaker": beat["speaker"], "text": beat["text"]})
    return out


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print(f"{MISSION2_TITLE} — clean vertical slice")
    print(f"Map: {MAP_W}x{MAP_H}")
    print(f"Intro shots: {len(mission2_intro_shots)}")
    print(f"Ending shots: {len(mission2_ending_shots)}")

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# save_system.py
# ------------------------------------------------------------

"""Simple 3-slot JSON save system for campaign progress."""
import json
import os
import time

SAVE_FILE = os.path.join(os.path.abspath("."), "save_data.json")
SLOT_COUNT = 3
_current_slot = 0


def _empty_slot(slot_index):
    return {
        "slot": slot_index,
        "occupied": False,
        "last_completed_mission": "",
        "last_completed_label": "No missions completed",
        "total_time": "00:00",
        "updated_at": 0,
        "stats": {},
        "flags": {},
    }


def _default_data():
    return {"version": 1, "slots": [_empty_slot(i) for i in range(SLOT_COUNT)]}


def load_data():
    if not os.path.exists(SAVE_FILE):
        return _default_data()
    try:
        with open(SAVE_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            data = json.load(f)
    except Exception:
        return _default_data()
    slots = data.get("slots", [])
    fixed = _default_data()
    for i in range(SLOT_COUNT):
        if i < len(slots) and isinstance(slots[i], dict):
            merged = _empty_slot(i)
            merged.update(slots[i])
            merged["slot"] = i
            fixed["slots"][i] = merged
    return fixed


def save_data(data):
    try:
        with open(SAVE_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(data, f, indent=2)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Save failed: {e}")


def select_slot(slot_index):
    global _current_slot
    _current_slot = max(0, min(SLOT_COUNT - 1, int(slot_index)))


def current_slot_index():
    return _current_slot


def get_slot(slot_index=None):
    idx = _current_slot if slot_index is None else max(0, min(SLOT_COUNT - 1, int(slot_index)))
    return load_data()["slots"][idx]


def slot_has_save(slot_index=None):
    return bool(get_slot(slot_index).get("occupied"))


def start_new_campaign(slot_index=None):
    idx = _current_slot if slot_index is None else max(0, min(SLOT_COUNT - 1, int(slot_index)))
    data = load_data()
    slot = _empty_slot(idx)
    slot.update({
        "occupied": True,
        "last_completed_mission": "",
        "last_completed_label": "Campaign started — Mission 1 in progress",
        "updated_at": int(time.time()),
        "stats": {},
        "flags": {"mission1_started": True},
    })
    data["slots"][idx] = slot
    save_data(data)


def save_mission_complete(mission_id, label, total_time, stats=None, flags=None, slot_index=None):
    idx = _current_slot if slot_index is None else max(0, min(SLOT_COUNT - 1, int(slot_index)))
    data = load_data()
    slot = _empty_slot(idx)
    slot.update({
        "occupied": True,
        "last_completed_mission": mission_id,
        "last_completed_label": label,
        "total_time": total_time,
        "updated_at": int(time.time()),
        "stats": stats or {},
        "flags": flags or {},
    })
    data["slots"][idx] = slot
    save_data(data)


def format_timestamp(ts):
    if not ts:
        return "never"
    try:
        return time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M", time.localtime(ts))
    except Exception:
        return "unknown"


def has_any_save():
    return any(bool(slot.get("occupied")) for slot in load_data().get("slots", []))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# settings_store.py
# ------------------------------------------------------------

"""Persistent user settings (display, audio, accessibility).

Stored as settings.json next to save_data.json. Loaded once on startup,
saved whenever the user changes an option in the settings menu.
"""
import json
import os

SETTINGS_FILE = os.path.join(os.path.abspath("."), "settings.json")

DEFAULTS = {
    "version": 1,
    # Display
    "display_mode": 0,        # 0 = Windowed, 1 = Fullscreen
    "resolution_idx": 0,      # index into menu.SETTINGS_RESOLUTIONS
    # Audio (0-100 ints, mirroring the existing master slider)
    "master_volume": 80,
    "music_volume": 80,
    "sfx_volume": 100,
    # Accessibility
    "reduced_motion": False,  # disables screen shake + bright flash overlays
}


def load():
    """Return the current settings dict, falling back to defaults."""
    data = dict(DEFAULTS)
    if not os.path.exists(SETTINGS_FILE):
        return data
    try:
        with open(SETTINGS_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            loaded = json.load(f)
        if isinstance(loaded, dict):
            for key, default_val in DEFAULTS.items():
                if key in loaded and isinstance(loaded[key], type(default_val)):
                    data[key] = loaded[key]
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Settings load failed, using defaults: {e}")
    return data


def save(data):
    """Persist the given settings dict. Silently ignores write errors."""
    try:
        payload = dict(DEFAULTS)
        payload.update({k: v for k, v in data.items() if k in DEFAULTS})
        with open(SETTINGS_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(payload, f, indent=2)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Settings save failed: {e}")
